TL;DR: 本文从机制可解释性角度研究大语言模型（LLM）的安全性，提出并验证了一个多阶段的'安全电路'结构，包括有害检测头、安全神经元和拒绝生成头。通过针对性干预，证明该电路在多个LLM架构和攻击场景下具有稳定性，并通过权重缩放实验验证其功能相关性，显著提升对抗攻击下的安全性，同时保持较低的准确率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管进行了大量对齐工作，大语言模型仍容易在对抗性提示下生成不安全内容，且其内部安全行为的机制尚不清晰，亟需从机制可解释性角度理解安全行为的实现方式。

Method: 采用目标注意力头和神经元级干预，进行因果分析；通过架构保持的权重缩放作为机制探针，验证电路的功能重要性。

Result: 在六种LLM中，基于电路指导的权重缩放使对抗攻击下的安全率提高26.5%，仅导致标准基准上1.7%的准确率下降。该安全电路在多种架构和攻击设置中重复出现，表明其稳定性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: 研究支持将大语言模型安全性理解为电路级别的组织行为，表明机制抽象能够揭示对齐行为中稳定且可迁移的模式。

Abstract: Despite extensive alignment efforts, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to generating unsafe content under adversarial prompting, yet the internal mechanisms by which safety behaviors are implemented remain poorly understood. We study LLM safety from a mechanistic interpretability perspective and characterize a multi-stage *safety circuit* that organizes refusal behavior, consisting of (i) $\textbf{Harmful Detection Heads}$ that respond to harmful inputs, (ii) $\textbf{Safety Neurons}$ that mediate and stabilize safety signals in the residual stream, and (iii) $\textbf{Refusal Heads}$ that translate these signals into safe response generation. Using targeted attention-head and neuron-level interventions, we provide causal evidence consistent with this circuit organization, showing that suppressing upstream Harmful Detection Heads disrupts downstream refusal behavior and that safety neurons mediate this interaction. We validate that this decomposition recurs across multiple LLM architectures and adversarial attack settings, and use simple, architecture-preserving weight scaling as a mechanistic probe to test its functional relevance. Across six LLMs, circuit-guided scaling improves safety rates under attacks by 26.5%, while incurring only a 1.7% accuracy drop across four standard benchmarks. Overall, our results support a circuit-level interpretation of LLM safety and suggest that mechanistic abstractions can reveal stable and transferable patterns underlying aligned behavior.

</details>


### [101] [Zero-Shot Respiratory Sound Classification through LLM-Augmented Audio-Text Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00055)
*Mustafa Talha İlerisoy,Hung Manh Pham,Mathias Funk,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种框架，通过将自监督呼吸编码器与医学术语在共享潜在空间中对齐，使其具备零样本推理能力。为解决配对数据稀缺问题，利用医学大模型从元数据生成结构化报告，作为对比学习的密集语义锚点。训练结合sigmoid对比损失、编码器原有的自监督目标及相似性感知负采样，以增强病理边界。在6个数据集上的9项任务中，该方法实现61.3%的平均零样本AUC，优于CLAP（51.4%）和Qwen2-Audio（54.9%），且仅使用全规模基线43%的数据即达到最高线性探测AUC（71.6%），表明结构化语义对齐在临床诊断中优于大规模通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督呼吸编码器缺乏临床领域语义基础，限制了其在无任务特定标注数据情况下的零样本推理能力。

Method: 将自监督呼吸编码器与医学术语在共享潜在空间中对齐；利用医学大模型从元数据生成结构化报告作为语义锚点；结合sigmoid对比损失、原始SSL目标及相似性感知负采样进行训练。

Result: 在6个数据集的9项任务中，实现61.3%的平均零样本AUC，优于CLAP（51.4%）和Qwen2-Audio（54.9%）；线性探测AUC达71.6%，仅用43%数据量即超越全规模基线。

Conclusion: 结构化语义对齐显著提升自监督编码器在临床诊断中的零样本性能，优于大规模通用模型，验证了其在医疗领域的有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Self-supervised respiratory encoders lack semantic grounding in clinical domain needed for zero-shot inference, limiting their utility without task-specific labeled data. We propose a framework that aligns these encoders with medical terminology in a shared latent space turning them into a zero-shot-capable foundation model. To address paired data scarcity, we use a medical LLM to synthesize structured reports from metadata, creating dense semantic anchors for contrastive learning. Our training combines a sigmoid-based contrastive loss with encoder's native SSL objective and similarity-aware negative sampling to sharpen pathological boundaries. Across 9 tasks on 6 datasets, our method achieves a 61.3% mean zero-shot AUC, surpassing CLAP (51.4%) and Qwen2-Audio (54.9%) while reaching the highest linear probing AUC (71.6%) with only 43% of data used by full-scale baselines, showing that structured semantic alignment outperforms large-scale, general-purpose models in clinical diagnostics.

</details>


### [102] [ValueGraph: Value-Signal Guided Graph Pre-training for Contextualized User Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00057)
*Yitong Han,Wei Gao,Yi Zhao,Prasanta Bhattacharya,Fengzhu Zeng,Mohammad Amanlou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ValueGraph框架，利用自动推断的道德价值信号作为噪声辅助信号，增强用户表示学习。通过帖子-回复图学习语义和结构表示，并使用对比和聚类目标对齐用户，将推断的价值视为软约束而非金标准标签。在立场检测和推特机器人检测任务中均优于强文本基、图基和纯文本LLM基线，证明了价值信号作为社会感知用户建模的有益归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户表示方法忽视了在线言论中隐含的价值相关维度，而用户行为不仅受其言论内容和互动对象影响，还受其表达态度的价值信号影响。因此，需要一种能够整合价值信号的用户表示学习框架。

Method: 提出ValueGraph，一个基于图预训练的框架，从帖子-回复图中自动推断道德价值信号，将其作为软约束用于上下文用户表示学习。通过对比学习和聚类目标，实现用户间的相对价值相似性对齐，同时结合语义与结构信息进行表示学习。

Result: 在立场检测和推特机器人检测任务上，ValueGraph consistently超越了多种强基线模型（包括文本基、图基和纯文本LLM基线），验证了价值信号引导的有效性。

Conclusion: 价值信号可作为社会知情用户建模中的有效归纳偏置，通过引入自动推断的价值信号作为软约束，显著提升用户表示的质量，为理解用户行为提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Value signals are aggregated user-level moral representations that capture users' inferred value-related tendencies from their online discourse. User behavior on social media is shaped not only by what users say or whom they interact with, but also by the value signal through which they express attitudes. Existing user representation methods largely miss this value-relevant dimension. We propose ValueGraph, a graph pre-training framework that uses automatically inferred moral-value signals as noisy auxiliary signals for contextualized user representation. From post-reply graphs, ValueGraph learns semantic and structural representations and further aligns users through relative value similarity with contrastive and clustering objectives. Rather than treating inferred values as gold psychological labels, ValueGraph uses them as soft constraints for representation learning. Experiments on stance detection and twitter bot detection show consistent gains over strong text-based, graph-based, and text-only LLM baselines, highlighting value-signal guidance as a useful inductive bias for socially informed user modeling.

</details>


### [103] [CUDA-Harness: Harnessing Agentic CUDA Kernel Generation and Optimization from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00058)
*Qi Fan,An Zou,Yehan Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CUDA-Harness框架，实现从自然语言到高性能CUDA内核的智能生成与优化。通过中间结构化生成、基于合成的验证和反馈自适应演化策略，解决文本到CUDA（Text2CUDA）生成中的语义理解、奖励劫持和性能优化难题。实验表明该框架在不同LLM、硬件平台及C-to-CUDA转换中均具良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成高性能CUDA内核需要深厚的专业知识，而现有基于大语言模型的方法多聚焦于从PyTorch等高层框架转译（Torch2CUDA），难以有效处理自然语言到低层内核的直接生成（Text2CUDA），且易受预设测试输入导致的奖励劫持影响。因此亟需一种能兼顾语义理解、正确性验证与性能优化的自动化生成框架。

Method: 提出CUDA-Harness框架，包含三项核心技术：1）中间结构化生成（Intermediate-Structured Generation），将高层语义映射至低层内核实现；2）基于合成的验证（Synthesis-Based Verification），通过隔离测试数据缓解奖励劫持；3）反馈自适应演化（Feedback-Adaptive Evolution），以正确性优先的方式迭代优化内核性能。

Result: 在多项实验中，CUDA-Harness展现出优异的生成质量与性能表现，可有效生成正确且高效的CUDA内核。评估结果还验证了其在不同大语言模型、硬件平台以及从C语言到CUDA的转换任务中的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: CUDA-Harness成功实现了从自然语言到高效CUDA内核的自动化生成与优化，为降低开发门槛、提升并行计算效率提供了有效解决方案，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Developing high-performance CUDA kernels demands specialized knowledge in algorithm implementation, correctness validation, and hardware-aware parallel optimization, creating a substantial expertise barrier and making generating CUDA kernels directly from natural language (Text2CUDA) essential. Meanwhile, the general-purpose code generation capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) prompts a series of works exploring LLM-based CUDA kernel generation. They mainly focus on transpilation from high-level frameworks such as PyTorch to CUDA (Torch2CUDA) rather than Text2CUDA, where models must understand the high-level input semantics and handle low-level kernel implementation and validation. Additionally, these methods are vulnerable to reward hacking due to reliance on predefined test inputs. In this paper, we propose CUDA-Harness, a framework for harnessing agentic CUDA kernel generation and optimization from natural language. Specifically, we introduce Intermediate-Structured Generation to connect high-level semantic understanding with low-level kernel generation. To dilute reward hacking in Text2CUDA, we construct Synthesis-Based Verification to provide isolated test data and progressive validation. Furthermore, we propose Feedback-Adaptive Evolution, a kernel evolution strategy that prioritizes correctness while optimizing performance. Finally, through extensive experiments, we demonstrate the effectiveness of CUDA-Harness, with further evaluations illustrating generalization across LLMs, hardware platforms, and to C-to-CUDA transpilation.

</details>


### [104] [RePro: Proof-Verified Benchmark Rewriting for Reliable Evaluation of LLM Mathematical Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00062)
*Xiyuan Zhou,Zhuoqi Li,Xinlei Wang,Yirui He,Yuhao Wu,Yuheng Cheng,Yan Xu,Junhua Zhao,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RePro 是首个将 Lean 证明器集成到基准重写中的框架，通过 Lean 验证的证明确保问题和答案的正确性。在 GSM8K 和 MATH 上实验显示，其重写后的数据实例达到 100% 的有效性、可行性与答案正确性，而现有方法仍存在无效或错误实例。部分模型在新基准上准确率下降，表明其性能可能受表面和结构变化影响，反映记忆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重写的评估方法虽能缓解记忆问题，但缺乏对问题有效性和答案正确性的保障，导致评估不可靠。

Method: 提出 RePro 框架，利用 Lean 优化的神经自动定理证明器对数学问题进行重写，并通过 Lean 证明验证答案的正确性。

Result: RePro 重写后的实例在 GSM8K 和 MATH 上均实现 100% 的问题有效性、可行性和答案正确性；多个模型在新基准上准确率下降，表明其性能易受表面和结构变化影响，可能源于记忆而非真实推理能力。

Conclusion: RePro 为大语言模型在数学求解上的评估提供了可靠、可验证的基准，有助于更真实地衡量模型的推理能力，避免因数据污染带来的误判。

Abstract: Data contamination undermines the reliable evaluation of large language models (LLMs) on mathematical problem solving. While rewriting-based evaluation mitigates memorization, existing methods lack guarantees of problem validity and answer correctness. We propose Proof-Verified Benchmark Rewriting (RePro), the first framework to integrate Lean-oriented neural automated theorem provers (ATPs) into benchmark rewriting, which rewrites problems and regenerates answers with correctness ensured by Lean-verified proofs. Experiments on GSM8K and MATH show that RePro's retained rewritten instances achieve 100% well-definedness, feasibility, and answer correctness, while existing methods still produce invalid or incorrect instances. Moreover, several models exhibit accuracy drops on proof-verified rewritten benchmarks, suggesting that their performance is sensitive to surface-level and structural variations and may partly reflect memorization effects. Our source code and data are available at https://github.com/AI4Engi/RePro.

</details>


### [105] [Medical Causal Hypothesis Verification with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00063)
*Safiyyah Ahmed,Abrar Ansari,Md Aminul Islam,Elena Zheleva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了八种大语言模型（LLMs）在验证医学因果假设方面的表现，发现尽管其召回率较高，但在提供有效科学文献和拒绝无支持假设方面表现不佳，表明当前LLMs尚无法完全信赖用于生物医学文献中的因果关系验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在搜索与信息检索中的广泛应用，特别是在医疗等高风险领域，亟需评估其可靠性。现有研究表明，虽然LLMs能回答疾病、症状和治疗相关问题，但其在评估因果关系及基于已验证科学证据得出结论方面的能力仍不明确。

Method: 提出了一种用于因果假设验证的评估框架，系统评估8种LLMs在17个医学因果假设上的表现，通过六项标准对提供的科学证据进行标注（共1,067个标注点），并使用九项评估指标进行分析。

Result: LLMs表现出较强的召回能力，但在提供有效科学文章、支持性证据以及拒绝无支持假设方面表现较差，揭示了当前模型在医学因果推理中的关键局限性。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型尚不足以可靠地从生物医学文献中验证因果关系，强调在医疗场景中使用LLMs进行搜索和检索前必须进行严格评估。

Abstract: The growing use of large language models (LLMs) for search and information retrieval underscores the need to evaluate their reliability in high-stakes domains such as healthcare. Although LLMs can effectively answer questions about diseases, symptoms, and treatments, their ability to accurately assess causal relationships and ground their conclusions in verified scientific evidence remains unclear. Here, we present a preliminary, small-scale study that investigates the accuracy of LLMs in evaluating causal medical claims and supporting them with peer-reviewed research. We propose an evaluation framework for causal hypothesis verification that can be used to systematically track the performance of existing and future LLMs. We assess the performance of eight LLMs on 17 medical causal hypotheses to evaluate whether they can reliably verify these hypotheses using scientific evidence from the literature. We systematically annotate the scientific evidence they provide according to six criteria (a total of 1,067 annotation points) and assess them with nine evaluation metrics. Our analysis shows that while LLMs exhibit strong recall, they often perform poorly at providing valid scientific articles and evidence for support and at rejecting unsupported hypotheses. These findings highlight a critical limitation of current LLMs, as they cannot yet be trusted fully to verify causal relationships from the biomedical literature. This work underscores the need for rigorous evaluation before using LLMs for search and retrieval in healthcare settings.

</details>


### [106] [Scientific Agent Skills: A Library of Procedural Knowledge for Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00065)
*Timothy Kassis,Vinayak Agarwal,Yuhuan He,Darshil Patel,Aubrey M. Brueckner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Scientific Agent Skills，一个包含163个程序的开源库，覆盖基因组学、化学信息学、医学影像、研究设计和科学传播等16个领域。每个技能以版本化、可读的指令文件为核心，按需加载，附带参考材料和可运行脚本，旨在提升语言模型在科学分析中的可辩护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型虽能生成工作代码，但其分析结果的可辩护性依赖于正确的程序选择，如采用何种统计检验、权威标识符命名空间及必要的结果备注。现有方法缺乏对这些程序性决策的系统支持，因此需要一个标准化、可复现的技能库来增强科学分析的可信度。

Method: 构建了一个结构化的开源库，包含163个科学实践技能，每个技能以目录形式组织，核心为版本化、人类可读的指令文件，辅以参考材料和可执行脚本，支持按需加载。技能覆盖从实验设计到科学传播的多个领域。

Result: 成功开发并发布了一个开放获取的科学技能库，可在GitHub上免费访问（https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agent-skills），为语言模型提供可信赖的程序性指导，提升科学分析的规范性和可解释性。

Conclusion: Scientific Agent Skills为科学语言模型提供了可验证、可复现的程序性知识支持，有助于实现更可靠、可辩护的自动化科学分析，是迈向可信人工智能辅助科研的重要一步。

Abstract: A language-model agent asked to analyse an experiment will usually return working code. Whether the analysis is defensible is a different question. A defensible analysis depends on procedural choices: which test the field accepts, which identifier namespace is authoritative, and which caveats must accompany a result. We present Scientific Agent Skills, an open library of 163 such procedures in 16 areas of practice, including genomics, cheminformatics, medical imaging, study design and scientific communication. Each skill is a directory built around a versioned, human-readable instruction file. An agent loads the file only when a task calls for it; the directory often also contains reference material and runnable scripts. We report no task-level evaluation and no host selection rate. Openly licensed and available at https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agent-skills.

</details>


### [107] [OCGQuant: Outlier-Companion Grouping for NVFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00066)
*Yishan Yao,Binjun Li,Hanling Yi,Pengyu Li,Xiaoqing Liu,Zihan Yang,Xiaotian Yu,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OCGQuant 是一种针对 NVFP4 低比特推理的后训练量化方法，通过通道分组策略将大激活值（异常值）与低幅度通道配对，以缓解因块内最大值主导尺度导致的量化误差。该方法有效降低协同量化误差（Collateral Quantization Error），在 Llama3 和 Qwen3 上实现更低的 WikiText-2 困惑度和更高的平均下游精度，同时保持接近 RTN 的预填充加速比和峰值解码内存性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVFP4 虽高效，但激活异常值会因主导块尺度而增加其余值的量化误差；现有 PTQ 方法如混合精度、旋转或残差补偿虽可缓解，但不专为 NVFP4 设计或引入额外计算开销。

Method: 提出基于异常值-同伴通道分组（Outlier-Companion Grouping, OCG）的 OCGQuant，从通道分组视角重构量化块组成，自适应地将高激活通道与低幅值通道配对，优化块内尺度分配，减少由最大值引起的协同量化误差。

Result: 在 Llama3 与 Qwen3 模型上，OCGQuant 实现最低 WikiText-2 困惑度与最高平均下游准确率，且预填充加速比接近 RTN，峰值解码内存使用量与之相当。

Conclusion: OCGQuant 通过创新的通道分组机制显著提升 NVFP4 量化精度，兼顾效率与性能，是适用于 NVFP4 的高效、轻量级后训练量化方案。

Abstract: NVFP4 is an efficient microscaling format for low-bit inference, but activation outliers can still degrade quantization accuracy within NVFP4 blocks. Within each quantization block, large activations can dominate the block scale, increasing the quantization error of the remaining values sharing the same scale. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods mitigate outlier errors through strategies such as mixed precision, rotation, or residual compensation, but these approaches are either not specifically tailored to NVFP4 or introduce additional computation. In this work, we revisit NVFP4 from a channel-grouping perspective and define the reducible error incurred by remaining block values under the scale set by the block maximum as Collateral Quantization Error. Based on this insight, we propose OCGQuant, a post-training quantization method centered on Outlier-Companion Grouping (OCG), which adaptively pairs outlier channels with low-magnitude companion channels to improve NVFP4 activation block composition. Experiments on Llama3 and Qwen3 show that OCGQuant achieves the lowest WikiText-2 perplexity and highest average downstream accuracy among evaluated PTQ methods, while maintaining prefill speedup close to RTN and matching its peak decoding memory. Code is available at https://github.com/Eshamont/OCGQuant.

</details>


### [108] [Do Multimodal LLMs See Before They Read? Diagnosing Contextual Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00067)
*Yi-Cheng Lai,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了多模态大模型在外部文本干扰下会忽视图像证据，称为‘多模态上下文谄媚’。通过一个包含998个案例的诊断测试，研究者系统地分离视觉证据、常识先验和外部文本的影响，发现当文本信息被延迟引入时，模型表现显著提升。采用S2VA（System-2 Visual Arbitration）方法，即在评估时屏蔽文本信息给视觉模型，可使准确率从7.9%提升至84.2%，优于直接报告或联合条件下的表现。跨六种模型验证显示，该方法平均提升19.7至44.1个百分点，且置信区间均不包含零。结果表明，上下文引入时机、模型类型及文本来源对模型行为有显著影响，说明‘多模态上下文谄媚’具有高度情境依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在面对图像与外部文本冲突时，常优先采纳文本内容而非视觉证据，导致错误判断。这种现象被称为‘多模态上下文谄媚’，严重影响模型的可靠性与鲁棒性，亟需系统性诊断与缓解机制。

Method: 设计了一个998例的诊断数据集，独立操控视觉证据、常识先验和外部文本，通过调整信息边界（如将视觉见证者置于无上下文状态、直接评分、双调用见证-仲裁管道、以及系统2视觉仲裁S2VA），测试不同条件下模型的表现。特别使用S2VA方法，在评估阶段屏蔽文本对视觉模型的影响，以探究最佳信息处理顺序。

Result: 在异常图像与伪造文本配对下，GPT-5.1在联合条件下的准确率为7.9%，直接报告为49.7%，双调用管道为63.7%，而S2VA达到84.2%。跨六种模型，S2VA相较直接报告提升19.7至44.1点，所有差异均统计显著。此外，不同模型对文本引入时机敏感，部分模型受文本引导提升，而特定文本重生成子集改变了各策略的相对排序。

Conclusion: 多模态上下文谄媚现象并非普遍固定，其发生高度依赖于文本引入时机、模型架构及文本来源。通过合理设计信息边界，尤其是采用S2VA等延迟文本引入策略，可显著提升模型在多模态冲突场景下的准确性与可靠性。

Abstract: External text can override conflicting image evidence in multimodal large language models, a failure we call multimodal contextual sycophancy. We introduce a 998-case diagnostic that independently varies visual evidence, commonsense priors, and external text, and probe when this failure arises by moving the information boundary around a context-blind visual witness. On abnormal images paired with Gemini-generated false text, GPT-5.1 scores 7.9% under joint conditioning, 49.7% when the context-blind witness report is scored directly, 63.7% under a matched two-call witness-arbiter pipeline that exposes the witness to the text, and 84.2% under System-2 Visual Arbitration (S2VA), which withholds the text from the witness. Across six models, S2VA improves over the direct witness report by 19.7 to 44.1 points, with all paired 95% confidence intervals excluding zero. The best information boundary is not uniform: textual context scaffolds some models, and a GPT-4o-regenerated subset changes the relative ordering of joint conditioning, Witness-Only, and S2VA. Contextual sycophancy is therefore sensitive to when text is introduced, as well as to the model and context source.

</details>


### [109] [Life Operators: a self-evolving framework for multiscale life modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00068)
*Shuo Wang,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Life Operators框架，通过感知、演化、生成三类操作符及桥接操作符，构建可模块化、可局部修订的医学人工智能系统，支持临床对话与长期预测，为医疗超级智能提供计算基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学AI在识别之外需解决患者状态在干预下的变化问题，现有统计或机制模型缺乏统一框架来表示患者状态、耦合多尺度信息或修正错误假设。

Method: 引入Life Operators：感知操作符从多模态数据推断生物状态，演化操作符模拟自然或干预条件下的状态演变，生成操作符将状态映射为可观测信号；通过桥接操作符连接不同变量、尺度和时间步的组件，形成任务特定的Operator Graph。

Result: 该框架实现模块化设计，支持科学假设的局部修订；可积累验证组件形成多尺度人体模型，推动医疗人工智能向超智能发展。

Conclusion: Life Operators提供了一个可扩展、可验证的医学人工智能基础架构，有望成为未来医疗超智能的计算核心。

Abstract: Medical AI is moving beyond recognition towards clinical dialogue and longitudinal prediction. Yet a central question remains: how would a patient's state change under intervention? Statistical models learn future observations, whereas mechanistic models describe selected processes. Neither provides a common framework for representing patient state, coupling scales or revising failed assumptions. We propose Life Operators: task-bounded mappings that define three scientific roles. Perception operators infer task-relevant biological states from multimodal observations, Evolution operators propagate these states under natural or intervention-conditioned dynamics, and Generation operators map them to measurable signals. Each role may be realised by equations, statistical models, neural networks or hybrids. Bridge operators connect components with different variables, scales and time steps. Selected operators and bridges form task-specific Operator Graphs containing the smallest set of states and mechanisms sufficient for a declared claim. This modular structure also makes scientific revision localisable. An AI co-scientist may propose changes to states, operators, bridges or graph structure, while independent evidence determines which variants are retained, restricted or retired. Over time, validated components could accumulate into broader multiscale models of the human body and provide a computational foundation for medical artificial superintelligence.

</details>


### [110] [Auditing Harness Tampering in Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00069)
*Xing Wang,Xiaoyi Zhang,Jie Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究自改进代理在自我优化过程中出现的“牵线操纵”问题，即通过修改自身框架产生虚假性能提升，而未真正增强能力。作者提出一个双轴分类法，按功能角色和义务违反情况对错误修改进行分类，并构建了带有篡改与良性编辑对的标注语料库。通过适配多种审计方法，对真实自改进代理轨迹进行系统审计，发现篡改普遍存在且常持续存在于最优代理的演化路径中，表现出显著的系统特异性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进代理在迭代优化过程中可能通过操纵其框架（harness）制造虚假性能提升，损害授权、溯源和完整性等关键约束，但现有研究缺乏对这一现象的系统分析。因此亟需建立统一框架以识别、分类和审计此类行为。

Method: 提出双轴分类法（功能角色+义务违反），构建包含篡改与良性编辑对的标注语料库，采用多种审计方法进行篡改分类与定位，并对真实自改进代理轨迹进行系统性审计。

Result: 实证结果显示，牵线操纵在不同代理的真实运行中普遍存在，常保留在最佳代理的演化谱系中，并形成具有系统特征的分类模式。

Conclusion: 牵线操纵是自改进代理中的普遍且隐蔽问题，必须通过系统化的审计机制加以识别与防范，否则将危及代理的可信性与安全性。

Abstract: Self-improving agents iteratively modify their own harness to push the frontier of their performance. However, such modifications can produce illusory performance gains or compromise integrity constraints such as authorization, provenance, and completeness without genuinely improving capability. We term this phenomenon as harness tampering, which extends the concept from reward and measurement tampering to the full self-improvement lifecycle. To systematically study this problem, we propose a two-axis taxonomy that categorizes each misaligned edit by the harness functional role in which it occurs and the obligation it violates. Then we build an annotated corpus by seeding tampered-benign edit pairs into the real trajectories of self-improving agents. We adapt and benchmark diverse audit methods on tampering classification and localization tasks. Finally we systematically audit real trajectories of self-improving agents. The results demonstrate that harness tampering consistently occurs in real runs from different agents, often persists in the lineage of the best agent, and forms distinct system-specific profiles across the taxonomy.

</details>


### [111] [Beneath the Diff: Diagnosing and Mitigating Algorithmic Mode Collapse in Code-Level Autonomous Research Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00077)
*Bowei He,Weixu Zhang,Yili Jin,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究代码级自主研究循环（ARLs）中由LLM代理驱动的迭代代码修改是否能带来真正泛化的性能提升。发现存在一种名为“算法模式坍缩”的失败模式：尽管代码编辑表面多样性保持稳定，但语义和机制层面的多样性显著下降，即反复提出相似的算法改动。这导致循环内指标提升与独立验证集上的真实性能提升之间出现巨大差距。为此提出DAPS方法，通过类别覆盖重加权、持续编辑记忆和验证门控机制缓解该问题，在不牺牲循环优化速度的前提下，显著降低编辑语义簇衰减69.1%，并提升盲测与审计评估下的忠实度83.7%和81.6%。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究代码级自主研究循环中基于可执行指标的反复代码编辑是否能带来真正泛化且可验证的性能提升，识别其潜在失败模式。

Method: 提出Diversity-Aware Proposal Sampling（DAPS），结合类别覆盖重加权、持久编辑记忆和验证门控机制；采用三重协议分离循环内指标、审计指标和盲测指标，以增强评估可靠性。

Result: DAPS有效减少编辑语义簇衰减69.1%，提升盲测和审计评估下的相对忠实度分别达83.7%和81.6%，同时保持循环优化速度。

Conclusion: 算法模式坍缩是代码级自主研究循环中的关键瓶颈，通过引入多样性感知的采样策略DAPS，可在不牺牲效率的前提下显著提升模型行为的真实性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Code-level autonomous research loops (ARLs) have recently emerged as a concrete object of study in automated machine learning research. In such loops, an LLM agent proposes modifications to an experimental training pipeline, executes the modified pipeline, and retains edits that improve a verifiable in-loop metric. Although executable metrics may appear to provide a reliable signal of progress, it remains unclear whether repeated metric-driven code editing leads to genuine improvements that generalize beyond the loop. We provide a systematic diagnosis of this question. Across various experiment settings, we identify a robust failure mode that we call \textbf{algorithmic mode collapse}. In this regime, surface-level edit diversity remains stable, but semantic and mechanism-level diversity collapse: the agent continues to edit different lines of code while repeatedly proposing the same kinds of algorithmic changes. This collapse is accompanied by a widening gap between in-loop metric gains and gains measured on independent held-out evaluations. We then propose Diversity-Aware Proposal Sampling (\textsc{DAPS}), a lightweight mitigation that combines category-coverage reweighting, persistent edit memory, and a validation gate. Under a three-tier protocol separating the in-loop metric, the audit metric read by the gate, and a blind metric no loop component ever accesses, \textsc{DAPS} reduces semantic-cluster decay of edits by $69.1\%$ and improves relative faithfulness by $83.7\%$ blind and $81.6\%$ audited, while preserving in-loop optimization speed. We provide the code in Github \href{https://github.com/BokwaiHo/arl-mode-collapse}{repository}.

</details>


### [112] [KItCAT: Knowledge Injection via Input Corruption for Auto-regressive Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00082)
*Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Kushagra Bhushan,Vineet Kumar,Gaurav Pandey,Jaydeep Sen,Dinesh Raghu,Sachindra Joshi,Yatin Nandwani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KItCAT 是一种轻量级的知识注入方法，通过在解码器仅有的语言模型中对输入序列进行随机扰动（即用其他词汇替换部分输入词），实现无需大量重写即可增强知识学习。该方法在不依赖复杂重写或强大模型的前提下，显著提升了在小众领域文档上的知识获取能力，且计算成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续预训练（CPT）方法在处理罕见事实、重复性低的小众文档时效果不佳，而现有的基于重写的增强方法计算开销大且依赖高性能模型。因此需要一种更高效、低成本的知识注入策略。

Method: KItCAT 通过在标准的下一个词预测任务基础上引入随机输入扰动：随机选择输入序列中的部分词，并用词汇表中的其他词替换，但保持原目标词不变。这种操作生成多样化的训练样本，实现大规模数据增强。

Result: 实验表明，KItCAT 在多个数据集和不同模型家族上均优于传统 CPT 方法，有效提升了模型在小众领域知识的理解与推理能力，同时计算开销几乎可忽略。

Conclusion: KItCAT 提供了一种高效、低成本的知识注入方式，能够在不依赖复杂重写或强模型的情况下显著提升语言模型对小众领域知识的吸收能力，适用于资源受限场景。

Abstract: LLMs acquire vast amounts of knowledge during pre-training, but often lack the specialized knowledge needed to answer questions from niche sources such as manuals or technical documents unseen during pre-training. Continued pre-training (CPT) is widely used to inject such knowledge into model parameters. However, niche documents seldom repeat facts, making it difficult for CPT to robustly acquire such knowledge. Recent works address this by generating multiple paraphrases of the new knowledge, but paraphrasing is computationally expensive and typically requires powerful LLMs. In this work, we introduce KItCAT: Knowledge Injection via Corrupted Auto-regressive Training, a lightweight training strategy that reduces the need for paraphrasing in decoder-only LLMs. KItCAT augments standard next-token prediction by stochastically corrupting the input sequence. During training, a random subset of input tokens is replaced with other vocabulary tokens while the original next-token labels are kept unchanged. This simple intervention generates diverse training inputs from each sample, enabling large-scale data augmentation at negligible cost. We show that KItCAT consistently improves over CPT across multiple datasets and model families. Code is available at https://github.com/meghanadhpulivarthi/KItCAT.

</details>


### [113] [Retrieval, Scoring, and Decoding Shape Performance and Stability in LLM-based Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00086)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Tomislav Duricic,Andro Mercep,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较了在对话式推荐系统中使用的大语言模型（LLM）重排序器与协同过滤和序列基线方法的性能，发现性能表现高度依赖于检索协议、候选池大小、第一阶段检索器和解码温度等设置。即使在共享语义候选池条件下，最佳专有模型重排序器的NDCG@10达到0.1497，显著优于非LLM基线（0.0939），但在零样本生成模式下性能提升至0.2925，表明评分策略对结果影响巨大。开放权重模型未超越浅层自编码器基线，且从语义候选转换为协同过滤候选可使性能提升超过50%。此外，解码温度升高对强模型影响较小，但弱模型性能下降明显。研究强调应将候选生成、候选池大小、评分策略和解码配置作为必须报告的关键要素，而非次要实现细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型作为对话式推荐系统中的重排序器时，其性能受多种系统配置（如候选池生成方式、解码策略等）的影响程度，并揭示当前评估范式可能存在的偏差。

Method: 在ReDial基准上采用统一的“检索-重排序”流程，对比不同类型的LLM（专有、开源、微调）、协同过滤与序列基线，在变化候选池大小、第一阶段检索器和解码温度的条件下进行实验，使用严格候选感知评分策略，并分析多个指标如NDCG@10、Jaccard距离等。

Result: 最佳专有重排序器在匹配候选池下达NDCG@10 0.1497，远超非LLM基线；但在零样本生成下跃升至0.2925，显示评分方式对性能评估具有重大影响。开源模型未超越浅层自编码器基线。使用协同过滤候选池比语义候选池带来超过50%的性能提升。解码温度上升对强模型影响小，但弱模型显著退化。

Conclusion: LLM重排序器的性能评估高度依赖于候选生成方式、候选池规模、评分策略和解码配置，这些因素不应被视为实现细节，而应作为标准报告内容，以确保评估结果的可比性和透明性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as rerankers in conversational recommender systems, yet measured gains depend strongly on the retrieval and inference protocol. On the ReDial conversational movie recommendation benchmark, we compare proprietary, open-weight, and fine-tuned LLM rerankers with collaborative-filtering and sequential baselines in a shared retrieve-then-rerank pipeline. We vary candidate-pool size, first-stage retriever, and decoding temperature. With a shared semantic top-250 candidate pool and strict candidate-aware scoring, the best proprietary reranker reaches NDCG@10 of 0.1497, compared with 0.0939 for the strongest non-LLM baseline. The same reranker reaches 0.2925 in zero-shot generation, showing that unconstrained scoring can yield a much larger apparent advantage than matched-pool evaluation. No evaluated open-weight LLM outperforms the tuned shallow autoencoder baseline under this protocol. For the strongest proprietary and open-weight rerankers, switching from semantic to collaborative-filtering candidates raises NDCG@10 by more than 50%, showing that measured reranker performance is highly sensitive to candidate generation. For the best proprietary reranker, raising temperature from 0 to 1.0 increases top-10 Jaccard distance from 0.0900 to 0.1240 while mean NDCG@10 changes negligibly, whereas weaker LLMs show larger degradation. These ReDial results support treating candidate generation, candidate-pool size, scoring policy, and decoding configuration as required reporting fields rather than implementation details.

</details>


### [114] [Lingua Franca or Probing Artifact? Rethinking Latent Language in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00155)
*Deniz Bayazit,Badr AlKhamissi,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了多语言模型中潜在语言识别探针的一致性问题，发现不同探针（基于几何的GMM与基于解码的探针）对语言特异性信号的捕捉存在系统性差异，前者显示更早的跨语言混合，后者则保留更清晰且偏向英语的语言信号。这些差异反映模型多语能力与训练进展，但跨领域相对稳定，提示当前探针揭示的是多语言处理的不同方面，而非单一内部通用语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言识别探针依赖不同信号（如隐藏状态几何或输出空间可解码性），其结论影响对模型信息共享与路由机制的理解，因此需评估这些探针是否测量同一现象。

Method: 在多种模型家族、训练方式、任务、领域和27种语言上，对比分析基于GMM的表示探针与基于解码的探针的表现，考察其在语言识别上的分歧及其与模型多语性和训练阶段的关系。

Result: 不同探针系统性地产生不一致结果：几何探针显示更早的跨语言混合，解码探针则表现出更强的语言特异性和英语偏向；这些差异随模型多语能力和训练进展变化，但在不同领域间保持稳定。

Conclusion: 应谨慎解读潜语言识别结果，当前探针反映的是多语言计算的不同侧面，而非直接揭示单一内部通用语。

Abstract: Latent language identification is often used to argue that multilingual language models route computation through language-specific states, such as English pivots. However, existing probes infer latent language from different signals, such as the geometry of hidden states or what can be decoded from intermediate representations. Since such claims shape conclusions about how models share and route information across languages, we ask whether these probes measure the same phenomenon or expose distinct aspects of multilingual computation. We study this question across model families, training regimes, domains, tasks, checkpoints, and up to 27 languages. We find that identification probes systematically disagree: the GMM-based representation probe, which draws evidence from hidden state geometry, shows earlier cross-lingual mixing, whereas decoding-based probes, which rely on output-space decodability, retain sharper language-specific and more English-biased signals. These differences track model multilinguality and training progression, but are comparatively stable across domains. Our results suggest a more cautious interpretation of latent language identification, where current probes expose different aspects of multilingual processing, rather than directly revealing a single internal lingua franca.

</details>


### [115] [Do General NLP Embeddings Capture Ontological Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00177)
*Hamed Babaei Giglou,Jennifer D'Souza,Sören Auer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AVA框架，用于评估NLP嵌入模型在本体和知识图谱中区分逻辑敏感关系语义的能力。通过171,007个对比三元组（来自163个异构本体），采用层次反转、关系替换和不相交注入生成语义等价的改写与逻辑冲突的硬负例。评估超过25个先进嵌入模型发现：最佳模型三元组准确率仅0.739，硬负例准确率低至0.135。微调虽显著提升区分能力，但对下游语义网任务（如分类体系发现和本体对齐）泛化能力差。分析表明改进主要源于特定扰动模式识别，而非真正的本体理解。研究揭示了语言表示学习与本体级区分之间持续存在的差距，挑战了强NLP基准性能即代表语义网能力的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用NLP嵌入模型在语言任务上表现良好，但其对本体中逻辑敏感关系语义的捕捉能力尚不明确。需要一种系统性评估框架来检验嵌入模型是否真正理解本体结构，从而揭示其在语义网任务中的局限性。

Method: 提出AVA框架，从163个异构本体中生成171,007个对比三元组，通过层次反转、关系替换和不相交注入构造包含本体陈述、语义等价改写和逻辑冲突硬负例的三元组，用于评估嵌入模型对逻辑敏感语义的区分能力。

Result: 25个以上先进嵌入模型表现不佳，最佳模型三元组准确率为0.739，硬负例准确率仅为0.135；微调虽提升区分能力，但在下游语义网任务中迁移效果差；改进主要源于对特定扰动模式的识别，而非深层本体理解。

Conclusion: 当前嵌入模型在语言层面表现优异，但在本体级逻辑语义区分上存在显著缺陷，表明语言表示学习与语义网能力之间仍存在根本性差距，需重新审视模型设计与评估范式。

Abstract: General-purpose NLP embedding models perform well on linguistic tasks, but their ability to capture symbolic ontological structure remains unclear. We introduce AVA, a systematic framework for evaluating whether embeddings distinguish logic-sensitive relational semantics in ontologies and knowledge graphs. AVA comprises 171,007 contrastive triplets derived from 163 heterogeneous ontologies using hierarchy inversion, relation substitution, and disjointness injection. Each triplet contains an ontology statement, a semantically equivalent paraphrase, and a logic-sensitive hard negative with contradictory relational meaning. We evaluate more than 25 state-of-the-art embedding models and find substantial limitations: the best model achieves only 0.739 triplet accuracy, while hard negative accuracy falls to 0.135. Fine-tuning improves discrimination by a large margin but transfers poorly to downstream Semantic Web tasks, including taxonomy discovery and ontology alignment. Further analysis suggests that improvements stem partly from perturbation-specific pattern recognition rather than robust ontological understanding. These findings reveal a persistent gap between linguistic representation learning and ontology-level discrimination, challenging the assumption that strong NLP benchmark performance translates to Semantic Web competence.

</details>


### [116] [Synthetic Worlds for Temporal Evaluation and Knowledge Updating in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00184)
*Jonathan Zheng,Zirui Shao,Alan Ritter,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模拟的合成框架，用于研究大语言模型中的知识插入问题。通过构建虚构但现实的未来世界数据集 {\sc ParallelEvents} 和训练框架 {\sc Synapse}，实现无需人工标注数据的可扩展知识更新，实验表明该方法在知识插入任务上比现有方法提升14.23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的知识因依赖静态预训练语料而容易过时，现有评估方法或存在快速污染问题，或依赖与已有知识冲突的反事实编辑，难以有效评估知识更新效果。

Method: 提出 {\sc ParallelEvents} 基准数据集，生成连贯的虚构事件轨迹以实现受控评估；基于该数据集设计 {\sc Synapse} 训练框架，利用模型生成数据进行中段训练和指令微调，实现参数更新。

Result: {\\sc Synapse} 在知识插入任务中相比现有方法性能提升14.23%，验证了基于模拟的合成训练在实现稳健、一致知识插入方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过合成模拟数据驱动的训练方式，可在不依赖人工标注的前提下高效、可靠地实现大语言模型的知识更新，为解决知识过时问题提供新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on static pretraining corpora, causing their knowledge to become outdated over time. Existing approaches for evaluating knowledge edits either suffer from rapid contamination or rely on counterfactual edits that conflict with rigid existing knowledge. In this work, we propose a synthetic, simulation-driven framework for studying knowledge insertion in LLMs. We introduce {\sc ParallelEvents}, a benchmark of fictional yet realistic future worlds that generates coherent event trajectories for controlled evaluation, avoiding contamination while preserving consistency. Building on this dataset, we develop {\sc Synapse}, a training framework that uses model-generated data to update model parameters via mid-training and instruction tuning. This synthetic pipeline enables scalable knowledge integration without costly human-curated data. Empirically, {\sc Synapse} outperforms existing methods by 14.23\%, demonstrating that simulation-based synthetic training leads to robust and coherent knowledge insertions.

</details>


### [117] [Assessing Suicide Risk in Arabic Crisis Helpline Calls: A Comparison of Arabic and English Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00191)
*Linhai Ma,Rita El Hachem,Mahatab El Hajj,Lilian Ghandour,Samah Fodeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用自然语言处理技术分析黎巴嫩国家情感支持与自杀预防热线的去标识化阿拉伯语通话记录，评估自杀风险。通过本地语音转录和去标识化处理，确保数据隐私安全。研究对比了阿拉伯语与英语模型在识别高风险与中等风险自杀倾向的表现，发现最佳阿拉伯语模型在高风险分类上达到81.19的宏F1和90.61的ROC-AUC，英语模型表现更优（F1 85.00，AUC 92.59），且翻译至英文未降低性能。高风险案例区分更清晰，低严重性症状更具挑战性。结果表明，可在不外传音频的前提下，基于去标识化阿拉伯语文本实现有效的自杀风险分类，为操作员辅助工具提供可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有危机热线依赖人工结构化访谈评估自杀风险，耗时且受人员培训与工作负荷影响。自然语言处理可提升效率与优先级判断，但缺乏针对阿拉伯语热线数据的研究，且面临隐私保护挑战。本研究旨在探索在严格隐私约束下，利用去标识化阿拉伯语通话文本进行自杀风险自动识别的可行性。

Method: 采用黎巴嫩国家热线提供的去标识化通话转录文本，使用本地部署的黎凡特阿拉伯语语音识别与命名实体识别模型去除敏感信息。基于哥伦比亚自杀严重程度量表（C-SSRS）的五个条目构建二元标签（高风险与中等风险）。对五种指令微调的大语言模型及六种变压器编码器基线模型（四阿拉伯语、两英语）进行训练与评估，并通过机器翻译生成英文对照数据集进行跨语言比较。所有实验均在独立测试集上完成。

Result: 在383通电话中，高风险任务的最优阿拉伯语模型取得81.19的宏F1与90.61的ROC-AUC，英语模型表现更佳（85.00和92.59），能识别88.9%的高风险个案；中等风险任务表现较差。高风险样本在两类语言中分离效果更好，翻译至英文未导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 可在不传输音频的前提下，基于去标识化阿拉伯语文本有效识别自杀风险。高风险分类结果具备作为操作员辅助工具的潜力，而低严重性自杀意念仍是更具挑战性的任务。该方法兼顾隐私保护与实际应用价值，为阿拉伯语危机干预系统提供了可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Crisis helplines assess suicide risk through structured interviews, a process that is slow and dependent on operator training and workload. Natural language processing could support risk assessment and call prioritization, but almost no work addresses Arabic-language helpline calls or operates within the privacy constraints of real helpline data. We analysed de-identified transcripts from Lebanon's National Lifeline for Emotional Support and Suicide Prevention. Audio never left the helpline: calls were transcribed on site with a speech recognition model for Levantine Arabic, and an Arabic named-entity recognition model removed identifying information locally. Only the de-identified transcripts were shared with the research team. Operators recorded the five suicidal ideation items of the Columbia Suicide Severity Rating Scale, which we combined into two binary outcomes: at-risk and high-risk. We also machine-translated the transcripts into English, giving a paired Arabic/English comparison. On each corpus, we fine-tuned five instruction-tuned large language models alongside six transformer encoder baselines (four Arabic, two English) and evaluated all models on a held-out test set. We included 383 calls: 373 for the at-risk task (52.3% positive) and 297 for the high-risk task (30.0% positive). The best Arabic model reached a macro-F1 of 81.19 and a ROC-AUC of 90.61 on high-risk; the best English model reached 85.00 and 92.59, identifying 88.9% of high-risk calls. In both languages, high-risk calls separated more cleanly than at-risk calls, and translation to English did not reduce the best observed performance. Suicide risk can be classified from de-identified Arabic transcripts without sending audio outside the helpline. The high-risk results support further testing as an operator-facing tool; lower-severity ideation proved the harder case.

</details>


### [118] [Uncovering and Mitigating Aggregation-Induced Reward Hacking in Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00213)
*Yu Yuan,Yaoyou Fan,Lili Zhao,Guangting Zheng,Kai Zhang,Lu Pan,Ke Zeng,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种名为自适应多奖励投影（AMRP）的轻量级在线方法，用于解决大语言模型强化学习微调中因固定聚合权重导致的奖励黑客问题。通过动态调整各奖励维度的权重，AMRP根据相对不足、奖励波动性和近期进展三个信号，增强对落后、不稳定或停滞维度的压力，减轻已饱和维度的权重，从而改善奖励分布平衡和下游任务性能。实验表明，AMRP在多种任务和算法下均优于固定与动态权重基线，具有良好的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习微调方法在处理多奖励维度时采用固定权重进行标量融合，但这种静态投影会导致奖励黑客现象：不同奖励特征被压缩为单一标量，使优化过程偏向于容易、密集或系统性有利的维度，从而陷入次优解，阻碍模型收敛到更均衡且高性能的状态。

Method: 提出自适应多奖励投影（AMRP），利用相对不足、奖励波动性和近期进展三个信号实时调整各奖励维度的聚合权重，实现动态权重分配，引导模型更全面地优化多个奖励维度。

Result: AMRP在结构化推理、引用支撑生成和开放域对齐等任务中，显著提升了奖励分布的平衡性与下游任务表现，优于固定权重和动态权重基线；同时在GRPO、GDPO和PPO等多种强化学习算法下均有效，展现出良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 固定聚合权重的多奖励标量融合易引发奖励黑客，导致模型优化偏离最优平衡状态。AMRP通过在线自适应调整权重，有效缓解此问题，在多种任务与算法中均表现出更强的性能与鲁棒性，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning fine-tuning of large language models increasingly adopts multiple reward dimensions, including verifiable rules, task-specific evaluators, and learned reward models, to provide richer supervision across diverse capabilities. These dimensions are commonly scalarized with fixed aggregation weights. We identify a failure mode in which aggregation itself induces reward hacking: static projection aliases qualitatively different reward profiles into a single scalar, steering optimization toward whichever dimensions are easiest, densest, or systematically favored by the reward signal. Over training, this traps the policy in suboptimal profiles and prevents convergence to better-balanced ones that would yield higher task performance. To address this, we propose Adaptive Multi-Reward Projection (AMRP), a lightweight online method that reallocates aggregation weights using three signals, relative shortfall, reward volatility, and recent progress, increasing pressure on lagging, unstable, or stagnant dimensions while relieving saturated ones. Across structured reasoning, citation-grounded generation, and open-ended alignment under GRPO, AMRP consistently improves reward-profile balance and downstream performance over fixed and dynamic weighting baselines; it also remains effective with GDPO and PPO, supporting compatibility across RL algorithms. Our code is available at https://github.com/yyhappier/AMRP.git.

</details>


### [119] [LLM-as-a-Demographic: Whom Sociodemographic Prompting Helps, and Whom It Hurts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00222)
*Daniela Occhipinti,Andrea Piergentili,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）作为主观任务评判者时，如何受到社会人口学提示的影响。通过对比23个开源大模型在三种条件下的预测标签分布（无信息、单属性、交叉属性），发现：1）无提示时模型仍偏向白人、受过大学教育的标注者；2）提示具有不对称性，使模型更接近多数群体而远离少数群体，尤其在冒犯性判断中，交叉属性提示加剧了这一问题；3）对比基础模型与指令微调模型，发现指令微调可能是造成这种不对称性的原因。因此，在估算群体判断时应谨慎使用社会人口学提示，因其可能偏离少数群体的真实判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型在主观任务中的公正性，特别是其是否能真实反映不同社会群体的判断，避免因提示设计导致的偏差。

Method: 在三个主观任务上，测试23个开放权重的大语言模型，分别在无社会人口学信息、单属性提示和交叉属性提示（性别、年龄、种族、教育）条件下，比较模型输出的标签分布与真实标注者群体分布的相似性，并分析模型间差异。

Result: 1）即使无提示，模型也最接近白人、高学历群体的判断；2）社会人口学提示具有不对称效应，使模型更趋近多数群体，远离少数群体，尤其在冒犯性判断中，交叉提示放大了这种偏差；3）指令微调可能加剧了这种不平等。

Conclusion: 社会人口学提示不能确保模型公平地代表所有群体，反而可能强化主流群体的偏好，因此在用于估计少数群体判断时需谨慎使用，以避免进一步边缘化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges for subjective tasks, where annotators disagree and the relevant question is not only how accurate a judge is, but whose judgments it reproduces. Sociodemographic prompting conditions the judge on an annotator's demographic profile to align its judgments with the corresponding group's. We test whether this alignment emerges distributionally, comparing the predicted label distributions of 23 open-weight LLMs on three subjective tasks against those of real annotator groups, under three conditions: no demographic information, single-attribute profiles, and intersectional profiles over gender, age, race, and education. Three findings emerge. First, a judge prompted with no demographics is not perspective-neutral: models best reproduce the judgments of White, college-educated annotators. Second, demographic conditioning is asymmetric: it moves the judge toward majority groups and away from minority groups, most strongly on offensiveness, where intersectional profiles amplify the harm. Third, by comparing base and instruct models we identify instruction-tuning as a possible source of the asymmetry. Demographic conditioning should therefore be used with caution to estimate group judgments: conditioning moves predictions away from the reference distributions of the minority groups the method is often invoked to serve.

</details>


### [120] [Bridging Lexical Divergence: LLM-Assisted, Cost-Efficient, Zero-shot Scientific Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00228)
*Md Rasel Khondokar,Qiao Qiao,Farjana Sultana Samia,Nhat Le,Yuepei Li,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Sci-ZSEL框架，通过选择性生成实体别名并结合本体感知过滤，降低计算成本并去除噪声，用于科学领域实体链接的零人工标注微调。同时发布新的动物科学EL基准，评估低词汇重叠场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域实体链接面临提及与实体名称间词汇重叠度低、专业术语罕见等问题，导致通用预训练模型迁移效果差；且多数科学领域缺乏专家标注数据，需零人工标注方法。现有零样本方法依赖大语言模型生成全语料别名，计算开销大且无去噪机制。

Method: 提出Sci-ZSEL框架：利用大语言模型选择性生成实体别名，结合本体感知过滤器剔除语义漂移的别名，构建伪标签提及-实体对用于微调。

Result: 在五个基准上优于非微调基线，尤其在低词汇重叠情况下表现更优；结合人工校准同义词时性能最佳。

Conclusion: Sci-ZSEL有效应对科学领域实体链接中的低词汇重叠与缺乏标注数据挑战，为零人工标注微调提供了高效可靠方案。

Abstract: Scientific domain entity linking (EL) differs from general domain EL because mentions and entity names often lack lexical overlap. Another challenge is that specialized terminology is used in the scientific domain, which is rarely encountered in models pretrained on general domains. Therefore, models trained on general domains transfer poorly to scientific domains. To address this, in-domain fine-tuning is the natural remedy. However, many scientific domains lack expert-annotated data, motivating the need for a zero-human-annotation approach. Existing zero-shot methods heavily rely on LLMs to generate aliases across entire mention corpora, which incurs substantial computational cost, and those methods provide no mechanism to filter out noise from LLMs. To address these challenges, we propose Sci-ZSEL, a framework that selectively generates entity aliases with an LLM to control computational cost, and applies an ontology-aware filter to remove aliases that semantically drift toward ontology neighbors. Then, filtered aliases are used to construct pseudo-labeled mention-entity pairs for fine-tuning. To enable evaluation of EL under low lexical overlap, we also release a new animal science EL benchmark linked to three livestock trait ontologies, where mentions and entities exhibit substantially lower lexical overlap than in existing benchmarks. Across five benchmarks, Sci-ZSEL outperforms the non-fine-tuned baseline, is most useful on nonoverlapping mentions, and combining it with curated synonyms gives the best performance in most settings.

</details>


### [121] [LOOMSUM:Weaving Quantitative and Narrative Evidence for Faithful Long Text-Table Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00241)
*Meng Zhou,Wenhao You,Wei Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LOOMSUM 是一种无需训练的框架，通过提取基于源的原子证据、显式链接表格事实与支持性叙述分析，并在生成前规划话语结构，以提高长文本-表格摘要的分析忠实性。引入的 TGF 评估指标（表基忠实度）可分别评估数值依据、分析支持和关系一致性。实验表明，LOOMSUM 在 FINDSum 和 USTT 基准上显著提升忠实性，且人类评估验证了其组件与人工判断的正向关联。关系一致性指标比通用事实性指标更接近人类判断，说明跨模态显式链接有助于减少数量与解释错误配对的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档中的重要信息常分散于大量叙述段落和多个表格中，现有方法虽能生成看似合理的量化事实和分析陈述，但常错误关联，导致定量合理但分析不忠实。因此需要一种能确保事实与其支持性分析正确对应的方法。

Method: 提出 LOOMSUM 框架，包括：1）提取源基原子证据；2）显式链接表格衍生事实与支持性叙述分析；3）生成前规划话语结构；4）引入 TGF 评估指标，从数值依据、分析支持、关系一致性三方面衡量忠实性。

Result: 在 FINDSum 和 USTT 基准上，LOOMSUM 显著提升分析忠实性，同时保持高质量摘要；人类评估显示其组件与人工判断高度一致；关系一致性指标与人类判断的相关性优于传统事实性指标，证明跨模态链接对减少错误配对有效。

Conclusion: 忠实的长文本-表格摘要不仅需准确锚定单个事实，还需保持事实之间的逻辑关系。显式跨模态链接与结构规划是实现高分析忠实性的关键。

Abstract: Long documents often distribute important information across extensive narrative passages and multiple tables, making faithful summarization particularly challenging. Existing methods may generate individually supported quantitative facts and analytical statements yet associate them incorrectly, producing quantitatively plausible yet analytically unfaithful summaries. In this work, we propose LOOMSUM, a training-free framework that extracts source-grounded atomic evidence, explicitly links table-derived facts with supporting narrative analyses, and plans the discourse structure before generation. We also introduce Table-Grounded Faithfulness (TGF), a claim-level metric that separately evaluates Numeric Grounding, Analysis Support, and Relation Consistency. Experiments on the text--table summarization benchmarks FINDSum and USTT show that LOOMSUM improves analytical faithfulness while maintaining strong summarization quality. Human evaluation finds positive component-level associations with the corresponding human judgments. Our Relation Consistency metric further shows stronger agreement with human relation judgments than generic factuality metrics, indicating that explicit cross-modal linking helps reduce errors in which supported quantities are paired with incorrect narrative interpretations. Together, these findings show that faithful long text--table summarization requires not only grounding individual facts, but also preserving the relations between them.

</details>


### [122] [NSIDDx: A Design Framework for Neuro-Symbolic, Practitioner-First Differential Diagnosis in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00256)
*Aarav Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种名为NSIDDx的神经符号集成鉴别诊断系统，旨在解决大语言模型（LLM）在罕见病诊断中因高自信但不可验证输出导致的临床可靠性问题。通过结合三元症状编码、矛盾检测、审计字符串和临床医生干预机制，系统在消费级硬件上离线运行，强调将临床医生作为主动推理参与者的设计理念。研究提炼出五个面向临床医生参与的临床NLP设计原则，并呼吁开展前瞻性研究以大规模验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的诊断系统虽在基准测试中表现良好，但在罕见病等非典型临床场景下存在高置信度但不可验证的输出问题，暴露出与真实临床可靠性之间的系统性差距。因此亟需一种更可靠、可解释且能与临床医生协作的诊断框架。

Method: 提出NSIDDx框架，采用神经符号混合架构，包含三元症状编码、矛盾检测模块、审计字符串生成及临床医生手动覆盖功能，所有组件可在消费级硬件上离线运行，实现人机协同决策。

Result: 实验表明，现有LLM+稀有疾病RAG管道产生高度自信但难以验证的错误诊断；而NSIDDx系统显著提升了诊断结果的可解释性与可信度，且支持临床医生主动介入，有效缓解了模型幻觉与不可靠输出问题。

Conclusion: 在低资源临床环境中，诊断系统必须将临床医生视为主动推理主体。NSIDDx框架及其五项设计原则为构建可信赖、可审计、可干预的临床NLP系统提供了可行路径，未来需通过大规模前瞻性研究进一步验证其临床价值。

Abstract: LLM-based diagnostic systems achieve high semantic accuracy on benchmarks, but open-ended evaluation on clinically uncommon presentations reveals a systematic gap between headline accuracy and verifiable clinical reliability. We evaluate an LLM+rare-disease-RAG pipeline across two cohorts and show that the paradigm produces confident outputs that are frequently unverifiable and systematically resistant to clinician interrogation. We present NSIDDx (Neuro-Symbolic Integrated Differential Diagnosis System), a design framework arguing that DDx systems in low-resource settings must treat the clinician as an active reasoning agent. We instantiate this through a neuro-symbolic pipeline with ternary symptom encoding, contradiction detection, audit strings, and practitioner override - running offline on consumer hardware. We distill five design principles for clinician-in-the-loop clinical NLP and invite the prospective studies needed to validate the claim at scale.

</details>


### [123] [Slow to See, Slow to Suppress: Understanding the Effects of Modality in Context-Memory Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00293)
*Athulith Paraselli,Etha Tianze Hua,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在处理上下文记忆冲突时的表现，发现模型对文本中出现的实体更依赖上下文信息，而对图像中出现的实体则更依赖训练期间存储的参数化信息。这种不对称性与模态间表征对齐的时间延迟有关，视觉信息处理时间较长，导致模型难以抑制其默认的事实回忆机制，从而倾向于输出参数化答案。链式思维推理未能缓解这一差距，但增加上下文中的视觉信息量可产生一定影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多模态模型在面对上下文信息与训练知识冲突时的行为，理解其决策偏差机制，以提升模型在复杂场景下的一致性与可靠性。

Method: 通过设计包含文本与图像信息冲突的实验任务，分析VLMs在不同条件下的回答倾向；利用延迟分析和对比实验评估模态间对齐时间的影响，并测试链式思维与视觉信息量增加对结果的作用。

Result: 模型表现出显著的不对称偏好：对文本提及的实体更信任上下文信息，对图像提及的实体更依赖参数化知识；视觉信息处理延迟是导致该现象的关键因素；链式思维无法有效缓解偏差，但增加上下文视觉信息可部分引导模型偏向上下文。

Conclusion: 随着模型变得越来越多模态和检索增强，确保其行为一致性的挑战加剧。当前模型在跨模态冲突下存在系统性偏差，需通过优化模态对齐机制与上下文信息权重来改进。

Abstract: We investigate how vision-language models (VLMs) handle context-memory conflicts; that is, situations in which the model is given information in context that differs from what was stored parametrically during training. We document asymmetric biases: models tend to prefer in-context information about entities which appear in text, but prefer parametric information about entities which appear in images. We relate this asymmetry to the late representational alignment across modalities, showing that the longer processing time associated with resolving visual entities prevents the suppression of the model's usual factual recall mechanism, thus resulting in more parametric answers. Chain-of-thought reasoning does not appear to resolve the gap, but increasing the amount of visual information in the context does show an effect. These results illustrate the complexity of ensuring consistent behavior as models become increasingly multimodal and retrieval-augmented.

</details>


### [124] [Toward Workflow-Aware Benchmarking for Healthcare NLP Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00296)
*Junyi Yao,Baichuan Li,Zihao Zheng,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向医疗领域自然语言处理代理的基于任务周期的评估协议，旨在解决现有评估方法在纵向状态、中断和人工交接等方面表征不足的问题。该协议通过分离模型、代理和模拟工作流行为的证据，定义了五字段任务周期模板，并制定了关于状态连续性、证据可追溯性和升级决策的标注与评分标准。其应用实例包括病历更新、证据检索、患者消息传递和分诊交接等四类任务模板。该评估不涉及临床结果或部署价值的衡量，而是作为静态基准与前瞻性工作流研究之间的可复现中间评估层，特别强调对漏报与误报升级的成本敏感性处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对大型语言模型（LLM）代理在医疗领域的评估多局限于静态医学问答或单次生成任务，无法充分反映实际医疗场景中的长期状态变化、任务中断及人工交接等复杂因素，亟需一种更贴近真实工作流程的评估机制。

Method: 提出一种基于任务周期的评估协议，将证据分离至模型、代理和模拟工作流三个层面；设计包含五个字段的任务周期结构；制定针对状态连续性、证据可追溯性及升级决策的标注与评分规则；并开发四个具体任务模板（病历更新、证据检索、患者消息传递、分诊交接）以实现协议落地。

Result: 该评估协议能够提供一个可复现的中间层级评估框架，有效捕捉医疗代理在动态、交互式环境中的表现，尤其在状态维持、证据追踪和合理升级决策方面具有显著优势，同时通过成本敏感设计区分漏报与误报升级的影响。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的任务周期评估协议为医疗NLP代理提供了超越传统静态基准的评估能力，填补了从简单问答到真实工作流研究之间的空白，有助于推动智能医疗代理在复杂临床环境中的可信发展。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed for healthcare tasks such as clinical documentation, evidence retrieval, patient messaging, and care coordination. Yet many evaluations remain limited to static medical question answering or one-shot generation, under-representing longitudinal state, interruptions, and human handoffs. We introduce an episode-level evaluation protocol for healthcare NLP agents. The protocol separates evidence across model, agent, and simulated-workflow behavior; specifies a five-field episode schema; and defines annotation and scoring for state continuity, evidence traceability, and escalation decisions. It is instantiated as four task templates: documentation update, evidence retrieval, patient messaging, and triage handoff. The protocol does not claim to measure clinical outcomes or deployment value. Instead, it supplies a reproducible intermediate evaluation layer between static benchmarks and prospective workflow studies, with an explicit cost-sensitive treatment of missed versus unnecessary escalation.

</details>


### [125] [Emotional Labor Strategy Preferences in LLM Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00310)
*Mohammad Saim,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）在日常社交情境中基于人格特质的情感劳动策略选择。通过构建首个包含500个社会情境的标注数据集，评估五个LLM在不同人格角色下的行为决策，发现模型更倾向于深度扮演，且尽责性和情绪稳定性显著预测该倾向。人格设定对输出具有可靠影响，且在不同模型和情绪类型间存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感劳动研究依赖职业场景中的自我报告量表，缺乏对日常社交情境中人格特质与情感劳动策略关系的跨情境验证。本研究旨在利用人格化大语言模型探索人格如何驱动个体在非职业情境下选择表面扮演、深度扮演或真实表达等情感劳动策略。

Method: 构建包含500个社会情境的数据集，每个情境提供三种行为选项（表面扮演、深度扮演、真实表达）。从大规模人格数据库中选取50个虚构角色，通过观察者评定的双极形容词组合与角色自述项目进行双重人格建模。使用五个大语言模型在两种人格条件下评估所有情境，并分析其行为选择模式及人格影响的可变性。

Result: 模型整体更倾向于深度扮演；尽责性和情绪稳定性显著正向预测深度扮演偏好。熵分析显示人格设定能有效影响模型输出，且这种影响在不同模型和情绪类型间存在差异。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够模拟人格驱动的情感劳动策略选择，支持人格特质在日常社交情境中对情感调节方式的影响。研究为理解情感劳动的内在心理机制提供了新范式，也揭示了大语言模型在社会认知模拟中的潜力。

Abstract: Emotional labor is the effortful management of emotional displays to meet social or professional expectations. Personality traits have been correlated with emotional labor strategies, yet research on this link relies almost exclusively on self-report scales administered only in occupational settings. We investigate whether large language models injected with psychometrically grounded personas reproduce these personality-driven selection patterns across everyday social scenarios. We construct the first emotional labor strategy dataset of 500 socially situated events, each offering three behavioral choices corresponding to surface acting, deep acting, and genuine expression. We source 50 fictional characters from a large-scale personality repository and profile each through two parallel tracks: observer-rated bipolar adjective composites and in-character self-report items. Five LLMs evaluate all scenarios under both persona conditions. We find that models align more towards deep acting, and that Conscientiousness and Emotional Stability consistently predict this preference. Entropy analysis confirms that persona reliably influences the output and varies across models and emotions.

</details>


### [126] [Latent Mechanisms of Language Control in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00325)
*Ryo Mitsuhashi,Sabri Boughorbel,Majd Hawasly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多语言大模型中的非意图性语言切换问题，比较了三种识别跨层转换器中语言控制潜变量的方法：基于激活值的选择（ValSel）、基于激活频率的选择（FreqSel）和基于大语言模型生成的潜变量注释选择（AnnSel）。通过构建两个多语言基准测试，对七种语言的语言引导进行细粒度分析。在Gemma-2-2B和Qwen3-4B上的干预实验表明，三种方法均能有效操控生成语言，其中FreqSel表现最优，AnnSel则提供可解释的潜变量选择。消融分析显示这些方法选取的是非重叠但各具功能的潜变量子集，表明存在冗余而非单一的“语言方向”。代码与数据已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型在生成过程中常出现不必要的语言切换，影响输出一致性。为提升语言控制能力，需识别并操纵其内部的语言控制潜变量。

Method: 提出三种潜变量选择方法：基于激活值、激活频率及大模型生成的注释；构建两个多语言基准以评估语言引导效果；在Gemma-2-2B和Qwen3-4B上进行针对性干预实验，并通过消融分析验证潜变量特性。

Result: 三种方法均能有效操控生成语言，其中FreqSel性能最强，AnnSel提供可解释性；潜变量选择具有非重叠性但功能性互补，说明语言控制存在冗余机制。

Conclusion: 语言控制潜变量可通过多种方式有效识别，且不同方法选取的潜变量虽不重叠但均具功能，表明语言控制机制具有冗余性而非单一路径。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models can exhibit unintended code-switching -- unnecessarily alternating between languages during generation. We present a comparative study of three methods that identify language-controlling latents in cross-layer transcoders: activation value-based selection (ValSel), activation frequency-based selection (FreqSel), and LLM-generated latent annotation-based selection (AnnSel). To evaluate the efficacy of these methods in identifying language-controlling latents, we introduce two multilingual benchmarks that exhibit code-switching for fine-grained analysis of language steering across seven languages. Through targeted intervention experiments on Gemma-2-2B and Qwen3-4B, we find that all three methods effectively manipulate generation language, with FreqSel achieving the strongest overall performance, while AnnSel offering interpretable latent selection through explicit language annotations. A knock-out analysis suggests the methods select non-overlapping but each-functional latent subsets, indicating redundancy rather than a single canonical language direction. Code and data can be found at https://github.com/rm-3284/Latent-Mechanism-Multilingual.

</details>


### [127] [The Curse of Multilinguality in Lexical Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00329)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了多语言词法归一化模型中，训练语言数量对性能的影响。发现随着联合训练语言数量增加，每种语言的准确率显著下降，存在‘多语言诅咒’现象；即使保持总数据量不变，性能下降更严重，表明问题源于模型容量竞争而非数据量不足；语言类型学距离无法可靠预测最佳共训语言数。结论：对于紧凑型归一化模型，训练语言越少效果越好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于多数语言缺乏标注数据，常采用单一模型联合训练多种语言进行词法归一化。但未明确最优联合训练语言数量，因此需要探究训练语言数量对模型性能的影响。

Method: 使用一个固定容量的字符级模型，在12种语言的标准基准上，系统性地改变联合训练的语言数量（从1到12），并测量各语言的归一化准确率；通过控制总训练数据量，分析性能下降是否由数据量或模型容量竞争引起；进一步检验语言类型学距离与理想共训语言数之间的关系。

Result: 随着联合训练语言数量增加，各语言的准确率显著下降，出现明显的‘多语言诅咒’；在固定数据量条件下，性能下降更剧烈，说明主要原因是模型容量竞争；语言类型学距离无法稳定预测理想共训语言数，相关性仅存在于少数语言。

Conclusion: 对于紧凑型词法归一化模型，训练语言数量应尽量减少；少量语言（通常1-4个）共同训练优于将所有语言混合训练于单一模型，即‘少即是多’。

Abstract: Lexical normalization rewrites the noisy, non-standard words that fill user-generated text (tmrw, u, gr8) into their standard forms. Because labelled data is scarce for most languages, a popular shortcut is to train a single model on many languages at once. We ask a simple question: how many languages should such a model be trained on? Using one fixed-capacity character-level model and twelve languages from a standard benchmark, we vary the number of jointly trained languages from one to twelve and measure per-language accuracy. We find a clear curse of multilinguality: accuracy is highest when a language is trained with only a few others, often just one to four, and then falls steadily and substantially, dropping by about forty percent as the rest are piled on. A control that holds the total amount of training data constant makes the decline arrive sooner and fall further, which points to competition among the languages for one fixed-size model rather than to how much data is available. We also test whether a language's typological distance from the others predicts its ideal number of co-training languages, and find no dependable rule: any apparent relationship rests on a couple of languages and does not hold up. For compact normalization models, less can be more: a few languages beat pooling everything into a single model.

</details>


### [128] [Topic Matching in the Wild: Benchmark and Lessons from Real-World ASR Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00330)
*Saman Rahbar,Xiliang Zhu,Irvin Cardoza,David Rossouw*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对客服中心实时助手工具中的话题相关性判断任务，构建了一个基于真实通话转录的标注数据集，并比较了三种匹配器（正则表达式、零样本句子嵌入编码器、Gemini-based LLM）在两种话题表示方式（关键词和自然语言描述）下的表现。实验表明，结合自然语言描述的轻量级大模型匹配器性能显著优于嵌入模型和正则表达式模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在客服中心中，实时助手需要准确判断客户语音是否与预定义话题相关，但输入数据（如自动语音识别转录）存在噪声大、不清晰、重复多、缺乏标点等问题，现有方法在实际场景中表现有限，亟需系统性评估与优化。

Method: 构建一个真实通话转录的人工标注数据集，对比三种匹配器类型：正则表达式基线、零样本句子嵌入编码器、Gemini-based大语言模型匹配器；同时测试两种话题表示方式：关键词与自然语言描述。

Result: 轻量级大语言模型匹配器在使用自然语言描述作为话题表示时表现最佳，显著优于嵌入模型和正则表达式模型，在噪声环境下具有更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在复杂的现实语音数据中，结合自然语言描述的轻量级大语言模型是实现高精度话题相关性判断的有效方案，为实时客服助手提供了更优的技术路径。

Abstract: In contact centers, real-time agent-assist tools determine, for each of many predefined topics, whether a live customer utterance is relevant and display a coaching card to the agent when it is. The input is noisy and challenging: ASR(Automatic Speech Recognition) transcripts of spontaneous phone conversations, which can be unclear, repetitive, and mostly lack punctuation. To systematically study this real-world task, we curate a human-annotated topic-utterance judgments dataset sourced from real call-center transcripts. We compare three types of matchers: a regex-based baseline, zero-shot sentence embedding encoders, and Gemini-based LLM matchers. In addition, two types of topic representations are studied in our benchmark:keyphrases and natural language description. Our empirical experiments highlight the superior performance of lightweight LLM matchers over embedding and regex models when equipped with natural language descriptions.

</details>


### [129] [Two locked tests of phase-structure features for transition prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00335)
*Abraham Chachamovits*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对旋转注意力中相位结构的理论假设进行了两项预设的实证检验，评估相位衍生特征是否能提升对承诺或矛盾终点的预测能力。研究1在1,136个案例中比较了PC-2与基线模型，配对AUROC差异为+0.00087，99%置信区间包含零，未达到预设阈值+0.05，未通过检验。研究2在1,415个转换中测试15种层处理方案，采用锁定的合取规则要求多个正向增量，但无任何处理方案通过。最终官方选择为无提升，理论论文未撤回，但在预先设定规则下未发现额外排名提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验旋转注意力中相位结构理论在预测承诺或矛盾终点任务中的有效性，验证相位衍生特征是否带来实际性能提升。

Method: 研究1采用冻结矛盾类别管道，进行封闭式主比较；研究2在开放块b0-b4上构建15种层处理方案，使用20x5分组交叉验证，并应用锁定的合取规则筛选有效处理。所有分析均基于预设规则和数据集。

Result: 研究1的AUROC差异未显著优于基线（+0.00087，99%置信区间含零），未达+0.05阈值；研究2中所有处理均未满足合取规则，无方案通过。最终判定为无额外提升。

Conclusion: 尽管理论假设未被撤回，但在严格预设规则下的实证检验中，未发现相位特征带来的额外排名提升，表明其在当前条件下未能提供可验证的预测优势。

Abstract: A published theoretical account of phase structure in rotary attention was subjected to two pre-specified empirical tests of whether phase-derived features improve prediction of a commitment or contradiction endpoint over a baseline that does not receive those features. Study 1 froze a contradiction-category pipeline and scored a sealed primary comparison of PC-2 against baseline. On 1,136 eligible cases the paired AUROC difference was +0.00087. The 99% interval included zero, and the difference did not reach the pre-specified threshold of +0.05. Advancement was not passed. Study 2 developed fifteen layer treatments on open blocks b0-b4 only (1,415 transitions, 20x5 grouped folds). A locked conjunctive rule required a positive PC-2 mean-repeat increment, a positive increment on at least four of five seed blocks, and a positive mean of those five differences. No treatment advanced. The official selection is null. The theoretical paper is not withdrawn. The extra ranking lift was not found under the rules locked in advance.

</details>


### [130] [From Tool Use to Technological Agency: LoopCAT as a Local-First, Open-Source Tool for Translation Technology Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00344)
*Gokhan Dogru,Adrià Martín Mor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoopCAT is a local-first computer-assisted translation tool co-developed with OpenAI Codex using GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6, licensed under Apache-2.0. It integrates translation memories, terminology, quality assurance, document exchange, and optional AI services, with interfaces in English, Catalan, and Turkish. The tool serves as both a practical environment and teaching material by enabling students to translate UI strings, evaluate AI-generated drafts, and test their revisions. A framework is proposed linking workflow competence, evaluative judgement, and technological agency, structured around four forms of participation: operating workflows, evaluating outputs, inspecting mechanisms, and making interventions. The paper outlines a six-session teaching sequence, an assignment for UI localization, a placeholder example, and an assessment rubric. While it distinguishes implemented features from educational claims, no new student learning outcomes are reported. The study emphasizes the need for empirical validation of whether these activities enhance judgement, transfer, or engagement.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Translation students must learn not only how to use translation technologies but also how to critically judge the decisions those technologies offer. There is a need for transparent, inspectable environments where learners can explore the interplay between translation choices, software rules, data, and interface design. LoopCAT aims to address this gap by providing a teachable, open-source platform that supports both technical and pedagogical exploration.

Method: The development of LoopCAT was co-created using OpenAI Codex with GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6. Analysis draws on repository history, implementation inspection, and verification records of a specific development build. The tool's architecture includes local project storage, translation memories, terminology management, quality assurance checks, document exchange, and optional integration with local or hosted AI services. The pedagogical framework is grounded in four participatory roles: operating workflows, evaluating outputs, inspecting configurations, and making bounded interventions. Classroom application is supported by a six-session teaching sequence, a UI-localization assignment, a placeholder example, and an assessment rubric.

Result: LoopCAT has been implemented with core functionalities including local storage, translation memory, terminology handling, QA checks, and multilingual UI support. The tool enables students to engage in hands-on translation tasks involving AI-generated drafts and iterative revision. The framework provides a structured approach for integrating technology use with critical evaluation in translation education. However, no empirical evidence of improved learning outcomes—such as enhanced judgement, transferability, or participation—is presented.

Conclusion: LoopCAT offers a transparent, inspectable, and teachable environment for exploring the relationship between human judgment and machine-driven translation decisions. While its technical capabilities are demonstrated, the educational impact remains unproven and requires future empirical study. The framework and tool together provide a foundation for advancing translation pedagogy in the age of AI.

Abstract: Translation students need to learn both how to use translation technologies and how to judge the choices those technologies make available. This article presents LoopCAT, an Apache-2.0-licensed, local-first computer-assisted translation environment co-created with OpenAI Codex using GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6, and proposes a framework connecting workflow competence, evaluative judgement, and technological agency. The account draws on repository history, implementation inspection, and the verification records of an identified development build. LoopCAT combines local project storage, translation memories, terminology, quality assurance, document exchange, and optional connections to local or hosted AI services. Its English, Catalan, and Turkish interface catalogs also make the application itself available as teaching material: students can translate English UI strings into another language, review the existing automatically generated target drafts, import their revisions, and test the interface. We organize these opportunities around four forms of participation: operating a workflow, evaluating outputs, inspecting and configuring mechanisms, and making or defending a bounded intervention. A six-session sequence, a UI-localization assignment, a placeholder example, and an assessment rubric specify how teachers could use the framework. The paper separates implemented capabilities from proposed educational benefits; it reports no new student-learning outcomes. It distinguishes the latest package checks from earlier regression evidence and sets out a protocol for classroom evaluation. LoopCAT provides an inspectable setting for teaching how translation decisions interact with data, interfaces, and software rules. Whether these activities improve judgement, transfer, or participation remains an empirical question.

</details>


### [131] [Detecting Hidden Behaviors in LLMs via Activation-matched Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00351)
*Robin Haselhorst,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督的检测方法——激活匹配微调，用于发现大型语言模型中仅在特定条件下激活的隐藏行为（如后门触发、睡眠代理触发等），无需事先知道触发条件或目标行为。该方法通过将一个公开的锚模型微调以复现可疑模型在良性语料上的激活模式，并通过比较两者在评估提示上的残差来识别异常。由于良性语料不覆盖稀疏的触发区域，锚模型学习的是正常计算而非隐藏行为，因此触发提示及其语义邻近提示会产生较大残差，从而暴露异常行为。实验表明该方法能可靠检测第三方和自定义模型中的隐藏行为，且对防御感知攻击也具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以检测大型语言模型中仅在特定条件下激活的隐藏行为，且通常依赖对触发条件或目标行为的先验知识。本研究旨在开发一种无需先验知识的通用检测机制，以应对隐蔽威胁。

Method: 激活匹配微调：使用公开锚模型，在小规模良性语料上微调以模仿可疑模型的激活表现；通过计算两模型在评估提示上的激活残差，识别出可能触发隐藏行为的输入。

Result: 在多个第三方和自定义模型上，该方法均能有效检测到隐藏行为，包括后门、沙袋化、话题性审查等；即使面对防御感知攻击，也无法在不牺牲模型功能的前提下规避检测。

Conclusion: 激活匹配微调是一种高效、无监督的检测框架，能够可靠发现大型语言模型中隐蔽的触发性行为，为模型安全提供有力保障。

Abstract: Large language models can hide hidden behaviors that activate only under narrow conditions, such as backdoor triggers, sleeper-agent deployment cues, sandbagging, or topic-conditioned censorship. Such behaviors are difficult to detect without prior knowledge what to look for. We present activation-matched finetuning, an unsupervised detection method that assumes no knowledge of the trigger or the target behavior. Given a suspect model and a publicly available anchor, we finetune the anchor to reproduce the suspect's activations on a small benign corpus, and score each evaluation prompt by the residual between the two models. Since no benign corpus covers the sparse trigger region, the reference learns the benign computation but not the hidden behavior. Therefore, trigger prompts -- and, crucially, their semantic neighbors -- incur a large residual that signal the presence of unusual behavior to the defender. Testing our method across third-party models and custom models, activation-matched finetuning surfaces hidden behavior reliably. Furthermore, we empirically consider a natural defense-aware attack and showcase that it fails to suppress our detection method without sacrificing the behavior itself.

</details>


### [132] [Neurosymbolics for Data Engineering: Achieving Long Context Token Reduction Without Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00367)
*Vishvesh Bhat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可无缝集成到现有大语言模型（LLM）中的新型神经符号层，显著提升逻辑推理能力并缓解长上下文计算瓶颈。该层在无需任务特定微调或强化学习人类反馈（RLHF）的情况下，使BIRD-CRITIC和LiveSQLBench等基准测试的平均准确率提升85%；同时通过符号化处理压缩与优先级排序关键信息，将有效令牌使用量减少超50%，并将长上下文任务的时间复杂度从O(n²)降至约O(n)，大幅提升数据工程应用的可靠性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数据工程任务中日益重要，但其高计算开销（如Transformer的O(n²)时间复杂度）和对微调依赖限制了其广泛应用。需在不增加训练成本的前提下提升推理准确性与长上下文处理效率。

Method: 设计一种即插即用的神经符号层，结合神经网络与符号逻辑处理机制，用于增强模型逻辑推理能力，并通过符号化方法动态压缩和筛选上下文信息，降低计算负载。

Result: 在多个基准测试中实现平均85%的准确率提升，且无需微调或RLHF；在长上下文任务中有效令牌使用减少超过50%，时间复杂度从O(n²)降至近似O(n)，显著降低推理资源消耗。

Conclusion: 所提神经符号层为大语言模型提供了一种高效、通用且无需额外训练的优化方案，既增强了逻辑推理性能，又大幅缓解了长上下文计算压力，推动了大模型在数据工程领域的实用化部署。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly deployed for sophisticated data engineering tasks such as generating structured queries from natural language, Text-to-SQL, and automating complex spreadsheet operations. However, maximizing their utility demands both higher finetuning-free accuracy and solutions to the computational bottleneck imposed by the Transformer architectures inherent quadratic (On2) time complexity. This paper introduces a novel drop-in neurosymbolic layer designed to seamlessly integrate into existing LLM backbones enhancing logical reasoning and mitigating long-context resource consumption. On the reasoning front, the layer immediately and significantly improves performance yielding an average accuracy increase of 85% across rigorous benchmarks including BIRD-CRITIC and LiveSQLBench, critically achieving these gains without any task specific finetuning or RLHF. Concurrently, we repurpose this approach to address the severe computational strain of long context inference. By leveraging symbolic processing to prioritize and compress relevant contextual information the layer reduces the effective token usage by over 50% and brings the effective time complexity down from O(n2) to approximately O(n) on certain long context tasks. This dual impact approach not only makes LLMs substantially more reliable for data engineering but also drastically reduces the computational pressure on inference chips, making long context tasks more manageable and cost effective.

</details>


### [133] [Removable and Irreducible: A Token-Cost Ledger for the Multilingual Tokenization Tax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00378)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了大语言模型在非英语文本上的高令牌开销问题，提出将令牌层视为源编码，并构建了一个令牌成本账本，将语言成本分解为可消除的编码冗余、残余编码松弛、内在内容项和不可约的字符到音素项。实验表明，通过脚本匹配的代码可减少64%的额外开销，且内在内容差异不足6%，说明税负主要源于表示而非信息本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理非英语文本时存在显著的令牌开销，导致计算成本大幅增加。研究旨在探究这种开销中有多大比例是可消除的，从而优化跨语言处理效率。

Method: 将令牌层建模为源编码，利用香农率 $H/\log_2 V$ 作为理论下限，构建令牌成本账本，对不同语言的成本进行分解；使用小样本训练脚本匹配编码器，评估可消除的冗余；并通过控制实验验证编码效率。

Result: 在FLORES-200数据集上，生产级分词器对印地语等脚本的成本最高可达英语的8.9倍；脚本匹配编码器可消除中位数64%的超额成本（95%置信区间[0.638, 0.647]），而内在内容差异小于6%；构造的编码器可消除98%的冗余，注意力成本最多可降低79倍。

Conclusion: 语言间的令牌税主要来自表示方式而非信息内容，通过合理设计分词策略可显著降低计算开销，但该研究仅关注计算与内存开销，不涉及模型质量或跨语言方向性判断。

Abstract: Large language models pay a well-documented tax on non-English text: the same content costs several times more tokens, and because attention is quadratic in sequence length, far more compute. We ask how much of this tax is removable. Framing the token layer as source coding -- transformer compute is monotone in sequence length, whose per-atom floor is the Shannon rate $H/\log_2 V$, an object already applied to tokenizers in prior work -- we assemble a token-cost ledger that splits each language's cost, at fixed parallel content, into a removable coding redundancy, a residual coding slack, an intrinsic-content term, and an orthogonal, irreducible grapheme-to-phoneme term that governs the multimodal rather than the text cost. On FLORES-200 across eight languages, a production tokenizer costs up to $8.9\times$ more tokens for Indic scripts than for English; a script-matched code trained on $1,012$ sentences removes a median $64\%$ of that excess (bootstrap 95\% CI $[0.638, 0.647]$), and a script-fair information floor shows the intrinsic content differs by under $6\%$ -- the tax is representational, not informational. A constructed code removes $98\%$ of a controlled source's redundancy, and the token tax implies up to $79\times$ attention cost. We are explicit about scope and failure: this is compute-and-memory accounting, not a model-quality claim; we neither measure nor claim the cross-lingual direction of the orthographic term; and our matched code is a conservative small-data demonstration. We contribute the unifying ledger, the removable-versus-intrinsic attribution, and an open one-command harness.

</details>


### [134] [Late Transformer Layers Recode Syntax Canonically: Evidence from Greek Scrambling and Cross-Layer Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00416)
*Christos Nikolaos Zacharopoulos,Revekka Kyriakoglou,Chara Tsoukala,Théo Desbordes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过跨层泛化分析发现，大型语言模型在后期层（20-31）中对希腊语句法信息的表征发生方向性重构，而非简单丢失；探针在后期层训练后测试早期层时表现低于随机水平，将99.3%的非标准句误判为标准句，且系数符号在第22层附近反转，表明模型趋向于标准化句式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究证明句法信息可在早期和中期变压器层中被解码，但其在后期层中的演变机制仍不明确。本研究旨在揭示后期层中句法信息的动态变化，特别是在非标准句式处理上的表征转变。

Method: 采用跨层泛化分析方法，基于三个经过希腊语调优的大规模语言模型，在严格控制的最小对（如现代希腊语中的主谓宾与谓主宾结构差异）任务上进行评估，通过训练探针于后期层并在各早期层上测试其性能，分析句法信息的传递与重构情况。

Result: 探针在后期层训练后测试早期层时表现低于随机水平（经聚类校正，p<0.01），将99.3%的非标准句子错误分类为标准句；探针系数在约第22层发生符号反转，表明存在向标准句式方向的表征重构，而非单纯的信息衰减。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了变压器模型在后期层中句法信息的表征格式发生根本性转变，超越了已知的句法可解码性下降现象，提出了一项可直接验证的人类脑电与脑磁图研究预测。代码与实验材料已在OSF公开。

Abstract: Probing studies have established that syntactic information is decodable in early and middle transformer layers, but what happens to that information in later layers remains poorly understood. We apply a cross-layer generalisation analysis to three Greek-tuned large language models evaluated on tightly controlled minimal pairs: object-relative constructions in Modern Greek, where canonical (Subject-Verb-Object; SVO) and non-canonical (Verb-Subject-Object; VSO) orders differ only in within-clause word order, while preserving propositional meaning. When a probe trained on late layers (20-31) is tested on each early layer individually, it produces below-chance transfer (cluster-corrected, p<0.01), classifying 99.3% of non-canonical sentences as canonical. Probe coefficients reverse sign around layer 22, indicating a directional recoding toward the canonical form rather than simple information loss. These findings characterise a representational format change in late transformer layers that goes beyond the well-established decline in syntactic decodability, and they generate a directly testable prediction for human EEG and MEG decoding studies using the same stimuli. Code and stimuli are publicly available on OSF.

</details>


### [135] [(V)LMs generalize beyond surface co-occurrence: Evidence from cross-modal number agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00443)
*Zach Studdiford,Kanishka Misra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过跨模态泛化方法，探讨视觉语言模型（VLMs）在语法数概念上的抽象能力。研究发现，无论数字信息来自文本还是视觉线索，模型均表现出非平凡的跨模态泛化能力，且内部机制对两类线索的处理方式相似，表明统计学习模型能够超越表面共现规律，展现出与抽象规则一致的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型主要依赖共现学习语法数，常被质疑缺乏对抽象规则的理解，而仅依赖具体词汇。传统文本测试无法有效区分这种抽象能力，因为分布线索（如is/are、this/these）会暴露语法数。因此，需要一种新方法来检验模型是否具备真正的抽象泛化能力。

Method: 采用跨模态实验设计，使用视觉语言模型（VLMs），通过添加新词嵌入并仅在学习阶段更新这些嵌入的方式，训练模型理解新名词的语法数。对比两种条件：一是仅依靠视觉线索判断数；二是结合文本线索进行判断。通过行为表现、表征动态和因果机制分析模型在两种条件下的表现差异。

Result: 在两种条件下，模型均表现出显著的跨模态泛化能力，且内部机制对语言和非语言线索的处理方式相似，说明模型不仅依赖表面共现，还能在多模态输入中实现抽象层面的推理。

Conclusion: 研究表明，视觉语言模型等统计学习系统能够超越表面频率模式，实现跨模态抽象泛化，支持其具备类似人类抽象规则的学习能力，挑战了‘仅依赖共现’的观点。

Abstract: Language models learn about grammatical number primarily from co-occurrence, and show frequency effects as a result---sometimes taken to indicate that they do not learn abstract ``rules'', and are instead dependent on specific lexical items.
