TL;DR: 该研究基于超过300万次编程提交的CodeInsight数据集，构建了一个评估模型在迭代式问题解决中预测表现变化的基准。研究比较了参数化、序列化和生成式模型，发现经调整的递归状态空间模型（RSSM）在四个课程中的三个上表现最佳；而基于大语言模型（LLM）的预测器虽准确度较低，但能生成完整代码，便于分析错误模式。研究还发现模型编码能力越强，其作为学习者行为预测器的表现反而越差，表明LLM更适合作为上下文驱动的生成式解题工具而非精确预测器。数据与代码已公开以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解迭代式问题解决过程中性能如何随尝试次数变化，以及错误和策略如何演变，对揭示人类学习和自主智能体的学习机制至关重要。现有研究缺乏大规模、细粒度的数据支持，尤其在编程学习场景中。因此需要一个能够捕捉多轮提交、反馈与修正过程的大规模数据集和统一评估框架。

Method: 构建CodeInsight数据集，包含3286名本科生在两年内完成的2门入门级C++课程的超300万次代码提交，涵盖测试用例级结果、时间戳和源码。在此基础上，设计统一校准与评分协议，评估包括参数模型、序列模型和生成式模型在内的多种方法。特别引入一种基于离散潜在变量的递归状态空间模型（RSSM），并使用大语言模型（LLM）生成每轮提交的完整代码进行对比分析。

Result: RSSM在四个课程中的三个上取得了最强的预测准确性；LLM模型虽然预测精度较低，但能生成完整的代码提交，使研究者可直接观察失败模式。此外，研究发现模型的编码能力越强，其对真实学生行为的预测能力反而越弱，说明LLM更应被视为一种受上下文引导的生成式求解器，而非忠实的行为预测器。

Conclusion: 本研究通过大规模数据和多模型比较，揭示了不同模型在迭代学习预测中的优势与局限。特别是指出高能力生成模型在模拟真实学习过程时存在偏差，强调未来需发展更贴近学习动态的建模方法。数据与代码的公开将推动该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Solving problems through repeated attempts is a sequential modeling task: at each step, the solver receives feedback and decides how to revise their solutions. Predicting whether performance improves, plateaus, or regresses across attempts is central to understanding any iterative problem-solving process in both human learners and autonomous agents. Beyond outcomes, modeling what errors persist and how strategies shift across attempts provides deeper insight into the mechanics of sequential learning. Studying these dynamics requires observing many solvers as they attempt, receive feedback, and revise. Programming courses with automated grading provide this setting, as students iteratively submit code to test suites and receive feedback on every attempt. We therefore curate CodeInsight, a large-scale dataset of over 3 million submissions from 3,286 undergraduates across 2 introductory C++ courses in 2 academic years, with test-case-level outcomes, timestamps, and source code. On this dataset, we build a benchmark that evaluates models spanning parametric, sequential, and generative traditions under a shared calibration-and-scoring protocol, including a Recurrent State Space Model (RSSM) adapted to track solver characteristics through discrete latent variables and an LLM-based predictor that generates explicit solutions. The adapted RSSM achieves the strongest predictive accuracy on three of the four courses. The LLM predictor is less accurate but produces full submissions at each attempt, enabling direct analysis of failure modes. We find that the model's coding proficiency is inversely related to predictive performance in this setting, with the LLM better understood as a generative solver conditioned on context rather than a faithful predictor of solver behavior. We publicly release our code and the dataset on request to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [173] [Calibration is the Bottleneck: An Action-Class Diagnostic of Multi-Turn Tool-Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00949)
*Kangjia Zhao,Jiajun Li,Haozhan Shen,Wei Chow,Linfeng Li,Hang Song,Lingdong Kong,Chen Zhi,Tiancheng Zhao,Songhua Liu,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多轮工具调用的诊断框架，通过分解失败模式为动作类校准偏差和执行失败两类，揭示了现有评估中被掩盖的校准问题。实验显示，开放权重模型虽在整体准确率上接近或超过闭源模型，但其内部存在显著的动作选择偏差，且该偏差无法被状态评分器察觉。通过上下文扰动可调整校准，但效果因模型家族而异，甚至方向相反。研究主张在多轮工具调用评估中引入动作类诊断以更真实反映模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多轮工具调用评估依赖聚合准确率，但该指标无法揭示不同情境下的性能不平衡，尤其难以发现动作类校准偏差这一隐藏失败模式。

Method: 提出基于四类动作（TOOL_CALL/ASK/REFUSE/CONFIRM）的诊断框架，引入自揭示上限Acc <= GAR（黄金动作召回率），通过边界违反（Acc > GAR）识别校准偏差，通过边界松弛（GAR >> Acc）定位执行失败。

Result: 诊断框架揭示了动作类校准偏差是重要且被忽视的失败模式；上下文扰动可重塑校准，但效果在不同模型家族间差异巨大，甚至方向相反（最高+11.5 vs -21.0个百分点）。

Conclusion: 多轮工具调用评估应补充动作类诊断，以暴露模型在各类场景中的真实行为，避免仅依赖聚合准确率带来的误导。

Abstract: Multi-turn tool calling is a core evaluation scenario for large language model (LLM) agents. On public tool-calling benchmarks, open-weight models now approach or even surpass closed-source frontier models in aggregate accuracy. However, this metric averages over many different multi-turn situations and obscures whether progress is balanced across them. We propose an action-class-oriented diagnostic framework that decomposes multi-turn failures into two orthogonal modes: action-class miscalibration and action-execution failure. The framework operates over a four-class action space (TOOL_CALL/ASK/REFUSE/CONFIRM) and introduces a self-revealing upper bound Acc <= GAR (Gold Action Recall); the two modes show up as bound violation (Acc > GAR, exposing state-grader masking of miscalibration) and large bound slack (GAR >> Acc, localizing execution failure within TOOL_CALL). We validate it on a panel of tool-calling models across multiple multi-turn benchmarks. Across our panel, the diagnostic reveals action-class miscalibration as a substantial failure mode the state grader cannot see. This gap inflates standing for heavily tool-trained families, which our diagnostic separates from families with context-appropriate action choice. Calibration is reshapable through context-only perturbations, but the reshape is heterogeneous: a single perturbation moves accuracy in opposite directions across families (up to +11.5 vs -21.0 pp on the same scenario), and its effect further depends on the perturbation mechanism. We argue that multi-turn tool-calling evaluations should supplement aggregate accuracy with action-class diagnostics that expose what the model actually does in each scenario.

</details>


### [174] [PersianAnonymizer: Evaluating LLM-Labeled Training for Efficient NER-based Anonymization in Persian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00958)
*Mohammad Hossein Shalchian,Mostafa Amiri,Amir Mahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过使用指令微调的大语言模型（LLM）生成标注数据，训练轻量级命名实体识别（NER）模型，实现波斯语客户聊天的实用化匿名化。比较了三种LLM（DeepSeek-V3-0324、GPT-OSS-120B、Qwen3-235B-A22B-Instruct-2507）在统一JSON协议下的零样本和少样本标注，构建四个语料库，并基于MatinaRoberta模型进行训练与评估。结果表明，来自OSS_ZeroShot的监督数据在宏平均F1和标签覆盖率召回率（LCR）上表现最佳，且训练后的NER模型可在单张消费级显卡RTX 3090上仅用约2分钟完成对4万条消息的标注，验证了该方法在高质量、低成本匿名化中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波斯语客户聊天数据的匿名化缺乏高效、低成本且高精度的解决方案。传统人工标注成本高昂，而直接使用大模型进行标注存在一致性与效率问题。因此，需要一种结合大模型标注能力与轻量级模型部署优势的实用路径，以实现工业级数据隐私保护。

Method: 采用三种指令微调的大语言模型生成波斯语聊天文本的实体标注，依据统一的JSON格式构建四类语料库（零样本与少样本）。基于MatinaRoberta架构训练轻量级token分类器，评估指标包括整体与类别层面的精确率、召回率、F1值，以及标签覆盖率召回率（LCR）。通过测试集上的标注延迟对比，分析大模型与轻量级模型在硬件资源上的性能差异，并利用词级Venn图量化不同标注者间的行为差异。

Result: OSS_ZeroShot语料库提供的监督信号在宏平均F1和标签覆盖率召回率（LCR）方面表现最优；训练出的NER模型可在单张RTX 3090 GPU上于约2分钟内完成对4万条消息的标注，显著优于大模型直接标注的延迟。跨标注者行为分析显示标注一致性良好，说明所选标注策略具备可复制性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过利用大语言模型生成高质量标注数据并训练轻量级NER模型，可实现波斯语工业数据的高效、低成本、高精度匿名化。该方法为实际应用提供了可行的技术路径，兼具性能与部署优势。

Abstract: We target practical anonymization of Persian customer chats by training a compact NER model from LLM-labeled supervision and selecting the best labeler for deployment. We compare three instruction-tuned LLMs: DeepSeek-V3-0324, GPT-OSS-120B, and Qwen3-235B-A22B-Instruct-2507, to produce span annotations under a shared JSON protocol, yielding four corpora (OSS_ZeroShot, Qwen_ZeroShot, Qwen_FewShot, DeepSeek_FewShot). A MatinaRoberta-based token-classifier is trained per corpus and evaluated with token-level Precision/Recall/F1 (overall and per-class). We also report Label Coverage Recall (LCR), the proportion of gold non-O tokens predicted as non-O, and quantify cross-labeler behavior via a token-level Venn on test annotations. Finally, we contrast test-set annotation latency of the LLMs on H200 nodes with the trained NER's test-time labeling on a single RTX 3090. Results show that supervision from OSS_ZeroShot yields the strongest macro-F1 and LCR, while the resulting NER labels an entire 40K-message test set in approximately 2 minutes on one consumer GPU. This establishes a practical path to high-quality, low-cost anonymization for Persian industrial data.

</details>


### [175] [Disclosure-Gated User Simulation for Companion-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00982)
*Yao Liu,Yu He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于门控机制的用户模拟器，通过条件化信息释放来解决现有大语言模型用户模拟中过度合作的问题。该机制采用五级阶梯式门控结构，融合为三个可观察层级，训练时结合合成数据与真实人类对话数据，使模拟器在运行时无需显式知道每条数据对应的门。实验表明，该模拟器在基准测试中显著提升排名稳定性（最大排名偏移超过随机种子变化的噪声范围），且系统得分无明显变化。作者定义了两个评估标准：排序保序性与得分尺度稳定性，仅所提模拟器满足两者。其排行榜与原始模拟器相关性高达0.993，而使用前沿大模型直接提示作为模拟器虽提升分数但不改变排名，掩盖了实际偏差。本文补全了原基准研究缺失的规范、消融实验、人类评估、负控和下游敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型作为用户模拟器存在过度合作问题，导致系统评分仅依赖提问数量而非真实交互质量，影响评估可靠性。需要一种更精准、可验证的用户行为建模方式。

Method: 设计并实现一个五级阶梯式门控机制，将信息释放条件化于代理状态；利用合成数据学习门控行为，真实数据校准人类反应模式；通过训练使模拟器自动判断信息层级，无需运行时标注；进行多维度评估包括消融实验、人类研究、负控及敏感性分析。

Result: 所提出的模拟器在12个系统测试中表现出极高的排名稳定性，最大排名偏移超过随机种子扰动范围，且系统得分无显著变化；其排行榜与原始模拟器高度一致（相关性0.993）；其他方法如直接使用前沿模型作为模拟器虽提升分数但不改变排名，无法察觉偏差。

Conclusion: 本文提出的门控用户模拟器是唯一同时满足排序保序性和得分尺度稳定性的方案，显著提升了评估环境的可信度与可复现性，填补了原有基准研究在规范性与验证性上的空白。

Abstract: Using a large language model to play the user is now standard in scalable evaluation. It has a repeatedly diagnosed failure: the simulated user is excessively cooperative, so a system under test can score by the sheer number of questions it asks rather than by making the user willing to speak. We answer with a disclosure gate conditioning information release on the companion agent's behaviour: its state is a ladder of five ordered gates, merged onto three observable depth layers. We specify, ablate, and audit it, and train a user simulator against that specification. Gating behaviour is learned from the training corpus's synthetic branch, while the real branch supplies how people speak and react; after training, the simulator need not be told at runtime which gate each item sits behind. The gate is a load-bearing component of the environment: on the English corpus of a published companion-agent benchmark (CompanionBench), once training no longer states per example which gate each item sits behind, the largest rank displacement across 12 systems under test exceeds the noise band set by re-running that environment under a new seed, while per-system scores show no detectable change. We state two acceptance criteria: a ranking must be order-preserving, and absolute scores must be scale-stable. Of the candidates we examine, only one passes both -- the simulator we release -- and its leaderboard correlates at 0.993 with the benchmark's original simulator. By contrast, prompting a frontier model as the simulator barely moves the ranking while shifting every score upward -- a shift invisible to anyone checking the ranking alone. The environment we specify is the one that benchmark already used. That publication describes the mechanism in about four hundred words, and we supply what it lacked: specification, ablations, human studies, negative controls, and downstream sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [176] [Inspicio: Open-Vocabulary, LLM-Based Sense Retrieval for Historical Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00998)
*Michele Ciletti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Inspicio，一个无需源语言词义清单或映射的开放词汇检索管道，用于将上下文中的词元链接到Open English WordNet中的同义词集。通过指令微调的大语言模型生成上下文的双语翻译、候选定义和词素，结合密集定义-同义词相似性、稀疏词素匹配和最大边际相关重排序进行混合检索。在拉丁语和古希腊语感知动词、PREMOVE数据子集及意大利语历时样本上评估，最佳配置在感知动词测试集上达到96% Recall@50，各组件均有显著贡献，并在跨语言和域外设置中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数历史和低资源语言缺乏完整的词典资源，传统词语消歧方法依赖于词义清单和词义映射，难以适用。需要一种无需预设词义库的开放词汇方法来处理这些语言。

Method: 使用指令微调的大语言模型生成上下文的双语翻译、候选定义和英语词素；通过密集定义-同义词相似性、稀疏词素匹配和最大边际相关重排序实现混合检索。

Result: 在拉丁语和古希腊语感知动词测试集中达到96% Recall@50，各组件均带来可衡量提升，在跨语言和域外场景中仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: Inspicio成功实现了对历史与低资源语言的开放词汇词义消歧，无需依赖源语言的词义清单或映射，为多语言词义理解提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Word Sense Disambiguation has advanced rapidly for English and a handful of well-resourced modern languages, but it continues to assume the existence of a sense inventory and a word-to-sense mapping in the source language (Navigli, 2026). These assumptions break down for most historical and low-resource languages, whose dedicated WordNets are either incomplete or still under construction. We present Inspicio, an open-vocabulary retrieval pipeline that links tokens in context to synsets of the Open English WordNet (McCrae et al., 2020) without requiring any source-language inventory or mapping. For each occurrence, an instruction-tuned LLM produces two English translations of the surrounding sentence, a small set of candidate dictionary-style definitions, and a few candidate English lemmas. These outputs drive a hybrid retrieval step that combines dense definition-synset similarity, sparse lemma matching, and Maximal Marginal Relevance re-ranking. We evaluate the pipeline across a 6x6 grid of LLMs and sentence-embedding models on a new bilingual set of manually annotated Latin and Ancient Greek perception verbs, on a subset of PREMOVE dataset (Farina, 2025), and on a diachronic sample of Italian. The best configuration reaches 96% Recall@50 on the perception-verb test set, with each component contributing measurable gains, and remains competitive in the out-of-domain and cross-lingual settings.

</details>


### [177] [Right Frame, Wrong Rule: Cultural Cues Expose the Financial Knowledge Gap They Were Meant to Close](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00999)
*Rania Elbadry,Ahmed Heakl,Saeed Almheiri,Fan Zhang,Muhra AlMahri,Xueqing Peng,Mohsinul Kabir,Shuyao Wang,Yi Han,Saadeldine Eletter,Duzhen Zhang,Preslav Nakov,Yuxia Wang,Fajri Koto,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在伊斯兰金融背景下，语言模型面对不同规范框架时的决策问题，提出‘规范多元主义’概念，并通过四选一分类法分离框架选择与框架内正确性。研究发现文化线索会引导模型偏向某一框架，但导致框架内答案错误，尤其在非前沿模型中表现明显；即使大模型在强信号下97%选择伊斯兰框架，仍有57-66%的选择是错误的。这揭示了‘刻板印象陷阱’，并支持‘能力条件路由假设’：模型可能倾向其更擅长的框架，而文化线索暴露了框架间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在存在多种规范框架时如何选择合适框架，并评估其在不同框架下的正确性，以揭示文化线索对框架选择的影响及潜在的认知偏差。

Method: 采用四选择分类法分离框架选择与框架内正确性，分析十二个模型、两种语言和五十个社会人口学特征信号下的表现，识别文化线索对框架选择和准确率的影响。

Result: 文化线索显著影响框架选择，导致非前沿模型准确率大幅下降；大型开放权重模型在强信号下97%选择伊斯兰框架，但其中57-66%为错误选择；两选择评估会误导性地显示高一致性，实际存在严重错误。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了语言模型在规范多元主义情境下的系统性偏差，强调需考虑框架选择与模型能力之间的关系，支持能力条件路由假设，提示未来应设计更精细的评估机制以避免文化线索带来的误导。

Abstract: When a question has valid answers under different normative frameworks, a language model must decide which framework to use and whether it can answer correctly within it. We call this setting normative pluralism and study it in Islamic finance using a four-choice taxonomy that separates framework selection from within-framework correctness. This separation reveals the stereotype trap: a cultural cue steers a model toward one framework, but the model selects an incorrect answer within that framework. Across twelve models, two languages, and fifty demographic signals, cultural cues change framework selection and reveal substantial differences in accuracy, especially among non-frontier models. Under the strongest signal, large open-weight models select the Islamic framework 97% of the time. A two-choice evaluation would report near-perfect alignment, although 57--66% of those selections are incorrect. These findings motivate, but do not directly test, the competence-conditioned routing hypothesis: models may favor frameworks where they are more accurate, while cultural cues may expose framework-specific competence gaps.

</details>


### [178] [Phrase-Localized Language-Contrastive Guidance: Training-Free Localized Accent Control for Code-Switching Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01016)
*Che Hyun Lee,Sangkwon Park,Donghun Kang,Dongwook Lee,Youngho Cho,Heeseung Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的推理框架Phrase-Localized Language-Contrastive Guidance (LCG)，用于在跨语言文本到语音合成中恢复代码切换短语的原生口音。该方法通过为每个语言区域分配独立的语言引导，并使用自注意力探查技术自动识别短语边界，实现对每个部分的精准语言引导，从而避免口音泄露并保持说话人身份和自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音合成在处理代码切换时，外语文本片段常带有主语言的口音，而非其原生口音，影响自然性和可懂性。需要一种无需微调或额外模型的方法来恢复外语文本的真实口音。

Method: 提出基于自注意力探查的短语边界检测方法，结合局部语言对比引导（LCG），为每个语言区域分别施加语言引导，实现跨语言语音生成中的精准口音控制。

Result: 在多种语言对上，LCG显著提升了代码切换短语的自然度，有效抑制了口音泄漏，同时保持了说话人身份和语音自然性，且无需任何微调或辅助模型。

Conclusion: LCG是一种高效、无需训练的推理框架，能够准确恢复代码切换语音中各语言片段的原生口音，显著提升跨语言语音合成的质量。

Abstract: Current speech synthesis struggles with code-switching, which mixes a foreign language phrase into a primary language utterance, causing the phrase to be spoken with the primary language's accent rather than its native one. We propose Phrase-Localized Language-Contrastive Guidance (LCG), a training-free inference framework that restores a native accent to code-switched phrases in cross-lingual text-to-speech. LCG replaces the single language guidance applied across the whole utterance with a separate guidance for each region, so each part is guided by its own language. To choose where to apply this localized guidance, we propose a self-attention probing technique that finds the phrase boundaries without external alignments. Together, these components generate speech in which each region carries the accent of its own language, requiring no fine-tuning or auxiliary models. Across diverse language pairs, LCG robustly increases the nativeness of the code-switched phrase while suppressing accent leakage, and preserving overall speaker identity and naturalness.

</details>


### [179] [PCoMoE: Shifting MoE Inference from Monolithic Expert Selection to Fine-Grained Path Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01024)
*Ziyan Gan,Fangxin Liu,Chenyang Guan,Junjie Wang,Ning Yang,Haomin Li,Xiang Li,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Zongwu Wang,Li Jiang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PCoMoE提出一种细粒度路径组合的Mixture-of-Experts推理框架，通过路径级计算建模和兼容性感知的层间剪枝策略，突破传统专家原子化执行的限制，实现高达1.31倍的推理加速并提升10%模型准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE框架将专家作为原子执行单元，限制了优化边界，导致细粒度的内部计算冗余未被充分挖掘。

Method: 提出路径级专家计算建模，设计兼容性感知的层间剪枝策略，并构建硬件友好的执行引擎以利用可复用的子专家结构。

Result: 实验表明PCoMoE在保持严格开销约束下，实现了最高1.31倍的端到端推理加速，并提升了10%的模型准确率。

Conclusion: PCoMoE通过细粒度路径组合重新定义MoE推理范式，在不增加额外开销的前提下显著提升效率与性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale Large Language Model (LLM) capacity efficiently by activating a sparse subset of experts per token. However, modern MoE inference remains heavily constrained by the rigid, whole-expert abstraction. Existing frameworks manage, schedule, or prune experts as atomic execution units, which fixes the optimization boundary too early and leaves fine-grained intra-expert computational redundancy underexplored. In this work, we present PCoMoE, a path-compositional execution framework that shifts MoE inference from coarse-grained expert selection to fine-grained path composition. PCoMoE incorporates a path-level formulation of expert computation, a compatibility-aware layer-wise pruning strategy to suppress low-value path combinations, and a hardware-friendly execution engine to exploit reusable sub-expert structures under strictly bounded overheads. Experimental results demonstrate that PCoMoE achieves up to a 1.31x end-to-end inference speedup while enhancing model accuracy by 10%. The code is available at https://github.com/gzyyy0/PCoMoE

</details>


### [180] [Lagged Coupling: Internal Representations Become Readable Before They Become Causal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01048)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在Pythia系列模型中，尽管线性探测能在训练早期（第1000步）读取目标变量，但沿该方向的控制（steering）几乎始终无效，表现出‘滞后耦合’现象。这种结构可分解为三个独立维度：(i) 内部可读性（从首个检查点起即饱和，AUROC ≥ 0.990）；(ii) 行为可读性（随规模增大逐步发展，12B模型仅在最终检查点达到0.909）；(iii) 因果有效性（几乎总是无效，偶有反向作用，仅一处正向脉冲未被解析）。读先于写（11/11单元），且表示空间头寸增长达57倍，但因果写入仅占头寸的0.11%以下。预注册实验显示单次启动假设结果为不确定（尺度斜率+0.24，95%置信区间[-0.60, +0.87]；时间投票3:3），由三轨分解解释。OLMo-2复现验证了方向存在但幅度减弱。研究警示不能仅凭探测准确率推断可控性，并揭示了表示形成远快于因果读出整合的发展瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究模型内部表示的可读性与因果控制有效性之间的不匹配现象，尤其是在不同规模和训练阶段下的动态变化，以揭示潜在的机制瓶颈。

Method: 采用线性探测、因果干预实验、多尺度模型分析（Pythia 160M–12B，八次检查点，四类任务）、预注册协议验证、以及对OLMo-2的复制实验，系统分解并量化内部可读性、行为可读性和因果有效性三维度。

Result: 内部可读性从早期即高度饱和；行为可读性随规模与训练逐步提升；因果有效性几乎恒为零或负，仅一个孤立正向信号；表示头寸大幅增长而因果写入微弱；预注册实验支持不确定性结论；复现验证方向存在但减弱。

Conclusion: 模型中的表示形成显著领先于因果读出的整合，导致探测准确性无法转化为有效控制，揭示了‘滞后耦合’这一结构性瓶颈，提示应谨慎依据探测结果推断模型可操控性。

Abstract: Across the full Pythia suite (160M-12B, eight checkpoints, four task families), a linear probe can read a target variable from the residual stream as early as step 1,000 at every scale -- yet steering along that same reading direction remains null-equivalent in 43 of 48 model-checkpoint cells. Internal readability systematically outruns causal efficacy, and the lag does not shrink with scale. We call this structure lagged coupling and decompose it into three dissociable tracks: (i) internal readability, saturated (AUROC >= 0.990) from the first checkpoint everywhere; (ii) behavioral readability, which develops gradually and progressively later at larger scales (12B reaches 0.909 only at the final checkpoint); (iii) causal efficacy, almost always null-equivalent, occasionally counterproductive early, with one isolated positive pulse (12B, step 8,000, z = +2.49) our grid cannot resolve. The ordering is dominantly read-before-write (11/11 units, no inversion). Representation headroom along the probe direction grows up to 57x with training and scale while causal write-in stays below 0.11% of headroom -- the variable is increasingly written into the representation and increasingly ignored by the readout. Under a fully pre-registered protocol, both single-onset hypotheses resolve INDETERMINATE (scale slope +0.24, 95% CI [-0.60, +0.87]; time vote 3:3) -- a disciplined negative explained by the three-track decomposition. A pre-registered OLMo-2 replication preserves the direction at attenuated magnitude. Our results caution against inferring steerability from probe accuracy and establish a developmental bottleneck: representation formation reliably outpaces causal readout consolidation.

</details>


### [181] [OUTLETS: Output-Length Prediction from Speculative Decoding Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01068)
*Weihuang Wen,Yingying Liu,Yichuan Liu,Wenqi Zeng,Li Zhou,Chumin Sun,Jie Sun,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OUTLETS利用推测解码框架中的草稿解码器产生的潜在表示，实现对输出长度的精准预测。该方法在不增加额外延迟的情况下，通过轻量级回归头实现低MAE预测，显著改善了请求调度效率，使短请求的P99延迟降低34.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型服务中输出长度的重尾分布导致资源分配和集群调度困难；现有长度预测方法存在延迟高或预测精度不足的问题，亟需高效且准确的解决方案。

Method: 识别推测解码与长度预测之间的结构关联，利用草稿解码器的潜在表示作为轨迹感知的长度预测信号，引入轻量级回归头构建预测模型。

Result: 在饱和去耦合服务场景下，OUTLETS预测可使标准调度策略更优地优先处理短请求，实现更均衡的请求分发，将短请求的P99延迟降低34.8%，且预测误差低于现有方法。

Conclusion: OUTLETS通过复用推测解码框架中的已有计算，实现了高效、高精度的输出长度预测，有效缓解了长尾分布带来的调度挑战，提升了系统整体性能。

Abstract: The heavy-tailed distribution of output lengths in Large Language Model (LLM) serving poses major challenges for resource provisioning and cluster scheduling. Although output-length prediction can mitigate these issues, existing approaches have key drawbacks: external proxy models add substantial latency and often have limited fidelity, whereas internal state-based methods are efficient but rely on shallow probes of current model states. We identify a structural connection between speculative decoding (SD) and length prediction: latent representations produced by the draft decoder in advanced frameworks (e.g., EAGLE-3) encode signals that are predictive of generation length. Building on this insight, we introduce OUTLETS (Output-Length Prediction from Speculative Decoding Backbones), which repurposes the speculative backbone as a trajectory-aware length predictor. When its draft representations are already computed for speculative decoding, OUTLETS adds only a lightweight regression head and achieves lower MAE than the evaluated methods. Under saturated disaggregated serving, OUTLETS predictions enable standard scheduling policies to prioritize shorter requests and distribute requests more evenly across decoding instances, reducing short-request P99 latency by 34.8%.

</details>


### [182] [Post-hoc Alignment of LLM-judges to Human Judgment Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01073)
*Sebastian Steindl,Nikos Voskarides,Alberto Gasparin,Diego Marcheggiani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了LLM作为裁判（LLMaJ）在自动评估中的表现，发现尽管其在预测单一聚合真实标签时接近人类水平，但在预测反映人类判断分布的软标签方面表现不佳。为此提出NAPHA方法，通过熵感知后处理对齐，显著提升软标签预测性能，尤其在高熵实例上效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMaJ评估通常仅与聚合的真实标签比较，忽略了人类标签变异（HLV）所包含的丰富信息，而近年来研究显示利用HLV具有潜力，因此需要系统性地研究LLMaJ在预测软标签上的表现并改进其能力。

Method: 提出NAPHA（熵感知后处理对齐）方法，首先将样本分配到离散熵类别，再通过专门训练的对齐模型进行路由，使LLM输出分布匹配人类判断分布（HJD）。

Result: NAPHA在多个基础LLM和数据集上均显著提升了软标签预测性能，尤其在高熵实例中改善明显；且通过模拟实验表明，提升熵类别预测准确性可进一步增强其实际效果。

Conclusion: LLMaJ在硬标签预测上已接近人类水平，但在软标签预测上仍有不足；NAPHA作为一种轻量级后处理对齐方法，能有效提升对人类判断分布的建模能力，尤其适用于复杂、多视角任务。

Abstract: The LLM-as-a-judge (LLMaJ) framework offers a cost-effective and reproducible solution for automatic evaluation. However, current evaluation practices typically compare LLMaJ judgments against aggregated ground-truth labels, overlooking the valuable information contained in Human Label Variation (HLV). Inspired by an increasing line of work that proposes to leverage HLV, we systematically study LLMaJ performance on predicting both a single, aggregated ground truth hard-label and unaggregated soft-labels that represent Human Judgment Distributions (HJD). Our results across five diverse datasets reveal that while LLMs achieve near human-level performance at hard-label prediction on most tasks, they exhibit poor performance when predicting soft-labels. To address this limitation, we propose NAPHA (eNtropy-Aware Post-Hoc Alignment), a simple yet effective lightweight post-hoc alignment method that matches the LLM distribution to the HJD by first assigning an instance to a discrete entropy class and then routing it to specialized, trained alignment models. We find that NAPHA consistently improves soft-labels prediction across base LLM models and datasets, with particularly strong gains on high-entropy instances where capturing diverse human perspectives is most critical. We also show via oracle experiments that improving entropy class prediction can substantially enhance NAPHA's practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [183] [StateSwap: Probing Support-Elimination Hidden States in Multiple-Choice Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01081)
*Chao Gao,Haijiang Liu,Qiyuan Li,Caicai Guo,Frank van Harmelen,Jinguang Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大语言模型在支持型与排除型框架下对同一多选题回答不一致的原因，通过设计最小差异化的双框架提示协议，利用未训练的特殊标记[STATE]作为干预接口，发现两种框架在中间层诱导出可分离的[STATE]激活。通过交换这些激活，系统性地改变预测结果并提升跨框架一致性，证明了激活的行为相关性。此外，基于双框架对比提取的均值差导向比对照激活添加方向表现出更受控的层间响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在不同提问框架下回答不一致的内在机制，尤其是框架是否引发不同的内部表示。

Method: 设计双框架协议，使用支持型与排除型提示，保持评估目标一致；引入未训练的[STATE]特殊标记，通过其残差流激活作为干预接口；分析激活在不同层的分布，并进行激活交换和方向引导实验。

Result: 两种框架诱导出可分离的[STATE]激活，集中在中间层；激活交换能系统性改变预测并提升跨框架一致性；均值差方向比对照添加方向具有更稳定的层间响应。

Conclusion: 框架差异导致大语言模型产生不同的内部表示，[STATE]激活是行为相关的关键因素，且可通过干预手段实现跨框架一致性提升。

Abstract: Large language models often answer the same multiple-choice question inconsistently when it is posed under support-oriented and elimination-oriented framings. We investigate whether these discrepancies arise from different internal representations induced by the two framings. We introduce a dual-framing protocol with minimally varied prompts that use either support- or elimination-oriented framing while keeping the evaluation target fixed. To probe the internal computation, we append an untrained special token, [STATE], and treat its residual-stream activation as an intervention interface. Across both models, the two framings induce separable [STATE] activations concentrated in intermediate layers. Swapping these activations between paired prompts systematically changes predictions and improves cross-framing agreement, providing intervention-based evidence that the activations are behaviorally relevant. Beyond instance-level substitution, mean-difference steering directions derived from the dual-framing contrast exhibit more bounded layer-wise responses than matched contrastive activation addition directions under the evaluated protocol.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond Magnitude: Contrastive Routing for Modular Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01100)
*Nikolaos Xiros,Dimitrios Damianos,Maria-Eleni Zoumpoulidi,Leon Voukoutis,Vassilis Katsouros,Georgios Paraskevopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In current Mixture-of-Experts architectures, routing is performed based on representations dominated by structure shared across all tokens, limiting expert specialization. We show that contrasting each token against an Exponential Moving Average of the layer's hidden states, rather than routing on absolute magnitude, concentrates the routing signal onto a low-dimensional, highly separable subspace. Building on this, we propose the Contrastive Routing Mechanism (CoRM), which scores each expert by the gap between its affinity for the incoming token and its affinity for this shared reference state, interpreted through a distinct per-expert projection. The resulting experts have routing boundaries that align with linguistic structure significantly more than the Top-k baseline. Our experiments show that CoRM improves average zero-shot accuracy by +0.67 to +1.69 points (Top-1) and +1.38 to +1.77 points (Top-2) over standard Top-k MoE baselines on nine zero-shot reasoning benchmarks, at the minimal cost of 2.9% added parameters and 2.6% added FLOPs per token.

</details>


### [185] [When Modality Gap Reduction Fails: Prediction-Level Hubness in CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01103)
*Shota Sato,Hajime Kiyama,Tosho Hirasawa,Mamoru Komachi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Modality gap correction in CLIP does not always improve zero-shot accuracy because it can cause prediction-level hubness, where predictions concentrate on a few classes due to altered decision margins. This effect is observed across both linear and learning-based correction methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand why reducing the modality gap between image and text representations in CLIP does not consistently improve zero-shot classification performance, despite better average alignment.

Method: Analyzing zero-shot classification decision structure using Linear correction as a tractable case; examining how modality gap correction affects class-wise decision margins and prediction concentration across multiple datasets.

Result: Accuracy degradation under gap correction is consistently linked to increased prediction concentration, indicating that modality gap reduction can harm downstream decision structure even when average alignment improves.

Conclusion: Modality gap correction should be evaluated not only by average cross-modal alignment but also by its impact on the downstream prediction distribution to avoid adverse effects on model generalization.

Abstract: Reducing the modality gap between image and text representations in CLIP is widely expected to improve cross-modal alignment and downstream performance. However, a smaller average image-text gap does not necessarily lead to consistent accuracy gains. We analyze this mismatch from the perspective of the decision structure in zero-shot classification, i.e. selecting the most similar class-text prototype for an input image. Zero-shot accuracy depends not only on average image--text alignment, but also on class-wise decision margins. Using Linear correction as an analytically tractable case, we show that modality gap correction can alter the relative decision structure among classes and cause predictions to concentrate on a small subset of classes. We refer to this output-space failure mode as prediction-level hubness. Furthermore, experiments across multiple datasets show that accuracy degradation under gap correction is consistently associated with increased prediction concentration, both for Linear correction and for learning-based correction methods. This provides a systematic explanation of why modality gap reduction does not consistently improve CLIP zero-shot accuracy from the perspective of downstream decision structure. Our results suggest that gap correction should be evaluated not only by average alignment, but also by its impact on downstream prediction structure.

</details>


### [186] [ClinTraceBench: Source-Verifiable Longitudinal Clinical Reasoning over EHR-Derived Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01111)
*Huimin Wang,Zhengyi Zhao,Yutian Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ClinTraceBench基准，评估八种历史表示方法在多访视患者轨迹上的表现，发现压缩策略导致关系信息丢失，且上下文长度与拒绝回答行为非单调相关；在成本-性能权衡上，Haiku模型在全上下文条件下优于Sonnet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床大模型助手需处理多访视患者轨迹，但现有紧凑历史表示方式（如检索、结构化时间线、LLM摘要、代理记忆）是否保留纵向推理所需信号尚无量化评估。

Method: 构建包含385个源自MIMIC-IV的经验证对话和事件ID溯源的基准数据集，设计九项任务分类（T1–T9），采用L0–L4确定性验证及L5人工审计（98.92%一致性），在6,271个问题上评估八种历史表示策略（包括无上下文基线、仅最后就诊、完整上下文、BGE-M3稠密检索、两种压缩方案、两种代理记忆系统）在四个骨干模型上的表现。

Result: 四点发现：(SP4) T3注入探针显示压缩导致关系信息丢失，即使有归属句在构造前，Mem0、A-Mem和LLM摘要也仅恢复0–5.3%的注入正例；(SP1) 压缩策略在多访视趋势和跨患者比较中存在聚合代价；(SP2) 仅限最后就诊与完整上下文之间的差距达+29.8pp（GPT-4o-mini）至+62.7pp（Haiku）；(SP3) 拒绝回答行为随上下文长度非单调变化；(SP4) 在帕累托前沿上，Haiku在完整上下文条件下优于Sonnet（成本$25.76 vs. $106.21），颠覆了“越大模型越优”的直觉。

Conclusion: 当前主流的历史表示策略在保留临床纵向信号方面存在显著缺陷，尤其在压缩和代理记忆场景下；模型效率与性能的权衡需重新审视，小模型在特定条件下可超越大模型。

Abstract: Clinical LLM assistants must reason over multi-visit patient trajectories, yet whether the compact history representations used to scale them---retrieval, structured timelines, LLM summaries, agentic memory---preserve the longitudinal signal clinical reasoning needs has not been measured. We introduce ClinTraceBench: 385 MIMIC-IV-derived verified dialogues with event-ID provenance, a nine-task taxonomy (T1--T9), and L0--L4 deterministic + L5 human-audit validation (98.92\% agreement). We evaluate eight history representation strategies---a no-context floor, \textit{last-visit-only}, \textit{full-context}, BGE-M3 \textit{dense-retrieval}, two compression schemes, and two agentic-memory systems (\textit{Mem0}, \textit{A-Mem})---across four backbones (DeepSeek-V3, GPT-4o-mini, Haiku~4.5, Sonnet~4.6) on 6{,}271 questions: 32 cells, 200{,}672 predictions. Four findings: (SP4) a controlled T3 injection probe isolates compression-induced \textit{relation} loss---with the attribution sentence present \textit{before} construction, \textit{Mem0}, \textit{A-Mem} and \textit{llm-summary} still recover only 0--5.3\% of the injected positives; (SP1) compressed strategies pay an aggregation tax on multi-visit trends and cross-patient comparisons; (SP2) the blind-to-full gap spans $+29.8$~pp (GPT-4o-mini) to $+62.7$~pp (Haiku); (SP3) abstention scales non-monotonically with context length. On the Pareto frontier Haiku dominates Sonnet under \textit{full-context} (\$25.76 vs.\ \$106.21), inverting the ``biggest backbone wins'' heuristic.

</details>


### [187] [EDRAC: Benchmarking Arabic Dialect Reading Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01113)
*Noor Abo Mokh,Kirill Chirkunov,Teresa Lynn,Nizar Habash,Reham Marzouk,Malik H. Altakrori,Younes Samih,Muhammed Abu Odeh,Nour Rabih,Rahaf Alshahrani,Hamad Alshehhi,Hamdan Al-Ali,Muhra Almahri,Besher Hassan,Mohamed Anwar,Abed Alhakim Freihat,Preslav Nakov,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EDRAC is the first large-scale benchmark for dialectal Arabic machine reading comprehension and generative QA, covering five major dialects with 499 passages and 4,977 QA pairs generated via human-LLM collaboration. It reveals significant gaps in semantic quality and dialectal fidelity, exposing limitations of current evaluation metrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dialectal Arabic is under-resourced compared to Modern Standard Arabic, especially in machine reading comprehension and question answering. Existing benchmarks focus on formal MSA or multiple-choice QA, lacking coverage of naturally spoken dialects.

Method: EDRAC uses a human-LLM collaborative pipeline involving iterative generation, LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, and human verification to create high-quality QA pairs from naturally occurring spoken interactions across five dialects.

Result: Benchmarking shows substantial gaps between semantic answer quality and dialectal fidelity, indicating that current evaluation metrics are insufficient for assessing dialectal Arabic generation.

Conclusion: EDRAC offers a realistic and challenging benchmark for advancing NLP research in dialectal Arabic, highlighting the need for improved evaluation methods tailored to spoken dialects.

Abstract: Dialectal Arabic (DA) remains under-resourced compared to Modern Standard Arabic (MSA), particularly for machine reading comprehension (MRC) and question answering (QA). Existing Arabic QA benchmarks primarily focus on formal written MSA or multiple-choice QA, with limited coverage of naturally spoken dialects. Here, we aim to bridge this gap. We introduce EDRAC, the first large-scale benchmark for dialectal Arabic machine reading comprehension (MRC) and generative QA, covering five major dialects: Egyptian, Moroccan, Emirati, Syrian, and Saudi Arabic. EDRAC contains 499 passages derived from naturally occurring spoken interactions and 4,977 corresponding QA pairs generated through a human--LLM collaborative pipeline combining iterative generation, LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, and human verification. We benchmark Arabic-centric and multilingual LLMs on EDRAC using lexical and semantic metrics. Our results reveal substantial gaps between semantic answer quality and dialectal fidelity, highlighting the limitations of existing evaluation metrics for dialectal Arabic generation. EDRAC provides a realistic and challenging MRC benchmark for future research on dialectal Arabic NLP.

</details>


### [188] [Overfitting Mitigation via Singular Value Decomposition in Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01135)
*Riza Setiawan Soetedjo,Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Katsuhiko Hayashi,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SVD-MBR improves text generation by using Singular Value Decomposition to filter out noise in utility metrics, leading to better performance across various evaluation metrics, especially with neural metrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBR decoding is prone to metric overfitting, where optimizing one metric harms others; this work aims to mitigate that by separating true consensus from noise in utility metrics.

Method: SVD-MBR frames the pairwise utility matrix as a noisy signal and applies low-rank approximation via SVD, keeping only the top-$k$ components to denoise and regularize decoding.

Result: SVD-MBR achieves significant improvements across generalized metrics, with stronger benefits for neural metrics due to their inherent low-rank structure.

Conclusion: SVD-MBR effectively regularizes MBR decoding by leveraging the low-rank nature of high-quality metrics, making it more robust and generalizable, particularly when using neural evaluation metrics.

Abstract: Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding enables high-quality text generation by selecting the hypothesis that maximizes a utility metric over sampled pseudo-references. However, it is highly susceptible to metric overfitting: it can irregularly inflate the chosen utility metric at the direct expense of other unoptimized evaluation metrics. To mitigate this, we introduce SVD-MBR, which frames the pairwise utility matrix as a noisy information signal. By computing a low-rank approximation via Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) and retaining only the top-$k$ components, we effectively decouple true consensus from metric noise. Experiments demonstrate that SVD-MBR successfully regularizes decoding, yielding substantial gains across a range of generalized metrics. Furthermore, we reveal that this denoising is metric-dependent: neural metrics encode a robust low-rank consensus ideal for SVD, whereas surface-level metrics struggle to separate signal from metric noise.

</details>


### [189] [Does task decomposition improve automatic NLG evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01139)
*Sebastian Steindl,Nikos Voskarides,Alberto Gasparin,Diego Marcheggiani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了带有和不带有任务分解的LLM-as-a-judge（LLMaJ）方法在多个自然语言生成（NLG）数据集上的表现。研究发现，任务分解并未带来性能提升，此前报道的增益实际上源于使用人工标签作为训练数据，而非分解本身。此外，当人工标签可用时，无需任务分解的LLMaJ可达到与人类标注者相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索任务分解是否真正提升LLM作为裁判在无参考自然语言生成评估中的表现，澄清以往结果中可能存在的偏差来源。

Method: 在多个NLG数据集上系统对比带任务分解与不带任务分解的LLMaJ方法，控制变量分析性能差异，并检验人工标签在训练中的影响。

Result: 任务分解并未带来性能提升；先前报告的改进主要归因于使用人工标签训练，而非分解机制；无需分解的LLMaJ在有真实标签时可媲美人类评估者。

Conclusion: 任务分解并非提升LLMaJ性能的关键因素，其有效性被高估；在有高质量人工标签的情况下，简单且高效的非分解式LLMaJ即可实现接近人类水平的评估性能。

Abstract: The LLM-as-a-judge (LLMaJ) framework has emerged as a promising solution for cheap, reproducible, reference-free Natural Language Generation (NLG) evaluation. Prior work seeks to improve LLMaJ by decomposing evaluation tasks into simpler sub-tasks. In this work, we systematically compare LLMaJ methods with and without decomposition on multiple NLG datasets. We find no evidence that LLMaJ with task decomposition leads to performance gains over a fair baseline that does not use decomposition. Instead, we find that previously reported performance gains in decomposition-based LLMaJ stem from using human labels as training data, and not task decomposition itself. Also, we find that, when human labels are available, LLMaJ without using task decomposition can perform comparably to human annotators.

</details>


### [190] [Subword Segmental BabyLMs: Learning to Tokenise for Sample-Efficient Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01151)
*Francois Meyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提交2026年BabyLM挑战赛，提出两种新的可学习子词分段语言模型：SubSegGPT（解码器架构）和SubSegDeBERTa（编码器架构），均在训练中联合学习分词与语言建模。SubSegDeBERTa在严格模式下表现最佳，提升零样本评估性能；SubSegGPT在严格小规模赛道上超越传统基于分词的基线模型。结果表明，可学习子词分词能提高预训练样本效率。分析显示，模型逐渐收敛到兼顾形态对齐与细粒度分割的子词单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准语言模型训练中，子词分词作为预处理步骤，但可能引入人工偏差。本文旨在探索将分词过程纳入训练，让模型自主发现优化训练目标的子词单位，以提升样本效率和模型泛化能力。

Method: 提出两种新模型：SubSegGPT（自回归解码器）和SubSegDeBERTa（掩码生成编码器），均在训练中联合学习子词分段与语言建模任务。通过在严格和严格小规模赛道上进行训练与评估，对比传统分词方法的表现。

Result: SubSegDeBERTa在严格赛道上取得最佳零样本性能，SubSegGPT在严格小规模赛道上显著优于基线。两模型均表现出更高的样本效率，且子词学习动态显示其趋向于平衡形态对齐与精细分割的最优子词结构。

Conclusion: 可学习的子词分段机制能够有效提升语言模型在低资源场景下的预训练效率，其自动发现的子词单位具有良好的语言学意义，为未来轻量级语言模型设计提供新思路。

Abstract: In the standard LM training pipeline, subword tokenisation is applied as a preprocessing step. Subword segmental language modelling is an alternative paradigm in which tokenisation is learned during training, allowing the model to discover subword units that optimise its training objective. In this paper, we present our submission to the 2026 BabyLM Challenge, for which we develop two new subword segmental LMs: SubSegGPT and SubSegDeBERTa. SubSegGPT is a decoder-only model that learns tokenisation during autoregressive pretraining. SubSegDeBERTa is an encoder-based model that jointly learns to generate and tokenise masked words. We train both for the Strict and Strict-small tracks. Our top submission to Strict is SubSegDeBERTa, which achieves notable gains in zero-shot evaluation. Our top submission to Strict-small is SubSegGPT, which outperforms tokenisation-based baselines. Our results show that learnable subword tokenisation can improve sample-efficiency for BabyLM pretraining. We analyse the subword learning dynamics of our models and find that tokenisation gradually converges on subword units that balance morphological alignment and fine-grained segmentation.

</details>


### [191] [LLMPEDIA: Browsing, Verifying, and Comparing the Parametric Encyclopedic Knowledge of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01182)
*Muhammed Saeed,Simon Razniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMPEDIA 是一个实时开放的百科全书，通过递归提取三个大模型（GPT-5-mini、DeepSeek-V3.2、Llama-3.3-70B）参数记忆中的约130万条文章内容，对其中原子命题进行基于维基百科和精选网络资源的审计，发现真实准确率仅为68.4%，远低于MMLU基准的90%以上，且30.5%的命题缺乏足够证据支持，揭示了现有评测中因固定题集导致的可用性偏差。该平台提供五种一键查看模式，支持逐条审查模型生成内容的可信度，推动对长尾知识与幻觉问题的透明化研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在标准评测如MMLU上表现优异，但这些评测仅覆盖有限的问题集合，存在显著的可用性偏差；作者旨在揭示模型知识中未被评测覆盖的大量模糊或不可验证内容，从而暴露其真实知识边界。

Method: 从三个主流模型家族的参数记忆中无检索地递归生成约130万篇文章，随机抽取样本后，使用维基百科和经筛选的网络资源对每条原子命题进行支持、反驳或证据不足的标注，构建可公开访问的动态百科系统。

Result: 在随机样本中，模型生成命题的真实准确率为68.4%，比MMLU低超过21个百分点，30.5%的命题无法判定真伪，表明大量知识处于‘长尾’或‘幻觉’状态，无法通过现有权威来源验证。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型的知识覆盖远未达到其表面性能所暗示的程度，许多声明既非真实也非明显错误，而是缺乏证据支持。LLMPEDIA 提供了一个透明、可交互的平台，使用户能够逐条检视模型输出的可信度，有助于推动对模型真实能力与局限性的理解。

Abstract: Flagship language models appear saturated on benchmarks like MMLU (Hendrycks et al., 2021), scoring above 90% - yet benchmarks test only what the experimenter thought to ask, the availability bias of fixed question sets. LLMPEDIA makes this bias measurable and browsable. We recursively materialized ~1.3M articles from three model families' parametric memory (GPT-5-mini, DeepSeek-V3.2, Llama-3.3-70B) without retrieval, then audited a stratified sample of atomic claims against Wikipedia and a curated web stack, coloring every claim supported, refuted, or insufficient (Saeed and Razniewski, 2026). On a uniform random sample the true rate is 68.4% - more than 21 pp below MMLU - with 30.5% of claims insufficient: assertions no benchmark probes and the world's largest encyclopedia cannot adjudicate - long-tail knowledge or plausible hallucination, the evidence cannot tell - extending to free text the coverage gap GPTKB established for triples (Hu et al., 2025). The resulting live, open encyclopedia lets visitors inspect this frontier one claim at a time through five one-click views - link-traversal exploration, claim-level factuality, cross-model and political-persona comparison, and a guided topic drill-down - each page, claim, and verdict at a stable URL. LLMPEDIA is live at https://llmpedia.net

</details>


### [192] [PersuaRL: Reinforcement Learning-Driven Multi-Expert Selection for Persuasive Dialogue Generation in Insurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01188)
*Rohan Kirti,Akash Ghosh,Aryan Vats,Niladri Ghosh,Shipra Shriparn,Roshni Ramnani,Anutosh Maitra,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的框架PersuaRL，用于提升大语言模型在保险领域对话中的说服力。为此，研究构建了InsureDial数据集，专门捕捉汽车保险对话中的说服性沟通特征。通过多专家模块的自适应策略探索与协调，PersuaRL在多个基准数据集上表现出显著优于基线模型的说服效果，验证了其在上下文敏感对话中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在事实性对话中表现优异，但在需要信任与清晰表达的保险等专业领域，缺乏有效的说服能力。因此，亟需提升对话系统在特定场景下的说服力，以增强用户信任与互动效果。

Method: 提出PersuaRL框架，结合强化学习，使对话代理能够根据动态对话上下文，自适应地选择和协调多个专家模块的策略，从而生成更具说服力的回应。同时构建了专用于保险领域的说服性对话数据集InsureDial，支持模型训练与评估。

Result: 在两个基准说服性对话数据集（包括InsureDial）上的自动评估与人工评估均表明，PersuaRL生成的回应在上下文相关性和说服力方面显著优于基线模型，展现出更强的适应性与交互效果。

Conclusion: PersuaRL框架有效提升了大语言模型在保险领域对话中的说服能力，为高信任度、高复杂度场景下的智能对话系统提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are revolutionizing digital communication by powering conversational agents deployed across domains such as customer service, digital sales, and insurance. These agents, built on LLMs, can understand user input, retrieve relevant information, and generate coherent responses. However, while they excel at factual communication, they often lack the ability to engage in truly persuasive, context-sensitive dialogue, especially in domains like insurance, where trust and clarity are critical. Building on this need within the insurance domain, our work focuses on improving the persuasiveness of digital agents, aka LLMs. To support this, we introduce InsureDial, a Persuasive Insurance Dialogue dataset, designed to capture the nuances of persuasive communication specific to motor insurance interactions. We introduce PersuaRL, a reinforcement learning-based framework that equips LLM-driven dialogue agents with the ability to adaptively explore, select, and coordinate strategies across multiple expert modules, guided by the evolving dialogue context, to achieve more effective persuasion. We conduct extensive automatic human and qualitative evaluations on two benchmark persuasion dialogue datasets, including our InsureDial. Our evaluations consistently demonstrate that PersuaRL outperforms baseline, generating contextually appropriate and highly persuasive responses.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Task-Specific Prompt with Global Context for Multi-Task Graph Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00047)
*Zhiyang Qiu,Yangtao Wang,Xiaocui Li,Yanzhao Xie,Siyuan Chen,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TPGC提出一种双先验提示初始化方法，通过任务先验和结构先验的协同作用，提升低资源场景下图预训练模型的迁移能力。该方法首先在辅助图上进行短时同源多任务预训练以获取任务感知表示，再利用这些表示提取全局结构上下文并生成分层提示向量。实验表明，在6个主流基准上，TPGC在少样本设置下表现优于现有基线，参数量更少、运行时间更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图提示学习方法使用随机初始化提示，导致提示空间与预训练目标及图结构特征对齐不佳，削弱了提示表示的任务相关性、结构感知能力和可迁移性。

Method: 提出TPGC框架，包含任务先验注入模块和结构先验注入模块：前者在辅助图上进行短时多任务预训练以获得任务感知表示；后者基于此表示提取全局结构上下文，并通过聚合结构信息节点嵌入生成分层提示向量。

Result: 在6个主流图分类和节点分类基准上，TPGC在少样本设置下均显著优于现有方法，具有更少的下游可调参数和更低的运行时间。

Conclusion: TPGC通过显式建模任务先验与结构先验的协同关系，有效提升了提示表示的质量与可迁移性，是低资源图学习中一种高效且鲁棒的提示初始化方案。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning is an effective paradigm to adapt pre-trained graph models to downstream tasks in low-resource scenarios. However, existing multi-task graph pre-training frameworks generally use randomly initialized prompts, leading to poor alignment between the prompt space, pretext objectives and graph structural characteristics. This greatly weakens the task relevance, structural awareness and transferability of prompt representations. To address this challenge, we propose TPGC, a dual-prior prompt initialization solution that explicitly models the synergy between task prior and structural prior. Specifically, the Task-Prior Injection Module first conducts a short homologous multi-task pre-training on an auxiliary graph, enabling prompt initialization to inherit optimization preferences associated with multiple pretext tasks. Built on the task-aware representations, the Structure-Prior Injection Module further extracts transferable global structural context from the auxiliary graph, converting it into layer-wise prompt vectors by aggregating structurally informative node embeddings. Extensive experiments on 6 mainstream benchmarks covering node and graph classification show that TPGC achieves consistently better performance under few-shot settings than state-of-the-art baselines, with fewer downstream tunable parameters and lower runtime. The code is available at https://github.com/Virgilqiu/TPGC

</details>


### [194] [REAL-Q: E2E LLM Quantization via Dynamic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049)
*Qian Zhang,Yaoming Li,Zhewen Tan,Yanshu Wang,Heng Lu,Kun Su,Zongwei Lv,Wenhan Yu,Yongge Ma,Yinjun Han,Ruikuang Liu,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REAL-Q 是一种新型的后训练量化（PTQ）方法，通过实时端到端损失对齐和细粒度动态块级梯度下降，解决了传统PTQ方法因简化目标函数导致的信息错位问题。该方法在每128列后进行优化，并结合滑动窗口机制实现层间平滑过渡，显著减少误差传播。在 LLaMA-3.1 (8B/70B) 和 Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) 上，于 W4A16 设置下，相比最先进全局引导方法，端到端KL散度降低高达约49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA PTQ方法为了保持解析可处理性，过度近似全局损失（忽略跨通道耦合、将输出行分组聚合），并冻结整个层的海森矩阵，无法随损失景观变化而更新，导致信息错位问题。

Method: 提出REAL-Q：采用端到端对齐的代理损失函数，通过在每个列块（128列）后应用细粒度动态块级梯度下降进行精细化修正，并结合滑动窗口机制确保层间平滑过渡，有效缓解误差传播。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1 (8B和70B) 与Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) 模型上，于W4A16设置下，REAL-Q相较最先进全局引导方法，端到端KL散度降低最多达~49%。

Conclusion: REAL-Q突破了传统PTQ中为解析可处理性牺牲精度的权衡，实现了高精度、低误差传播的高效量化，是当前LLM量化部署的重要进展。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under strict resource constraints. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by column--a phenomenon we call information misalignment. We propose REAL-Q (Real-time E2E-loss Aligned LLM Quantization), a novel PTQ paradigm that breaks this compromise: instead of diluting the objective for the sake of analytic tractability, REAL-Q targets an end-to-end-aligned surrogate of the global loss and refines it via fine-grained, dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent applied after every column block (128 columns). By coupling this fine-grained correction with a sliding window mechanism for smooth cross-layer transitions, REAL-Q effectively mitigates error propagation across the network. On LLaMA-3.1 (8B and 70B) and Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) at W4A16, REAL-Q reduces end-to-end KL divergence by up to ~49% relative to state-of-the-art globally-guided methods.

</details>


### [195] [Convergence issues in Relational Concept Analysis based on AOC-posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00054)
*Xavier Dolques,Agnès Braud,Alain Gutierrez,Marianne Huchard,Florence Le Ber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在使用AOC-poset替代概念格时，关系概念分析（RCA）收敛性丧失的问题。尽管RCA在概念格基础上具有收敛性保证，但在AOC-poset上不再成立。论文揭示了其原因，识别了可恢复收敛性的条件，并提出了一种新的收敛变体：一旦创建关系属性便永不删除，从而保证收敛，但可能引入最终结构中不存在的概念引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，为避免组合爆炸并聚焦于最信息丰富的部分，常采用AOC-poset替代完整的概念格。然而，当将RCA应用于AOC-poset时，原有的收敛性不再保证，这影响了方法的可靠性与可用性，因此有必要深入研究该问题并提出解决方案。

Method: 通过形式化分析和反例构造，探讨RCA在AOC-poset上的行为；识别导致非收敛的关键因素；提出基于‘永久保留关系属性’的改进算法，并验证其收敛性。

Result: 证明了在一般情况下RCA在AOC-poset上不保证收敛；提出了在特定条件下恢复收敛的方法；设计并验证了一种新的收敛型RCA变体，虽可能导致冗余属性，但确保过程终止。

Conclusion: RCA在AOC-poset上的收敛性无法普遍保证，但可通过限制属性操作或引入保守策略来恢复。提出的收敛变体在保持结构简洁的同时实现了计算稳定性，适用于对效率与确定性要求较高的场景。

Abstract: Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) is an approach for conceptual classification building and rule discovery from a binary table describing a set of objects by a set of attributes. Extensions have been proposed to deal with non-binary and more complex data, such as Relational Concept Analysis (RCA) for multi-relational data. RCA aims to highlight groups of objects characterized by their relationships with other groups of objects. The richer and more complex nature of the underlying data allows RCA to produce richer results than FCA, at the expense of higher computational and interpretive complexity. The most commonly used conceptual classification structure in FCA is the concept lattice. However, in many applications, concept lattice substructures, such as AOC-posets, are preferred over the full lattice, either to mitigate combinatorial blow-up or to focus on the most informative parts of the structure. Indeed, in an AOC-poset, only concepts introducing an object or an attribute are represented, which makes AOC-posets smaller and easier to compute and use than concept lattices. Although RCA was originally defined on concept lattices, it can also be instantiated on AOC-posets. RCA is iterative and its convergence is guaranteed in the lattice-based setting, but this guarantee is lost when using AOC-posets. In this paper, we investigate this loss of convergence in detail. We show why convergence is no longer guaranteed in the general case, identify conditions under which it can still be ensured, and discuss how a dataset can be transformed to recover convergence. We also propose a convergent variant of the process, which preserves the AOC-poset structure: relational attributes, once created, are never removed, which guarantees convergence at the price of attributes that may refer to concepts absent from the final structures.

</details>


### [196] [DISTAL: Distillation and Self-Supervised Pretraining for Structure-Agnostic Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00059)
*Weiran Wang,Xintong Huo,Yueying Wang,Yusi Fan,Wenyan Wang,Xin Feng,Ruihao Xin,Lan Huang,Kewei Li,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DISTAL是一种结构无关的材料属性预测框架，通过结合自监督组成预训练与结构感知知识蒸馏，在低数据环境下实现高效预测。它利用145个组成衍生描述符在虚拟组成空间中学习可迁移的组成表示，并从预训练的ALIGNN教师模型中蒸馏结构知识到一个组成条件的学生模型，从而在推理时无需结构输入即可利用结构先验。该方法融合显式组成描述符、预训练潜在特征和蒸馏结构特征，显著提升多任务性能，在39个基准任务中优于参考基线37个任务，证明了组合预训练与结构蒸馏的互补性，为小数据材料信息学中的纯组成预测提供了有效路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低数据设置下，材料属性预测面临标签样本少的问题，而高精度模型通常依赖晶体结构信息，限制了其在早期筛选阶段的应用。当结构信息缺失或不可用时，如何实现准确且鲁棒的属性预测成为关键挑战。因此，需要一种不依赖结构输入但能利用结构先验知识的方法。

Method: DISTAL采用双先验框架：首先在大规模虚拟组成空间中使用145个组成衍生描述符进行自监督组成预训练，以获得可迁移的组成表示；然后通过知识蒸馏将预训练的结构感知模型（ALIGNN）中的结构知识转移到一个仅依赖组成的学生模型中，使学生模型在推理时无需结构输入也能受益于结构先验。最终整合组成描述符、预训练特征与蒸馏结构特征，构建统一的预测管道。

Result: 在39个基准任务上，包含所有三类信号的多模态配置表现最佳，有37个任务超越参考基线，整体性能优于所有其他特征组合。结果表明，组成预训练与结构蒸馏提供互补先验，有效支持在小数据条件下实现稳健的纯组成预测。

Conclusion: DISTAL通过结合组成预训练与结构知识蒸馏，成功实现了无需结构输入的高性能材料属性预测，验证了两种先验信号的互补性，为低数据场景下的材料设计提供了实用且有效的解决方案。源代码和预训练模型可在指定链接获取。

Abstract: Materials property prediction remains difficult in low-data settings, where many target properties are supported by only a limited number of labeled samples. Models with the strongest predictive accuracy often depend on crystal structures, which restricts their use in early-stage screening when structural information is limited or unavailable. To address this challenge, we propose DISTAL, a dual-prior framework for structure-agnostic materials property prediction that combines self-supervised compositional pretraining with structure-aware knowledge distillation. DISTAL first learns transferable compositional representations from a large virtual composition space using 145 composition-derived descriptors. It then distills structural knowledge from a pretrained ALIGNN teacher into a composition-conditioned student. This setting allows structural priors to be used during training without requiring structural inputs at inference. By integrating explicit compositional descriptors, pretrained latent features, and distilled structural features within a unified prediction pipeline, DISTAL captures complementary signals that are difficult to recover from any single representation alone. Across 39 benchmark tasks, the best-performing multimodal configuration combines all three signals, and improves over the reference benchmark on 37 tasks. DISTAL achieves the strongest overall performance among all evaluated feature combinations. These results indicate that compositional pretraining and structural distillation provide complementary priors and offer a practical route to robust composition-only prediction in small-data materials informatics. The source code and the pre-trained models are anonymously available at: https://osf.io/eq96d/overview?view_only=451617f42f7849e08750bd1852b48980 and will be released at the official link after acceptance.

</details>


### [197] [ReNFT: Repairing Mode Collapse in Reward Post-Training via Internal Probability-Mass Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00061)
*Yuchen Bao,Chao Wen,Haowei Wang,Ruoxin Chen,Donghao Luo,Jiahui Zhan,Wenjian Huang,Shen Chen,Yiting Wang,Taiping Yao,Chengjie Wang,Shouhong Ding,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReNFT提出一种内部概率质量重校准方法，修复奖励后训练中因模式坍缩导致的低多样性问题，通过无条件探测和双路径反事实生成，实现对已坍缩适配器的修复，同时保留高奖励性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖外部信号或接口来缓解扩散生成器在奖励后训练中的模式坍缩问题，但无法修复已坍缩的适配器且难以保持原有奖励性能。本文旨在解决这一缺陷，提出一种无需外部干预、可逆修复坍缩适配器的方法。

Method: ReNFT首先使用无条件探测识别'反中心'提示以暴露提示无关偏差；随后通过两条策略主导的混合路径，从同一提示和初始噪声生成对比反事实样本：一条探查冻结基础方向的被抑制替代方案，另一条揭示后训练的无条件倾向；利用自适应翻转保护机制进行奖励排序，分配拉力与推力角色，并通过联合成对的NFT更新完成修复。

Result: 在PickScore和GenEval基准上，ReNFT在保持98.9%和99.0%原始奖励性能的同时，分别将DreamSim-Div提升58.8%和55.0%，显著改善了生成多样性，且无需外部干预，为现有方法提供互补方案。

Conclusion: ReNFT证明模式坍缩本质上是概率质量的抑制而非删除，可通过生成器内部机制实现可逆修复。该方法不依赖外部信号，能有效恢复多样性并维持高奖励性能，为扩散模型后训练提供了新的内生修复范式。

Abstract: Reward post-training of diffusion generators inevitably concentrates probability mass on a few reward-favored modes, a mode collapse that erases within-prompt diversity. Existing methods for mitigating collapse rely on external signals or interfaces, augmenting the reward with perceptual objectives, adjusting reference regularization, or modifying the text encoder, but none repairs an adapter that has already collapsed while preserving the acquired reward. We observe that online post-training primarily reallocates probability mass over capabilities inherited from pretraining rather than learning new visual content. Collapse is therefore suppression, not deletion, and can be reversed from within the generator. We propose ReNFT, which repairs a high-reward, low-diversity adapter through internal probability-mass recalibration. Unconditional probes first prioritize "anti-hub" prompts where the prompt-independent bias is easiest to expose. Two policy-dominated mixed routes then generate matched counterfactual proposals from the same prompt and initial noise, one probing the frozen base direction for suppressed alternatives and the other exposing the post-trained unconditional tendency. Reward ranking with an adaptive flipping guard assigns pull and push roles, and a joint-and-paired NFT update realizes the repair. On PickScore and GenEval, ReNFT retains 98.9% and 99.0% of NFT's reward while improving DreamSim-Div by 58.8% and 55.0%, respectively, offering a complementary alternative to external interventions.

</details>


### [198] [Attention Sensitivity Is Not Enough: Dissociating Attention-Level and Behavioural In-Context Learning under Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00064)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在上下文学习（ICL）中，注意力机制作为模型上下文敏感性代理的可靠性。通过引入ICS（In-Context Sensitivity）和ICL-GAP指标，在Llama-2-7B上进行四臂消融实验，发现尽管注意力指标（ICS）被最大化至接近理论上限，但行为表现（如MMLU准确率）反而下降，揭示了注意力代理与实际行为之间的Goodhart效应。进一步分析表明，注意力变得尖锐且分离，但信息流向格式化和示范内容而非标签。随机标签验证显示行为探测器仍具动态范围，而基于预训练计算的目标能保持高准确率与适度敏感性。结论强调：注意力层面的ICL诊断指标必须经过行为差距的验证才能作为训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验注意力机制作为上下文学习敏感性代理的可靠性，尤其是在其被优化后是否仍能真实反映模型的行为变化。

Method: 提出ICS（平均行距离）和ICL-GAP（行为准确率差距）作为评估指标；在Llama-2-7B上实施四臂消融实验，使用$\armKL$正则化最大化ICS；分析注意力分布、行为表现及终点统计；通过随机标签协议验证行为探测器有效性；测试行为门控和预训练锚定目标的效果。

Result: ICS被成功提升至1.413（接近理论上限），但ICL-GAP几乎为零，MMLU准确率从0.371降至0.279，显示注意力代理与行为表现严重脱节；注意力变得尖锐且分离，但信息不指向标签；随机标签实验确认行为探测器有效；行为门控部分缓解问题，而预训练锚定目标维持高准确率与适度敏感性。

Conclusion: 注意力层面的ICL诊断指标只有在经过行为差距验证后，才能作为可靠的训练目标，否则可能因Goodhart效应导致误导性优化。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets large language models adapt to new tasks from demonstrations, and fine-tuning can erode this behaviour. Many preservation diagnostics inspect attention: if attention changes when demonstrations change, the model is treated as context-sensitive. This paper asks how far that proxy can be trusted once it is optimised. We formalise \emph{In-Context Sensitivity} (ICS), the average row distance between last-token attention on matched and mismatched demonstration prefixes, and pair it with \emph{ICL-GAP}, the behavioural accuracy gap between the same prefixes. In a controlled four-arm ablation on Llama-2-7B, an ICS-maximising regulariser ($\armKL$) drives ICS to $1.413$, within $0.5\%$ of its geometric ceiling. The behavioural readout tells a different story: ICL-GAP stays near zero and MMLU accuracy moves from $0.371$ to $0.279$, a Goodhart dissociation of the bounded attention proxy. Endpoint statistics locate the mechanism: attention grows sharp and near-disjoint across prefixes yet routes to formatting and demonstration-body tokens rather than labels. A random-label protocol confirms that the behavioural probe family retains dynamic range at the same checkpoints. In a constructive sweep, behaviour gating partially mitigates the effect, while objectives anchored to pretrained computation hold the high-MMLU, moderate-ICS region that divergence maximisers leave. The main lesson is diagnostic: attention-level ICL proxies earn their place as training targets only after validation against behavioural gaps.

</details>


### [199] [RW-LoRA: Communication-Efficient Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00078)
*Xingran Chen,Rohit Bhagat,Ghadir Ayache,Rawad Bitar,Yanmin Gong,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于随机游走的LoRA微调方案，通过单个模型令牌在网络中遍历并顺序更新，避免了全局同步和多模型副本的维护，显著降低通信与计算开销，并提供非凸目标下的收敛性保证。实验表明该方法在多个NLP任务上性能优于或相当，且通信与计算成本大幅减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式LoRA方法依赖集中式聚合或需频繁同步的去中心化通信，导致高通信开销和聚合误差。为解决这些问题，本文提出一种无需多副本、无全局同步的新型微调机制。

Method: 设计一种基于随机游走的单令牌传播机制：一个模型令牌在节点间随机移动，每个节点在其本地数据上进行微调更新，随后将令牌传递至下一个节点，实现顺序更新。该过程避免了多模型副本间的同步与聚合操作。

Result: 在多种NLP任务和图拓扑结构下，所提方法在保持竞争力性能的同时，显著降低了通信与计算成本；相比基于gossip的LoRA，通信量减少超过50%，训练效率更高。

Conclusion: 提出的随机游走式LoRA微调方案有效解决了分布式环境下通信开销大、同步复杂的问题，具备理论保障与实际优势，适用于资源受限的分布式场景。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have become a standard approach for adapting large foundation models. Adopting fine-tuning to distributed settings faces several challenges. Most existing distributed LoRA methods rely on centralized aggregation, and gossip-based decentralized LoRA requires repeated synchronization among multiple model copies. Both methods incur significant communication overhead and introduce errors due to simultaneous aggregation of multiple model updates. In this paper, we take a different perspective and propose a random-walk-based LoRA fine-tuning scheme. Instead of maintaining multiple model replicas, a single model token traverses the network and is updated sequentially using local fine-tuning objectives. This design eliminates the need for global synchronization, substantially reduces communication and computation costs, and avoids aggregation errors. We provide rigorous convergence guarantees for non-convex objectives under standard assumptions. Through empirical results on multiple NLP tasks and graph topologies, we show that the proposed method achieves competitive task performance with substantially less communication and computation than gossip-based LoRA.

</details>


### [200] [Stochastic complexity of vectors containing cluster structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00084)
*Daniel Nicorici,Olli Yli-Harja,Jaakko Astola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于归一化最大似然（NML）模型计算包含聚类结构的编码向量的随机概率（最短码长）的问题，该问题在基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则的数据聚类中具有重要的理论与实际意义。传统方法计算最短码长的时间复杂度为多项式级别，而本文通过引入递推公式高效计算NML模型的归一化常数，将时间复杂度降低至线性级别，证明了该问题是可解的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则的数据聚类中，准确估计最佳聚类数量和聚类结构至关重要。然而，传统的NML模型计算最短码长的方法存在计算复杂度过高的问题，限制了其在大规模数据中的应用。因此，亟需一种更高效的计算方法。

Method: 提出一种新的递推公式，用于高效计算NML模型中的归一化常数，从而实现对包含聚类结构的编码向量的最短码长的快速计算。

Result: 新方法将计算最短码长的时间复杂度从多项式级别降低到线性级别，显著提升了计算效率，使大规模数据聚类中的应用成为可能。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入递推公式，成功将NML模型下聚类结构编码的最短码长计算问题转化为一个线性时间可解问题，为基于MDL的数据聚类提供了高效的理论支持。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of computing the stochastic probability (shortest code length) of the encoded vectors containing cluster structure using Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) model. This is of great theoretical and practical importance in data clustering based on Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle, such as for estimating the best number of clusters and best cluster structure for the data. Straightforward computation of the shortest code length of the vector containing cluster structure based on the NML model requires polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters. We show that this is a tractable problem by introducing a recursion formula for the efficient computation of normalizing constant from the NML model. The time complexity of the new formula is linear opposed to previous polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters.

</details>


### [201] [Foundation models for electricity price forecasting and battery arbitrage: Can they replace market-specific forecasting models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00089)
*Arkadiusz Lipiecki,Rafał Weron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了九种来自五个基础模型家族的变体在零样本模式下对德国、波兰和西班牙2021-2025年电力价格预测的表现，评估指标包括点预测与概率预测精度以及电池储能套利的经济价值。结果表明，只有TabPFN模型在所有市场和所有统计指标上均显著优于基准模型；然而，其统计优势并未直接转化为经济优势：在无限制出价和风险较高的分位数策略下表现最佳，而在风险容忍度较低时，分布深度神经网络基准模型更具盈利能力。因此，基础模型无法普遍替代市场特定模型，其价值取决于模型架构和决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨基础模型是否能在无需任务特定训练的情况下，取代专门设计用于电力价格预测的模型，并评估其在不同决策场景下的实际经济价值。

Method: 对比九种来自五个基础模型家族的变体，在零样本模式下进行评估，使用德国、波兰和西班牙2021-2025年的数据，采用点预测、概率预测准确性和电池储能套利的经济价值作为评估指标。

Result: TabPFN模型在所有市场和所有统计指标上均显著优于基准模型；但在经济价值方面，其表现依赖于策略风险水平——在高风险策略下表现最优，而低风险策略下不如分布深度神经网络基准模型。

Conclusion: 基础模型无法普遍替代市场特定模型，其有效性取决于模型架构与具体决策问题的风险特征。

Abstract: Foundation models promise accurate forecasts with little or no task-specific training, but whether they can replace models designed specifically for electricity price forecasting remains unclear. We compare nine variants from five foundation model families, evaluated in zero-shot mode, with two state-of-the-art electricity price forecasting benchmarks in Germany, Poland, and Spain over 2021-2025. Their performance is assessed in terms of point and probabilistic forecasting accuracy, as well as economic value in battery energy storage arbitrage. Only the TabPFN models consistently and significantly outperform the benchmarks across all three markets and all statistical measures. However, this statistical dominance does not translate directly into economic dominance: TabPFN performs best under unlimited bids and riskier quantile-based strategies, whereas the Distributional Deep Neural Network benchmark is more profitable when risk tolerance is lower. Thus, foundation models cannot universally replace market-specific models, and their value depends on both model architecture and the decision problem.

</details>


### [202] [Assessing Alignment and Stability of Feature Importance Explanations via Weight of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00090)
*Eddie Conti,Claudio Daka,Álvaro Parafita,Antonio L. Alfeo,Axel Brando,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于权重证据（WoE）的假设检验框架，将特征重要性方法（FIMs）嵌入其中，通过量化观测证据对特征重要性假设的支持程度，实现对FIMs的系统性评估。该框架可结合领域知识、真实标签或FIM自身作为参考假设，支持对比性和证据驱动的分析，理论和实证结果验证了其有效性与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征重要性方法（FIMs）仅提供归因分数，难以揭示模型决策背后的深层逻辑，缺乏对解释一致性和可信度的评估手段。因此亟需一种更严谨、可解释的评估框架来衡量FIMs与先验知识的一致性及稳定性。

Method: 提出基于权重证据（WoE）的假设检验框架，将特征重要性视为待验证的假设，通过计算证据对假设的支持强度，实现对FIMs的量化评估；支持多种参考假设来源（如领域知识、真实值、FIM自身），并建立WoE与归因方差之间的理论联系。

Result: 在LIME和SHAP的实验中，该框架能有效评估不同设定下的解释质量，揭示FIMs与先验假设的一致性及变异程度，证明其具备良好的适用性与灵活性。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的基于WoE的假设检验框架为特征重要性方法提供了新的评估视角，强调对比性与证据支持，是一种互补性强、理论坚实且实践可行的解释评估工具。

Abstract: Feature importance Methods (FIMs) are widely used in Explainable AI to interpret model predictions, yet attribution scores alone often provide limited insight into the underlying reasoning process. In this work, we introduce a novel perspective by embedding FIMs within a hypothesis-testing framework based on Weight of Evidence (WoE). We quantify how strongly the observed evidence supports any given hypothesis on feature importance. The reference hypothesis can stem from domain knowledge, ground truth, or be derived from the FIM itself. This formulation enables a principled evaluation of FIMs, capturing both their alignment with prior knowledge and their variability. We further provide theoretical results linking WoE to attribution variance. Empirical results shows the applicability and flexibility of our strategy analyzing LIME and SHAP explanations in settings with different reference hypotheses. Overall, our framework offers a complementary tool for assessing FIMs through a contrastive, evidence-based lens.

</details>


### [203] [Safin-1: Safety from Within through Memory-Native State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00092)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Zhekai Chen,Cheng Jin,Jingnan Zheng,Yi Zhang,Zhongtian Ma,Jiawei Zhou,Sirui Chen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Xiang Wang,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Youbang Sun,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Safin-1 是一种基于记忆路由与状态演化的基础模型家族，通过 MARCH 架构实现结构化记忆状态和内容条件路由，支持在测试时无需重复修改主干网络即可适应持久能力状态，从而实现安全能力的内在化。该方法将记忆从被动记录转变为行为演化的主动基质，在安全任务中表现出显著改进，并在长上下文理解、检索和效率方面验证了其通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期复杂任务需要模型积累信息、维持内部状态并持续适应；安全应是模型本身的内在属性，而非依赖外部约束或事后对齐。

Method: 采用 Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH) 架构，实现结构化记忆状态与内容条件路由，支持测试时的持续能力状态适应，构建安全状态接口以实现安全能力的原生计算。

Result: 在下游安全任务中，安全状态接口实现了有效的状态适应，显著提升安全性；同时在通用能力、长上下文理解、检索和效率方面表现优异，验证了架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 本工作为安全作为原生可维护能力提供了初步架构探索，未来仍需大量研究以实现更广泛的安全内生愿景。

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require foundation models to accumulate information, maintain internal states, and adapt over extended interactions. Safety should be an intrinsic property of the model itself, rather than a behavioral constraint relying solely on external safeguards or post-hoc alignment such as supervised fine-tuning. This motivates Safety from Within, where safety-relevant capabilities are represented and invoked through the model's native computation. We present Safin-1, a family of foundation models realizing this principle through memory routing and state evolution. Safin-1 is built on Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that maintains structured memory states and selectively retrieves relevant historical information through content-conditioned routing. It supports test-time adaptation of persistent capability states without repeatedly modifying the backbone, enabling controlled specialization over a shared foundation. We investigate this interface on downstream safety tasks through a Safety State, demonstrating effective state-based adaptation with substantial safety improvements. More broadly, the routed-state interface unifies contextual memory and persistent capability adaptation within the model's native computation, reframing memory from a passive record of prior context into an active substrate for maintaining and evolving model behavior. Evaluations across general capabilities, long-context understanding, retrieval, and efficiency further validate Safin-1. These findings provide a path toward safety as a state-native and adaptively maintainable capability. This work is only an initial architectural exploration of Safety from Within, and substantial further work is needed to realize this broader vision.

</details>


### [204] [Local Reference Geometry Residual Augmentation for Imbalanced Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00093)
*Chuanhang Qiu,Yanran Xu,Yue Wang,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了不平衡时间序列分类中的表示层问题，指出在少数类样本支持减少后，特征空间在少数类区域的局部可靠性可能下降。为此提出轻量级后处理模块Local Reference Geometry (LRG)，通过分析训练特征的局部暴露和类别混合风险，对固定特征进行标准化位移和LDA投影残差增强。在UCR/Bake Off Redux基准测试中，LRG显著提升各类表示（学习、预训练、固定）性能，尤其在高风险区域表现突出。消融实验表明增益主要来自附加的有符号局部残差，而非其他常见方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不平衡时间序列分类中，尽管已有多种方法调整训练分布、目标函数或阈值，但缺乏对特征空间局部可靠性的评估。少数类样本在高不平衡情况下可能位于稀疏、以多数类为主或混合的特征空间邻域，影响模型性能。因此需要一种机制来诊断并修复这种局部几何失效问题。

Method: 提出Local Reference Geometry (LRG) 模块，作为固定特征提取器与分类头之间的后处理组件。利用训练特征计算局部暴露度与类别混合风险，并基于邻近训练几何结构生成标准化有符号位移，结合LDA投影残差对原始特征进行增强。

Result: 在多个不平衡时间序列数据集上，使用LRG后的模型性能显著优于原始版本，涵盖学习型、预训练型和固定特征表示；即使与其他训练阶段干预或分类器修正结合，仍保持增益。消融实验确认增益主要来源于有符号局部残差的添加，而非原型距离、亲和特征等其他形式。进一步分析验证了局部几何失效假设：少数类区域随不平衡加剧而更易被多数类主导，且LRG增益集中在高风险区域。

Conclusion: 局部特征空间的几何失效是不平衡分类中的关键问题。LRG作为一种轻量级后处理方法，能有效诊断并修复该问题，显著提升模型在少数类上的表现，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Imbalanced time series classification is often addressed by changing the training distribution, objective, logits, or final threshold. These interventions address important biases, yet leave a representation-level question unmeasured: after minority support is reduced, does a learned feature space remain locally reliable around minority regions? We identify a training-local geometry failure: under imbalance, minority cases can lie in sparse, rest-dominated, or mixed feature-space neighborhoods, even when the representation retains useful global class structure. To diagnose and repair this failure, we propose Local Reference Geometry (LRG), a lightweight post-hoc feature augmentation module applied between a fixed feature extractor and the classifier head. Using training features only, LRG measures local exposure and class-mixture risk, then augments each fixed feature with a standardized signed displacement from nearby training geometry and an LDA-projected residual summary. On controlled UCR/Bake Off Redux imbalance benchmarks, paired raw-versus-LRG comparisons show gains for learned, pretrained, and fixed representations, including when LRG is combined with training-level interventions and post-encoder classifier corrections. Ablations show that the gain comes from the signed local residual appended to the original feature, rather than from generic prototype distances, affinity features, scalar statistics, or VLAD-style codes. Further analyses support the proposed local-geometry failure hypothesis: minority neighborhoods become increasingly rest-exposed under imbalance, training-local risk identifies error-prone regions, and LRG gains concentrate in those high-risk regions.

</details>


### [205] [Faster Than Flash: Exploiting Attention Sparsity for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00097)
*Zhigeng Liu,Zhiyuan Ning,Ruixiao Li,Xiaoran Liu,Yuerong Song,Min Zhang,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Faster Flash Decoding (FFD) 是一种软硬件协同设计框架，旨在解决长上下文大语言模型在解码时受内存带宽瓶颈和注意力机制二次复杂度限制的问题。通过将选择器与计算单元融合为全融合内核，并用低比特量化的内容感知扫描替代外部元数据索引，同时引入动态自适应稀疏的 top-delta 策略，实现无需训练、即插即用的高效解码。该方法可复用扫描结果进行计算，达到最高 11.6 倍内核级加速，支持 256K 上下文长度，端到端吞吐提升 2.37 倍。在 RULER 和 LongBench 上的实证表明，其在保持模型精度的同时实现高稀疏比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文 LLM 解码受限于内存带宽瓶颈和注意力机制的二次复杂度，而传统基于元数据的度量方式存在内存开销大，自适应选择策略则计算效率低，二者难以兼顾。因此亟需一种新型架构来突破内存墙并提升解码效率。

Method: 提出 FFD 框架，将选择器与计算模块融合为全融合内核；采用低比特量化实现内容感知扫描，替代外部元数据索引；引入 top-delta 策略实现分布自适应稀疏，无需全局同步；支持扫描结果复用于计算，提升整体效率。

Result: 在硬件层面实现最高 11.6 倍的内核级速度提升，支持长达 256K 的上下文长度，端到端吞吐提升 2.37 倍；在 RULER 与 LongBench 上验证了模型精度无损失，同时实现高稀疏比。

Conclusion: FFD 通过软硬件协同设计，有效突破长上下文解码中的内存墙限制，提供了一种无需训练、可直接部署的高效解决方案，显著提升了长序列处理能力。

Abstract: The development of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by the memory bandwidth bottleneck and quadratic complexity of the attention mechanism during decoding. To overcome the inherent trade-offs between the memory overhead of metadata-based metrics and the computational inefficiency of adaptive selection strategies, we present Faster Flash Decoding (FFD), a novel hardware-algorithm co-design framework designed to break the memory wall in long-context decoding. FFD integrates the selector and computer into a fully fused kernel, replacing external metadata indices with content-aware scanning via low-bit quantization. Furthermore, we introduce the top-delta strategy, which dynamically filters blocks to achieve distribution-adaptive sparsity without global synchronization. Offering a training-free and plug-and-play solution, FFD also enables the reuse of scanning results for computation, achieving up to 11.6x kernel-level speedup and scaling to 256K context length, with 2.37x end-to-end throughput improvement. Empirical validation on RULER and LongBench confirms that FFD maintains model accuracy while delivering high-ratio sparsity, with code available at https://github.com/qluoluo/faster-flash-decoding

</details>


### [206] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种领域自适应的检索增强生成框架，用于镁合金腐蚀知识的合成，通过微调三个开源语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B、Mistral-7B）并结合混合密集-词汇检索管道，在3,309个专家验证的问题-答案对上训练，实现高达143-194%的Token F1提升，系统忠实度达0.964，上下文召回率达0.988。引入的Reason Map框架可构建定向证据图，识别因果方向颠倒和无支持的推理跳跃，突破传统事实性指标局限。该方法在新发表文献和内部电化学数据上验证了趋势级泛化能力，具备跨领域通用性，为可信AI辅助知识合成提供可复用范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法虽能准确预测腐蚀速率，但缺乏对机制的可解释性；在安全关键材料工程中，不仅需要高精度预测，还需具备机制可辩护的推理能力，而当前的事实性评估指标无法有效衡量此能力，因此亟需一种兼具准确性与可解释性的知识合成方法。

Method: 采用领域自适应的检索增强生成框架，基于3,309个专家验证的QA对微调三个开源语言模型，并集成混合密集-词汇检索管道；引入Reason Map框架，通过构建命题图来系统检测因果关系错误和推理漏洞，实现对生成内容的深层可解释性评估。

Result: 在镁合金腐蚀任务中，检索增强生成系统取得143-194%的Token F1提升，系统忠实度达0.964，上下文召回率0.988；盲测验证显示其在新文献和实验数据上具有趋势级泛化能力；Reason Map成功识别出传统指标无法发现的因果颠倒和推理缺陷。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架不仅提升了腐蚀知识合成的准确性与可信度，还提供了可解释的机制推理能力，其模块化设计可推广至其他工程领域，为实现安全、可信的AI辅助知识系统提供通用解决方案。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess.
