This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>


### [207] [Good Memory Has ECC: Evaluating the Memory of Vision-Language Models Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00103)
*Shmuel Berman,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ECCBench基准，从效率、压缩性和校准性三个维度评估大模型在超出自身容量时的记忆能力，发现预训练视觉语言模型对文本有压缩记忆但对视频没有，且校准性差；非Transformer架构在压缩与校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅关注长文本或视频中的准确率，忽略了真实长时任务中关键的记忆特性，需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 设计ECCBench基准，通过效率（FLOPs）、压缩性（输入可压缩性对记忆影响）和校准性（系统在不确定时是否拒绝回答）三方面评估模型记忆性能。

Result: 预训练VLMs对文本具有压缩记忆能力但对视频无此能力，且在两类数据上均表现不佳的校准性；部分非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer。

Conclusion: ECCBench能有效揭示模型在超容量记忆下的真实能力，非Transformer架构可能更适合用于长时序智能体任务。

Abstract: Memory is widely viewed as an important unsolved problem for LLMs and VLMs, and current benchmarks typically evaluate it by testing accuracy over long text or video. However, accuracy alone misses properties that matter for real long-horizon tasks. We introduce ECCBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that measures memory beyond a system's capacity--its raw accuracy at a specific budget--via three axes we call ECC: efficiency--the computation, in FLOPs, needed to answer from memory; compression--whether compressible inputs are remembered more accurately or efficiently; and calibration--whether the system abstains in response to its own uncertainty and the cost of an error. We find that pretrained VLMs compress their memory over text but not video and are poorly calibrated on both. Among a broader set of memory backbones, several non-Transformer architectures achieve better compression-calibration tradeoffs than RoPE Transformers, suggesting they may be useful components for agents operating over long horizons.

</details>


### [208] [Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00129)
*J. M. Diederik Kruijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受生物进化启发的AI/ML模型集群机制——'天生有瑕，进化完美'(Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution)，通过主动对模型参数施加偏离最优的突变，使群体在非平稳环境中持续提升性能。该机制利用模型多样性作为统计对冲，证明在一般条件下可保证后悔值减少，并在合成线性回归任务中验证其有效性：在约80%的环境变化中选出最优模型，且通过推理融合实现集体优势。最佳效果出现在突变率与环境漂移率匹配时，研究还设计了自适应控制器以实现实际应用。该机制为构建更接近生物智能的AI系统提供了通用设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的问题常发生不可预测的动态变化（非平稳性），导致现有AI/ML模型性能下降。尽管自然选择和遗传变异在生物进化中成功应对了此类挑战，但传统AI/ML方法难以维持模型多样性，因优化过程趋向收敛。因此，亟需一种能持续适应环境变化的新型学习机制。

Method: 提出一个由多个AI/ML模型组成的群体（群集），对各模型的系数进行有意的、偏离最优的突变；通过群体内多样性维持对抗环境漂移的能力；基于四条定理证明该机制在一般条件下可保证后悔值减少；使用合成线性回归任务进行实验验证，并引入自适应控制器动态调节突变率以匹配未知的环境漂移率。

Result: 在80%以上的环境变化中，突变群集能够选出表现最优的模型；通过推理融合策略，个体优势可转化为集体优势；当突变率与环境漂移率相匹配时，性能提升最为显著；自适应控制器有效提升了机制的实际可用性。

Conclusion: ‘天生有瑕，进化完美’机制为解决非平稳环境下的模型退化问题提供了一种可推广的解决方案，其原理与生物进化高度相似，暗示它可能是实现类生物智能的关键缺失要素之一，具有广泛应用于鲁棒自适应AI系统的设计潜力。

Abstract: The performance of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) models degrades when the problem they were trained on drifts. This is a near-universal feature of real-world problems, which often change unpredictably. Biological evolution has achieved intelligence by overcoming this obstacle through natural selection acting on heritable variation. AI/ML techniques have long incorporated forms of natural selection, but it has been challenging to maintain model diversity as optimization naturally drives convergence. Here we show that a swarm of AI/ML models subjected to deliberate mutations of their model coefficients away from optimality can reliably and sustainably improve performance in changing environments by acting as a statistical hedge against non-stationarity. We call this mechanism 'Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution', reflecting that the collective performance gain goes at the expense of individual performance. We prove via four theorems that the resulting regret reduction is guaranteed under general conditions, establishing the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism as a generalizable design principle for AI/ML systems. We validate these results on synthetic linear regression tasks, demonstrating that the mutated swarm delivers the best model in $\sim80\%$ of environment changes and that inference synthesis successfully translates this individual advantage into a collective one. The mechanism proves to be most effective when the mutation drift rate matches the drift rate of the environment. We outline a simple, adaptive controller that enables practical applications by tuning the mutation drift rate to match the unknown drift rate of the environment. The close analogy of the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism to biological evolution suggests it may have been a critical missing ingredient for the organic discovery of AI forms that more closely mimic biological intelligence.

</details>


### [209] [Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning for Goal-Directed Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00189)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Anna G. Green,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为精英加权监督微调（EW-SFT）的新方法，通过奖励引导选择高分分子进行模型更新，无需依赖生成轨迹的对数概率，适用于多种生成模型和分子设计任务。在多个基准测试中表现优于传统优化器，具有统一性和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于策略梯度的强化学习优化方法依赖于特定模型架构的生成轨迹对数概率，导致难以跨架构复用；而传统监督微调缺乏奖励信息的动态反馈。因此需要一种不依赖复杂机制、能统一适用于不同生成模型与设计任务的优化方法。

Method: EW-SFT通过奖励筛选高得分分子（精英集），并使用模型自身的预训练损失对这些分子进行监督微调，更新过程不涉及轨迹级奖励权重，仅依赖已评分样本和原生损失函数。

Result: 在两个激酶参考化合物上，基于3D形状对齐的优化中，EW-SFT consistently 超过原始优化器；在四个外部参考化合物上的2D相似性优化中也表现更优；在样本效率基准测试中达到与无轨迹强化学习相当的性能。

Conclusion: EW-SFT是一种统一且高效的分子生成优化方法，适用于多种生成模型、设计任务和评估方式，无需复杂奖励建模，可广泛部署。

Abstract: Goal-directed optimization is essential for steering molecular generators to propose candidates with desired properties. However, it is often implemented with policy-gradient reinforcement learning, which requires a generation-trajectory log-probability whose form depends on the model architecture and generation procedure. This makes an optimizer difficult to reuse across architectures and conditional generative designs. Supervised fine-tuning needs none of that machinery, but its update is driven by a fixed dataset, so the reward never enters the update. We introduce Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT), which uses reward to guide elite selection of high-scoring molecules, and updates the model by its own pretraining loss on that set. Ablations show that reward information is passed primarily through elite selection, rather than through continuous weighting within the selected set. Because the update consumes only scored molecules and the model's native loss, the same rule applies across autoregressive, masked-diffusion, and discrete-flow generators, and across de novo, motif-extension, and linker-design tasks. Under a fixed budget of 3D shape alignment oracle calls on two kinase reference compounds, EW-SFT consistently outperforms the corresponding native optimizers. It further improves goal-directed optimization under a 2D similarity oracle on four held-out references and achieves comparable performance on a sample-efficiency benchmark without a trajectory-level RL formulation. These results demonstrate that EW-SFT is a unified and effective optimizer across molecular generators, design constraints, references, and oracles.

</details>


### [210] [WHALE: A Simple Recipe for Joint Harness-Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00196)
*Haechan Kim,Yoonho Lee,Gisang Lee,Chelsea Finn,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WHALE 是一种交替优化模型权重和控制流程代码（harness）的框架，通过在线拒绝采样微调和元 harness 搜索实现。它在三个领域（搜索问答、数学推理、国际象棋谜题）中显著优于仅优化权重或 harness 的方法，提升 4.15-24.38 个百分点的准确率，并减少资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型性能受权重和 harness 共同影响，单独优化任一组件会因另一组件固定而受限。现有方法仅优化权重和文本提示，未充分探索更广泛的 harness 优化。

Method: 提出 WHALE 框架，包含两个阶段：在当前 harness 下更新模型权重，随后在新模型下搜索更优 harness；使用在线拒绝采样微调与元 harness 搜索实现两阶段；采用固定时长或自适应耐心机制控制切换时机。

Result: 在 SearchQA、数学推理和国际象棋任务中，WHALE 在 best mean@8 准确率上优于 weight-only、harness-only 和 Fast-Slow Training 方法，提升 4.15-24.38 个百分点；小步交错更新优于分阶段优化，在准确率与推演成本之间取得更好平衡。

Conclusion: WHALE 证明了权重与 harness 联合优化的有效性，强调二者动态协同的重要性，且其高效性与可扩展性使其适用于多种智能体任务。

Abstract: Agent performance depends jointly on the model parameters and the executable harness code that manages context and control flow. Optimizing either component in isolation can leave the system bottlenecked by its frozen counterpart: weight updates can change which harness is effective, while harness updates can change which model capabilities are exposed. Existing joint-adaptation methods optimize weights and textual prompts but leave the broader harness fixed. We propose Weight-Harness Alternating LEarning (WHALE), a simple recipe that alternates two phases: updating the model under the current harness, then searching for a better harness under the updated model. We instantiate these two phases with online rejection-sampling fine-tuning and Meta-Harness, respectively. When to switch is a key design choice: to separate real improvements from noise without over-optimizing against a changing counterpart, WHALE uses either fixed phase durations or an adaptive patience rule over training signals. Using Qwen3.5-2B/4B agents across three domains (search question answering, mathematical reasoning, and chess puzzles), WHALE outperforms weight-only, harness-only, and Fast-Slow Training by 4.15-24.38 percentage points in best mean@8 accuracy. Either component can be the bottleneck: harness search matches peak weight-only accuracy with far fewer rollouts in SearchQA, but improves math accuracy only after a weight update. Small interleaved updates also outperform stagewise weight-then-harness optimization in accuracy and rollout cost. The code is available at https://github.com/krafton-ai/WHALE.

</details>


### [211] [QTEA: Ternary LLMs with Sparse Residual Salient Weight and By-Column Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00224)
*Yipin Guo,Arun M George,Jie Fu,Tareq Mahmoud,Sixue Xing,Siddharth Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QTEA is a sub-2-bit post-training quantization (PTQ) framework for large language models (LLMs) that quantizes weights to ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. It employs semi-structured 1:4 sparsity within selected columns to maintain hardware efficiency, introduces column-wise rescale refinement to reduce reconstruction error, and mitigates order-dependent error propagation via error decay. QTEA achieves significant accuracy gains and lower perplexity on Qwen3-14B and Llama3-8B, while enabling up to 7.2× faster per-token generation through a lookup-table-based kernel.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing weight-only PTQ methods struggle with generalization across models and suffer severe accuracy loss below 2 bits, especially when using unstructured sparsity which harms hardware efficiency. There is a need for a high-performance, hardware-friendly sub-2-bit quantization method that preserves model accuracy.

Method: QTEA quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. It applies semi-structured 1:4 sparsity within salient columns to maintain GPU-friendly execution. Column-wise rescale refinement alternately updates per-column scales and ternary assignments. Error decay is introduced to counteract order-dependent error accumulation in GPTQ-style quantization.

Result: On Qwen3-14B, QTEA achieves an effective 1.7 bits per weight, improves average accuracy by 16.7% over the strongest ternary PTQ baseline, and reduces perplexity by 1.40× (WikiText) and 2.61× (C4). On Llama3-8B, it gains 6.6% in accuracy and reduces perplexity by 1.34× and 1.95× respectively. A lookup-table-based kernel enables 7.2× faster per-token generation than FP16.

Conclusion: QTEA provides a highly effective, hardware-efficient sub-2-bit PTQ solution for LLMs that significantly improves accuracy and inference speed without sacrificing model generality, demonstrating strong performance across multiple models and benchmarks.

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) can alleviate the computational burden of serving large language models (LLMs) at scale. However, existing PTQ methods often fail to generalize across models and suffer severe accuracy loss below 2 bits. Many leverage unstructured sparsity to mitigate this loss, but at the cost of regularity and GPU-friendly execution. We present QTEA, a sub-2-bit PTQ framework that quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. To maintain hardware efficiency, residuals are assigned to selected columns with semi-structured \(1{:}4\) sparsity within the salient columns. We further add column-wise rescale refinement to GPTQ-style column-by-column quantization, alternately updating per-column scales and ternary assignments to reduce reconstruction error. We also identify order-dependent error propagation in GPTQ and introduce error decay to attenuate late-stage error accumulation. On Qwen3-14B, QTEA compresses all weights to an effective 1.7 bits per weight while improving average accuracy over the strongest ternary PTQ baseline by 16.7\%. It also achieves 1.40\(\times\) and 2.61\(\times\) lower perplexity on WikiText and C4 respectively. This trend holds on Llama3-8B, where QTEA obtains a 6.6\% accuracy gain and 1.34\(\times\)/1.95\(\times\) lower perplexity on the same datasets. Finally, we develop a lookup-table based kernel that achieves 7.2\(\times\) faster per-token generation over an FP16 baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Intelligent-Microsystems-Lab/QTEA.

</details>


### [212] [Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive Generative Model for PDEs in Complex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00297)
*Zi Wang,Minghui Xu,Tapan Mukerji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoLAMP 是一种用于求解复杂微米级多物理场偏微分方程（PDE）的几何感知潜在自回归生成模型。它采用图结构上的双编码器架构，联合捕捉全局拓扑与细粒度几何特征，实现从真实空间场到紧凑潜在表示的有效转换；在潜在空间中，利用带有流匹配的因果自注意力变换器建模时间动态，支持稳定且可扩展的分块自回归预测；灵活的解码器可在任意点重建高分辨率物理场。该方法在三个复杂几何下的多物理场基准数据集（反应流、热对流、弹性）上表现稳定，全程滚动预测误差低，为微米尺度复杂几何中的几何感知学习提供了系统性研究，并揭示了基于流匹配的潜在自回归 PDE 建模中分块时间推进的新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解具有高度复杂 $\mu$m 尺度曲折几何结构的多物理场偏微分方程仍是科学计算中的重大挑战，传统方法在复杂几何下难以高效、准确地建模物理场演化。需要一种能够有效捕捉几何细节并实现稳定长时间预测的新型生成模型。

Method: 提出 GeoLAMP 模型，采用图表示的双编码器架构以联合捕获全局拓扑与局部几何特征；在潜在空间中引入因果自注意力变换器结合流匹配机制以建模时间动态；设计灵活解码器实现任意点的高分辨率物理场重建；通过分块自回归方式实现高效时间推进。

Result: GeoLAMP 在三个复杂几何多物理场基准数据集上均表现出最稳定的自回归性能，全程预测误差低且收敛良好，验证了其在复杂几何下建模物理场演化的有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: GeoLAMP 为微米尺度复杂几何中的多物理场 PDE 求解提供了一种高效的几何感知学习框架，展示了基于流匹配的潜在自回归建模在分块时间推进中的潜力，推动了复杂结构中物理场模拟的智能化发展。

Abstract: Solving multiphysics partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a major challenge in scientific computing, especially for highly complex $μ$m-scale tortuous geometries critical to energy and chemical engineering. We address this challenge by proposing a Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive generative Model for PDEs (GeoLAMP) for solving physics within highly irregular and tortuous structures. GeoLAMP introduces a dual-encoder architecture on graph representations to jointly capture global topology and fine-scale geometric features, enabling an effective transition from real-space fields to compact latent representations. In the latent space, we propose a causal self-attention transformer with flow matching to model temporal dynamics, allowing stable and scalable block-wise autoregressive prediction. A flexible decoder reconstructs high-resolution physical fields on arbitrary points. We establish three multiphysics benchmark datasets in complex geometries, covering reactive flow, heat convection, and elasticity. GeoLAMP consistently achieves the most stable autoregression performance on these datasets, maintaining low errors throughout the entire rollout horizon. Our results provide a systematic study of geometry-aware learning for PDEs in $μ$m-scale complex geometries and offer new insights into block-wise time marching of latent autoregressive PDE modeling via a flow matching framework.

</details>


### [213] [Do LLMs Know Your Neighborhood? Auditing LLM Priors for Neighborhood-Level Mobility Prediction and Structural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00345)
*Saad Mohammad Abrar,Eesha Kurella,Arnav Dadarya,Naman Awasthi,Kazi Tasnim Zinat,Vanessa Frias-Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了零样本大语言模型（LLMs）在预测美国四个都会区的普查街区组级出行模式方面的表现，使用匿名的Cuebiq数据构建点级、轨迹级和时间层面的出行结果，并与监督学习基线模型进行比较。结果显示，监督模型平均准确率为0.580，而最佳LLM仅为0.435，空间范围类结果可预测性最强但LLM与基线差距最大。通过方向一致性分析发现，LLMs往往依赖于粗粒度且稳定的先验知识，对受保护群体变量存在不对称处理，表明其预测虽能部分还原宏观出行模式，但缺乏结构基础，需通过实证对齐和偏见审计来验证可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于高精度轨迹数据通常受版权、限制和隐私保护，难以获取，因此需要探索替代方法以支持城市规划、交通管理、公共健康和应急响应等领域的研究。大语言模型（LLMs）具备生成合理出行轨迹和预测个体移动的能力，但其在推断聚合层面（如社区级）出行模式方面的能力尚不明确，亟需系统评估其有效性与潜在偏差。

Method: 本研究基于匿名的Cuebiq数据，构建点级、轨迹级和时间维度的出行结果指标，结合社会人口学和建成环境特征作为预测变量。采用零样本大语言模型（LLMs）进行预测，并与监督学习基线模型对比。引入方向一致性分析，检验LLM所隐含的预测因子效应是否与实际观测数据中的普通最小二乘法（OLS）和Jonckheere-Terpstra趋势一致。

Result: 监督学习模型平均准确率达到0.580，而最佳大语言模型仅达0.435，表明其性能显著低于监督模型。空间范围相关的出行结果具有最强的可预测性，但也是LLM与基线之间差距最大的类别。方向一致性分析揭示，LLMs普遍依赖于粗略、稳定的先验假设，且对受保护群体变量存在不对称处理，反映出潜在偏见。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够在一定程度上从城市语境中恢复聚合出行模式，但其预测结果缺乏坚实的结构基础，不能直接视为真实行为反映。在应用前必须进行实证对齐检验和偏见审计，以确保其可靠性和公平性。

Abstract: Human mobility is central to urban planning, transportation, public health, and emergency response, yet fine-grained trajectory data are often proprietary, restricted, and privacy-sensitive. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by generating plausible mobility traces and predicting individual movement, but their ability to infer aggregate neighborhood-level mobility remains unclear. We evaluate zero-shot LLMs on Census Block Group-level mobility prediction across four U.S. metropolitan areas using anonymized Cuebiq data to construct point-level, trajectory-level, and temporal mobility outcomes, paired with sociodemographic and built-environment predictors. We compare LLM predictions with supervised baselines and introduce a directional alignment analysis to test whether LLM-implied predictor effects agree with empirical OLS and Jonckheere-Terpstra trends. Supervised models achieve 0.580 average accuracy, compared with 0.435 for the best LLM, with spatial extent outcomes showing the strongest predictability but also the largest LLM-baseline gaps. Directional analysis shows that LLMs often rely on coarse, stable predictor-level priors that remain similar across outcomes and cities, including asymmetric treatment of protected-group predictors. Overall, LLMs can partially recover aggregate mobility patterns from urban context, but their predictions should not be treated as structurally grounded without auditing empirical alignment and potential bias.

</details>


### [214] [Deterministic LLM Inference Across GPU Kernels: Power-of-Two INT8 Quantization Scales and the Limits of Tolerance-Based Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00363)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了针对量化GEMM核的合规性测试套件的检测能力，发现基于容差的测试对五种后处理故障（如缩放精度、双舍入、乘法顺序等）完全无感，因为这些故障仅导致输出变化一个bfloat16间距，而容差设置为一个间距时无法识别此类差异。只有在权重缩放为2的幂时，其中一个故障才被检测到。通过将所有权重缩放重量化为最近的2的幂，CUTLASS与Triton在所有线性层上实现位级一致，并生成完全相同的标记序列，显著提升一致性。此前报告的157%困惑度上升是由于未同步重量化权重造成的伪影，实际中99.8%的误差源自权重-缩放不匹配而非2的幂约束本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估量化GEMM核的合规性测试套件在真实模型中的检测能力，揭示其局限性，并提出改进方法以实现更高的一致性与可复现性。

Method: 在Qwen3-1.7B模型上对INT8流水线注入九个故障，覆盖8,232个层-故障-配置组合，系统分析不同故障对输出的影响；通过重量化权重缩放至最近2的幂，验证其对实现一致性的作用，并对比不同配置下的困惑度与生成结果。

Result: 五种后处理故障在容差为一个bfloat16间距时均无法被检测，仅一种在功率为2的幂时被发现；将所有权重缩放重量化为2的幂后，CUTLASS与Triton在所有线性层上达成位级一致，生成相同文本序列，且困惑度变化极小（<1%），先前观测到的大幅上升实为权重-缩放不匹配所致。

Conclusion: 基于容差的合规性测试套件仅能保证预条件成立、操作数共享及差异在单间距内，不足以确保功能等价；通过将权重缩放强制为2的幂，可实现跨框架的位级一致性和可复现性，是一种有效且可部署的实践。

Abstract: Conformance suites for quantized GEMM kernels ask whether two implementations agree within a tolerance. We measure what such a suite can detect. Injecting nine faults into a reference INT8 pipeline over 8,232 layer--fault--regime cells of Qwen3-1.7B, we find that every one of five epilogue faults -- scale precision, double rounding, multiplication order, output truncation, fused ordering -- moves the output by at most a single bfloat16 spacing, and by exactly one whenever it moves it at all, across 5,880 cells. A tolerance of one spacing is therefore blind to the entire class by construction: four of the five faults are detected by no check in the suite, and the fifth only under power-of-two scales. Faults that violate the accumulator's exactness preconditions, or that break operand sharing, are detected without exception, and a null fault never fires. What a tolerance-based suite of this shape establishes is therefore narrower than interchangeability: that the preconditions hold, that operands are shared, and that differences stay within one spacing. The power-of-two constraint that exposes the one detected fault is also deployable. Requantizing every weight scale to its nearest power of two makes CUTLASS and Triton agree bitwise at every linear layer (196/196 and 252/252, against 8/196 and 10/252 under the checkpoints' own scales) and yields byte-identical generated token sequences at 1.7B, 8B and 14B (8/8 prompts, against 0/8 at all three). Observed perplexity point estimates are +0.32%, -0.28% and +0.48%; the 90% intervals cover zero at the two smaller sizes but not at 14B, reaching +0.71% and +0.76%. A previously reported +157% perplexity for this intervention was an artifact of a probe that rewrote scales without requantizing the weights; separating the effects attributes 99.8% of it to the resulting weight--scale mismatch rather than to the power-of-two constraint itself.

</details>


### [215] [Counterfactual Fragility Certificates: Exposing High-Confidence Brittleness under Structured Evidence Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00366)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为反事实脆弱性证书（CFC）的模型无关协议级审计证书，用于评估表格决策系统中单个预测在证据失效时的结构支持程度。通过定义证据失效轨迹，CFC以贪心翻转预算、归一化边际坍塌面积、退化阈值和脆弱性主导得分等指标量化预测的脆弱性。在七个表格基准上，CFC-FDS在识别高置信度但脆弱案例方面表现优异，达到0.915 AUROC，显著优于现有方法。即使在多种扰动、特征重要性评估和自然场景下的证据不可用测试中，其优势依然存在。在20%审查预算下，CFC-FDS可捕获88.9%的脆弱高置信案例，远超传统置信度与能量分数（31.8-37.4%）。此外，作者还探索了脆弱性感知正则化和脆性感知温度校准的应用。整体而言，CFC为揭示传统评分评价体系忽略的高置信度脆弱性提供了具体可靠框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型在高测试准确率和良好校准性下仍可能因关键特征失效而产生错误预测，而现有方法如不确定性估计、解释性分析或扰动检测仅提供标量分数或归因图，无法回答‘在特定证据失效条件下，该预测如何失去支持’这一根本问题。因此亟需一种可重计算、可审计的机制来追踪预测的结构脆弱性路径。

Method: 提出反事实脆弱性证书（CFC），构建基于证据失效的有序轨迹，利用贪心翻转预算、归一化边际坍塌面积、退化阈值及脆弱性主导得分进行量化；采用模型无关设计，适用于各类表格式学习模型（线性、树模型、集成、神经网络等），并通过多维度验证其有效性。

Result: CFC-FDS在七种表格数据集上实现0.915的AUROC，比最强非证书方法高出0.405；在20%审查预算下可捕获88.9%的脆弱高置信案例，远超传统方法的31.8–37.4%；在多种扰动、基线选择、种子变化、预算限制和真实世界不可用场景中均保持优越性能。此外，脆弱性感知正则化与温度校准也展现出实用潜力。

Conclusion: CFC提供了一个可操作、可审计的可靠性框架，能够有效暴露传统评分体系未能捕捉的高置信度脆弱性，推动机器学习系统向更稳健、可解释的方向发展。

Abstract: High test accuracy and good aggregate calibration do not show whether an individual prediction is structurally supported by its evidence. In tabular decision systems, failures often occur when a feature family becomes unavailable, delayed, noisy, stale, or low-trust while the model remains highly confident. Existing calibration, uncertainty, selective-prediction, explanation, and perturbation methods provide scalar scores or attribution maps, but not a recomputable audit object answering: under a declared evidence-failure protocol, what trajectory makes this prediction lose support? We introduce Counterfactual Fragility Certificates (CFC), a model-agnostic protocol-level audit certificate-not a formal robustness certificate-that maps each prediction into an ordered evidence-failure trajectory summarized by greedy flip budget, normalized margin-collapse area, degradation thresholds, and fragility dominance score. Across seven tabular benchmarks and strong linear, tree-based, boosting, and neural baselines, CFC-FDS identifies independently brittle high-confidence cases with 0.915 AUROC, improving over the strongest non-certificate score by +0.405. The advantage persists across perturbation, permutation-importance, group-SHAP, baseline-choice, seed-variance, budgeted-review, and naturalistic field-unavailability checks. Under a 20% review budget, CFC-FDS captures 88.9% of brittle high-confidence cases, compared with 31.8-37.4% for confidence and energy scores. We also evaluate fragility-aware regularization and brittleness-aware temperature correction as secondary uses. CFC provides a concrete reliability framework for exposing high-confidence brittleness missed by ordinary score-centric evaluation.

</details>


### [216] [Adapting Without Gradients: Affine Statistics Transport and What Its Certificate Can Tell You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00374)
*Salim Khazem,Ibrahim Mohamed Serouis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CASTER，一种无需梯度的冻结模型测试时自适应方法，通过在判别子空间中存储源类别统计量，利用目标批次均值估计共享仿射变换，并解析地传输源类别分布以进行分类。该方法无需反向传播、优化器状态或源特征库，在4个主干网络和7个数据集上27/28场景优于k-NN，且状态开销减少18倍。引入残差到边距的可传输性证书，有效识别不安全传输，结合门控机制使平均性能从-3.35提升至+1.69点，且对阈值鲁棒。该证书具有机制特异性，仅接受约4.3%的Tent更新，但保留其可用增益的0.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应（TTA）方法依赖模型参数在推理时可更新，但在冻结模型、无批归一化架构或资源受限场景下失效。需要一种无需参数更新、低存储开销的替代方案，尤其适用于推理加速器和第三方模型部署。

Method: CASTER采用无梯度方式，将源类别统计信息编码于判别子空间；基于目标批次统计量估计类共享仿射变换；通过解析传输将源分布映射至目标域；引入残差到边距的可传输性证书以判断是否安全执行传输，并设计门控机制动态控制传输行为。

Result: 在28个基准设置中，CASTER在27个上超越相同冻结特征下的k-NN，中位状态开销降低18倍；在ImageNet-C上，无条件传输损失高达21.2点，而使用证书后，平均性能由-3.35点转为+1.69点，且性能对阈值变化不敏感；证书对Tent等其他方法具有机制特异性，仅接受极小比例更新，但能保留少量增益。

Conclusion: CASTER是一种轻量级、无需参数更新的测试时自适应方法，适用于冻结模型部署，其提出的可传输性证书提供了明确的安全信号，有助于判断何时应启用或禁用传输操作，为实际应用提供可靠保障。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) typically assumes that model parameters can be updated at inference time. This assumption is restrictive for inference-only accelerators, frozen or third-party models, and memory-constrained deployments, and standard BatchNorm-based TTA configurations may also become inactive on architectures without BatchNorm. We study adaptation when the learned model must remain frozen. We introduce CASTER, a gradient-free method that stores source class statistics in a discriminative subspace, estimates a class-shared affine transformation from target-batch moments, and analytically transports the source class distributions before classification. CASTER requires no backward pass, optimizer state, or stored source feature bank. Across four backbones and seven datasets, it outperforms k-NN on identical frozen features in 27 of 28 backbone-dataset settings while retaining a median of 18x less state. Affine transport is not always reliable. On ImageNet-C, where batches contain only 64 samples for 1000 classes, unconditional transport loses 21.2 top-1 points. We therefore introduce an empirical residual-to-margin transportability certificate. Across 307 evaluation cells, every transport losing more than 10 points has certificate value above 3.9, although benign and destructive regimes are not perfectly separated. Gating converts an average $-3.35$-point effect of unconditional transport into a +1.69-point gain, and performance remains within 0.3 points of the best threshold over a broad threshold range. Finally, we show that this certificate is mechanism-specific: when applied to Tent, it accepts only $4.3\%$ of updates and preserves 0.6% of Tent's available gain. These results position CASTER as a lightweight adaptation mechanism for frozen-model deployment, together with an explicit account of when its safety signal is informative and when it is not.

</details>


### [217] [Neural means and kernel corrections for operator learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00389)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文结合神经网络与精确的Matérn核回归，对残差和学习特征进行建模，在两个公开的模拟问题上评估其性能。在结构力学问题中，测试误差达到4.55%，与最优已发表架构持平；在低数据条件下，误差为5.38%，优于此前6.49%的结果。在OCO-2辐射传输模拟中，该方法在三个光谱带中的两个上超越了已有高斯过程模型，且在特征空间中核函数表现优于原始状态空间。研究揭示了目标在固定有效维度下本征空间范数下降约四十倍的机制，并通过理论证明其有效性。当两种方法表现相当时，所有训练架构的残差相关性高于0.86，共享成分在多样性与样本量上均呈平坦特性，表明此前观测到的性能平台实为数据本身属性。支持性结果包括一个预测堆叠效果的二阶矩恒等式、最优恢复验证证书及一种分布无关的覆盖区间，是唯一在测试中保持有效的不确定性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络与高斯过程模型在复杂模拟任务中各有优劣，但缺乏有效融合二者优势的方法。尤其在低数据场景下，如何提升模型精度与不确定性估计可靠性成为关键挑战。本文旨在通过结合神经网络的非线性表达能力与精确核方法的统计可解释性，构建更稳健、准确且具备可信不确定性的混合模型。

Method: 提出一种混合建模框架：首先使用神经网络拟合输入与输出之间的非线性关系，随后对网络残差及其学习特征应用精确的Matérn核回归。通过分析残差相关性、特征空间变化与目标函数的本征尺度压缩现象，揭示性能提升的内在机制。同时引入二阶矩恒等式预测堆叠效果，提供最优恢复证书与分布无关的覆盖区间以保障不确定性估计的有效性。

Result: 在结构力学基准测试中，模型达到4.55%测试误差，与最优现有方法持平；在低数据条件下（5.38%）显著优于此前6.49%的记录。在OCO-2辐射传输模拟中，于两个光谱带超越已有高斯过程模型，且在特征空间中核方法反超原始空间。研究证实目标函数在特征空间中本征范数大幅降低（约四十倍），从而解释性能提升原因。所有架构残差相关性超过0.86，共享成分稳定，表明先前观察到的性能平台源于数据本质而非模型限制。此外，提出的不确定性信号在各种测试中保持有效。

Conclusion: 本文提出的神经网络与精确核回归相结合的混合方法，在多个真实模拟任务中表现出卓越性能，兼具高精度与可靠不确定性估计。研究揭示了特征空间中目标函数尺度压缩的关键机制，并提供了理论工具（如二阶矩恒等式、最优恢复证书）来指导模型设计与评估。该框架不仅超越了现有方法，还为复杂系统建模中模型融合与不确定性量化提供了新范式。

Abstract: We combine neural network means with exact Matérn kernel regressions of their residuals and of their learned features, and evaluate the pairing on two public emulation problems with published baselines: the structural-mechanics benchmark of de Hoop et al. and the OCO-2 radiative-transfer emulator of Lamminpää et al. On structural mechanics the combination reaches 4.55% test error, matching the best published architecture, and 5.38% against a published 6.49% in the low-data regime. On OCO-2 it improves on the published Gaussian-process emulator on that problem's own test points, outright on two of the three spectral bands; the same kernel that trails the network tenfold on the raw state overtakes it on the network's features, and we measure why (the target's squared native-space norm drops about fortyfold at fixed effective dimension) and prove the mechanism. Where the two families tie instead, the residuals of every architecture we train correlate above 0.86 and their shared component is flat in diversity and sample size, which reads the published plateau as a property of the data. Supporting results include a second-moment identity that predicts stacking outcomes from measured correlations, an optimal-recovery certificate, and a distribution-free coverage band, the only uncertainty signal that survives our tests.

</details>


### [218] [A Multi-Branch Feature Fusion Approach for Health Misinformation Detection and Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00403)
*Mkululi Sikosana,Sean Maudsley-Barton,Oluwaseun Ajao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多分支融合框架，用于检测和刻画健康伪信息在在线社交网络中的传播。该框架基于详尽可能性模型（ELM）和计划行为理论（TPB），融合了基于Transformer的语义、修辞线索、立场表示及心理动机代理变量，在统一的多任务架构中实现高效分析。研究引入可解释的后处理辅助指标——认知传播得分（CPS），通过文本衍生的心理学特征捕捉论证复杂度、情感强度和内容传播潜力，以支持在缺乏真实互动数据时的传播风险推理。在三个基准数据集（Constraint、COVID-19_FNIR、Monkeypox）上的实验表明，该模型在二分类任务中表现优异，于COVID-19_FNIR上达到高达0.9999的ROC-AUC；在传播排序任务中，当具备真实互动监督时，与真实传播排名高度一致（Monkeypox, Spearman's ρ=0.9952），且在代理监督下也表现出极高的对齐度（COVID-19_FNIR, ρ=0.9954）。相较于现有基线，该模型在Constraint和COVID-19_FNIR上提升了检测性能，而Monkeypox仍具挑战性，反映出领域与信号特异性差异。消融实验证明心理与修辞分支带来超越语义嵌入的互补增益。整体框架将认知理论与神经建模结合，增强了透明性并支持可扩展的伪信息监控，未来需进一步验证CPS与人类中心传播判断的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前健康伪信息在在线社交网络中的传播具有高度隐蔽性和复杂性，传统方法依赖单一特征或简单分类模型，难以有效捕捉其传播机制与心理动因。尤其在缺乏真实用户互动数据的情况下，现有方法难以进行可靠的传播风险评估。因此，亟需一种融合心理学、修辞学与深度学习的可解释框架，以提升对伪信息传播路径的理解与监测能力。

Method: 提出一种基于多分支融合的神经架构，整合基于Transformer的语义特征、修辞线索、立场表示以及由心理学驱动的代理变量。通过统一的多任务学习框架，同时完成伪信息检测与传播风险预测。引入认知传播得分（CPS），利用文本中提取的心理学特征（如论证复杂度、情感强度、内容病毒式传播潜力）生成可解释的后处理评分，支持在无真实互动标签情况下的传播风险推理。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上均取得优异性能：在二分类任务中，于COVID-19_FNIR数据集上达到0.9999的ROC-AUC；在传播排序任务中，当有真实互动监督时，与真实传播顺序高度一致（Monkeypox, ρ=0.9952），在代理监督下也保持极高一致性（COVID-19_FNIR, ρ=0.9954）。相比基线模型，该框架在检测性能上显著提升，尤其在约束性数据集和疫情相关数据集上。消融实验表明心理与修辞分支提供额外增益，证明多源信息融合的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究构建了一个融合认知理论与深度神经网络的多分支融合框架，实现了对健康伪信息传播的高效检测与可解释的风险评估。通过引入认知传播得分（CPS），模型在缺乏真实互动标签时仍具备强大的推理能力，为大规模伪信息监控提供了新的技术路径。未来工作需在真实人类判断层面验证该指标的效度，以推动系统向更可信、可信赖的方向发展。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-branch fusion framework for detecting and characterising the propagation of health misinformation in online social networks (OSNs). Grounded in the Elaboration Likelihood Model (ELM) and the Theory of Planned Behaviour (TPB), the model fuses transformer-based semantics with rhetorical cues, stance representations, and psychologically motivated proxies in a unified multi-task architecture. In addition to binary classification, we introduce the Cognitive Propagation Score (CPS), an interpretable post-hoc auxiliary score computed from psychologically motivated, text-derived cues capturing argument complexity, emotional intensity, and content-derived virality potential, to support diffusion-risk reasoning when engagement ground truth is incomplete or unavailable. Experiments on three benchmark datasets, Constraint, COVID--19\_FNIR, and Monkeypox, show strong classification performance, achieving ROC--AUC up to 0.9999 on COVID--19\_FNIR, while propagation-oriented ranking achieves near-perfect agreement when engagement-derived supervision is available (Monkeypox, Spearman's $ρ= 0.9952$) and similarly high ranking alignment under proxy-based supervision on COVID--19\_FNIR ($ρ= 0.9954$). Compared with representative literature baselines, the fusion model improves detection on Constraint and COVID--19\_FNIR, while Monkeypox remains more challenging, reflecting domain- and signal-specific differences. Ablation analysis further indicates that psychological and rhetorical branches provide complementary gains beyond semantic embeddings. Overall, the framework bridges cognitive theory and neural modelling to improve transparency and to support scalable misinformation monitoring, with future work required to validate CPS against human-centred diffusion judgements.

</details>


### [219] [How Temporal Correlations Shape Memory in Linear Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00420)
*Arnol Manuel Fokam,Fasseu Sieyondji Akpevwoghene,Edem Fiifi Dawson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过精确求解相关输入下的学习动态，揭示了相关性如何影响线性循环神经网络（LRNN）的记忆构建。相关性增加了保留过去信息的成本，导致网络在训练中记忆先验信息减少。研究发现：（1）相关性不仅改变学习的最终状态，还重塑学习过程，使记忆先经历积累、超调再部分消除；（2）记忆开关由输入与前一输入的相似度决定，不受序列长度或远距离相关性影响；（3）最佳网络结构会自动形成无需显式设计的直通路径，当有额外隐藏维度时，训练可自发生成零误差的无记忆路径。结果表明，相关数据使递归网络转变为变化检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在相关输入下线性循环神经网络如何学习并维持记忆，尤其关注相关性对学习动态和网络结构的影响。

Method: 精确求解线性循环神经网络在相关输入下的学习动力学，分析记忆成本随输入相关性的变化，并推导出记忆开关阈值和最优网络结构的形成机制。

Result: 相关性增加记忆成本，导致网络最终保留更少过去信息；记忆行为呈现积累-超调-清除的动态过程；记忆开启与否由当前输入与前一输入的相似度决定；训练能自发构建直通路径以实现零误差。

Conclusion: 输入相关性是决定网络是否学习记忆的关键因素，其作用体现在记忆成本上，而非序列长度或长程相关性；相关数据使递归网络倾向于检测变化而非存储历史。

Abstract: The linear recurrent neural network (LRNN) is a simple model for studying how much memory a network builds up as it trains. For uncorrelated inputs, earlier work found that training itself settles the network between keeping the past and reacting only to the present. Real sequences are correlated, and we solve the learning dynamics exactly for correlated inputs. In the solution, keeping the past carries a cost. The whole effect of correlation lands on that cost. This cost reduces to the earlier one when inputs are uncorrelated and grows once they are positively correlated. Three findings follow. (1) Correlation reshapes the course of learning, not only its end. Memory builds, overshoots, and is partly removed, and the settled network keeps less of the past. (2) Memory switches off at a threshold set by one number, how much each input resembles the one just before it. Neither sequence length nor longer-range correlation moves this threshold. Memory is worth keeping only when the task needs the previous input more than the current input already supplies it through correlation with the past. (3) The best network changes too. Zero error demands a feedthrough, a path that passes the current input straight to the network's output and remembers nothing, and training builds it unprompted when given one spare hidden dimension. Our work turns one property of the input into a prediction of whether a network learns memory and explains why correlated data turns recurrent networks into change detectors.

</details>


### [220] [Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00444)
*Sheng Jia,Xiao Wang,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan,Rein Houthooft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAPO提出一种自适应裁剪机制，针对强化学习中奖励可验证（RLVR）任务的策略优化问题。传统方法使用固定重要性采样（IS）裁剪边界，导致在困难任务上的稀有正确轨迹与简单任务上的大量正确轨迹被同等抑制，而前者携带更强的学习信号。GAPO基于反KL信任区域视角，根据轨迹优势动态调整裁剪阈值，使高学习信号轨迹获得更多更新空间。该方法无需奖励重塑，兼容标准PPO/GSPO目标函数，在数学推理和编码基准上显著提升Pass@1和Pass@k指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定重要性采样裁剪边界对不同难度任务的正确轨迹一视同仁地进行抑制，忽视了困难任务中稀有正确轨迹携带更强学习信号的特点，导致探索效率低下。

Method: 提出Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization (GAPO)，通过动态调整重要性采样裁剪边界以匹配轨迹优势，基于反KL信任区域理论设计，保持原有目标函数不变，仅修改裁剪阈值。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama模型上，GAPO在数学推理与编码任务中均优于固定裁剪与优势重塑基线，显著提升Pass@1和Pass@k表现，尤其在基础模型通过率较低的情况下效果更明显。

Conclusion: GAPO是一种有效的插件式改进方法，通过自适应裁剪机制更好地利用稀有但高价值的正确轨迹，提升强化学习在复杂推理任务中的学习效率与性能。

Abstract: Group relative policy optimization for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically uses a fixed importance-sampling (IS) ratio clipping boundary across all rollouts. We identify a key limitation: rare correct rollouts on harder problems and abundant correct rollouts on easier problems are clipped at comparable rates, despite contributing very different learning signals. Rollouts with low group success exhibit larger IS ratios and carry stronger gradient signal for exploration and solving new problems, yet are disproportionately suppressed by fixed clipping.
  To address this, we propose Group Adaptive Clipping Policy Optimization (GAPO), a plug-in modification to GRPO methods that adapts the clipping boundary to the rollout advantage. GAPO is motivated by a reverse-KL trust-region perspective, which suggests that rollouts with larger learning signal should receive proportionally greater update headroom. GAPO requires no reward shaping and preserves the standard PPO/GSPO surrogate while adapting only the clipping threshold. Across Qwen and Llama models, GAPO consistently improves both Pass@1 and Pass@k over fixed clipping and advantage-shaping baselines on math reasoning and coding benchmarks where the pass rates by the base model are relatively low.

</details>


### [221] [CRAD: Class-wise Reliability-Aware Distillation for Decentralized Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00446)
*Baraa Bilbeisi,Mengchen Fan,Baocheng Geng,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种去中心化的知识蒸馏框架，通过客户端在本地评估同伴模型并基于软预测进行知识蒸馏，解决了传统联邦学习中架构同质性和数据非独立同分布（non-IID）的问题。针对教师模型预测融合的可靠性问题，提出了类别感知的可靠性加权蒸馏方法（CRAD），该方法通过过滤不一致教师并按类别精度与置信度加权聚合，提升模型性能。在多个图像分类基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖参数平均，要求客户端具有相同模型架构且对非IID数据敏感；而真实场景中模型异构和数据分布差异普遍存在，因此需要一种无需统一架构、无需中央服务器、不泄露原始数据的新型协作学习范式。

Method: 构建去中心化知识蒸馏框架，每个客户端在本地评估其他客户端的模型快照，并基于其输出的软标签进行知识蒸馏；提出CRAD方法，根据每类别的教师预测一致性与可靠性（精度/逆方差）进行筛选与加权聚合，支持异构架构和非IID数据。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和PathMNIST三个图像分类任务上，面对严重非IID数据和异构模型结构，CRAD显著优于现有方法，提升了全局准确率。

Conclusion: CRAD通过引入类别级可靠性感知机制，在无需共享原始数据或中心服务器的前提下，有效应对了模型异构与数据非独立同分布的挑战，为实际部署中的联邦学习提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning (FL) relies on parameter averaging, which forces clients to be doubly homogeneous: it demands an identical architecture and degrades under non-IID data. Real-world deployments usually break both assumptions. We sidestep both by building a decentralized knowledge distillation framework in which each client evaluates its peers' model snapshots on its own local data and distills from the resulting soft predictions. Because knowledge is transferred through the shared class posterior, clients are free to run different architectures; and because every teacher is evaluated on the student's own device, raw data never leaves the client, with no central server or public dataset required. Within this setting, we identify and address an under-examined problem: how to combine the peer teacher predictions. Existing methods, like uniform averaging, ignore how knowledge reliability varies across teachers and classes. We propose Class-wise Reliability-Aware Distillation (CRAD), which, per class, first discards teachers that disagree with the peer consensus and then takes a weighted average of the rest, weighting each teacher by its per-class reliability (precision, or inverse variance). Since the variance of an accuracy from $n$ samples scales as $1/n$, support enters automatically: among the teachers that survive filtering, a teacher is trusted for a class to the degree that it is both accurate and well-evidenced for it. On three image-classification benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and PathMNIST colon pathology), across heterogeneous architectures under severe non-IID skew, CRAD consistently outperforms competing methods in global accuracy.

</details>


### [222] [HBQ: Hierarchical Scaling Block Quantization with Hardware-Efficiency-Aware Design for Accurate LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00450)
*Chun-Ting Chen,Dongmin Han,Hangyeol Mun,Jake Hyun,Arnab Raha,Amit Agarwal,Mark Anders,Mohamed Abdelfattah,Jae-sun Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层块量化（HBQ），通过大块尺寸与低开销尾数（SIG）缩放，实现高效且高精度的LLM部署。HBQ-A在W4A5下达到W4A16精度，比NVFP4更省面积；HBQ-E进一步降低17%硬件成本，性能优于现有BQ方法。28nm ASIC验证显示，相比最先进WoQ，HBQ在相同精度下面积/能效提升2.3×/4.6×，系统能耗降低1.6–3.3×，速度提升1.5–3.0×，并保持最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统块量化（BQ）在块大小与精度间存在权衡：增大块大小可提升硬件效率，但会损害精度。现有方法受限于小块设计及传统的幂次缩放或整数缩放，无法有效缓解大块带来的量化误差。为此，需探索更优的量化策略以兼顾效率与精度。

Method: 提出分层块量化（HBQ），采用大块结构最大化硬件效率，并引入低开销尾数（SIG）缩放进行二级量化。通过合理分配量化级别并考虑激活与权重分布差异，有效补偿大块量化误差。同时，在28nm ASIC中实现权重、激活及KV缓存的HBQ，并集成部分和BQ方案以降低EMA能量开销。

Result: HBQ-A在仅使用W4A5的情况下实现W4A16精度，所需芯片面积小于NVFP4；HBQ-E在减少17%硬件成本的同时，精度高于所有现有BQ方法。28nm ASIC实测表明，相较于最先进WoQ，HBQ在相同精度下实现2.3×面积效率、4.6×能效提升，系统能耗降低1.6–3.3×，速度提升1.5–3.0×，并保持最佳精度。

Conclusion: HBQ通过大块结构与创新的SIG缩放机制，突破了传统BQ在效率与精度之间的权衡限制，显著提升了LLM推理的硬件效率与能效表现，是面向高效部署大语言模型的有力方案。

Abstract: Block Quantization (BQ) is a promising approach for efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs), enabling low-precision computation with controlled accuracy degradation. Compared to scalar weight-only quantization (WoQ), BQ quantizes both weight and activation, offering higher hardware efficiency and end-to-end inference on a unified datapath, but its design space, spanning bit-width, block size, scaling, and numeric formats, remains underexplored.
  We provide hardware/benchmark results through design space exploration (DSE). We find that increasing block size improves hardware efficiency by amortizing dequantization and accumulation costs, but degrades accuracy. This trade-off limits conventional BQ methods.
  Motivated by this insight, we propose Hierarchical Block Quantization (HBQ). Unlike prior methods [1], [2], which use small blocks and conventional Power-of-Two (PoT) or integer-based scaling, HBQ uses large blocks to maximize efficiency and introduces low-overhead significand (SIG) scaling for second-level quantization. By allocating quantization levels effectively and accounting for distinct activation and weight distributions, SIG scaling compensates for large-block errors more effectively than prior PoT and INT schemes. HBQ-A (accurate) achieves W4A16-level accuracy using only W4A5 while requiring less silicon area than NVFP4. HBQ-E (efficient) further reduces hardware cost by 17% while maintaining higher accuracy than all existing BQ methods.
  We implemented a 28nm ASIC accelerator applying HBQ to weights, activations, and KV cache, and integrated a novel partial-sum BQ scheme to further reduce EMA energy.
  Compared to state-of-the-art WoQ, HBQ delivers $2.3\times$/$4.6\times$ higher area/energy efficiency at the same accuracy level; $1.6$--$3.3\times$ system energy reduction and $1.5$--$3.0\times$ speedup over prior BQ methods while providing best accuracy.

</details>


### [223] [Can LLMs Use Relational Transformer Embeddings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00457)
*Francisco Galuppo Azevedo,Clarissa Lima Loures*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索将冻结的关系编码器嵌入作为软令牌注入大语言模型（LLM）的融合策略，以结合关系结构处理与语言推理能力。实验中将关系变压器（RT）的嵌入通过学习的MLP投影和LoRA适配注入Qwen3.5-4B，并经过链式思维监督微调（SFT）和基于组的强化学习（GSPO）训练。在RelBench上的10个二分类任务上评估了四种监督设置（单任务、同数据集、跨数据集、全任务），结果表明混合模型并未稳定优于独立的RT模型，表现常低于随机水平，对序列化格式和关系令牌预算敏感，且在强化学习训练中不稳定。研究报告了这些负面结果并分析失败原因，认为软令牌融合需更强的对齐目标和模式感知设计才能成为可靠的关联预测路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种无需损失性文本序列化的融合策略，使关系编码器处理多表结构，大语言模型处理语言与推理，实现更高效的关系预测。

Method: 将冻结的Relational Transformer（RT）嵌入通过学习的MLP投影和LoRA适配注入Qwen3.5-4B；先进行链式思维监督微调（SFT），再使用组基强化学习（GSPO）训练；在10个二分类任务上评估不同监督设置下的性能。

Result: 混合模型未一致优于独立的RT模型，表现常低于随机水平，对序列化格式和关系令牌预算高度敏感，在强化学习训练中不稳定。

Conclusion: 软令牌融合策略目前不可靠，需更强的对齐目标和模式感知设计，方可用于有效的关系预测任务。

Abstract: Injecting frozen relational-encoder embeddings as soft tokens into a large language model (LLM) is a conceptually appealing fusion strategy: the encoder handles multi-table structure, the LLM handles language and reasoning, and no lossy text serialization is required. We test this hypothesis concretely by injecting embeddings from a frozen Relational Transformer (RT) into Qwen3.5-4B via a learned MLP projection and LoRA adaptation, trained first with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on chain-of-thought reasoning traces and then with group-based reinforcement learning (GSPO). We evaluate across 10 binary classification tasks on 6 relational databases from RelBench, under four supervision regimes: single-task (ST), within-dataset (WD), cross-dataset (CD), and all-task (ALL). The hybrid model does not consistently outperform standalone RT: it is frequently below random, highly sensitive to serialization format and relational-token budget, and unstable under RL training. We report these negative results and analyze the failure modes, arguing that soft-token fusion requires stronger alignment objectives and schema-aware design before it can serve as a reliable route to relational prediction.

</details>


### [224] [Context Window Failures in Relational Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00460)
*Denis Oliveira Correa,Francisco Galuppo Azevedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Animus 是一个合成的金融数据集，用于测试高基数关系数据上的模型表现。研究发现，现有三种关系深度学习模型在原始数据上表现不佳（R² ≤ 0.18），但通过简单的时序预聚合处理后，性能显著提升至 R² 达 0.65，表明当前模型对高基数数据尚不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估当前关系深度学习模型在高基数多表数据上的表现，特别是面对大量相关记录时的适应性。

Method: 构建合成金融数据集 Animus，包含多达数万条交易记录；对比三种最新模型（RT、Griffin、RelGT）在原始数据与预聚合数据上的预测性能。

Result: 原始数据上模型表现差（R² ≤ 0.18），经时序预聚合后性能显著提升至 R² = 0.65，说明当前模型依赖预处理，难以直接处理高基数数据。

Conclusion: 当前关系基础模型尚未准备好应对真实世界中高基数的多表数据，需要更强大的建模能力或更智能的预处理机制。

Abstract: Recent Relational Deep Learning architectures have been proposed as foundation models for multi-table relational data, yet they impose constrained neighborhood budgets that force row truncation when an entity has many related records. We introduce Animus, a synthetic financial dataset in which predicting customer income requires aggregating up to tens of thousands of transactions. On the raw representation, three recently proposed models (RT, Griffin, RelGT) achieve $R^2 \le 0.18$; a single, routine, temporal pre-aggregation step recovers $R^2$ up to $0.65$. This questions whether current relational foundation models are ready for high-cardinality real-world data.

</details>


### [225] [Higher Structures in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00472)
*Michael L. Roberts,Carlos Zapata Carratalá. Nicholas J. Cooper,Lijun Chen,François G. Meyer,Danna Gurari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了高阶张量操作在深度学习中的重要性，进行了对训练后神经网络中高阶现象的创新性实证研究，提出了多层感知机的超图推广，并探讨了与进化算法的联系，最后讨论了未来研究的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示高阶张量操作在深度学习中的潜在作用，理解训练后神经网络中的高阶现象，推动模型结构和优化方法的创新。

Method: 实证研究、超图建模、与进化算法的关联分析。

Result: 发现了高阶张量在神经网络中的显著存在，提出超图化多层感知机的新架构，揭示了与进化算法的潜在联系。

Conclusion: 高阶张量操作对深度学习具有重要意义，未来研究应深入探索其理论基础与应用潜力。

Abstract: We provide an expository introduction on the importance of higher-arity tensor operations to deep learning. Then, we conduct a novel empirical investigation of higher-arity phenomenon in trained neural networks, introduce a hypergraphical generalization of the multilayer perceptron, and explore connections to evolutionary algorithms. We conclude with a discussion of promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [226] [AdaptNTK: Adaptive Uncertainty Quantification and Active Learning for Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00488)
*Prajwal Ananth,Shuwen Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaptNTK is a single-model framework for uncertainty quantification in machine learning interatomic potentials, using a regularized Mahalanobis distance in empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) feature space. It enables efficient, recursive uncertainty updates without retraining, reducing redundancy in active learning batches. It achieves high correlation with force errors and outperforms ensembles in accuracy and speed on rMD17 and Transition-1X datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing uncertainty quantification methods in machine learning interatomic potentials face trade-offs between computational cost and reliability, and struggle with redundancy during batch acquisition. There is a need for an efficient, single-model approach that can update uncertainty sequentially without retraining.

Method: AdaptNTK measures uncertainty as a regularized Mahalanobis distance in fixed NTK feature space. The method allows recursive uncertainty updates after each data selection, avoiding retraining and reducing redundancy in the acquisition batch.

Result: On rMD17, AdaptNTK achieves Spearman correlation of 0.68 and Pearson correlation of 0.71 with force errors, matching a three-member ensemble in error retention. In active learning, it achieves the lowest force errors on both rMD17 and Transition-1X, especially excelling on transition-state configurations. It provides a 2.6-fold speedup per cycle compared to ensemble methods.

Conclusion: AdaptNTK offers a computationally efficient, single-model solution for uncertainty quantification in machine learning interatomic potentials, enabling data-efficient active learning through sequential updates and reduced redundancy.

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials bridge the gap between quantum chemical precision and classical computational speed, enabling molecular dynamics simulations with first-principles accuracy. Their reliability is often improved through active learning, which iteratively expands the training set by identifying uncertain, out-of-distribution configurations. Existing uncertainty-quantification methods often involve a trade-off between computational cost and reliability, and generally cannot account for redundancy as an acquisition batch is assembled. Here, we introduce AdaptNTK, a single-model framework that measures uncertainty as a regularized Mahalanobis distance in empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) feature space. With the NTK features fixed during acquisition, the uncertainty depends on the acquired configurations but not their reference labels. This allows the uncertainty to be updated recursively after each selection without retraining, reducing redundancy within an acquisition batch. On held-out rMD17 data, AdaptNTK achieves the highest mean correlations with force errors (Spearman 0.68, Pearson 0.71) and matches a three-member ensemble in error retention. In active learning experiments, AdaptNTK achieves the lowest force errors across rMD17 and Transition-1X, with particularly strong performance on transition-state configurations in Transition-1X. AdaptNTK provides a 2.6-fold speedup per Transition-1X cycle relative to the ensemble, providing efficient single-model uncertainty estimation with sequential updates for data-efficient active learning.

</details>


### [227] [A hybrid quantum-classical neural network for learning to route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00489)
*Marcus Rolf Peter Ritt,Alexsandro Santos da Rosa Júnior,Marcos Vinicius Reballo,Cesar Augusto do Amaral,Fernando Augusto Caletti de Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了混合量子-经典神经网络在学习路径规划启发式算法中的应用，重点考察小型量子神经网络是否能替代高性能注意力机制路由模型中参数量大的模块，同时保持解决方案质量。实验表明，在车辆路径问题中，编码器前馈层的替换是最有前景的设计方案，可使模型参数减少56.6%，在中小规模实例上与经典神经网络基线表现接近，但在大规模实例上差距逐渐扩大。与经典路由算法相比，后者在固定欧几里得测试集上仍具竞争力甚至更优。因此，研究未发现量子优势或求解器主导地位，但确认编码器前馈替换是一种可行的混合模块压缩策略，适用于神经组合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小型量子神经网络能否有效替代经典注意力机制路由模型中高参数模块，以实现模型压缩并维持解决方案质量，推动量子增强型神经组合优化的发展。

Method: 采用混合量子-经典神经网络架构，针对容量受限的车辆路径问题，设计并评估编码器前馈层的量子替代方案，通过对比不同规模实例上的性能表现，分析其参数效率与解的质量。

Result: 编码器前馈层替换使模型参数减少56.6%，在中小规模实例上表现接近经典基线，但在大规模实例上性能差距扩大；经典路由算法在固定欧几里得测试集上仍具显著优势。

Conclusion: 本研究未发现量子优势或求解器主导现象，但验证了编码器前馈替换作为混合模块压缩策略在神经组合优化中的可行性。

Abstract: This work studies hybrid quantum-classical neural networks for learning routing heuristics. Specifically, this paper asks whether small quantum neural networks can replace parameter-heavy modules inside a competitive attention-based routing model while maintaining solution quality. For the capacitated vehicle routing problem, encoder feed-forward replacement emerges as the most promising design: it reduces the number of model parameters by 56.6% while keeping the hybrid model close to the classical neural baseline at small and medium instance sizes, although the gap grows for larger instances. This work also compares to classical routing algorithms, which remain highly competitive and often superior on the fixed Euclidean test sets. Our results therefore do not indicate quantum advantage or solver dominance, but identify encoder feed-forward replacement as a viable hybrid-module compression strategy for neural combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [228] [VATO: A Vortex-Force-Aware Transformer Operator for Unsteady Separated Aerofoil Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00507)
*Xingxin Yang,Zhan Zhang,Yichen Li,Juan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VATO（Vortex-Force-Aware Transformer Operator），通过将涡力图（VFM）方法与几何感知神经算子结合，提升非定常分离流的预测精度。VATO-S在训练阶段引入局部VFM力贡献场监督，不增加模型大小或推理成本；VATO-A则利用VFM贡献和敏感性场优先选择对力相关源位置进行残差交叉注意力。在双边缘平板机翼的54条轨迹数据上测试，两种方法在1-20毫秒预测范围内显著降低速度、压力、涡量误差，并在超出训练范围50%时仍保持优异性能，尤其在力相关输出上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高保真CFD虽能精确模拟非定常分离流中的非线性分离与涡脱落动力学，但计算成本高，难以用于设计与控制中的反复使用。标准场级代理模型未能区分对气动载荷贡献最大的流动区域，导致预测精度受限。因此需要一种既能高效预测又能聚焦关键力贡献区域的新方法。

Method: 提出VATO框架，包含VATO-S与VATO-A两种变体：(1) VATO-S通过训练阶段添加局部VFM力贡献场监督，实现无额外参数与推理开销的改进；(2) VATO-A利用VFM贡献与敏感性场指导残差交叉注意力机制，优先关注对力有重要影响的流动区域，增强模型对关键物理过程的捕捉能力。两者均基于几何感知神经算子架构，融合涡力图方法以强化力相关特征学习。

Result: 在双边缘平板机翼的54条轨迹测试中，VATO-S在1-20毫秒预测范围内使速度、压力、涡量误差分别降低10.4%、1.0%、15.6%；VATO-A则分别降低15.8%、7.5%、31.2%。其中，VATO-S在基于VFM的阻力误差最小，而VATO-A在基于压力的升力与阻力误差最低。当预测时间超出训练范围50%时，VATO-A仍保持26.9%的涡量误差降低，且所有四种力读数均有显著改善，表明其具备更强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 力感知的神经算子学习能够有效提升非定常分离流中流场预测与气动功能量（如升力、阻力）的准确性，尤其在长时预测与外推场景下表现优越，为高效高精度气动设计与控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of unsteady separated flows is challenging because the aerodynamic loads depend on nonlinear separation and vortex-shedding dynamics. Although high-fidelity CFD resolves these mechanisms, its cost limits repeated use in design and control. Standard field-level surrogate training, however, does not distinguish the flow regions that contribute most strongly to the aerodynamic loads. We introduce VATO (Vortex-Force-Aware Transformer Operator), which couples the Vortex Force Map (VFM) method to a geometry-aware neural operator through two complementary mechanisms. VATO-S adds training-only supervision of the local VFM force-contribution field, with no increase in model size or inference cost. VATO-A uses VFM contribution and sensitivity fields to prioritise force-relevant source locations for residual cross attention. The methods are evaluated on unsteady CFD data for double-edged-plate aerofoils over 54 trajectories from nine geometries. Over lead times of 1-20~ms, VATO-S reduces velocity, pressure, and vorticity errors by 10.4\%, 1.0\%, and 15.6\%, respectively, while VATO-A achieves reductions of 15.8\%, 7.5\%, and 31.2\%. VATO-S gives the lowest VFM-derived drag error, whereas VATO-A gives the lowest pressure-derived lift and drag errors. Over lead times extending 50\% beyond the training range, VATO-A retains a 26.9\% reduction in vorticity error and larger improvements in all four force readouts, despite reduced gains in velocity and pressure. These results show that force-aware operator learning can improve both flow-field prediction and aerodynamic functional accuracy in unsteady separated flows.

</details>


### [229] [Learning Task-Specific Antibody Representations via Function-Aware Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00518)
*Ayan Goel,Thomas A. Walton,Amirali Aghazadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为功能感知掩码（function-aware masking）的预训练算法，通过将掩码位置与特定功能先验（如IMGT注释或结构预测）对齐，引导抗体语言模型学习更具功能特异性的表示。该方法在结构相关任务上提升达14%，在CDR相关任务上提升达5.9倍；进一步设计混合掩码策略整合多类先验，实现多任务性能均衡优化。结果表明，有意识的掩码放置是一种无需额外参数即可引入功能归纳偏置的有效机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体语言模型虽通过掩码语言建模预训练获得重要表征，但掩码过程本身未被充分利用作为诱导偏置的手段。抗体具有多种生物学先验，需更精准地针对不同功能目标优化模型学习过程。

Method: 提出功能感知掩码策略，依据功能先验（如IMGT注释、结构预测）指导掩码位置选择，并开发融合多先验的混合掩码方法以平衡多个功能目标的重建能力。

Result: 功能感知掩码显著提升对应任务性能：结构相关任务最高提升14%，CDR相关任务最高提升5.9倍；混合掩码策略有效实现多目标性能平衡。

Conclusion: 有意识的掩码放置可作为一种无参数机制，在抗体语言模型训练中有效引入功能归纳偏置，提升下游任务表现。

Abstract: Antibody-specific language models pretrained via masked language modeling (MLM) learn representations that are critical for downstream sequence design and property prediction tasks. Yet, the corruption process itself is rarely leveraged as a source of inductive bias during pretraining. While preferentially masking complementarity-determining regions (CDRs) improves binding-related predictions, antibodies possess diverse biological priors over a variety of functions. Herein, we introduce function-aware masking, a family of pretraining algorithms that align mask placement with specific functional priors (e.g., from IMGT annotations or structure predictions) to shape the learned representation space. We show that these specialist masking strategies significantly improve performance on their respective objectives, yielding up to a 14% gain on structure-related tasks and up to a 5.9x improvement on CDR-related tasks. To further improve performance across multiple functional axes, we develop hybrid masking strategies that integrate multiple priors, balancing reconstruction over binding, structural, and biophysical objectives. Our results demonstrate that informed mask placement provides a parameter-free mechanism for imposing functional inductive biases in antibody language model training.

</details>


### [230] [Why Multi-Layer Message Passing Works: Completeness Theory for Graph Neural Network Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00528)
*Pingbing Ming,Han Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了超图神经网络（一种具有三体消息传递的不变架构）是势能面的通用逼近器。主要贡献是多层完备性理论：在稀疏、截断图上，L层消息传递可达到与拥有完整L跳邻域相同的表现力，前提是配置满足泛化性、重叠条件和连通性条件。该结果为实践中使用每层截断小于物理相互作用范围的多层消息传递提供了首个严格理论支持，这正是几乎所有基于图神经网络的机器学习原子间势能模型所采用的设置。由此推导出DPA3和CHGNet架构均具备通用逼近能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络在机器学习原子间势能中的应用广泛依赖于多层消息传递结合局部截断，但缺乏严格的理论依据。本文旨在为这种实践提供数学上的合理性解释，尤其是验证其表示能力是否足够强大以逼近任意势能面。

Method: 提出并建立了多层消息传递的完备性理论，通过分析稀疏图上的消息传播过程，证明当系统配置满足特定几何条件时，有限层截断的消息传递能够等价于全范围邻域信息访问。利用对称性约束和函数逼近论工具，构建了超图神经网络的逼近能力框架。

Result: 证明了在满足泛化性、重叠性和连通性条件下，多层截断消息传递可实现与全范围邻域等效的表示能力；进一步证明DPA3和CHGNet等主流架构具备通用逼近潜力。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证实了多层截断消息传递的有效性，为当前主流机器学习势能模型的设计提供了坚实的理论基础，并揭示了超图神经网络在逼近复杂势能面方面的强大能力。

Abstract: We prove that the Hypergraph Neural Network, an invariant architecture with 3-body message passing, is a universal approximator for potential energy surfaces. Our main contribution is a multi-layer completeness theory. We show that $L$ layers of message passing on sparse, cutoff-based graphs achieve the same representational power as having access to the full $L$-hop neighborhood, provided the configurations are generic, satisfy an overlap condition and a connectivity condition. This provides the first rigorous justification for the common practice of using multi-layer message passing with a per-layer cutoff smaller than the physical interaction range, the setting used by virtually all practical graph neural network based machine-learned interatomic potentials. As immediate consequences, we show that both DPA3 and CHGNet architectures inherit universal approximation.

</details>


### [231] [DeSyR: A Decoupled Symbolic Recovery Framework with PINN-Guided Structure Search and Physics-Informed Coefficient Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00530)
*Pancheng Niu,Jun Guo,Qiaolin He,Jingcai Guo,Yanchao Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeSyR 是一种用于微分方程符号恢复的解耦框架，通过物理信息神经网络引导搜索候选拓扑并初步估计系数。在固定拓扑后，仅基于控制方程和约束条件对系数进行精炼，并通过门控选择与验证。对于线性固定拓扑参数化，理论分析表明教师误差的继承是可控的；在满足良好设定、可表示性、零残差可达性和离散确定性条件下，物理驱动的精炼可条件性地恢复精确系数；非线性情形下则为局部保证。在15个微分方程问题、18种配置下的实验显示，候选层面收敛率达99.23%，所有含自由参数的精炼均收敛，中位相对 $L_2$ 误差低于 $2.31\times10^{-14}$，且精炼使误差降低8至14个数量级。结果表明，即使教师数据不完美，只要保留目标可实现的拓扑并确保物理精炼收敛，最终系数仍能准确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从神经网络近似中恢复紧凑的显式符号解面临挑战，尤其当不完美的教师数据指导符号拓扑搜索和系数估计时。现有方法易受教师误差污染，导致最终符号解失真。因此需要一种能够有效解耦拓扑发现与系数精炼、并抑制教师误差传递的新框架。

Method: 提出 DeSyR 框架：首先利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）反复搜索生成候选拓扑及初步常数；一旦拓扑固定，仅基于控制方程和边界/初始条件进行系数精炼；随后通过门控机制筛选并验证候选解。对线性情形给出教师误差继承的理论分析，证明在有限权重混合数据-物理拟合下，误差项以 $O(β^{-1})$ 衰减；并建立在特定假设下的精确系数恢复条件。

Result: 在15个微分方程问题、18种配置下评估，候选层面收敛率高达99.23%；所有涉及自由参数的精炼均成功收敛；配置层面中位相对 $L_2$ 误差低于 $2.31\times10^{-14}$；相同拓扑下，精炼使误差降低8至14个数量级。结果表明，即便教师数据有误差，只要保留合适拓扑且精炼收敛，最终符号解仍可高精度恢复。

Conclusion: DeSyR 有效实现了从不完美神经教师中解耦拓扑发现与系数精炼，显著降低了教师误差对最终符号解的影响。只要目标拓扑被保留且物理精炼收敛，即使教师数据存在噪声或偏差，也能恢复高精度的符号解，为复杂系统建模中的符号回归提供了可靠路径。

Abstract: Recovering compact explicit solutions from neural approximations is challenging when imperfect teacher data guide symbolic topology search and coefficient estimation. We present DeSyR, a decoupled symbolic recovery framework for differential equations. A physics-informed neural network guides repeated searches to construct candidate topologies with provisional constants. Once a topology is fixed, its coefficients are refined solely from the governing equation and prescribed constraints, followed by gated selection and verification. For linear fixed-topology parameterizations, we characterize teacher-error inheritance and show that finite-weight mixed data--physics fitting retains an $O(β^{-1})$ teacher-dependent contribution when the teacher error projects onto the model space. Under well-posedness, representability, zero-residual attainment, and discrete determinacy, physics-only refinement conditionally recovers exact coefficients; for nonlinear parameterizations, the corresponding guarantees are local. DeSyR is evaluated on 15 differential-equation problems across 18 configurations covering high-order, space--time, multidimensional, nonlinear, and coupled systems. A candidate-level audit yields a 99.23% convergence rate among free-parameter refits, while every selected refinement involving free coefficients converges. Configuration-level median refined relative $L_2$ errors are $2.31\times10^{-14}$ or lower. In same-topology comparisons, refinement reduces error by eight to fourteen orders of magnitude. These results show that an approximate neural teacher can guide topology discovery without imposing its error scale on final recovered coefficients, provided a target-capable topology is retained and physics-only refinement converges.

</details>


### [232] [GenONet: A Generative operator Network for High-Resolution Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00544)
*Mohammad Kian Golkar,Luciano Alves de Oliveira,Mohammad Khanjani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenONet introduces a novel Spatio-Temporal U-DeepONet architecture using DeepONet within a GAN framework for long-range precipitation forecasting up to 3 hours. It leverages continuous-time dynamics learning, adversarial training, and a physics-informed loss based on the Moisture Conservation Equation to produce sharp, physically consistent forecasts. The model outperforms baselines in both quantitative metrics and qualitative coherence, especially for high-intensity events and longer lead times.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: High-resolution precipitation nowcasting is challenging due to rapid storm evolution. Existing deep learning models struggle with long forecast horizons, producing blurry and physically implausible results. There is a need for models that maintain structural integrity and physical consistency over time.

Method: GenONet combines a Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) as a generator within a Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) framework. It learns continuous-time precipitation dynamics, uses adversarial training with a spatio-temporal discriminator to enhance forecast sharpness, and incorporates a physics-informed loss derived from the Moisture Conservation Equation to improve physical plausibility.

Result: GenONet achieves higher scores across most evaluation metrics, particularly for high-intensity precipitation and longer lead times. Qualitative results show structurally coherent forecasts that preserve storm features, unlike baseline models that degrade into indistinct patterns. Ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of the physics-informed loss.

Conclusion: By integrating operator learning with adversarial training and physics-informed regularization, GenONet significantly improves long-range precipitation forecasting accuracy and physical realism, offering a promising solution for severe weather prediction.

Abstract: High-resolution precipitation nowcasting is critical for reducing the impacts of severe weather but remains difficult because of rapid storm evolution. Deep learning models have shown great promise for this task, but their predictive skill often deteriorates over longer forecast horizons. This leads to increasingly blurry forecasts that fail to capture the complex, non-linear evolution of storm systems. In order to address these limitations, we introduce Spatio-Temporal U-DeepONet (GenONet), a novel architecture for long-range precipitation forecasting up to 3 hours, specifically designed to produce sharp and physically consistent results. GenONet's architecture pioneers the use of a Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) as a generator within a Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) framework for this task. The DeepONet learns the continuous-time dynamics of precipitation, ensuring stability over long forecast horizons. Adversial training against a spatio-temporal discriminator compels the model to produce sharp, coherent forecasts, while a physics-informed loss regularizer, derived from the Moisture Conservation Equation, improves physical plausibility in our ablation setting. Quantitative evaluations show that our model achieves consistently higher scores on most of the metrics, especially for highintensity events and at longer lead times. Qualitatively, GenONet produces structurally coherent forecasts that maintain their integrity, whereas baseline models degrade into indistinct patterns. Finally, an ablation study confirms the benefit of this physics-informed loss, highlighting the strength of combining operator learning with adversarial training.

</details>


### [233] [Manifold-Aware General Coded Computing for Straggler-Resilient Distributed Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00552)
*Parsa Moradi,Mohammad Ali Maddah-Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向通用编码计算（GCC）的流形感知编码策略，通过利用数据内在的低维流形结构来改进编码设计，从而在存在延迟节点的情况下显著降低均方恢复误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码计算方案未显式利用输入数据的内在结构；而在机器学习等任务中，数据结构正是计算所依赖的关键信息，因此应保留并利用这些结构而非通过源编码消除它们。

Method: 受图基流形学习启发，提出一种基于数据自然流形的编码策略，使编码样本遵循数据的真实低维几何结构，而非人为强加无关的低维结构。

Result: 在神经网络推理和高维多项式求值实验中，所提方法在存在延迟节点时，显著降低了均方恢复误差，优于标准的GCC方案。

Conclusion: 编码计算应充分利用数据的内在几何结构，流形感知编码能有效提升系统鲁棒性与计算精度，为未来编码计算设计提供新思路。

Abstract: Existing coded-computing designs do not explicitly exploit the intrinsic structure of the input data. In communication systems, statistical structure and redundancy are often removed through source coding (or compression) before channel coding is applied. This principle, however, does not transfer directly to coded computation. In many computational tasks, particularly in machine learning, the structure of the data is precisely what the computation seeks to exploit to infer outputs or learn meaningful patterns. Consequently, coded-computing schemes should preserve and leverage this structure in their code design, rather than ignoring or eliminating it through source coding.
  This observation motivates a different perspective on code construction. In many channel-coding schemes, such as Reed-Solomon codes, coded symbols are generated by evaluating a low-dimensional algebraic representation at selected points. In contrast, many high-dimensional datasets naturally concentrate near low-dimensional manifolds. In this paper, we exploit this intrinsic geometry by designing coded samples that follow the natural manifold of the data, rather than imposing an artificial low-dimensional structure unrelated to the data distribution. Inspired by graph-based manifold learning, we propose a manifold-aware encoding strategy for general coded computing (GCC). Experiments on neural network inference and high-dimensional polynomial evaluation demonstrate that the proposed strategy consistently and significantly reduces the mean squared recovery error under straggling compared with standard GCC.

</details>


### [234] [EEG-VID: Task-Guided Latent Predictive Pretraining for EEG Decoding and Assistive Target Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00566)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yuxuan Wu,Yanzi Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEG-VID提出了一种任务引导的潜在预测预训练框架，用于在会话和受试者变化下进行脑电图（EEG）解码。该方法通过指数移动平均目标编码器从近期历史中预测未来的潜在EEG状态，并结合弱任务指导，随后进行监督微调。在VIG-48和BCI竞赛IV-2a/IV-2b数据集上，阶段1在42个匹配骨干-数据集-协议对比中，有41个提升了平均准确率，包括所有12个留一受试者设置，最大提升达16.22个百分点。在48区域跨日VIG-48任务中，EEG-VID达到6.52%的Top-1和30.50%的Top-5准确率。在一项六名参与者离线机器人场景研究中，候选约束目标选择达到40.24%，远超25%的机会水平。结果表明，任务引导的潜在预测是一种可迁移的预训练策略，适用于EEG解码和场景约束辅助目标选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG解码方法在面对会话和受试者间差异时性能下降明显，缺乏有效的跨受试者和跨会话迁移能力。因此需要一种能够有效利用未标注数据进行预训练、并具备强泛化能力的框架。

Method: EEG-VID采用任务引导的潜在预测机制：利用指数移动平均目标编码器从近期脑电历史中预测未来潜在状态；引入弱任务信号作为指导；最后通过监督微调提升具体任务性能。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，EEG-VID显著提升了分类准确率，尤其在留一受试者设置中表现优异；在跨日任务中实现6.52%的Top-1和30.50%的Top-5准确率；在离线机器人场景中，候选约束目标选择达到40.24%，优于随机水平。

Conclusion: 任务引导的潜在预测是一种高效且可迁移的预训练策略，能够显著增强EEG解码模型在跨会话与跨受试者场景下的泛化能力，适用于实际应用中的辅助目标选择任务。

Abstract: We propose EEG-VID, a task-guided latent predictive pretraining framework for EEG decoding under session and subject shifts. EEG-VID predicts future latent EEG states from recent history using an exponential-moving-average target encoder and weak task guidance, followed by supervised fine-tuning. Across VIG-48 and BCI Competition IV-2a/IV-2b, Stage 1 improves mean accuracy in 41 of 42 matched backbone-dataset-protocol comparisons, including all 12 leave-one-subject-out settings, with a maximum gain of 16.22 percentage points. On the 48-region cross-day VIG-48 task, EEG-VID achieves 6.52% Top-1 and 30.50% Top-5 accuracy. In a separate six-participant offline robot-scene study, candidate-constrained target selection reaches 40.24% versus a 25% chance level after subject-specific calibration. These results support task-guided latent prediction as a transferable pretraining strategy for EEG decoding and scene-constrained assistive target selection.

</details>


### [235] [GeoPAR: Large-Scale Multi-Agent Combinatorial Optimization with Geometry-Guided Parallel Autoregressive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00577)
*Wenjian Wu,Zesheng Jia,Jiaying Tang,Benyuan Yang,Jin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoPAR 是一种用于可扩展多智能体组合优化的几何引导并行自回归强化学习框架，通过引入投影窗口稀疏几何机制、稀疏边偏置注意力和缓存引导的冲突感知分配，有效提升了大规模实例上的零样本泛化能力，并减少了推理步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行自回归神经求解器在大规模实例上性能下降，主要由于对局部几何结构建模不足以及冲突任务选择仅在动作生成后处理，导致效率与准确性受限。

Method: GeoPAR 提出三个核心组件：(1) 投影窗口稀疏几何机制，通过多方向投影构建轻量级局部候选邻域；(2) 稀疏边偏置注意力，将几何关系注入节点表示；(3) 缓存引导的冲突感知分配，利用几何缓存抑制独占任务的重复选择。

Result: 在异构车辆路径规划和开放式多配送中心取送货问题上，GeoPAR 显著提升了大规模零样本泛化性能，大幅减少推演步数，同时保持高效推理。

Conclusion: GeoPAR 通过几何信息增强与冲突抑制机制，有效解决了大规模多智能体组合优化中的效率与精度难题，为复杂优化问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent combinatorial optimization problems are notoriously challenging due to their NP-hard nature. Recent parallel autoregressive neural solvers improve inference efficiency by allowing agents to make decisions simultaneously, but their performance often degrades on large-scale instances. This is largely attributable to weak modeling of local geometric structures and the fact that conflicting task selections are handled only after action generation. To address these limitations, we propose GeoPAR, a geometry-guided parallel autoregressive reinforcement learning framework for scalable multi-agent combinatorial optimization. GeoPAR integrates three key components: (1) a projection-window sparse geometry mechanism that builds lightweight local candidate neighborhoods through multi-directional projections, (2) sparse edge-biased attention that injects these geometric relations into node representations, and (3) cache-guided conflict-aware assignment that reuses the geometric cache during decoding to suppress duplicate selections of exclusive tasks. Experiments on heterogeneous vehicle routing and open multi-depot pickup-and-delivery problems show that GeoPAR improves large-scale zero-shot generalization while substantially reducing rollout steps and maintaining efficient inference.

</details>


### [236] [CRAFT: Fine-Tuning Pre-hoc Explainability in AI-native 6G RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00590)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Vijay K Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT（Cold-start Reasoning Alignment via Fine-Tuning），一种数据驱动的方法，用于在电信领域实现前因性推理（pre-hoc reasoning），解决小语言模型（SLMs）在6G无线接入网（RAN）中因冷启动问题导致的推理与标签学习分离的问题。通过自动生成并验证（输入, 推理轨迹, 标签）三元组数据集，使用低秩适应（LoRA）进行微调，显著降低计算开销和时间成本。在TRACTOR和IC xApp数据集上，CRAFT在准确率和F1分数上分别达到86.5%和94.6%，且无解析失败；而直接使用GRPO或SFT+GRPO方法的性能不足28%和53.5%的F1，并伴随大量解析错误。此外，CRAFT初始化的策略可作为后续GRPO微调的稳健起点，在多种奖励函数下表现稳定且无解析失败。最终，CRAFT能耗比GRPO基线低59%，为可审计、可持续部署的6G AI提供可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电信领域大语言模型（LLM）训练范式（如RANSTRUCT风格的监督微调）仅支持事后解释（post hoc rationalization），即解释在决策后生成，无法保证决策过程的可审计性。前因性推理（pre-hoc reasoning）要求在输出标签前生成因果推理链，更符合可解释性和可信AI需求。然而，将主流的强化学习方法（如GRPO）直接移植到电信场景时，面临冷启动障碍：模型难以同时学习正确推理格式和准确预测标签。因此亟需一种高效、可靠的方法来突破该瓶颈。

Method: 提出CRAFT方法，核心包括：1）设计数据生成机制，自主构建高质量的（输入, 推理轨迹, 标签）三元组数据集，并通过验证确保其一致性与正确性；2）利用低秩适应（LoRA）对小语言模型（SLMs）进行高效微调，大幅减少计算资源与时间消耗；3）验证该方法在不同任务设置下的有效性，并进一步探索其作为后续强化学习（如GRPO）初始化基础的潜力。

Result: 在TRACTOR和IC xApp两个真实电信数据集上，CRAFT实现了高达86.5%的准确率和94.6%的F1分数，且无解析失败；相比之下，直接使用GRPO或SFT+GRPO的方法性能极低（<28%准确率，<53.5% F1），且存在严重解析错误。此外，基于CRAFT初始化的策略在多种奖励函数下均表现出一致的高性能与稳定性，且整体能耗较GRPO基线降低59%。

Conclusion: CRAFT成功克服了电信领域中前因性推理的冷启动难题，提供了一种高效、可扩展、低能耗的解决方案，不仅提升了推理质量与系统可审计性，还为6G RAN中部署可信AI奠定了坚实基础。其生成的数据与微调流程具备良好的可迁移性，适合作为后续强化学习优化的初始策略，是迈向全AI原生移动网络的关键一步。

Abstract: The next generation of mobile networks is envisioned as fully AI-native, with AI-RAN architectures embedding small language models (SLMs) to perform reasoning over real-time telemetry. The state-of-the-art training paradigms for telecom LLMs, exemplified by RANSTRUCT-style supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on curated instruction data, are limited to post hoc rationalization. Here, the explanations, when produced at all, are generated after or independently of the decision, leaving the decision process unauditable. Pre-hoc reasoning, where a causal reasoning trace is produced before the output label, is preferable, and the broader LLM reasoning literature has made real progress toward it via RL methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Here we observe that transplanting this recipe into the telecom setting runs into a cold-start barrier: SLMs either learn to output the desired format or learn to predict the label, but rarely both. We identify this barrier and propose CRAFT, which stands for Cold-start Reasoning Alignment via Fine-Tuning, a data-centric method to autonomously generate a verified dataset of (input, trace, label) triplets. CRAFT fine-tunes SLMs on this verified data using low-rank adaptation (LoRA), requiring substantially less compute and wall-clock time than GRPO-based methods. On the TRACTOR and IC xApp telecom datasets, CRAFT achieves up to 86.5% and 94.6% for accuracy and F1 with no parse failures, while direct GRPO and SFT+GRPO fail to exceed 28% and 53.5% F1 with multiple parse failures. We further show that CRAFT-initialized policies serve as a robust foundation for subsequent GRPO fine-tuning, as under diverse reward functions the performance remains consistent with no parse failures. Finally, we demonstrate that CRAFT consumes 59% less energy than GRPO-based baselines, making it a sustainable path to deployable, auditable AI in 6G RAN.

</details>


### [237] [Topological Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00597)
*Benoît Guérand,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于拓扑数据分析（TDA）的新型大语言模型行为控制框架——拓扑引导（Topological Steering），通过激活空间的拓扑表示实现更鲁棒的行为调节，克服了传统方法对局部扰动敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为控制方法直接干预激活或特征空间，对异常值、分布偏移、噪声等局部扰动敏感，亟需更稳健的控制机制。

Method: 利用持久图（persistence diagrams）将激活空间的拓扑结构进行建模，通过拓扑表示实现对大语言模型行为的引导与调控。

Result: Topological Steering在多个模型家族和不同规模的模型上均能一致地改变模型行为，表现出良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 拓扑引导框架成功将拓扑数据分析引入大语言模型的行为控制，为构建更稳定、可解释的行为调节机制提供了新思路。

Abstract: With the rapid rise of large language models (LLMs), controlling undesirable model behaviors has become increasingly important. Existing behavioral control methods typically intervene directly in activation or feature space, but such approaches can be sensitive to outliers, distributional shifts, noise, and other local perturbations. Motivated by Topological Data Analysis (TDA), which captures global rather than purely local structure, we propose Topological Steering, a new framework for steering LLM behavior through the topological representation of activation spaces. Using persistence diagrams, our method connects activation-based steering with TDA and enables more robust behavioral control. We show that Topological Steering consistently modifies LLM behavior across multiple model families and model sizes.

</details>


### [238] [Confess What You Know: Forget-Set Misalignment with Model Knowledge in LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00605)
*Miso Kim,Georu Lee,Seungwon Jeong,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型（LLM）的机器遗忘技术常假设预定义的遗忘集与模型记忆内容匹配，但在实际隐私场景中，原始训练数据不可访问，导致遗忘集不匹配。本文提出两种情况：在‘欠遗忘’中，遗忘集遗漏了记忆信息，泄露持续存在；在‘超出知识范围遗忘’中，算法被迫‘遗忘’模型从未学习过的内容，扰动参数并降低模型效用。通过梯度级分析，发现这些行为源于遗忘目标的不一致，而非特定优化选择。为此，作者提出无需数据的CONFS框架，通过诱导和形式化模型的记忆知识来构建与模型对齐的遗忘集。在合成、多模态和真实世界基准测试中，CONFS在多个指标上接近黄金标准表现，并在遗忘-效用平衡方面具有竞争力，同时比其他无需数据的遗忘集构造方法更好地保持了模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实隐私场景中，原始训练数据通常不可访问，导致传统机器遗忘方法中的遗忘集与模型实际记忆内容不匹配，引发遗忘不足或过度遗忘问题，影响模型隐私保护效果和实用性。因此需要一种无需依赖原始数据、能准确识别模型记忆内容并构建对齐遗忘集的方法。

Method: 提出CONfession-to-Forget-Set (CONFS)框架，采用数据盲方式，通过诱导模型自述其记忆知识，进而构建与模型内部知识对齐的遗忘集，避免因遗忘目标不一致带来的性能下降。

Result: 在多种合成、多模态及真实世界基准测试中，CONFS在多个评估指标上接近黄金标准表现，实现了良好的遗忘-效用平衡，并显著优于其他无需数据的遗忘集构造方法，在保持模型效用方面更具优势。

Conclusion: 遗忘集不匹配是制约机器遗忘性能的关键因素。CONFS通过构建与模型对齐的遗忘集，有效缓解了这一问题，在无需访问原始数据的前提下实现了高效且实用的隐私保护。
