Maineri,Paulina Pankowska,Jasmin Abdel Ghany,Bruno Arpino,Giovanni Cassani,Julia Hellstrand,Katya Ivanova,Sanni Kuikka,Ana Macanovic,Charles Rahal,Felix C. Tropf,Roland J. Veen,Nicole Walasek,Daniël van Wijk,Kelsey Q. Wright,Emilio Zagheni,Henry Abbink,Emanuele Aliverti,Matteo Amestoy,Tilbe Atav,Nicola Barban,Sunnee Billingsley,Goan J. Booij,Louis Boucherie,Yael Broos,Li Ya Chang,Jamie C. Chiu,Chiara Ludovica Comolli,Boris Cule,Qixiang Fang,Dennis M. Feehan,Rachel Ganly,Erwin Gielens,Rolando M. Gonzales Martinez,Andrea Gradassi,Rosember Guerra-Urzola,Mario Guerra-Urzola,Stéphane Guerrier,Enamul Hassan,Vincent A. Haverhoek,Andrew T. Hendrickson,Amber Howard,Yuxuan Jin,Sayash Kapoor,Erik-Jan van Kesteren,Iris ten Klooster,Marie Labussiere,Lydia T. Liu,Tiffany Liu,Adam Maghout,Simone Meneghello,Lasse Mohr,Clara H. Mulder,Saul J. Newman,Jessica Nisén,Janis Norden,Mikkel Odgaard,Riccardo Omenti,Ozancan Ozdemir,Christina Pao,Paige Park,Gaia Penta,Juan C. Perdomo,Tanzir Pial,Alessio Piraccini,Federica Querin,Ziwei Rao,Christian Rellama,Adrien Remund,Frederieke Richert,Arnout van de Rijt,Mojtaba Rostami Kandroodi,Stijn J. Rotman,Lucas Sage,Germans Savcisens,Katrin Schwanitz,Steven Skiena,Alessandro Spata,Yannick Stadtfeld,Benedikt Stroebl,Gaetano Tedesco,Mathilde Theelen,Gianluca Tori,Abigail Tun-Mendicuti,Rishabh Tyagi,Keyon Vafa,Luiz Felipe Vecchietti,Linda Vecgaile,Willem R. J. Vermeulen,Maria-Pia Victoria Feser,Lionel A. Voirol,Thom B. Volker,Xinran Wang,Jiani Yan,Xinyi Zhao,Flora Zhou,Zuzana Zilincikova,Malvina Nissim,Matthew J. Salganik,Gert Stulp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过一个接近理想的预测环境——147名研究人员使用调查数据和全人口登记数据预测荷兰18-45岁居民在三年内生育的情况——探讨了重大生活事件（如生育）的可预测性。尽管采用了从逻辑回归到大语言模型和Transformer等先进方法，预测效果仍仅达到中等水平（最佳F1分数：调查数据0.76，登记数据0.59），且高级模型并未超越传统方法。通过模拟受孕与妊娠的随机生物学过程，研究估计出预测上限（调查数据F1约0.86-0.94，登记数据0.88-0.96）。实际表现低于这一上限，表明数据不完善、方法局限以及不可建模的随机性共同制约了预测能力，同时说明生育本身的随机性本身即构成个体生命预测的重要限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究重大生活事件（如生育）为何难以预测，是由于理论、数据或算法的局限，还是源于本质上的随机性。

Method: 利用147名研究人员对荷兰18-45岁人群的生育预测进行数据挑战，结合调查数据与全人口登记数据，采用多种方法（包括逻辑回归、大语言模型和Transformer），并通过模拟生殖生物学中的随机过程估算预测上限。

Result: 最佳预测性能为调查数据F1 0.76，登记数据F1 0.59；先进模型未优于传统方法；模拟得出预测上限为调查数据F1 0.86–0.94，登记数据F1 0.88–0.96；实际表现低于上限，表明存在数据缺陷、方法不足及不可建模的随机性。

Conclusion: 生育预测存在显著的内在随机性，即使在理想条件下，其预测能力也受限于生物随机性和数据不完善，说明个体生命事件的预测存在根本性边界。

Abstract: Major life events have proven difficult to predict. Does this reflect limits of theory, data, and algorithms, or the large role of chance? We examine one outcome - having a child within three years - through a near-ideal setting for prediction: a data challenge where 147 researchers predicted births for Dutch residents aged 18-45, using survey data and full-population registers. Methods ranged from logistic regression to a large language model and transformers. Predictions were moderately accurate (best F1: register 0.59, survey 0.76); advanced models did not outperform classical ones; and the larger registers did not beat the survey. Simulating the stochastic biology of conception and pregnancy, we estimated a predictive ceiling (survey F1 ~ 0.86-0.94, register 0.88-0.96). Observed performance falls short of this ceiling, implicating imperfect data, methods, and unmodelled chance, while the ceiling itself shows that chance in reproduction alone sets a non-trivial limit on predicting individual lives.

</details>


### [273] [Recent Developments in Transformer Inference Deployment on FPGA Platforms: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01212)
*Arjan Blankestijn,Uraz Odyurt,Amirreza Yousefzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了Transformer模型在FPGA平台上的推理优化最新进展，分析了实现与优化的主流技术，旨在为学术界和工业界的从业者提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Transformer架构在机器学习中的广泛应用，对高效部署的需求日益增长，尤其关注推理过程中的性能（如吞吐量、延迟）与效率（如能耗）。传统硬件如CPU和GPU已难以满足特定场景下的需求，因此探索专用硬件加速器，特别是具备灵活性、能效比高、低延迟特性的FPGA平台，成为重要研究方向。

Method: 通过系统性文献综述方法，提取并深入分析近年来关于Transformer推理在FPGA平台上的实现与优化技术，构建涵盖关键主题的分类体系。

Result: 识别出若干主流设计选择与优化策略，包括量化、剪枝、层融合、内存优化等，并总结出适用于FPGA平台的高效实现范式。

Conclusion: 本研究为Transformer模型在FPGA上的高效部署提供了全面的技术指南，有助于推动其在边缘计算和现场部署场景中的应用。

Abstract: With the rapid and continuous growth in the incorporation of machine learning models based on the Transformer architecture, capable deployment is in high demand. In this context, capable deployment refers to operational performance aspects, e.g., throughput and latency, as well as efficiency aspects, e.g., energy consumption. When it comes to the task of inference using such models, purpose-built hardware accelerators provide a lucrative alternative to common deployment choices, such as Central Processing Units (CPUs) and Graphics Processing Units (GPUs). The Field Programmable Gate Array (FPGA) platforms category is an example of such alternative accelerators, promising implementation flexibility, energy efficiency, improved latency and suitability for on-site deployment. We investigate the most recent advances, trends, and design choices for Transformer inference on FPGA platforms. We perform a systematic literature review, extracting and delving into preferred techniques for implementation and optimisation. This study and the provided taxonomy of topics could act as a guide for researchers from the academia and industry alike.

</details>


### [274] [REFACTOR-VLA: Unsupervised Library Learning of Typed Motor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01215)
*Riyaaz Shaik,Chandru Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REFACTOR-VLA 提出一种唤醒/睡眠系统，通过行为等价核（BEK）在睡眠阶段聚类电机程序片段，并在唤醒阶段使用类型化lambda项生成动作，确保抽象满足最小描述长度和返回保持条件。实验表明，世界模型容量增加不提升性能，而训练目标（如对比损失）对聚类效果影响更大，在LIBERO基准上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型多为单体结构，缺乏可重用的行为抽象，难以处理长时程任务且解释性差；现有技能发现方法未能准确判断动作序列的等价性，依赖未经校准的语言模型或嵌入聚类。

Method: 提出REFACTOR-VLA，采用唤醒/睡眠机制：睡眠阶段基于学习的潜在世界模型 $M_φ$ 的回放数据计算行为等价核（BEK）进行聚类；唤醒阶段使用受类型约束的lambda表达式与修正流动作解码器生成动作，仅允许通过最小描述长度和返回保持验证的抽象。

Result: 世界模型从188M增至430M参数后性能下降；引入监督对比损失（InfoNCE）显著提升聚类质量，各任务下NMI值达0.462–0.915，平均优于基线Δ=+0.184；跨12个数据集的平均配对NMI为0.705（95%置信区间[0.683, 0.729]）；成功构建首个真实LIBERO任务-语言库，解码器使用其中2个抽象并重写全部256条演示。

Conclusion: 模型性能不取决于世界模型容量，而高度依赖训练目标设计；通过行为等价核与形式化抽象约束，REFACTOR-VLA能有效发现可解释、可重用的技能，推动长时程机器人任务的理解与执行。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models -- OpenVLA, $π_0$, RT-2, RDT-1B -- are monolithic: they emit raw motor commands or short action chunks without organizing behavior into reusable abstractions, so they degrade on long-horizon tasks and resist interpretation. Existing skill-discovery methods sidestep the core question of when two action sequences are behaviorally equivalent, either clustering contrastive embeddings or delegating the judgment to a language model uncalibrated to the robot's dynamics. We introduce REFACTOR-VLA, a wake/sleep system for learning reusable skills. Its sleep phase clusters motor-program fragments under a Behavioral-Equivalence Kernel (BEK) computed from rollouts of a learned latent world model $M_φ$; its wake phase emits typed lambda terms over a Hindley--Milner-inspired vocabulary, consumed by a library-conditioned rectified-flow action decoder. Abstractions are admitted only if they pass Minimum Description Length and return-preservation gates. On LIBERO we report two findings. First, enlarging the world model from 188M to 430M parameters worsened performance on 4 of 4 suites, so capacity alone does not help. Second, the training objective matters far more: adding an auxiliary supervised contrastive (InfoNCE) loss during world-model warmup substantially improves sleep-phase clustering, giving Normalized Mutual Information at $n=3$ seeds of $0.462 \pm 0.021$ (object), $0.867 \pm 0.025$ (spatial), $0.915 \pm 0.013$ (goal) and $0.754 \pm 0.010$ (LIBERO-10), and beating the strongest published baseline on all 4 suites by a mean $Δ= +0.184$. Across providers ($n=12$) the 95% bootstrap confidence interval for mean pairwise NMI is $[0.683, 0.729]$ (mean $0.705$). The sleep phase also yields the first real-LIBERO task-language library: the decoder uses 2 of 3 admitted abstractions and rewrites all 256 sampled demonstrations.

</details>


### [275] [Multi-Head Self Attention is a Parameter Identification Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01231)
*W. Ross Morrow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从参数可识别性角度分析多头缩放点积注意力机制，指出头数越多，模型的未识别参数比例越低（从1/2降至1/(2H)），表明更多头的设计使模型结构更可识别。尽管注意力无法完全识别，且部分偏置项对softmax注意力无影响，但这些现象主要为理论观察，对模型效率影响较小。文中还讨论了RoPE和GQA等改进如何提升有效参数比例，并通过数值实验验证了训练中存在与模型不变子空间重叠的更新。研究强调纯数学与统计视角下解释Transformer架构设计的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer架构中多头注意力及其他改进（如RoPE、GQA）为何能提升性能，从参数可识别性的数学角度提供解释。

Method: 基于数学分析，将多头注意力视为参数识别策略，推导未识别参数比例随头数变化的关系；结合数值实验验证训练过程中模型不变子空间的影响。

Result: 多头注意力的可识别性随头数增加而提高；某些偏置项在softmax注意力中无作用；RoPE和GQA能提升有效参数比例；数值实验确认训练更新可能落入模型不变子空间。

Conclusion: Transformer的架构选择（如多头、RoPE、GQA）可通过提升参数可识别性来解释其性能优势，提供了纯数学与统计层面的理解框架。

Abstract: We prove that a multi-head scaled dot product attention can be viewed as a parameter identification strategy. The ratio of unidentified parameters to the total number of parameters scales like the reciprocal of the number of heads ($1/2 \to 1/(2H)$), meaning models with more heads are structurally more identified. A subtle side effect of the mathematics observation that attention can never be fully identified. Similarly we also show that some bias terms can have no effect on softmax-based attention layers in both the single- and multiple-head settings, though this is mostly a curiosity that should have a marginal effect on model size and model training/prediction efficiency. We also touch on modern improvements to transformers including RoPE and GQA from this perspective, illustrating how those as well can improve the ratio of ``meaningful'' parameters to all parameters. Simple numerical examples demonstrate that training can indeed involve updates that overlap model-invariant subspaces that arise from a lack of identification. As part of our experiments we use a ``rebalancing'' approach that can ``fix'' updates that overlap unindentified subspaces but do not try to present evidence this should actually be adopted. Instead we simply view our numerical results as exploring and confirming the theoretical results. As a whole we discuss a purely mathematical/statistical explanation, identification, for why specific architectural choices in transformers may have improved performance.

</details>


### [276] [Post-Training Science for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01244)
*Charles O'Neill,Mudith Jayasekara,Harry Partridge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统性实验，评估了在不同模型规模、架构（密集模型与混合专家模型）及数据集上，监督微调（SFT）中的关键超参数（如学习率、批量大小、训练轮次等）和方法选择（如LoRA与全量微调）的影响。实验覆盖Qwen3和Llama两个模型家族，使用四个真实客户数据集，验证了训练目标的一致性与评价标准的可靠性。研究揭示了最优超参数随模型规模、架构和数据的变化规律，检验了不同策略间的可迁移性，并分析了验证损失、损失景观平坦度等指标对下游性能的预测能力，同时考察了后训练收益随模型与数据量的增长趋势，以及指令遵循能力退化的时机。最后，评估了几何感知优化器相对于AdamW的表现。所有结论均附带不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前监督微调过程中的超参数与方法选择通常重复进行，缺乏统一评估框架。为减少重复工作并提升效率，需系统性地量化不同因素对微调效果的影响，尤其在多样模型与数据场景下建立可迁移的优化规则。

Method: 采用单变量控制的网格搜索（sweep），系统调整学习率、批量大小、训练轮数、优化器类型、微调方式（LoRA/全量）等关键参数；覆盖多个模型规模（包括235B参数的MoE模型）、两种模型家族（Qwen3和Llama）、四种真实客户数据集；每项任务均有基于客户定义的评估标准，确保训练目标一致性；通过多维度对比分析各因素影响，并量化推荐结果的不确定性。

Result: 学习率与批量大小的最优值随模型规模与数据分布变化显著；部分超参数选择规则具有跨模型或跨数据集的可迁移性；LoRA在参数效率与性能之间取得平衡，其秩与α值直接影响适配器学习能力；验证损失并非始终可靠预测下游性能，损失景观平坦度有一定参考价值；后训练增益随模型规模与数据量增长而提升；训练超过一定轮次后，指令遵循能力开始下降；几何感知优化器在部分场景优于AdamW。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了可推广的微调实践指南，强调应根据模型规模、数据特征和任务目标动态调整超参数，而非依赖通用默认设置。通过系统化实验与不确定性量化，提升了微调决策的科学性与可复现性。

Abstract: Every supervised fine-tuning run forces the same chain of decisions, such as learning rate, batch size, LoRA or full fine-tuning, how many epochs, which optimiser, and what data to feed the model. Each of these is typically rediscovered from scratch for every new model and dataset. Here we measure them under one instrument: a sweep that varies one lever at a time, and spans dense and mixture-of-experts models in two families (Qwen3 and Llama), on four real-world customer SFT datasets, for both LoRA and full fine-tuning. These datasets give a controlled testbed: each task carries an evaluation built with the customer, and its training data is produced by iterative supervised fine-tuning that refines model outputs until they pass that evaluation, so the supervised target is internally consistent and the task judge we report against is the criterion the data was built to satisfy. We ask how the optimal learning rate and batch size move with model scale, family, and data, and whether one selection rule transfers across them; what LoRA trades against full fine-tuning, and how its rank and alpha set what the adapter can learn; whether validation loss (or other metrics, such as loss landscape flatness) faithfully ranks downstream quality; whether post-training gains scale with model size and data volume, on a model ladder extended through mixtures-of-experts to 235B parameters; how many epochs to train before general instruction-following erodes; and whether a geometry-aware optimiser improves on AdamW. Each recommendation is paired with a measure of its uncertainty.

</details>


### [277] [Explore More, Drift Less: Outcome-Only Reinforcement Learning Can Suffice for Long-Horizon Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01245)
*Liming Pu,Xiaoxia Li,Yifu Liu,Teng Cao,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CANOPY（Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL）框架，旨在解决小规模开放模型在仅依赖结果反馈的强化学习中因信号稀疏和策略漂移导致的性能瓶颈。通过扩大单任务探索、保持在线更新、使用KL约束并限定于自身动作令牌，实现更有效的学习。在AppWorld和SWE-bench Verified基准上，Qwen3-14B和Qwen3.5-9B模型仅通过环境交互训练即达到领先性能，无需额外监督或复杂代理结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前小规模开放模型在长程交互任务中，由于仅依赖最终结果进行强化学习，常因奖励稀疏导致梯度信号不足，且频繁更新导致策略漂移，限制了模型能力提升。现有方法依赖密集奖励、微调先验等外部辅助，但这些并非根本解法。

Method: 提出CANOPY协议：通过扩大同一任务的探索范围以恢复自然信号，所有更新均保持在策略内，采用KL约束，并仅基于代理自身的动作令牌进行优化；测试时利用扩展的交互预算获得性能提升。

Result: Qwen3-14B在AppWorld测试正常与挑战集分别取得86.9和67.6分，超越公开榜单（2026年2月）；相同方法使Qwen3.5-9B在SWE-bench Verified上提升16.6分。证明仅通过代理强化学习即可将长程能力内化至小型开放模型。

Conclusion: 强化学习可直接将长程交互能力内化到小型开放语言模型中，无需依赖任务特定监督或复杂代理架构。CANOPY框架有效克服信号稀疏与策略漂移问题，为高效自主学习提供新路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a natural way to post-train LLM agents for long-horizon interactive tasks judged only by end-of-task verification, yet a shared belief holds that outcome-only RL soon hits a ceiling on small open models. Recent work therefore compensates around the training with denser rewards, SFT priors, skill libraries, curated memory, or multi-agent orchestration. We argue the ceiling is an artifact of two failures of common practice. Signal starvation: group-relative RL with sparse outcome-only rewards yields a gradient only when a task's rollout group mixes successes and failures, so under-scaled exploration silences exactly the hardest, most instructive tasks. Policy drift: squeezing many updates out of a small task pool degrades the policy itself, as an unanchored objective lets the sampling distribution collapse exactly when saturation has already made informative groups rare. We present CANOPY (Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL), a minimalist protocol attacking both directly: scale same-task exploration until the natural signal reappears, keep every update on-policy, KL-anchored, and confined to the agent's own action tokens, then cash in an enlarged interaction budget at test time. On AppWorld, a long-horizon interactive coding benchmark, a Qwen3-14B policy trained with CANOPY through environment interaction alone--without task-specific supervision, auxiliary credit signals, or elaborate agent scaffolding--topped the public leaderboard (Feb. 2026; Test-Normal TGC 86.9, Test-Challenge 67.6), and the same design principles lift Qwen3.5-9B on SWE-bench Verified by 16.6 points. Agentic RL alone thus internalizes long-horizon capability directly into a small open model; we plan to release the complete training stack at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/SignalCoverageRL.

</details>


### [278] [Solving In-Table Prediction Problems by Deep Neural Networks with Performance Evaluation Using Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01262)
*Xiao Zhao,Daniela Oelke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在表格数据中，通过深度神经网络（NN）预测任意选定列值的问题，称为表内预测（ITB）。与传统监督学习和表格插补不同，该方法采用自监督学习，随机掩码列作为目标进行训练。针对连续特征的表格数据，提出一种新型神经层以同时嵌入数值和空值，并通过合成数据生成测试集，评估MLP、ResNet和Transformer三种架构的表现。结果表明，在样本量充足且嵌入长度较大时，基于注意力机制的Transformer表现最佳。但研究基于受控的合成数据，仅涵盖少量列，因此仅为初步、局限性的探索，不能推广至真实世界表格数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格式深度学习方法多依赖显式目标特征进行监督学习，而本文旨在探索无需显式目标的自监督方式，即利用深度神经网络从表格内部列间关系中学习模式，从而实现对任一列的预测，为表格数据建模提供新视角。

Method: 提出一种自监督学习框架用于表内预测（ITB），通过随机掩码部分列并将其作为预测目标；设计一种新型神经层处理连续特征中的数值与空值；基于预定义的列间关系生成合成数据，并采用改进的掩码策略构建测试集；比较MLP、ResNet和Transformer三种网络结构在合成数据上的表现。

Result: 在足够大的训练样本和较大的嵌入维度下，基于注意力机制的Transformer模型显著优于MLP和ResNet；但在小样本或低维嵌入条件下，性能差距缩小。实验验证了自监督框架的有效性，但结果受限于合成数据环境。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统探讨了表内预测（ITB）问题，证明在特定条件下，注意力机制网络可有效捕捉表格列间复杂关系。然而，由于实验基于合成数据且列数有限，结论不适用于一般真实世界表格数据，未来需在真实数据上进一步验证。

Abstract: Tabular deep learning (TDL) leverages neural networks (NN) to extract patterns from tabular data. Traditional TDL methods follow a supervised learning paradigm, where a target feature is explicitly given. In this work, however, we explore a different approach by employing deep NNs to learn relationships among individual columns within a given table. We investigate whether NNs can predict the values of arbitrarily selected columns in a given table based on the remaining known columns. We call this problem In-Table Prediction (ITB), which is slightly different from table imputation methods and the pretraining task of TDL. Three potential usage scenarios are identified, which, to our best knowledge, have not been extensively studied in the literature. A self-supervised learning approach is applied to address this problem by randomly selecting columns to be masked out and used as learning targets. This work focuses on tabular datasets containing only continuous features. To handle missing values in continuous features, a novel neural layer is proposed to embed both numerical and empty values. Synthetic data is generated based on predefined column relationships, with empty values inserted using two distinct mechanisms. Additionally, an adapted masking strategy is employed to create test data. Performances of three NN architectures, namely MLP, Resnet and Transformer, are evaluated using the generated synthetic data. We conclude that, the attention-based structure outperforms the other two networks, when a sufficiently large number of training examples is available and a relatively large embedding length is chosen. We stress that these findings are obtained under controlled, synthetic conditions with a small number of columns and it should therefore be regarded as an initial, narrowly-scoped investigation rather than a general characterization of ITP on real-world tabular data.

</details>


### [279] [Position: Privacy Is a Claim, Not a Property of Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01273)
*Jiachen Zhao,Antonia Januszewicz,Taeho Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出，合成数据在机器学习研究中虽被广泛使用，但其在隐私敏感场景中的应用已从基于假设的推理风险声明，转变为仅凭数据生成方式外观推断的隐私属性。作者认为这反映了社区对隐私证据标准的隐性变化，而非对隐私原则的误解。通过分析主要机器学习会议的近期论文，发现合成数据常用于隐私敏感场景却缺乏明确威胁模型、推理风险或可验证的隐私声明。因此，隐私保障往往不透明、难以验证，且少数群体记录面临更高风险。论文主张将隐私视为明确、基于证据的科学主张，并建议学术会议建立规范，要求隐私相关声明必须清晰界定、可测试、可质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前合成数据在隐私敏感场景中的使用缺乏明确的隐私保障机制，导致隐私风险不可验证，尤其对少数群体构成更大威胁。作者旨在揭示这一问题背后的隐性标准变化，并推动建立更严格的隐私声明规范。

Method: 通过对主要机器学习会议的近期论文进行实证分析，考察合成数据在隐私敏感场景中的使用情况，评估其是否包含明确的威胁模型、推理风险和可验证的隐私声明。

Result: 发现合成数据在隐私敏感设置中广泛使用，但多数研究未明确说明威胁模型、推理风险或可验证的隐私主张，导致隐私保障隐含且不可靠，少数群体风险更高。

Conclusion: 应将隐私视为需明确、可验证、可反驳的科学主张，并建议机器学习会议采纳相应规范，以提升隐私声明的透明度与可信度。

Abstract: Synthetic data has become a common component of machine learning research. While widely adopted, its use in privacy-sensitive contexts has quietly shifted from a claim of residual inference risk under stated assumptions to an appearance-based property inferred from data generation itself. In this position paper, we argue that this shift reflects an implicit change in community standards for what counts as sufficient privacy evidence, rather than a misunderstanding of well-established privacy principles. Drawing on an empirical analysis of recent publications across major ML venues, we show that synthetic data is frequently used in privacy-sensitive settings without explicit articulation of threat models, inference risks, or falsifiable privacy claims. As a result, privacy assurance often remains implicit, difficult to verify, and unevenly distributed, with heightened exposure for rare and minority records. We argue for treating privacy as an explicit, evidence-based scientific claim and recommend that ML venues adopt norms requiring privacy-relevant assertions to be clearly scoped, testable, and contestable.

</details>


### [280] [The Constitutional Coverage Trilemma in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01275)
*Natalija Mitic,Soona Sedahmed A. O.,Mamadou Selly Ly,Moustapha Cisse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了前沿大模型在安全、帮助性、诚实性、自主性和公平性等价值维度上的配置是否满足人类需求。通过审计23种主流大模型的默认设置并调查1649名美国参与者的价值偏好，发现人类需求广泛覆盖五大价值，但模型供给狭窄且持续偏离需求，尤其在自主性和帮助性上严重不足，导致大量用户处于‘宪法无家可归’状态。提出仅需两个配置（诚实性和自主性）即可显著优于现有全部23种模型，证明了稀疏配置方案的有效性，并提出了预算内多元主义三难困境的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿大模型在设计中隐含了对安全、帮助性、诚实性、自主性和公平性等价值的排序，但这些排序是否能满足人类多样化的价值观需求尚不明确。随着大模型应用日益广泛，其价值取向与社会需求之间的错配可能带来系统性风险，因此亟需评估模型供给与人类需求的匹配程度。

Method: 结合基于改写控制的审计方法，分析23种主流大模型架构的默认价值观配置；同时开展针对1649名美国参与者的成对权衡实验，构建人类需求分布；通过几何方法估算供给与需求的重叠度，并评估不同配置组合的福利表现。

Result: 人类需求覆盖所有五项价值，但单个价值最多仅占三分之一人群；23种模型构成的供给空间仅覆盖需求空间约2%（保守估计），无一模型将帮助性或自主性置于首位；5/6模型家族中自主性下降，5/6中公平性上升，4/6中安全性上升，且趋势具有家族内部单调性；仅用两个配置（诚实性、自主性）的菜单即可使平均遗憾降低47%，增加三个顶点后，平均与最弱势群体的遗憾分别减少81%和64%。

Conclusion: 当前大模型的宪法配置未能充分响应人类需求，存在显著的供给-需求错配。解决这一问题的关键不在于增加模型种类，而在于设计更精炼、更具代表性的多配置菜单。研究揭示了预算内多元主义三难困境的现实可行性，并验证结论在多种福利衡量和路由机制下的稳健性。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems function as \emph{constitutional institutions}: each deployed model encodes an implicit ranking among safety, helpfulness, honesty, autonomy, and equity. We ask whether the supply of frontier constitutional types covers human demand. Combining a paraphrase-controlled audit of the as-shipped default constitutions of $23$ frontier LLM archetypes with a pairwise-tradeoff study of $1{,}649$ US participants on the same instrument, we report three facts. \emph{Demand is broad}: it spans all five values, with the largest constituency under one-third. \emph{Supply is narrow and drifting}: the $23$-archetype hull occupies ${\sim}2\%$ of the demand hull under conservative noise-matched estimation ($0.10\%$ at full audit precision), no archetype puts helpfulness or autonomy first ($37\%$ of users are constitutionally homeless), and across six model families autonomy decreases in $5/6$, equity increases in $5/6$, and safety increases in $4/6$, with monotone within-family version trends (order-permutation $p = 0.013$) and the autonomy decline concentrated in scenarios where safety is not at stake. The drift's importance is directional: \emph{away} from a value already undercovered, mechanically worsening the welfare floor for the least-served users. \emph{The fix is sparse}: a $2$-vertex menu $\{e_{\mathrm{HON}}, e_{\mathrm{AUT}}\}$ beats the full $23$-archetype frontier by $47\%$ on mean regret (CI $[43\%, 52\%]$); three vertex additions cut mean/worst-group regret by up to $81\%$/$64\%$. We formalize these findings as a budgeted-pluralism trilemma, show the binding regime is empirically realized, and verify the conclusions are robust to distance-based welfare and to degraded routing. The instrument and audit harness are described in full in the appendices.

</details>


### [281] [One-Layer Transformer Provably Learns Multiclass One-Nearest Neighbor in Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01311)
*Skanda Athreya,Yutong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将一層Transformer與最近鄰分類器在二分設定下的等價性結果擴展到多類情況，利用單純形編碼證明了一層Transformer搭配argmax分類頭在多類設定下行為上等同於一近鄰分類器，解決了先前研究在多類情形中依賴非標準四捨五入方法的缺憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究在多類設定中未能使用實際應用中常見的argmax分類頭，而是採用非標準的四捨五入方法，導致理論與實務之間存在差距，因此需要建立更符合實際的等價關係。

Method: 透過單純形編碼技術，將多類問題轉化為幾何空間中的向量表示，並分析一層Transformer在該表示下的輸出行為，證明其與一近鄰分類器在多類情境下完全一致。

Result: 一層Transformer搭配argmax分類頭在多類設定下與一近鄰分類器具有完全相同的行為，證實了理論上的等價性，且不依賴非標準處理方式。

Conclusion: 本研究成功將一層Transformer與最近鄰分類器的等價性從二分推廣至多類，並使用標準的argmax分類頭，彌補了以往理論與實際應用之間的落差，強化了對Transformer結構的理論理解。

Abstract: We extend recent work establishing an equivalence between one-layer transformers and nearest-neighbor classifiers in the binary setting to the multiclass case. By leveraging the simplex encoding, we show that one-layer transformers with an argmax classification head behave identically to a one-nearest-neighbor classifier in the multiclass setting. This closes a gap left by prior work, whose multiclass result relied on a non-standard rounding-based approach rather than the typical argmax head used in practice.

</details>


### [282] [Bandits in Prod: Hyperparameter Optimization at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01335)
*Louis Abraham,Tuan-Anh Nguyen,Nicolas Devatine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在无代表验证数据的生产系统中，通过实时请求评估配置的在线超参数优化（OHPO）问题，将其建模为混合与条件搜索空间中的无限多臂老虎机问题。提出IMABO框架，结合任意选择策略与提议新配置的预言机，采用无需重启的IMOSS策略，其活跃集随$ t^β $增长，并证明了累积分位数后悔上界为$ O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2}) $。实验中结合三种实用预言机（TPE、基于坐标带子的当前最优变异、预训练表格基础模型），均优于随机基线，在多种场景下实现最低累积后悔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生产系统无法依赖代表性验证数据，只能通过在真实请求上运行并观察噪声反馈来评估配置，如大模型代理中的推理时决策（模型选择、检索深度、提示策略等）。现有方法难以应对复杂的混合与条件搜索空间，亟需一种高效的在线超参数优化框架。

Method: 将OHPO形式化为无限多臂老虎机问题，设计通用框架IMABO，融合任意选择策略与新配置提议预言机；引入无重启的IMOSS策略，其活跃集以$ t^β $速度增长，并利用分位数后悔分析理论证明性能边界。结合三种实际预言机：树状帕尔岑估计器（TPE）、坐标带子驱动的当前最优变异、预训练表格基础模型。

Result: IMABO在多种OHPO任务中表现优异，包括经典机器学习模型调优和大语言模型代理配置，显著降低累积后悔，优于随机基线和其他基准方法。三种预言机均有效提升性能，其中预训练基础模型尤其高效。

Conclusion: IMABO框架提供了一种灵活且高效的在线超参数优化解决方案，适用于复杂、动态的生产环境。通过合理设计活跃集增长策略与预言机，可在缺乏验证数据的情况下实现快速收敛与低后悔，对构建自适应智能系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Many production systems can assess a configuration only by using it on live requests and observing noisy feedback. Modern agentic systems are a prominent example, with inference-time choices such as model selection, retrieval depth, prompting strategy, and decoding temperature, yet often with no representative validation data. We formalize this setting as Online Hyperparameter Optimization (OHPO) and cast it as an infinitely many-armed bandit over mixed and conditional search spaces. We introduce IMABO, a general framework that combines any bandit policy for choosing among already sampled configurations with any oracle for proposing new ones. We instantiate it with IMOSS, a restart-free anytime policy whose active set grows as $t^β$, and prove an expected cumulative quantile-regret bound of $O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2})$, where $β\in(0,1)$ controls active-set growth and $p_ρ$ lower-bounds the probability that a proposed configuration falls in the top-$ρ$ fraction of the search space. We combine IMOSS with three practical oracles: a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator, an incumbent-mutation oracle driven by a per-coordinate bandit, and a pretrained tabular foundation model, all three improving over the uniform random oracle baseline. IMABO obtains the lowest cumulative regret across diverse OHPO settings, from tuning classical machine-learning models to configuring LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [283] [SMELT: Scaling Laws for Compute-Matched MoE Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01343)
*Shaowen Wang,Ge Zhang,Kairong Luo,Yuhao Wu,Shaofan Liu,Jiaheng Liu,Wenhao Huang,Shen Yan,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Looped Transformers enhance effective depth by iterating shared layers, but prior evaluations often conflated architectural benefits with increased FLOPs. This paper studies looping in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) Transformers under strict budget matching—per-token FLOPs, non-embedding parameters, and KV cache. Through ablation, the authors propose SMELT (Sparse MoE Transformer, middle layers loop twice), which loops the middle half of layers twice while matching a baseline on all three budgets. Scaling SMELT up to 54B parameters and fitting Chinchilla-style scaling laws reveals that SMELT reduces training FLOPs by 6.8–18.0% at compute-optimal points. Gains extend beyond validation loss to downstream tasks, especially in code generation, and increase with sequence length and in-context examples. Mechanistic analysis shows the second loop reduces attention sink effects and redirects attention mass toward relevant tokens, indicating an inductive bias that drives performance. The study demonstrates that looping can yield measurable gains even under tight budget constraints.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To isolate the true architectural benefit of looping in Transformers by eliminating confounding factors like extra FLOPs, parameters, and KV cache usage, enabling fair comparison between looped and unlooped models.

Method: The authors conduct controlled experiments on MoE Transformers with precise matching of per-token FLOPs, total non-embedding parameters, and KV cache size. They perform ablation studies to identify optimal looping patterns and introduce SMELT: looping the middle half of layers twice. They scale SMELT across four sizes up to 54B parameters and fit Chinchilla-style scaling laws for each architecture to evaluate compute efficiency.

Result: SMELT achieves lower loss with less compute compared to the baseline, saving 6.8–18.0% of training FLOPs on the compute-optimal frontier. Performance gains transfer to downstream tasks, particularly in code generation, and grow with longer sequences and more in-context examples. Mechanistic analysis confirms reduced attention sink and improved focus on content-relevant tokens.

Conclusion: Looping can provide meaningful performance improvements in Transformers even when all computational and memory budgets are matched. SMELT offers a practical, scalable recipe for depth reuse that delivers real efficiency gains and stronger inductive biases, demonstrating that architectural innovation can outperform mere parameter scaling.

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by iterating a shared block of layers, but most evaluations compare at fixed model size, conflating architectural advantage with extra FLOPs. We study looping on Mixture-of-Experts Transformers while closely matching per-token FLOPs, total non-embedding parameters, and KV cache. Through a series of ablations, we arrive at a recipe we call SMELT (Sparse MoE Transformer, middle layers Loop Twice), which loops the middle half of layers twice while matching the unlooped Baseline on all three budgets. We scale SMELT across four sizes up to 54B non-embedding parameters and fit a separate Chinchilla-style scaling law for each architecture. SMELT's loss drops faster with compute, saving 6.8--18.0\% of training FLOPs on the compute-optimal frontier. The advantage transfers to downstream benchmarks beyond what validation loss predicts, is largest on Code, and grows with sample length and the number of in-context examples. Mechanistic analysis shows that the second visit reduces the attention sink and redirects mass toward content-relevant tokens, an inductive bias that may underlie the observed performance gains. These results show that looping can improve Transformers even under budget matching, offering a practical recipe that turns depth reuse into measurable gains.

</details>


### [284] [Contribution-Aware Bandwidth Allocation for Multimodal Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01406)
*Iason Ofeidis,Leandros Tassiulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种名为ModalShare的新方法，用于在多模态边缘计算中优化数据上传的资源分配。通过基于Shapley值的贡献度评估，动态调整不同模态的保留比例（keep-ratio），实现更优的压缩与性能平衡。相比传统等比例分配方式，在相同上传预算下显著提升模型准确率，尤其在CREMA-D和MVSA数据集上分别提升15.4%和12.4%，且对多种压缩器、数据集和预算均表现出鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型训练依赖数据中心，而边缘设备难以承载多模态编码器；虽然Split Learning允许将部分计算留在设备端，但其上行传输负担大，且现有压缩方案对各模态采用相同的保留比例，导致资源分配不合理，未能考虑各模态对最终预测的实际贡献。因此需要一种更智能的跨模态资源分配机制。

Method: 提出ModalShare框架，利用服务器端对已接收激活值集合进行Shapley值分析，量化每个模态对预测结果的边际贡献，并据此动态设定各模态的keep-ratio。该过程不增加上行流量或客户端计算开销，也不依赖模态标签信息。

Result: 在5倍压缩比下，相较于等比例保留策略，ModalShare在CREMA-D和MVSA数据集上分别提升了15.4%和12.4%的准确率；在三种压缩器、三个数据集和四种上传预算设置下均表现优异，证明了其有效性与泛化能力。同时发现现有压缩器在多模态场景下性能不佳，而ModalShare可有效恢复被浪费的性能增益。

Conclusion: ModalShare通过引入基于贡献度的跨模态资源分配机制，显著提升了多模态边缘学习中的压缩效率与模型精度。该方法无需额外通信开销，具备良好的适应性，为高效多模态边缘计算提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly the default option for perception at the network edge, yet they are trained almost entirely in the datacenter, because a client holding several sensor streams cannot host an encoder per modality. Split Learning makes such training feasible by keeping only the first layers on the device, at the cost of an uplink that must carry smashed activations for every modality at every step. Existing compression schemes give each modality the same keep-ratio, so the shared budget is divided in proportion to smashed-activation dimension, a quantity unrelated to how much each modality contributes to the fused prediction. We make that division an explicit decision and call it inter-modality allocation: under a fixed uplink budget, every policy transmits the same expected payload and differs only in how that payload is split across modalities. Our allocator, ModalShare, sets each modality's keep-ratio from a Shapley contribution score that the server computes over coalitions of activations it has already received. Measuring this score adds no uplink traffic and no client-side computation, and needs no prior knowledge of which stream is which. ModalShare improves accuracy over equal keep-ratios by 15.4 and 12.4 percentage points on CREMA-D and MVSA at matched payload in 5x compression, with strong performance across three compressors, three datasets, and four budgets. We show that existing compressors underperform in multimodal settings, with ModalShare recovering what gains are left behind.

</details>


### [285] [Predicting Subsurface Abnormalities Growth using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01417)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Hoda Azari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的GPR数据预测框架，结合CNN、空间特征通道注意力（SFCA）、ConvLSTM与时间特征帧注意力（TFFA）模块，通过自适应计算通道与时间注意力权重，提升对地质或结构中电磁波传播特性的建模能力。模型将物理规律嵌入深度学习架构，显著提高预测精度，助力桥梁等基础设施状态评估，推动非破坏性检测（NDE）向更精准、可解释的方向发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GPR数据分析依赖经验模型，难以准确反映地下介质的物理特性；同时，现有深度学习方法缺乏对电磁波传播物理规律的约束，导致预测结果可能违背物理实际。为提升预测准确性与可解释性，需融合物理先验知识与数据驱动方法，以实现更可靠的基础设施健康监测。

Method: 构建一个融合卷积神经网络（CNN）、空间特征通道注意力（SFCA）、ConvLSTM及时间特征帧注意力（TFFA）的PINN模型，通过自适应机制动态调整视觉与时间特征的权重，将电磁波传播的物理方程作为损失函数的一部分进行约束，确保模型输出符合物理规律。

Result: 实验表明，该模型在多个真实和合成的GPR数据集上表现出更高的预测精度，尤其在复杂地质条件下的异常区域识别能力显著优于传统深度学习模型，且生成结果具备良好的物理一致性。

Conclusion: 将物理信息神经网络引入GPR数据处理是可行且高效的，不仅能提升预测性能，还能增强模型的可解释性，为基础设施健康监测提供可靠工具，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: The research explores the pioneering integration of Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) into the domain of Ground-Penetrating Radar (GPR) data prediction. This research presents a detailed development framework for a specialized PINN model, proficient at interpreting and forecasting GPR data, much like how medical imaging models predict tumor behavior. By harnessing the synergy between deep learning algorithms and the physical laws governing subsurface structures or in medical terms, human tissues the model effectively embeds the physics of electromagnetic wave propagation into its architecture. This ensures that predictions not only align with fundamental physical principles but also mirror the precision needed in medical diagnostics for detecting and monitoring tumors. The suggested deep learning structure comprises three components: a CNN, a spatial feature channel attention (SFCA) mechanism, and ConvLSTM, along with temporal feature frame attention (TFFA) modules. The attention mechanism computes channel attention and temporal attention weights using self-adaptation, thereby fine tuning the visual and temporal feature responses to extract the most pertinent and significant visual and temporal features. By integrating physics directly into the neural network, our model has shown enhanced accuracy in forecasting GPR data. This improvement is vital for conducting effective assessments of bridge deck conditions and other evaluations related to civil infrastructure. The use of Physics Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) has demonstrated the potential to transform the field of Non-Destructive Evaluation (NDE) by enhancing the precision of infrastructure deterioration predictions. Moreover, it offers a deeper insight into the fundamental mechanisms of deterioration, viewed through the prism of physics-based models.

</details>


### [286] [Provably Safe Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01418)
*Tingting Ni,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在模拟器与现实世界差异的情况下，如何安全地从模拟环境迁移到真实环境中的强化学习问题。提出了一种基于无奖励安全强化学习框架的方法，设计了一个计算高效的算法，在保证探索安全的前提下，利用模拟器信息显著减少真实世界交互次数，并能为任意潜在奖励函数计算出接近最优的可行策略。该方法通过理论分析证明了使用模拟器对降低真实世界样本复杂度的贡献，其效果取决于模拟器与真实系统的差距大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界中进行强化学习往往面临高样本成本和安全约束，而直接从模拟器迁移策略可能因模拟与现实之间的差异导致性能下降。因此需要一种能够在保障安全的前提下，有效利用模拟器信息来减少真实世界数据收集量并学习到近似最优策略的安全迁移方法。

Method: 将安全的模拟到现实迁移问题建模为奖励自由的安全强化学习问题，设计了一种利用模拟器信息以减少真实世界交互的高效算法，确保安全探索，并能够针对任意潜在奖励函数计算出近似最优的可行策略。

Result: 所提算法在理论上证明了其能够有效降低真实世界的样本复杂度，且该降低程度与模拟器与真实系统间的差异相关；同时保证了探索过程的安全性，并可获得近似最优的可行策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法通过合理利用模拟器信息，在保障安全的前提下显著减少了真实世界的数据收集需求，实现了高效且安全的模拟到现实迁移，为机器人、医疗等高风险领域中的强化学习应用提供了理论支持和技术路径。

Abstract: To mitigate the sample complexity of real-world reinforcement learning (RL), a common practice is to first train a policy in a simulator, where samples are cheap, and then deploy the learned policy in the real world with the hope that it generalizes effectively. Such direct sim-to-real transfer is not guaranteed to succeed: simulator-trained policies can be suboptimal in the real world due to sim-to-real mismatch. Correcting this mismatch requires collecting data from the real system, but in many applications, such as robotics and healthcare, this data-collection process is itself subject to safety constraints. This gives rise to the problem of safe sim-to-real transfer: how can an agent exploit an imperfect simulator while ensuring safe real-world data collection and learning a near-optimal feasible policy for the target system? We address this problem by formulating safe sim-to-real transfer within the framework of reward-free safe RL. We design a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits simulator information to provably reduce real-world interaction while ensuring safe exploration and enabling the computation of a near-optimal feasible policy for any potential reward function. Our real-world sample complexity bound characterizes the benefit of using the simulator in terms of the sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [287] [CATeye: Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning for Voucher Abuse Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01425)
*Tian Tian,Shuaicheng Niu,Hao Kuang,Yuanhang Hu,Dong Li,Zhiqi Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CATeye提出一种新的图神经网络框架，用于应对电商中促销券滥用的动态欺诈检测问题。针对属性与拓扑结构耦合漂移导致模型性能下降的挑战，通过两个可学习选择器（AIS和EIS）分别过滤非不变属性和采样不变子图，构建多视图表示以增强域不变特征并抑制特定域差异。在Lazada及公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著优于现有基线，最高提升8.61%的平均F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商中的促销券滥用行为随时间和区域快速演变，导致数据分布漂移，现有检测模型难以适应，需频繁重训练。传统方法未能有效处理属性与拓扑结构之间的耦合漂移问题，影响图神经网络的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出耦合属性-拓扑不变性学习框架CATeye，包含两个核心组件：(1) 属性不变性选择器（AIS），通过节点自适应掩码筛选出非变化属性；(2) 边不变性选择器（EIS），基于保留的不变属性采样不变子图并剔除非不变边。结合生成的不变与非不变组件构建多视图，并采用视图特定目标函数强化域不变表示，抑制领域特异性噪声。

Result: 在Lazada自有数据集和公开基准数据集上，CATeye均显著优于九种主流域泛化与图异常检测基线方法，平均F1分数最高提升达8.61%，验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CATeye有效缓解了属性与拓扑耦合漂移带来的负面影响，通过显式建模不变特征提升图神经网络在动态欺诈检测任务中的泛化能力，为复杂环境下的在线安全防护提供了新思路。

Abstract: Voucher abuse poses a major challenge in e-commerce, where malicious users exploit promotional vouchers for profit. Unfortunately, fraud patterns evolve rapidly over time and across regions, causing distribution shifts that degrade existing detection models unless retrained frequently. To tackle this, we propose the Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning framework (CATeye). The key challenge arises from coupled attribute-topology shift, where edges built from attribute proximity cause environment-driven attribute shift to induce shifted topology, thereby amplifying variant signals through GNN message passing. CATeye sees through such coupled shifts with two learnable selectors. First, an Attribute Invariance Selector (AIS) learns node-adaptive masks to filter out non-invariant attributes. Then, conditioned on retained invariant attributes, an Edge Invariance Selector (EIS) samples an invariant subgraph and isolates non-invariant edges. Using the resulting invariant and non-invariant components, CATeye constructs multiple views and applies view-specific objectives to emphasize domain-invariant representations while suppressing domain-specific variations. Experiments on both a proprietary dataset from Lazada, a major Southeast Asian e-commerce platform, and a public benchmark show that CATeye consistently outperforms nine strong domain generalization and graph anomaly detection baselines, achieving up to an 8.61% improvement in average F1 score over the strongest baseline. Source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tian0426/CATeye.

</details>


### [288] [TRIAGE: Three-level Routing and Intelligent Agent Guidance for Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01428)
*Ruocan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIAGE是一个三层次路由框架，通过将历史执行轨迹抽象为可复用的技能（TaaS），显著降低大语言模型代理在ReAct范式下的令牌消耗。它将查询分为三类：直接复用（零开销）、技能替换（零开销）和完整ReAct（新查询）。在1,007个安全监控查询上实现62.3%的令牌节省，跨域验证在ToolBench上达76.3%减少，且支持在线学习与技能自动提取，形成效率正向循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct范式虽然强大，但每次查询都从头开始推理，重复步骤无法复用历史经验，导致效率低下。需要一种机制来高效利用过往执行轨迹，减少冗余计算。

Method: 提出TRIAGE框架，引入TaaS（轨迹即技能）概念，将历史执行路径抽象为可复用的技能；设计三层次路由策略：直接复用、技能替换、全量ReAct；结合自动技能提取机制，实现经验积累与系统优化的闭环。

Result: 在1,007个安全监控任务中实现62.3%的令牌节省，其中56.0%的查询通过技能替换零成本处理，5.5%为直接复用；在15个领域的ToolBench测试中实现76.3%的令牌减少；在线学习实验显示，系统在前100次查询内命中率从0%升至57%，平均令牌成本从198降至74.7。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过经验复用与技能抽象，实现了大语言模型代理在工具使用中的高效进化，具备显著的性能提升与广泛适用性，为构建自适应智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents based on the ReAct paradigm have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tool use and task execution. However, ReAct suffers from a fundamental efficiency problem: every query triggers a complete reasoning loop from scratch, and similar queries repeat identical steps without leveraging historical experience. We propose TRIAGE,a three-level routing framework that reduces token consumption by reusing historical execution trajectories. Its core innovation is TaaS (Trajectory-as-a-Skill), which abstracts historical execution trajectories into reusable skills, realizing 'experience as a service'. TRIAGE classifies queries into three levels: (1) Direct Reuse-identical queries, 0 tokens; (2) Skill Substitution-similar queries, 0 tokens via deterministic parameter substitution; (3) Full ReAct-novel queries, automatically stored for future reuse. In large-scale experiments on 1,007 security monitoring queries, TRIAGE achieves 62.3% token savings, with 56.0% of queries at Level 2 and 5.5% at Level 1, both executing at zero cost. Cross-domain validation on ToolBench (15 domains, 345 queries) achieves 76.3% token reduction, confirming the generalizability of semantic routing. An online learning experiment demonstrates cold-start-to-mature evolution: the L2 hit rate rises from 0% to 57% within the first 100 queries, and the average token cost drops from 198 to 74.7. We also propose an automatic Skill extraction mechanism that distills high-frequency trajectory patterns into deterministic Skills, creating a positive feedback loop of 'the more you use it, the more efficient it becomes'.

</details>


### [289] [Learning Sparse Decision Trees via Transformer Variational Auto-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01430)
*Giacomo Fidone,Alessio Cascione,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREVIS 是一种基于树变换变分自编码器（TTVAE）潜在空间探索的决策树学习方法，通过将决策树映射到潜在表示，将离散搜索空间转化为连续空间，实现基于梯度的优化。该方法在保持预测性能的同时，显著提升了决策树的结构稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策树学习算法主要关注预测性能，忽视了结构稀疏性等其他重要属性的联合优化。为解决这一问题，提出一种能够同时优化多种目标的新方法。

Method: 利用树变换变分自编码器（TTVAE）将决策树映射到潜在空间，构建可微分的代理模型，从而在连续空间中进行梯度优化，替代传统的离散搜索。

Result: 实验表明，TREVIS 学习得到的决策树在预测性能上达到现有近优算法水平，同时显著提高了结构稀疏性。

Conclusion: TREVIS 为决策树的多目标优化提供了一种有效框架，兼顾预测性能与结构简洁性，适用于对透明性和可解释性要求高的高风险决策场景。

Abstract: Decision trees are among the most widely used models in machine learning, largely due to their transparent decision logic, making them well-suited for high-stakes decision-making contexts. However, most existing learning algorithms focus on predictive performance, overlooking the joint optimization of other desirable properties, such as structural sparsity. In this work we propose TREVIS, an approach for learning decision trees with respect to complex objectives, based on the exploration of the latent space of a Tree Transformer Variational Auto-Encoder (TTVAE). By mapping decision trees onto latent representations, TREVIS replaces the discrete search space with a continuous one, enabling gradient-based optimization via a differentiable surrogate model. We experiment with TREVIS for learning decision trees that jointly optimize predictive performance and sparsity. Results show that TREVIS discovers decision trees matching the predictive performance of existing near-optimal algorithms while improving their structural sparsity.

</details>


### [290] [Efficiently Estimating Optimal Hyperparameter Scaling Laws through Power-Law Entropy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01431)
*Zhiliang Chen,Sebastian Ament,David Eriksson,Maximilian Balandat,Eytan Bakshy,Jihao Andreas Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES)的新方法，用于高效估计大规模语言模型（LLM）训练中最佳超参数的缩放规律。传统方法需进行大量计算密集型网格搜索，而PLES通过多保真度贝叶斯优化，以计算成本为权重，自适应选择能最大程度降低缩放规律不确定性且成本最低的实验配置，显著减少计算开销。在合成基准、代理模型和真实LLM预训练任务中，PLES仅用不到传统方法十分之一的计算资源即达到高精度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法估算最优超参数缩放规律需要大量计算资源的网格搜索，效率低下，亟需一种更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出基于多保真度贝叶斯优化的计算成本感知获取函数PLES，通过选择每单位成本下最能降低缩放规律不确定性的实验配置，实现自适应实验设计。

Result: 在多种评估场景中，PLES在计算成本低于传统方法1/10的情况下，准确收敛到最优超参数缩放规律。

Conclusion: PLES是一种高效、低成本的方法，能够精准估计大模型训练中的最优超参数缩放规律，显著降低对大规模调优计算资源的需求。

Abstract: Optimal hyperparameter scaling laws describe how the best hyperparameters for large language model (LLM) training change with model and data scale, enabling practitioners to predict optimal configurations at production scales without expensive large-scale tuning. However, estimating these scaling laws conventionally requires exhaustive grid searches over thousands of training runs, consuming enormous computational resources. We introduce Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES), a computational cost-aware acquisition function built on multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization that efficiently estimates optimal hyperparameter scaling laws through adaptive experimentation. A key innovation in PLES is that it searches for candidates that reduce the overall uncertainty of a scaling law estimate, instead of optimizing a single objective function. At each iteration, PLES selects the candidate configuration that maximally reduces the uncertainty of the scaling law estimates per unit computational cost, naturally favoring informative small-scale experiments. We evaluate PLES on synthetic benchmarks, surrogate models fitted to real LLM training data, and actual LLM pre-training runs. Across all settings, PLES converges to accurate optimal hyperparameter scaling laws using less than one-tenth of the computational budget required by conventional grid search and other baselines.

</details>


### [291] [Edge-Girth as a Structural Edge Feature for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01441)
*Lilian Marey,Charlotte Laclau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于边环长（edge-girth）和其多重性的结构描述符，用于增强图神经网络（GNN）的表达能力。该描述符通过单次广度优先搜索即可精确计算，无需预先指定子图大小，克服了传统固定大小子图计数方法的局限性。在ZINC-12k回归任务中，引入该描述符的EGAGNN模型在104k参数下表现显著优于同类模型，且仅需一半通道数即可达到与最多计数至8阶循环的字典相当的效果；而在图区分任务中，证明该方法仍受限于1-WL，当所有边的最短环长度与数量相同时，描述符变为常数，导致模型退化为1-WL能力。在BREC基准测试的400对图中，无一能被区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于消息传递的图神经网络（GNN）表达能力受限于1维Weisfeiler-Leman测试（1-WL），无法区分某些结构不同的图。虽然通过注入预计算的子图计数（如三角形、环等）可提升性能，但这类方法需事先确定子图大小，选择往往不依赖数据，存在局限性。本文旨在提出一种无需预设子图大小、可自动捕捉任意长度环结构的新型结构描述符，以增强GNN的表达能力。

Method: 提出边环长（edge-girth）及其多重性作为每条边的结构不变量，通过一次广度优先搜索（BFS）精确计算。将该描述符嵌入到门控消息传递架构中，构建EGAGNN模型。使用该描述符在多个基准上评估其在回归与图区分任务中的表现，并进行理论分析，揭示其在特定情况下的表达能力边界。

Result: 在ZINC-12k回归任务中，EGAGNN在104k参数下达到比最接近的门控对比模型低3倍的均方误差（MAE）。在图区分任务中，尽管在多数情况下优于传统方法，但在所有400对BREC基准图中，无法区分那些每条边具有相同最短环长度和数量的图对，表明其表达能力受限于1-WL。此外，该描述符仅用两倍通道数即达到计数至8阶环的字典效果，而计数至4阶的字典则无明显优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的边环长与多重性描述符有效提升了图神经网络的表达能力，尤其在回归任务中表现优异，但其理论上限仍受制于1-WL。当图中所有边的最短环结构一致时，该描述符失去区分能力，模型退化回原始消息传递框架。因此，该方法虽避免了预设子图大小的缺陷，但仍无法突破1-WL的理论限制，未来需探索更丰富的全局或局部拓扑信息来进一步提升。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNN) based on message passing are provably no more powerful than the one-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman colour-refinement test (1-WL): two graphs it cannot tell apart receive identical representations, however deep or wide the network. A common remedy augments node or edge features with precomputed structural descriptors, most often counts of a fixed small subgraph such as triangles or longer cycles, but such counts require committing in advance to the size of the substructure counted, a choice usually made blind to the data. We study a descriptor that avoids this choice. The edge-girth of an edge is the length of a shortest cycle through it, and its multiplicity is the number of such shortest cycles; together they form a per-edge invariant that reports cycles of arbitrary length, computable exactly by a single breadth-first search per edge. Injected into a gated message-passing architecture, EGAGNN, it reaches a test MAE a factor three below the closest gated comparator on the ZINC-12k regression benchmark at 104k parameters; against bounded cycle-counting descriptors under the same architecture, it matches only a dictionary counting cycles up to length eight, using twice as many channels, while a dictionary capped at length four performs no better than no structural information at all. On graph discrimination we prove a matching limitation: on graphs where every edge sees the same number of shortest cycles of the same length, the descriptor becomes constant and any model built on it collapses back to the 1-WL bound. This holds without exception across all 400 pairs of the BREC benchmark: not one of the 90 such pairs is distinguished.

</details>


### [292] [Diffusion as a Training Curriculum for Timestep-Free Iterative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01449)
*Mariia Drozdova,Aidan Sirbu,Pietro Miotti,Robert Obryk,Mayalen Etcheverry,Eyvind Niklasson,Blake Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散模型的去噪器与递归推理结合，通过引入持久隐藏状态并移除时间步条件，构建了一个可任意深度运行的即时求解器。该方法在Sudoku-Extreme上达到99.90%的精确求解率，在Maze-Unique上达到98.93%。令人惊讶的是，推理时无需逐步去噪，仅通过每步注入最大噪声即可高效探索解空间并收敛至正确解。训练中仍需有序退火噪声以维持学习效果，表明扩散模型的核心贡献在于训练阶段的去噪课程，而非推理时的采样过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型和递归推理虽均为迭代过程，但信息传递机制不同；如何使扩散模型在推理中具备持续提升精度的能力，并避免复杂结构如并行轨迹、候选选择或外部验证器，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 在扩散去噪器中引入持久隐藏状态，移除时间步条件，使其更新规则共享且可无限迭代；推理时采用固定最大噪声注入策略，不进行逐步去噪；训练阶段仍使用有序退火噪声以保证学习效果。

Result: 在Sudoku-Extreme任务上实现99.90%的精确求解率，在Maze-Unique任务上实现98.93%的求解率；即使在无渐进去噪的情况下，也能稳定收敛至正确解；证明了噪声注入机制的有效性及训练阶段扩散课程的重要性。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在本工作中主要贡献于训练阶段的去噪课程，而非推理阶段的采样过程；通过简单噪声注入与共享更新机制，实现了高效的单轨迹解空间探索与高精度求解，为构建任意深度推理模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models and recursive reasoners are both iterative, but they carry information across iterations differently. We add a persistent hidden state to a diffusion denoiser and remove its timestep conditioning, leaving a single shared update that can be run to arbitrary depth. The result is an anytime solver: accuracy keeps improving with inference depth far beyond the rollout lengths and backpropagation window used in training, reaching 99.90% exact solve on Sudoku-Extreme. We also obtain 98.93% solve rate on Maze-Unique. Surprisingly, progressive denoising is unnecessary at inference: holding corruption at its maximum by replacing every non-clue variable with fresh Gaussian noise at each step retains near-perfect solving and converges to stable solutions. This simple noise-injection mechanism enables a single trajectory to efficiently explore the solution space and settle on the correct answer without parallel rollouts, candidate selection, or external verifiers required by prior reasoning models. Nonetheless, ordered annealed corruption remains critical during training, which suggests that diffusion's primary contribution to our anytime solver is not a sampling procedure at inference, but a denoising training curriculum.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperWorld研究了文本环境中状态序列化对学习符号世界模型的影响，比较了原始观测与三种符号化表示（独立句子、成对三元组、以实体为中心的超边单元）。结果表明，超边表示在中小规模模型、分布外测试和规划任务中表现最佳，尤其在有限模型容量或环境差异时具有显著优势，证明高阶状态组织是一种有效且简单的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索状态序列化结构在文本环境中的作用，提升语言模型代理对环境动态的预测与规划能力，尤其是在符号动作效果学习中。

Method: 设计并对比四种状态表示：原始观测、独立句子、成对三元组、实体中心超边单元；所有变体使用相同训练目标：给定状态和动作，预测符号效应或判断动作不可行。

Result: 超边表示在0.5B–1.5B模型上表现最优，尤其在分布外测试中提升明显；大模型缩小差距，成对三元组在分布内匹配率可接近或略超超边，但超边在事实F1和可行性检测上更优；下游贪心规划中成功率最高。

Conclusion: 高阶状态组织（如超边表示）是一种简单但有效的归纳偏置，显著提升符号世界模型在模型容量受限或环境分布变化下的性能，尤其适用于实际应用中的鲁棒性需求。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [294] [I-CARE: Analysis of interference-related phenomena in a controllable, diverse and representative unlearning setting for text-to-image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00003)
*Leonardo Santiago Benitez Pereira,Marcos Escudero Viñolo,Luis Herranz Arribas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出I-CARE，一种用于系统研究生成式机器遗忘中概念干扰的框架。该框架通过形式化定义任务、度量标准和报告模板，使干扰现象可被一致评估与复现，推动该领域的科学进展。尽管不提供新算法或基准，但通过实证演示验证了其有效性，并开源了配套工具与可视化界面，便于广泛使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式机器遗忘研究中，对本应保留的语义相关概念在遗忘过程中意外退化的干扰问题缺乏系统性定义与评估方法，导致研究结果难以比较与复现。

Method: 提出I-CARE框架，包含对干扰现象的形式化定义、标准化的任务设定、度量指标及结果报告模板，支持跨场景、跨算法的可重复分析。

Result: 在多个主流算法与数据集上验证了I-CARE的有效性，能够揭示不同遗忘设置下的干扰模式，具备实际应用价值。

Conclusion: I-CARE为生成式机器遗忘中的干扰研究提供了可扩展、可复现的标准化方法，有助于推动该领域从经验性实验向系统性科学演进。

Abstract: Machine unlearning studies the removal of knowledge from an AI model, making the system forget a concept it previously learned. Despite rapid progress in generative machine unlearning, the unintended degradation of semantically related concepts that should have been retained (henceforth, interference) remains poorly characterized and inconsistently evaluated. This paper introduces I-CARE, a methodology that formalizes interference as a first-class object of study in generative unlearning. Rather than proposing a new benchmark or unlearning algorithm, I-CARE provides formal definitions for tasks, metrics, and templates for reporting results, enabling the systematic and reproducible study of interference across unlearning settings. While our methodology is designed to remain valid as models and unlearning algorithms evolve, decoupling long-term scientific insight from transient empirical results, we present a feasibility demonstration with state-of-the-art algorithms and frequently used datasets. The results demonstrate that I-CARE enables meaningful analysis of interference patterns across multiple unlearning settings, establishing the practical applicability of the framework. The software implementation of the methodology is provided in an open-source framework, together with a web-based graphical interface that enables exploration of the outcomes of this study without requiring direct interaction with the codebase or specialized data analysis tools.

</details>


### [295] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究针对具有确定需求量但需求到达时间随机的有限周期多物品容量约束生产计划问题，提出基于离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP）的建模方法，并通过遗传算法（GA）求解。对比确定性模型发现，随机到达时间显著增加状态数、转移数、求解时间和内存压力。实验表明，GA在330个基准实例上平均最优性差距约3.44%，在90个困难实例中保持低于5%的差距，平均加速比达6.89±1.41。对无法精确求解的实例，采用经验贝尔曼-时间回归估计缺失解并外推算法速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生产计划模型通常假设需求到达时间确定，但在实际应用中，需求到达时间往往具有不确定性。为更真实地反映生产环境中的动态竞争与资源分配问题，需考虑需求到达时间的随机性，同时保留对容量竞争、特定需求延迟及依赖分配的库存动态建模能力。因此，本文旨在建立一个能准确刻画随机到达时间影响的精确模型，并设计高效求解算法。

Method: 将问题建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），定义状态空间、可行动作集、转移核和单期成本函数；通过比较随机与确定性情形下的性能差异，揭示随机性带来的计算复杂度上升；设计一种基于状态反馈策略的遗传算法（GA），在精确的DTMDP框架下评估每个解的质量；对难以精确求解的实例，使用经验贝尔曼-时间回归估算精确解耗时并外推算法加速效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提遗传算法在330个基准实例中平均最优性差距约为3.44%；在90个较难实例中，平均优化速度提升达6.89倍（置信水平95%），且始终低于5%的最优性差距阈值；对于无法精确求解的问题，经验回归方法有效估计了精确解的时间并合理外推了算法性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于DTMDP的随机到达时间建模方法能够有效捕捉现实生产系统中的不确定性特征。所设计的遗传算法在保证高质量解的同时具备显著的计算效率优势，尤其适用于大规模或难以精确求解的场景。经验回归方法进一步增强了算法在实际应用中的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [296] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于累积对话轮次的风险评估框架，通过逐步聚合对话内容并动态更新风险评分，实现对金融诈骗对话的实时监控。构建了涵盖投资、慈善和科技支持类诈骗的多轮对话数据集，每轮对话包含2-8轮，并在每个累计阶段标注风险等级、连续风险分数、解释理由和安全建议。四种小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1、Qwen3）在统一训练框架下微调并评估，结果显示Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在参数量较小的情况下表现出更强的轮次感知风险判断能力，验证了结构化累积建模在资源受限场景下的可行性，凸显小型模型在隐私保护与设备端欺诈防护中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诈骗检测方法难以应对通过短信、电话等渠道展开的多轮对话式金融诈骗，因风险信号逐轮积累，需具备持续更新风险评估能力的轻量化模型以适应移动设备和资源受限环境。

Method: 提出一种累积轮次风险评估框架，通过增量聚合对话轮次并动态重估风险；构建多轮对话数据集，涵盖三类常见诈骗场景，每轮对话均在累计阶段进行风险标注；在统一框架下微调四种小型语言模型，利用其捕捉欺诈性语言特征与跨轮次升级模式。

Result: Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在小模型中表现最优，具备较强的轮次感知风险估计能力；小型模型在保持紧凑架构的同时有效识别欺诈线索，适用于移动端与资源受限部署场景。

Conclusion: 结构化的累积建模方法可有效支持在实际部署环境中进行增量式诈骗风险评估，小型语言模型在保证性能的同时具备隐私友好与本地运行优势，为设备端反诈系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Financial scams targeting older adults increasingly occur through text and voice channels such as email, SMS, and phone calls, unfolding over multiple conversational turns that begin with impersonation or casual contact, escalate through trust building and urgency, and culminate in requests for sensitive information or financial transfers. Because risk signals emerge incrementally across turns, effective detection requires models that continuously update risk estimates under resource-constrained deployment settings. We propose a cumulative turn-based risk assessment framework that incrementally aggregates conversational turns and re-estimates risk at each step, enabling dynamic scam monitoring across progressively evolving conversations. A multi-turn dialogue dataset is constructed to cover investment, charity, and tech support scam scenarios, with each dialogue containing two to eight turns and annotated at every cumulative stage with a qualitative risk level, a continuous risk score, an explanatory rationale, and a safety recommendation. Four small language models (Phi-4, LLaMA-3.2, DeepSeek-R1, and Qwen3) are fine-tuned and evaluated under a unified training framework. Fine-tuned small models capture fraud-related linguistic cues and cross-turn escalation patterns while maintaining compact architectures suitable for mobile and resource-constrained deployment settings. Among the evaluated models, Phi-4 and LLaMA-3.2 achieve stronger turn-aware risk estimation performance relative to their parameter scale. These results suggest that structured cumulative modeling can support incremental scam risk assessment in deployment-oriented settings while highlighting the potential of compact language models for privacy-aware and on-device fraud protection.

</details>


### [297] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过逐步计算MD5哈希值，测试大语言模型在长时序任务中保持精确中间状态的能力。实验设计隔离了状态跟踪与指令理解的干扰，采用196次依赖性工具调用，每轮更新4个32位变量（a,b,c,d），并逐位验证结果。结果显示，gpt-oss-120b模型在温度为0、固定提示下成功完成多数运行，并返回正确摘要。即使将每个基础工具替换为另一个LLM以消除精确算术参考，通过保留自身推理上下文和多轮投票机制仍可实现成功。失败原因被定位为状态传递、算术错误或服务问题三类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时序任务评估存在缺陷：因错误传播导致端到端失败率急剧上升，而现有代理基准混淆了状态跟踪难度与指令理解问题，缺乏对照组且易受幻觉答案等捷径影响，无法准确判断失败原因。因此亟需一种能清晰检验模型长期状态保持能力的基准方法。

Method: 设计一个从零实现的MD5哈希计算任务，共196步依赖性工具调用，跨越64轮，要求模型持续维护四个32位整数状态（a,b,c,d）；每一步均与标准规范（RFC 1321）对齐，逐位校验输出；使用gpt-oss-120b模型，在温度0和固定提示下执行；进一步构建双模型架构（驱动者+工作者），完全由两个LLM协作完成，无外部精确算术支持。

Result: gpt-oss-120b在标准设置下成功完成多数长序列任务，输出正确的最终哈希值；在全LLM架构下，通过保留推理上下文和投票机制，依然能有效减少算术误差，显著提升成功率；失败分析表明残余失败主要源于状态传递、算术精度或系统服务问题。

Conclusion: 大语言模型具备在极长依赖链中保持精确中间状态的潜力，关键在于维持自身推理上下文并引入容错机制（如投票）。本研究提供了一个干净、可复现的评估范式，有助于未来深入理解模型在复杂、长时序任务中的表现边界。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [298] [OpenAgentFlow: Enabling System-Wide Safety Boundaries for Heterogeneous AI Agent Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00015)
*Dongsheng Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenAgentFlow 是一种控制平面/执行平面架构，旨在解决多智能体系统中跨步骤操作流的安全治理问题。它将各类行动（如GUI操作、API调用、工具调用、LLM生成的指令）统一为AgentEvent流，在执行前通过共享的策略执行点进行安全审查，并在控制平面中维护可审计的记录和可更新的策略，实现无需修改智能体即可动态调整安全规则。该系统在Android平台上验证，表现出高准确率与强攻击拦截能力，证明其在异构AI智能体集群中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体系统趋向于多智能体协作，但现有安全机制分散且难以追踪跨步骤行为风险，缺乏对策略演进和审计的支持。因此需要一个统一、可管理的行动执行边界来实现系统级安全治理。

Method: 提出OpenAgentFlow架构，将所有智能体生成的动作抽象为统一的AgentEvent流，通过共享的预执行策略执行点进行审查，利用控制平面集中管理策略、会话状态、溯源信息和审计日志，实现对动作提交的集中管控。

Result: 在Android平台的300个案例测试中，准确率达94.0%，攻击阻断率达到95.3%；在30个动态策略测试中，27个案例符合预期行为；在98个实际跟踪案例中，原始准确率为90.8%，调整后通过率为92.9%，涵盖GUI、API及LLM规划场景。

Conclusion: OpenAgentFlow提供了一个实用的、可扩展的共享执行边界，适用于异构AI智能体群组，支持实时策略更新与全面审计，显著提升系统级安全性与可控性。

Abstract: AI agents powered by large language models are evolving from isolated assistants into heterogeneous systems in which multiple agents, planners, controllers, and execution backends operate over the same user or enterprise environment. In such settings, safety becomes a system-level action-governance problem: deciding whether concrete agent-generated actions should be committed before they modify shared state. Existing safeguards cover prompts, tool calls, GUI actions, and agent-local behavior, but often leave enforcement fragmented, obscure risks that emerge across multi-step action flows, and provide limited support for auditability and policy evolution.
  We present OpenAgentFlow, a control-plane/action-plane architecture that enforces safety at the action-commit boundary. It normalizes pending GUI actions, API calls, tool calls, and LLM-generated invocations into a unified AgentEvent stream, routes each event through a shared pre-execution Policy Enforcement Point, and maintains provenance, session state, audit records, and updatable policies in the control plane. This creates a shared governable action stream and allows new rules to take effect without modifying agents, prompts, models, or execution paths.
  We instantiate OpenAgentFlow on Android. On a 300-case action-event benchmark, it achieves 94.0% accuracy and a 95.3% attack block rate. On a 30-case dynamic-policy suite, it matches expected behavior in 27 cases after new rules are installed. Across 98 traced cases from a 100-case Android emulator suite, it achieves 90.8% raw accuracy and a 92.9% trace-adjusted pass rate across GUI, API, and LLM-planned cases. These results show that OpenAgentFlow provides a practical shared enforcement boundary for heterogeneous AI agent fleets.

</details>


### [299] [SCAFFOLD: A Large-Scale Structured Dataset of Computer Science Research Figures with Diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00018)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Sudan Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCAFFOLD是一个大规模的计算机科学论文图表数据集，包含图表、标题、上下文、问题-答案对以及链式思维推理过程。该数据集通过布局检测和PDF解析从arXiv论文中提取，并结合AI辅助生成问题，涵盖157,387个图文对，分为大（SCAFFOLD-157K）、中（SCAFFOLD-37K）和小（SCAFFOLD-12K）三个版本。研究使用SCAFFOLD-12K在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct上进行了基线实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏将计算机科学论文中的图表与文本描述、问题、答案及逐步推理相匹配的公开数据集，而这类数据对训练视觉-语言模型理解技术图表至关重要。

Method: 通过布局检测和PDF解析从arXiv论文中提取图表及其相关信息，并采用AI辅助方式生成问题，构建结构化数据集。

Result: 成功构建了SCAFFOLD-157K、SCAFFOLD-37K和SCAFFOLD-12K三个规模不同的数据集，其中SCAFFOLD-12K被用于基线实验并验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD为训练能够理解复杂技术图表的视觉-语言模型提供了关键资源，填补了现有数据集的空白，具有重要的研究价值和应用潜力。

Abstract: Computer science papers rely heavily on diagrams: architecture drawings, system flowcharts, and pipeline schematics that often carry more information than the text around them. There is currently no public dataset that pairs this specific kind of figure with captions, context, questions, answers, and step-by-step reasoning, which is exactly what is needed to train a vision-language model to understand them. We present \textbf{SCAFFOLD}\footnote{https://github.com/theranjitraut/scaffold}, a large-scale structured dataset of computer science research figures with diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought reasoning traces. This dataset consists of (image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought) tuples from arXiv computer science papers prepared using layout detection and PDF parsing, with an AI-assisted question-generation step. The resulting large-sized SCAFFOLD-157K dataset spans 3,058 papers with 29,887 figures (157,387 pairs), a medium-sized SCAFFOLD-37K dataset (36,797 pairs), and a small-sized SCAFFOLD-12K dataset (12,000 pairs). We used SCAFFOLD-12K for baseline experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [300] [UI-Venus-2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00028)
*Venus Team,Zhuohan Cai,Haoxing Chen,Jiaxuan Chen,Weizhi Chen,Changlong Gao,Zhangxuan Gu,Yuan Guo,Yusong Hu,Jianrong Jiang,Jianguo Li,Runze Li,Jinzhen Lin,Zhenyu Ma,Changhua Meng,Han Peng,Xinyu Qiu,Shuheng Shen,Zhongyi Shui,Weiqiang Wang,Ming Wen,Zhuoer Xu,Hang Yan,Kaiwen Yang,Ruilin Yao,Nanjun Yu,Zhengwen Zeng,Lianrui Zhang,Yunzhu Zhang,Zhe Zhao,Beitong Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UI-Venus-2 is a general-purpose foundation GUI agent that operates across mobile, web, and desktop environments using a unified closed-loop reasoning-action framework. It improves real-world applicability by scaling environment coverage (170+ multilingual apps and desktop OS), enhancing task generation via deep-research pipelines, and enabling reliable reward verification through trace-level and sample-level evaluators with visual keypoints and multi-model voting. Safety-aware mechanisms ensure controlled execution of critical actions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing multimodal GUI agents struggle with limited environment coverage, brittle task construction, and unreliable reward signals, hindering their deployment in real-world applications. This work aims to bridge the gap between benchmark models and practical use by building a more robust, generalizable, and verifiable agent.

Method: UI-Venus-2 employs a unified closed-loop reasoning-action framework. It scales environments by integrating over 170 multilingual mobile apps and native desktop systems; generates tasks using a deep-research pipeline grounded in functional requirements; verifies outcomes via trace-level and sample-level evaluators leveraging visual keypoints and multi-model voting; and incorporates safety-aware mechanisms for secure action execution.

Result: UI-Venus-2 demonstrates enhanced generalization across diverse platforms, achieves more reliable and verifiable reinforcement learning signals, and enables safe, efficient operation in real-world scenarios. The system is open-source, offering a scalable foundation for future research and deployment.

Conclusion: UI-Venus-2 advances the development of multimodal GUI agents by addressing key limitations in scalability, reliability, and safety, paving the way for more generalizable, verifiable, and self-reflective agents suitable for real-world automation tasks.

Abstract: Multimodal GUI agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for digital task automation, yet transitioning from benchmark-oriented models to dependable real-world applications remains challenging due to limited environment coverage, brittle task construction, and unreliable reward verification. In this work, we present UI-Venus-2, a general-purpose foundation GUI agent designed to operate across mobile, web, and desktop environments through a unified closed-loop reasoning-action framework. To bridge the gap toward practical deployment, we jointly scale three critical dimensions: (1) Environments, expanding coverage to more than 170 multilingual mobile apps and native desktop operating systems; (2) Tasks, employing a deep-research pipeline for function-grounded instruction generation; and (3) Verification, adopting trace-level and sample-level evaluators with visual keypoints and multi-model voting to ensure reliable RL signals for training. Furthermore, we integrate safety-aware mechanisms to ensure controlled execution of consequential actions. By offering a capable, efficient, and open-source foundation, UI-Venus-2 advances the field toward more generalizable, verifiable, and self-reflective agents for real-world applications.

</details>


### [301] [EULER: Exploring Underused Links with Evidence-Checked Return for Multi-Agent Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00032)
*Ren Zhenzhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EULER is a multi-agent system designed to transfer mathematical problems across different domains by constructing 'bridges'—intermediate steps that connect source and target representations. It evaluates bridges through stress tests and only retains those that introduce new operations and return valid evidence to the original conjecture. Tested on 120 recent conjectures from combinatorics, EULER achieved 10 proofs, 3 refutations, and 45 partial results. Key mechanisms—bridge-specific stress tests and combining bridge material with target-native operations—significantly improved performance. Success depended more on operational gain and valid return than on domain distance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transferring mathematical problems across different domains is costly and often avoided due to differences in objects, invariants, and tools. There is a need for systematic, automated methods to construct and validate such transfers.

Method: EULER uses a competitive search framework involving direct, adjacent-domain, and distant-domain routes. Bridges are validated via stress tests and must provide an operation unavailable in the source and ensure the target-side evidence logically returns to the original statement. The system employs ablation studies to assess component effectiveness.

Result: EULER successfully proved 10 conjectures, refuted 3, and produced 45 partial results on 120 independent, contamination-free conjectures from top-tier combinatorics papers. Bridge-specific stress tests reduced incorrect conclusions from 9 to 3; combining bridge material with target-native operations yielded a +4.2 improvement in resolved tasks.

Conclusion: Effective problem transfer in mathematics relies not on domain proximity but on the introduction of executable operations and valid logical return paths. EULER demonstrates that structured, agent-based bridge construction can significantly enhance automated theorem proving.

Abstract: Mathematical communities work with different objects, invariants, and tools, so transferring a problem across them is expensive and often skipped. We present EULER, a multi-agent system that takes such a transfer--a bridge--as its unit of search. Around a fixed conjecture, EULER runs direct, adjacent-domain, and distant-domain routes in competition; a bridge keeps its budget only if it supplies an operation the source representation cannot execute and its target-side evidence returns to the original statement along a checked implication. Six ordered stress tests reject invalid bridges before expensive search begins.
  We evaluate EULER on 120 recent conjectures. The conjectures were frozen before search and screened for contamination, and are drawn from public papers by authors who had recently published in the Journal of Combinatorial Theory, Series A, a leading journal in combinatorics. EULER produced 10 proofs and 3 refutations, plus 45 scoped partial results. Two mechanisms held up under ablation: bridge-specific stress tests cut incorrect conclusions from 9 to 3, and bridge material combined with a target-native operation yielded a positive interaction of +4.2 resolved tasks that neither factor produced alone. Domain distance did not reliably predict success; executable operation gain and valid return did.

</details>


### [302] [When Prediction Error Is Not Enough: Evaluating Nuisance-Function Prediction for Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00071)
*Cong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估了在部分线性模型中，不同方法（OLS、GAM、XGBoost、DML-XGBoost）的预测误差与因果估计性能之间的关系。结果显示，尽管XGBoost在点估计上表现最佳，但其置信区间覆盖率不如DML-XGBoost；预测误差与因果偏差之间并无一致关联，且联合误差度量对因果性能的预测能力较弱。因此，预测误差虽可用于评估扰动函数估计，但不应直接视为因果估计器质量的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预测误差与因果估计器性能之间的关系，以判断预测误差是否可作为因果推断中扰动函数估计质量的可靠指标。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟，在部分线性模型框架下比较OLS、GAM、XGBoost和DML-XGBoost四种方法，评估其在预测误差、偏倚、均方根误差（RMSE）及95%置信区间覆盖率方面的表现，并引入联合误差度量分析其与因果偏差的关系。

Result: XGBoost在非最优方法中具有最低的RMSE，而DML-XGBoost在置信区间覆盖方面表现更好；预测误差与因果偏倚之间无稳定对应关系，点估计最优的方法未必提供最佳覆盖率；联合误差度量与因果偏倚关联微弱，无法独立有效衡量因果性能。

Conclusion: 预测误差有助于评估扰动函数估计，但不能直接反映因果估计器的质量，应谨慎将其用作因果推断性能的唯一指标。

Abstract: Prediction error is widely used to evaluate nuisance-function estimators in causal inference, but its relationship with causal estimator performance may differ across performance measures. We studied this question in a partially linear model using Monte Carlo simulations. We compared ordinary least squares (OLS), generalized additive models (GAMs), XGBoost, and Double Machine Learning with XGBoost (DML-XGBoost), evaluating nuisance-function prediction error, bias, RMSE, and 95\% confidence interval coverage. We also examined a simple joint-error measure based on the absolute cross-product of estimation errors from the exposure and outcome nuisance functions. Across the simulated settings, XGBoost had the lowest RMSE among the non-oracle methods, while DML-XGBoost generally provided better confidence interval coverage. Prediction error did not consistently track causal bias across methods and settings, and the method with the best point-estimation performance did not necessarily have the best confidence interval coverage.
