TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为波函数反向传播（WFB）的新学习框架，将神经响应建模为可学习的振幅、波数、角频率和相位的波函数形式，通过相位与时间间隔Δt关联，实现对时间信息的显式建模。该方法导出了标准梯度与基于拉普拉斯算子的空间曲率修正项，并在轨迹预测任务中验证了其有效性。实验表明，使用真实时间间隔的WFB相比传统前馈网络（FFN）平均位移误差（ADE）降低20.4%；在去除时间泄漏的位置仅评估中仍优于参数匹配的ReLU模型，且比带Δt显式输入的FFN低2.1%的均值ADE。随机时间间隔的WFB表现最佳，说明波表示有效，但性能提升未必源于时间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络通常将时间视为辅助特征或假设均匀采样，难以有效建模时间动态变化。本文旨在通过引入波函数形式的结构化表示，显式建模时间间隔与状态之间的关系，从而提升序列学习中的时间敏感性与预测精度。

Method: 提出波函数反向传播（WFB），将神经响应表示为可学习的波参数（振幅、波数、角频率、相位），利用波函数的相位与时间间隔Δt建立映射关系，推导出可微的梯度更新规则及基于拉普拉斯算子的空间曲率修正项，并在前馈轨迹预测器中进行验证。

Result: 在运动特征条件下，实时间间隔的WFB使平均位移误差（ADE）相比原始FFN降低20.4%；在位置仅评估中，相比参数匹配的ReLU控制组降低10.4%，并优于带Δt显式输入的FFN（2.1%更低）。随机时间间隔的WFB取得最低均值ADE，表明波表示有效，但性能提升不完全依赖于时间对齐。

Conclusion: WFB是一种有效的结构化前馈学习范式，能够通过波函数形式自然编码时间信息，显著提升轨迹预测性能。该工作为后续神经网络架构设计中引入时空波形建模提供了理论基础与实证支持。

Abstract: Conventional neural networks learn predominantly through affine transformations followed by nonlinear activations, while elapsed time is often treated as an auxiliary feature or assumed to be uniformly sampled. This paper introduces Wave Function Backpropagation (WFB), a wave-parameterized learning formulation in which neural responses are represented by learnable amplitude, wavenumber, angular frequency, and phase. The formulation associates an observed state with its temporal interval Delta t through the phase of a differentiable spatiotemporal wave. We derive standard WFB gradients and a spatial-curvature correction based on the Laplacian of the wave response. WFB is instantiated in a deliberately feed-forward trajectory predictor to provide a controlled proof of concept; sequence learning is outside the scope of the present evaluation. With motion features, STD-WFB using real intervals reduces average displacement error (ADE) by 20.4% relative to the original FFN baseline. In a new position-only evaluation that removes temporal leakage through precomputed velocity and acceleration, real-interval WFB reduces ADE by 10.4% relative to the original FFN and remains competitive with parameter-matched ReLU controls, obtaining 2.1% lower mean ADE than the matched FFN with explicit Delta t. Shuffled-interval WFB attains the lowest mean ADE, indicating that the present evidence supports the effectiveness of the wave representation but does not attribute the gain to interval alignment. These results establish WFB as a viable structured feed-forward learning formulation and define a clear basis for subsequent architectural studies.

</details>


### [338] [CoVer: Conflict-Aware Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00508)
*Shuning Zhang,Dai Shi,Bohao Chu,Hui Wang,Yuwei Chuai,Yifan Wang,Jingruo Chen,Simin Li,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ContraNote 是一个大规模的真实世界数据集，用于评估社会媒体事实核查中的证据级和聚合级冲突解决。CoVer 是一个三阶段的事实裁定框架，通过规范化证据模式、验证事实共识与支持来优先处理证据并减少噪声干扰。在多个基准测试中表现优异，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会媒体事实核查面临证据级和聚合级冲突，错误信息常模仿权威新闻源，亟需真实世界数据集和有效框架来应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出 CoVer 框架，包含三个阶段：证据模式归一化、事实共识与支持验证，以提升证据权重并抑制噪声影响。

Result: 在 ContraNote 数据集上，CoVer 在冲突检测任务中达到 86.0% 准确率、68.0% 宏平均 F1、64.5% 平衡准确率；在优先级排序任务中达 88.5% 准确率、88.5% 宏平均 F1、89.2% 平衡准确率；在 CONFACT-HumC 和 CONFACT-ModC 上分别达到 88.4% 和 89.4% 准确率，优于当前最优基线。

Conclusion: CoVer 框架结合 ContraNote 数据集，在处理社会媒体事实核查中的复杂冲突问题上展现出卓越性能，为未来研究提供了有力工具与基准。

Abstract: Social media fact-checking has long been challenged by evidence-level and aggregation-level conflicts, where erroneous evidence mimics authoritative news sources. To capture this challenge and support conflict verification tasks, we present ContraNote, a large-scale real-world dataset curated from X's Community Notes system. It includes 33,686 posts for evaluating evidence-level conflict resolution, and 54,474 instances for evaluating aggregation-level prioritization. Additionally, we propose CoVer, a factual adjudication framework with three-stage pipelines: evidence schema normalization, factual consensus and support verification. This prioritizes evidence over noise to prevent it from compromising the final verdict. Technical evaluations show that CoVer achieves strong performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines across ContraNote (86.0% Acc., 68.0% mac. F1, 64.5 bal. Acc. on Conflict; and 88.5% Acc., 88.5 mac. F1 and 89.2 bal. Acc. on Prioritization), CONFACT-HumC (88.4% Acc.) and CONFACT-ModC (89.4% Acc.).

</details>


### [339] [When the Algorithm Becomes the Brand Crisis: A Sociotechnical Theory of Distributed Responsibility and Accountable Transparency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00510)
*Mohammad Saleh Torkestani,Taha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种社会技术过程理论，用以解释在人工智能系统中责任归属的复杂性。当技术因果、用户控制与治理责任分散于开发者、部署者、供应商和用户之间时，传统品牌危机模型无法充分回应责任分配问题。文章区分了AI事件、组织危机与组织丑闻三类不同情境，指出事件配置影响责任归因，归因进而影响对能力、诚信、公平与关系的评估，而公众道德化可能但非必然将事件升级为丑闻。研究还提出‘可问责透明’作为应对策略，包含及时通知、可理解的解释、角色责任承认、补救措施、纠正证据及申诉渠道。实证支持对责备、信任、满意度、企业评价与沟通可信度的局部、近端推论，而非长期声誉或品牌价值等宏观结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人工智能系统在市场中的广泛应用（如聊天机器人、推荐系统、自动化决策）导致其故障、误用和误导性表现频发，引发责任归属难题。传统品牌危机模型难以处理技术因果、客户控制权与治理职责在多方主体间的分布式特征，因此亟需新的理论框架来解析责任分配机制及其对品牌的影响。

Method: 通过结构化、联邦式范围合成方法，整合经验证的学术文献与一手资料，构建一个概念性社会技术过程理论。采用多源证据融合与批判性分析，厘清AI事件、组织危机与组织丑闻之间的区别，并建立责任归因与后果评估的逻辑链条。

Result: 提出了区分三类情境的理论框架：AI/算法事件、AI相关组织危机、AI相关组织丑闻；揭示了事件配置如何决定责任归因路径；证明了可问责透明作为响应机制的有效性；发现对责备、信任、满意度等指标的短期影响强于对品牌声誉或市场表现的长期影响。

Conclusion: 该理论有效调和了现有研究中关于算法参与是否缓冲负面品牌反应的矛盾结论，强调责任归属取决于具体情境配置。同时，提出‘可问责透明’作为关键响应策略，有助于提升组织在危机中的合法性与公众信任，但其效果主要体现在近端认知层面，而非持久的品牌资产积累。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly enact market-facing promises through chatbots, recommendation systems, automated decisions, and generative interfaces. Their failures, misuse, and misrepresentation raise a question that conventional brand-crisis models do not fully specify: how do stakeholders assign responsibility when technical causation, customer-facing control, and governance duties are distributed across an AI system, developer, deployer, vendor, and user? This conceptual paper develops a sociotechnical process theory from a structured, federated scoping synthesis of verified academic and primary sources. It distinguishes an AI/algorithmic incident from an AI-related organisational crisis and, in turn, from an AI-related organisational scandal. The framework proposes that incident configuration shapes actor-specific attribution; attribution informs capability, integrity, fairness, and relationship appraisals; and public moralisation may, but need not, escalate an incident into scandal. The theory offers a reconciliation of findings that algorithm involvement can buffer negative brand reactions in some settings while robot and chatbot failures can redirect responsibility to an associated firm in others. It introduces accountable transparency as a proposed response configuration that combines timely notice, an intelligible account, role-responsibility acknowledgement, remedy, evidence of correction, and recourse. The evidence supports conditional, proximal inferences about blame, trust, satisfaction, firm evaluation, and communication credibility more strongly than claims about durable reputation, brand equity, or market performance.

</details>


### [340] [ISO-RAG: Isoperimetric Noise Control for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00513)
*Siyuan Zhang,Hanchen Wang,Dong Wen,Ying Zhang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ISO-RAG 是一种几何感知的检索增强生成框架，通过将知识图谱投影到双曲庞加莱球中，预先计算节点的等周轮廓，从而在检索过程中剪枝无关边，限制搜索空间至局部子图。该方法通过调控个性化页面排名（PPR）扩散过程，实现精确且低延迟的收敛，在多跳问答任务上显著优于现有基线，平均召回率提升10.0%，下游精确匹配提升4.3%，有效解决了全局遍历带来的语义漂移与高延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集检索在多跳问答中难以处理复杂推理路径；基于图的RAG虽能捕捉多步关系，但存在严重语义漂移和高在线延迟问题，源于噪声全局图遍历。

Method: 将知识图谱投影至双曲庞加莱球空间，预计算每个节点的等周轮廓，用于在检索阶段剪枝冗余边，缩小搜索范围至局部子图，并利用该结构优化个性化页面排名（PPR）的扩散过程，实现高效精准检索。

Result: 在多跳问答基准测试中，ISO-RAG相较于现有最优方法，平均检索召回率提升10.0%，下游精确匹配提升4.3%，同时显著降低延迟，实现了更优的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: ISO-RAG通过几何感知的局部化检索机制，从根本上消除了全局遍历的延迟瓶颈，为多跳问答提供了高效、精准的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates large language models (LLMs) hallucinations, yet conventional dense retrieval struggles with the complex reasoning paths of multi-hop question answering (QA). Graph-based RAG captures multi-step relationships but suffers from severe semantic drift and high online latency due to noisy global graph traversals. Thus, we propose ISO-RAG (ISOperimetric Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a geometry-aware RAG framework. By projecting the underlying knowledge graph into a hyperbolic Poincare ball to precompute node-wise isoperimetric profiles, ISO-RAG prunes spurious edges during retrieval, restricting the search space to a strictly localized subgraph. This topological purification regulates Personalized PageRank (PPR) diffusion driving the retrieval process, ensuring exact and low-latency convergence. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that ISO-RAG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by average absolute gains of 10.0% in retrieval recall and 4.3% in downstream exact match, achieving a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off by fundamentally eliminating the latency bottleneck of global traversals. Our source code is available at https://github.com/ZaiizaiZHANG/ISO-RAG.

</details>


### [341] [Feedback-Assisted Trust Propagation over Document Relation Graphs for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00543)
*Zhuoheng Li,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrustPropRAG通过构建文档关系图并利用多跳传播机制，基于有限的人工反馈估算文档可靠性，从而提升RAG系统的检索质量和答案准确性，尤其在反馈稀疏和噪声环境下表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖外部语料库，但这些语料可能包含过时、矛盾、嘈杂或不可靠的文档，影响回答的可靠性。现有方法仅基于直接比较的文档对传播可靠性信号，难以覆盖更广范围。因此需要一种能跨多跳传播可靠性信号的方法，以增强整体系统鲁棒性与可信度。

Method: 提出TrustPropRAG，将文档间关系建模为图结构，通过求解一个联合考虑成对文档关系与用户反馈的优化问题，计算每个文档的可信度得分，并利用该得分指导文档选择与信任感知的答案生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，TrustPropRAG在检索质量与精确匹配率上均优于基线模型，且在人工反馈稀疏和噪声条件下仍保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: TrustPropRAG有效解决了RAG系统中因外部语料不可靠带来的风险，通过图结构上的多跳传播机制扩展了有限的人工反馈，显著提升了系统可靠性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems rely on external corpora that may contain outdated, contradictory, noisy, or unreliable documents, introducing reliability risks. Prior work has leveraged document relations to improve the answer reliability of RAG. To propagate reliability signals beyond directly compared document pairs, we propose TrustPropRAG, which structures document relations as a graph and estimates document reliability through multi-hop propagation across the graph. TrustPropRAG anchors this propagation with a limited set of human feedback on document reliability, extending these costly-to-collect feedback-based reliability signals across the whole corpus. Specifically, based on the constructed document relation graph, TrustPropRAG estimates a trust score for each document by formulating and solving an optimization problem that jointly captures pairwise document relations and user feedback. These scores are then used to improve the selection of reliable documents and support trust-aware answer generation. Evaluation results show that TrustPropRAG improves both retrieval quality and exact match over baselines, and remains robust under sparse and noisy feedback.

</details>


### [342] [VoiceLongMemEval: Do Assistants Remember How You Sounded?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00570)
*Ramit Pahwa,Parivesh Priye,Apoorva Beedu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VLME基准，评估模型在长对话历史中对语音语调、情感等副语言信息的理解能力，发现现有模型因忽略这些信息而表现不佳，音频原生模型则能更好利用语音信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体和大模型系统需处理长期连续对话历史，但现有基准忽视人类与代理交互中的语气、情感等副语言信息，导致评估不全面。

Method: 构建包含情感标签、语调描述和语音事件的语音长记忆评估基准（VLME），采用三阶段对抗门控机制确保仅依赖文本的模型无法通过，验证不同模型对副语言信息的利用能力。

Result: 主流语言模型在缺乏副语言信息时准确率显著下降（0.09-0.38提升），提示证据后达0.61-0.69；标准ASR系统会丢失关键语音信号；音频原生模型直接从语音中提取线索，性能优于盲视文本模型（0.354-0.412 vs. 0.325）。

Conclusion: 副语言信息对长对话理解至关重要，现有模型普遍存在‘情感缺口’，未来需重视语音信号的整合以提升对话系统的真实交互能力。

Abstract: With the growing scale of multi-agent architectures and large language models, deployed AI assistants are increasingly tasked with reasoning over long, continuous, multi-session conversation histories. Current benchmarks evaluate this dialogue history as information retrieval over long horizon, temporal reasoning, or knowledge updates, while crucially ignoring the fundamental dynamics of human-agent interaction, i.e. how they said it. To address this gap, we present VoiceLongMemEval (VLME) benchmark, where every answer depends on paralinguistic metadata (emotion labels, prosody descriptors, and voice events) attached to conversational turns, which is otherwise unrecoverable from the words alone. Every item passes a three-stage adversarial gate, ensuring that a strong language model fails when given only the transcript. Evaluating leading frontier and open-weight models reveals a pervasive affect gap; providing text-track paralinguistic metadata yields a 0.09 to 0.38 accuracy boost (0.61 to 0.69 when prompted with evidence hints), while standard ASR pipelines systematically discard this signal. Additionally, audio-native models successfully extract these cues directly from speech (0.354 to 0.412 vs. 0.325 blind). Code and dataset will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [343] [Residual Sparsification via Output Importance for Compressing Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00575)
*Seungwoo Jung,Dohyeok Kwon,Seungmin Cha,Junseok Lee,Yeonho Yoo,Chuck Yoo,Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PARSER提出一种新的残差稀疏化方法，通过将压缩目标从最小化单个矩阵误差转向保持专家输出误差，引入输出重要性以衡量对输出误差的实际贡献，从而显著提升压缩后模型的准确性。实验表明，在相同内存压缩率下，PARSER在Qwen和DeepSeek上分别将精度差距缩小1.41倍和1.44倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差稀疏化方法独立压缩每个残差矩阵，仅关注最小化单个矩阵的压缩误差，但这种目标与保持模型整体准确率不一致，因为误差会在多层投影和隐藏表示中传播，导致专家输出误差累积，进而影响模型性能。

Method: 提出PARSER方法，通过引入输出重要性来重新定义压缩目标，使压缩过程更关注对专家输出误差的直接影响，从而有效抑制误差传播。

Result: 在相同峰值内存减少条件下，PARSER在Qwen和DeepSeek模型上分别比现有方法将精度差距缩小1.41倍和1.44倍，显著提升了压缩后的模型性能。

Conclusion: 传统的独立矩阵压缩目标存在偏差，而基于输出误差的压缩策略能更有效地保持模型准确性，PARSER通过引入输出重要性实现了这一目标，为MoE模型高效压缩提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models efficiently, but they demand massive GPU memory. To cope with such demand, models are commonly compressed to reduce their memory footprint. Residual sparsification is a representative compression technique that decomposes each projection matrix of an expert into a shared base matrix and per-expert residual matrix, and then compresses the residuals. Existing sparsification methods compress each residual matrix independently by minimizing its compression error, thereby minimizing the error of each projection matrix. However, our analysis shows that this objective is misaligned with preserving model accuracy after compression. In an expert, the final output is produced through computations coupled across multiple projections and hidden representations. Therefore, even small errors in individual matrices can propagate through hidden representations and projection interactions, leading to large expert output errors and accuracy degradation. To address this misalignment, we propose PARSER, a new residual sparsification method that shifts the compression objective from minimizing isolated matrix errors to preserving the expert output error. PARSER achieves this by introducing output importance, which measures the actual contribution to the expert output error. Our experiments show that, compared with existing methods, PARSER narrows the accuracy gap to the uncompressed model by 1.41$\times$ on Qwen and 1.44$\times$ on DeepSeek, while achieving the same peak memory reduction.

</details>


### [344] [Consistency Without Alignment: Item-Sensitive Language Models Indistinguishable From Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00576)
*Cris Huynh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过一个抽象自桌游《欺骗：香港谋杀案》的强制选择信号任务，揭示了模型的项敏感性（item-sensitivity）虽普遍存在，但并不能充分证明其任务能力。尽管所有21个模型-评分规则组合均表现出项敏感性，但其中8个与随机选择无统计差异，5个甚至表现劣于随机，且项敏感性与随机偏离程度的相关性仅r=0.30。研究提出‘一致性但不一致’（consistency without alignment）的概念，指出依赖项敏感性、排列一致性或自我一致性评估的方法存在根本缺陷。此外，基于字面相似性的基线模型表现优于多数语言模型，加入语用层反而使模型更趋随机而非接近贝叶斯参考。标准多选题格式在此任务中未传递有效信息。研究强调需引入独立参照标准以验证模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估语言模型的任务能力常依赖项敏感性等指标，但这些指标是否真正反映模型理解力尚存疑问。本研究旨在检验项敏感性作为任务胜任证据的有效性，并揭示其潜在误导性。

Method: 在抽象自桌游《欺骗：香港谋杀案》的强制选择信号任务中，使用七种语言模型、两种模型家族、一次后训练消融实验及三种独立评分规则，系统测试模型行为。参考基准包括最优拟合策略、后验最大化策略和均匀随机选择，均具有闭式解。通过预注册工具对模型输出进行量化分析，比较其与各基准的一致性与距离。

Result: 所有21个模型-规则组合均显示项敏感性，但8个与随机选择无显著差异，5个表现劣于随机；项敏感性与远离随机的程度相关性低（r=0.30）。字面相似性基线优于多数模型，加入语用层反而降低性能。标准多选题格式未传递有效内容信号。

Conclusion: 项敏感性是任务能力的必要条件，但非充分条件。当前评估范式若仅依赖一致性指标而无独立参照，可能导致错误结论。研究呼吁引入独立基准以确保评估有效性，并警示现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Item-sensitivity, defined as whether a model's choice depends on the specific input rather than on its own output prior, is widely reported as evidence of task competence. We show this evidence is necessary but not sufficient using a forced-choice signalling task abstracted from the board game Deception: Murder in Hong Kong. In this environment, the reference points against which a coordinate should be judged (a fit-maximising strategy, a posterior-maximising strategy, and uniform random selection) are all computable in closed form. Across seven language models, two model families, a post-training ablation, and three independent scoring rules, every one of 21 model-by-rule cells is reliably item-sensitive. Yet 8 of those 21 cells are not statistically distinguishable from a chooser that ignores the item and selects at random, and 5 score worse than random at describing the target. Item-sensitivity and distance from random correlate at only r = 0.30. We call this consistency without alignment and argue it generalises to any evaluation that relies on item-sensitivity, permutation consistency, or self-consistency without an independent reference for the measured quantity. We further find that a literal-similarity baseline with no pragmatics outperforms most tested language models, that adding a pragmatic layer over two baseline similarity sources moves choosers toward random rather than toward the Bayesian reference, and that a standard labelled multiple-choice format carries no measurable content signal here. All results represent the model side of a pre-registered instrument; a matched human condition is designed and piloted but not yet collected.

</details>


### [345] [Same Request, Different Boundary: Evaluating Cybersecurity Assistance across Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00578)
*Rui Yang,Yang Hong,Yichao Xu,Zhengyu Liu,Ziyang Li,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出3R-Bench基准，评估大语言模型在网络安全请求中的拒绝、重复和修改行为，发现先前对话历史显著影响模型响应，且对话分解导致合规性大幅下降，反馈修复效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全专用数据集未考虑请求的对话上下文，导致模型在高风险场景中难以区分恶意与合法请求，亟需一种机制在不阻碍合法用户的情况下阻止滥用。

Method: 构建3R-Bench基准，包含150个真实世界网络安全请求，并引入两种对抗性对话设置，评估8个大语言模型在不同对话历史下的响应行为。

Result: 先前拒绝历史使模型合规率从62.0%升至85.1%，而接受历史则出现相反趋势；对话分解使直接响应的合规率从501/800降至172/800，且失败反馈仅部分恢复性能。

Conclusion: 对话上下文对大语言模型的安全响应有显著影响，当前模型在复杂对话场景下易受误导，需改进上下文理解与安全决策机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can solve complex problems, but their misuse in high-risk domains can lead to severe consequences. Model providers therefore restrict assistance for potentially harmful requests. Refusing all cybersecurity requests would therefore harm legitimate users. Providers need a mechanism to block malicious use without denying legitimate assistance to defenders. Existing cybersecurity-specific datasets evaluate this mechanism, but none considers the conversational context of a request. We introduce 3R-Bench (Refusal, Repetition, and Revision), a benchmark of 150 real-world cybersecurity requests augmented with two adversarial conversational settings, and evaluate eight LLMs on it. Prior assistant behavior strongly changes responses to an unchanged request: among 376 available pairs from a 400-pair panel, compliance rises from 62.0% after refused history to 85.1% after accepted history. The opposite pattern appears under dialogue decomposition. In comparison, compliance falls from 501/800 direct responses to 172/800 after dialogue; among 738 pairs returning model-authored text in both conditions, the decrease is 45.1 points. Failure feedback recovers only a small fraction of this loss.

</details>


### [346] [Socrates went Nuclear: Comparing Interaction Strategies for AI systems in a Learning Context using Brain Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00584)
*Alexandre Clin Deffarges,Nataliya Kosmyna,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究比较了三种用户与AI互动的学习模式：无限制对话机器人、基于提示的苏格拉底式引导机器人，以及基于脑电波信号实时调整难度的自适应辅导系统。结果表明，无限制聊天机器人在学习成效上优于其他两种模式（p < .03, d > 0.80），但自适应系统显著提升了用户的认知参与度（p = .018）。聚类分析显示，无限制模式用户多采用直接获取答案的策略，而苏格拉底模式用户起初尝试推理，随后逐渐脱离。因此，无限制AI带来的高学习成果可能源于即时后测，而非深层理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨无限制AI访问是否减少学习所需认知努力，或是否真正促进知识获取。

Method: 50名参与者在零先验知识背景下学习核安全规程，经历视频教学、前测、三种不同AI交互条件下的评估阶段和即时后测。使用Muse头戴设备监测脑电波以评估认知参与度。三种条件分别为：(1) 无限制聊天机器人；(2) 苏格拉底式提示引导；(3) 基于脑信号自适应调整难度的系统。

Result: 无限制聊天机器人在学习增益上显著高于其他两种模式（p < .03, d > 0.80），自适应系统在EEG认知参与度上显著更高（p = .018）。聚类分析显示，无限制模式用户倾向直接获取答案，苏格拉底模式用户初期尝试推理但逐渐脱节。

Conclusion: 无限制AI虽带来较高学习成绩，但未必反映深层学习，其效果可能主要归因于即时后测的时间点，而非真正的认知内化。

Abstract: Does unrestricted AI access bypass the cognitive effort required for learning, or does it streamline knowledge acquisition? This paper reports on a study where we compare three designs for user-AI interaction in a learning context: (1) an unrestricted conversational bot like ChatGPT, (2) a pedagogically constrained bot that guides through hints without giving final answers, which we refer to as the Socratic mode; and (3) a non-conversational adaptive tutoring system that adjusts difficulty in real-time based on the user's cognitive engagement derived from the brain signals. Fifty study participants were tasked with learning about nuclear safety protocols, a domain chosen for its zero-prior knowledge baseline. The participants progressed through an instructional video, a pre-test, an AI-driven assessment phase, which varied in the three conditions, and an immediate post-test. The nature of the questions centered primarily on factual knowledge acquisition, but it still required participants to have a global understanding of the concepts in order to answer the questions correctly. A Muse headband was used to derive the cognitive engagement of all users in all conditions. The unrestricted chatbot produced higher learning gains (delta) than both constrained modes (p < .03, d > 0.80), while the adaptive condition generated significantly higher EEG engagement (p = .018). The cluster analysis of chatbot usage and discussion patterns by users showed that most participants in the unrestricted-mode adopted a direct answer-retrieval strategy, while participants in the Socratic-mode initially attempted to reason through the hints before progressively disengaging. Consequently, this also suggests that the success of the unrestricted AI is not an evidence of deeper learning, but rather a result of the immediate post-test evaluation after the training phase.

</details>


### [347] [Control-Data Flow Separation: Stable Prompt Optimization in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00621)
*Wentao Zhang,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Junaid Ahmad Bhatti,Utkarsh Soni,Yifan Nie,Eugene Wen,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出控制-数据流分离机制，将执行协议（如消息路由、输出格式）转化为类型化、可验证的程序对象，而任务相关语言保持为可优化的数据流，从而避免提示词优化对系统协议的破坏。实验表明，在多种任务中实现了100%的协议有效性并提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体大模型系统中，提示词同时承担生成内容和定义执行协议的双重角色，导致优化内容时可能意外破坏协议，引发系统失败。

Method: 提出控制-数据流分离设计：将执行关键的控制逻辑表示为类型化、可验证的程序对象，任务相关内容仍以自然语言形式作为可优化的数据流进行通信。

Result: 在合成推理、协作评审生成和保险评级等任务中，该框架实现了100%的最终协议有效性，并持续提升任务表现。

Conclusion: 通过分离控制与数据流，可在不损害执行协议的前提下安全地优化多智能体系统的提示词，显著提升系统鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Prompt optimization can improve multi-agent LLM systems, but the prompts being optimized often serve two entangled roles: generating task-relevant content and specifying execution-critical protocols, such as message routing, output formatting, and termination signals, on which the underlying code relies. As a result, a prompt edit intended to improve content generation can inadvertently corrupt the protocol and cause the entire agent pipeline to fail. Our key observation is that these two roles have different representations: execution protocols are typically structured, while task-relevant content is usually expressed in unstructured language. Based on this, we propose control-data flow separation, where execution-critical control is represented as typed, validated program objects, while task-relevant language remains the optimizable data flow for agent communication. This design allows optimizers to improve multi-agent behavior without exposing the routing or formatting interface to prompt drift. Across synthetic reasoning, collaborative review generation, and insurance rating workflows, our framework empirically achieves 100% eventual protocol validity while consistently improving task performance.

</details>


### [348] [REVISE: Validity-Guided Recovery for Online Revisions in Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00643)
*Ruoling Qi,Xuaner Wu,Penghang Liu,Jian Chen,Yirui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度恢复机制Revise，用于解决并发执行中正确性与效率的权衡问题。当发生代理修订时，Revise通过交集分析数据和控制依赖，精确识别受影响的工作区域，停止无效任务，保留已验证的有效进展，并仅重算受影响部分。该方法在真实编码代理日志中表现出显著的在线恢复潜力：118个会话在消息队列处理前仍保留可观测工作；167次重叠响应中，排队到完成的时间重叠达到p95为56.55秒。在300次复杂修订/提交测试中，Revise与最新版本基准一致，无过时输出或影响。相比完整重启和后缀重算，其模型调用减少40.6–56.0%和31.3–43.6%。在高负载下，进一步降低修订到正确完成的令牌数13.26%，提升SLO吞吐量3.07–5.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恢复策略在正确性与效率之间存在不平衡：要么允许可能过时的工作继续，牺牲正确性；要么完全重启或重算线性后缀，浪费未受影响的有效进度。需要一种更精细、平衡的恢复机制以兼顾两者。

Method: Revise是一种基于有效性的运行时系统，采用细粒度恢复策略。当收到修订时，首先将修订增量与记录的数据和控制依赖进行交集分析，通过部分执行的DAG传播影响范围，识别出真正受影响的任务。然后停止无效工作，保留最早冲突之后的有效进展，仅重算受影响区域。不完整的溯源信息会保守地扩大恢复范围，而重用的结果在提交前需重新验证。

Result: 实验表明，Revise在真实编码代理轨迹中具有显著的在线恢复能力：118个会话在新消息到达前仍保持可观测工作；167次重叠响应中，排队至完成时间重叠达56.55秒（p95）。在300次复杂修订场景中，其表现等同于最新版本基准，无任何过时输出或副作用。在未修改的LangGraph和LLMCompiler应用上，使用Qwen3-14B模型，相比全量重启减少40.6–56.0%模型调用，相比后缀重算减少31.3–43.6%。在服务压力下，修订到正确完成的令牌数减少13.26%，SLO好用率提升3.07–5.43%。

Conclusion: Revise成功实现了正确性与效率之间的精细平衡，通过精准识别影响范围并最小化重算，显著提升了结构化代理工作流在并发环境下的性能与可靠性，适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: Agent revisions expose a fundamental correctness--efficiency trade-off during concurrent execution. Discarding ongoing work preserves latest-version correctness but wastes progress that may remain valid, whereas reusing prior work preserves efficiency but risks propagating stale state into outputs and tool effects. Existing recovery strategies resolve this trade-off in an imbalanced way with coarse-grained policies: they either favor efficiency by allowing potentially stale work to continue, or favor correctness by restarting the workflow or recomputing a linear suffix from the earliest conflict, thereby discarding unaffected progress. We present \textsc{Revise}, a validity-guided runtime for fine-grained recovery in structured agent workflows. When a revision arrives, \textsc{Revise} first intersects its delta with recorded data and control dependencies and propagates the resulting impact through the partially executed DAG to identify affected work. It then stops invalid work, preserves validity-established progress beyond the earliest conflict, and recomputes only the affected region. Incomplete provenance conservatively expands recovery, while reused results are revalidated before commit. Analysis of real coding-agent traces show online recovery opportunities: 118 sessions retain observable work before a queued later message is delivered; across 167 overlapping assistant responses, enqueue-to-completion overlap reaches 56.55~s at p95. Across 300 challenging revision/commit executions, \textsc{Revise} matches a latest-version oracle with no stale outputs or effects. On unmodified LangGraph and LLMCompiler applications using Qwen3-14B, it reduces model calls by 40.6--56.0\% relative to full restart and by 31.3--43.6\% relative to suffix recomputation. Under serving pressure, it further reduces revision-to-correct-completion tokens by 13.26\% and improves SLO goodput by 3.07--5.43\%.

</details>


### [349] [DramaChain Bench: An End-to-End Benchmark for Short-Drama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00646)
*Haoyuan Shi,Mingtao Chen,Shuo Jiang,Ziyan Chen,Xuyi Sheng,Yiming Liu,Ying Zhang,Miao Wang,Jianxiang Lu,Fanyang Lu,Songyuanyi Lu,Xiele Wu,Zhichao Hu,Yuhong Liu,Richeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DramaChain Bench 是首个评估短剧制作全流程的基准，涵盖从剧本到成片的每个阶段。它包含五个评估维度，共63个具体指标，通过专业标注和自动化判别系统实现精准质量评估，验证了上游错误会沿生产链传递，强调视频生成并非决定最终质量的唯一因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估视频生成阶段，忽略上游各环节对脚本意图的忠实度及多镜头间的连贯性，无法全面评估短剧制作质量。

Method: 构建 DramaChain Dimensions 维度体系，设计 DramaChain Agent 和 Labeling System 实现多阶段公平比较与高质量标注，结合 DramaChain Agentic Judge 实现自动评分与证据积累。

Result: 人工标注显示上游缺陷会持续传递至下游，最终成片质量受全链条影响；自动化系统在模型排名上达到平均 PLCC 0.918，可替代人工标注实现零成本新模型准入。

Conclusion: 短剧质量由整个生产链共同决定，DramaChain Bench 提供了一套可扩展、可追踪、高精度的全流程评估框架，为未来短剧生成模型的发展提供可靠基准。

Abstract: Commercial short-drama production follows a multi-stage chain: script, storyboard, keyframe imagery, shot-level video, and the finished short drama. Most existing benchmarks evaluate solely the video-generation stage using pre-authored inputs instead of real upstream pipeline outputs. This leaves two critical questions unanswerable: whether each stage adheres to the original script intent (rather than only its immediate input prompt), and whether disparate shots remain coherent after assembly into multi-episode releases. We present DramaChain Bench, the first short-drama benchmark that evaluates every stage of the complete production chain. It is built upon three in-house systems sharing one dimension system, DramaChain Dimensions: five evaluation axes instantiated at every stage, resolving into 63 leaf dimensions. DramaChain Agent is calibrated against commercial short-drama platforms in both workflow and finished short-drama quality, enabling stage-wise fair comparison across models. DramaChain Labeling System has each of the 5,785 items scored independently by three professional annotators, with all defects spatio-temporally localised and selected from a predefined defect list. This process produces 17,488 valid scores and 255,925 traceable attribution records. The human annotations confirm that upstream defects cascade across the pipeline, demonstrating that final episode quality is not governed by video generation alone. DramaChain Agentic Judge then scores every leaf dimension automatically, gathering evidence over multiple agentic rounds before judging against a per-item checklist; it reproduces the model ranking at a mean PLCC of 0.918, enough to admit new models at no annotation cost.

</details>


### [350] [Self-Reports Are Not Verification: Environment-Grounded Auditing of LLM Operators in Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00652)
*Enrong Pan,Ryan Zhou,Ting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过环境驱动的审计机制，评估语言模型代理在自我报告中的信心与推理合理性。结果表明，模型高估成功概率达4.8至9.3倍，信心与校准性、区分度在不同模型间不一致；推理传承对表现提升有限（约250名内），且基于适应度的选择无法显著提升报告质量。因此，自报告应视为需验证的主张，而非可靠性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型代理依赖自报告的信心和推理过程作为监控信号，但这些信号虽方便获取却未经验证。为解决可信度问题，需建立基于环境反馈的审计机制，以客观评估其自报告的真实性与有效性。

Method: 构建环境驱动的审计系统，采用进化式Contexto搜索，每轮生成的假设均获得精确排名反馈，无需人工标注。通过200次运行，覆盖五种配置与三种模型家族，收集12,249条自报告数据，进行三类假设检验：信心是否校准、推理是否影响后续行为、基于适应度的选择是否提升质量。

Result: 所有假设均未成立：模型普遍高估前100名成功率（4.8–9.3倍）；信心与校准性、区分度在不同模型中分离；对754个推理干预的控制实验显示，真实推理仅带来约250名内的微弱收益；适应度选择与随机选择在报告准确性上无显著差异，尽管搜索行为差异明显。

Conclusion: 语言模型的自报告不应被视为可靠证据，而应作为需通过环境反馈加以验证的主张。当前机制下，其信心与推理缺乏实质性可信基础。

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly propose actions, observe external feedback, and explain their own behavior. Their confidence and rationales are convenient monitoring signals, but convenience is not verification. We introduce an environment-grounded audit in which every intermediate proposal receives an exact outcome. A language model operates an evolutionary Contexto search whose feedback function assigns every valid guess an exact rank without human annotation. Across 200 runs spanning five configurations and three model families, four reporting configurations produce 12,249 self-reports. We test three assumptions: stated confidence is calibrated, inherited rationales affect later proposals, and fitness-based selection improves report quality. All three fail. Operators overstate top-100 success by factors of 4.8 to 9.3, while calibration and discrimination dissociate across model families. Controlled interventions on 754 inherited rationales bound any measured benefit of the genuine rationale to roughly 250 ranks. Neither fitness-based nor random selection produces a detectable selection differential or parent-to-offspring transmission in report accuracy, despite sharply different search behavior. Agent self-reports should therefore be treated as claims to verify against the environment, not as evidence of their own reliability.

</details>


### [351] [SciTrue: Reliable Scientific Claim Validation with Frontier and Open Language Models at the NTCIR SciClaimEval Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00654)
*Qiming Bao,Neşet Özkan Tan,Siyuan Wang,Mark Gahegan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciTrue团队在NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval任务中通过基准测试11个前沿开源多模态模型，采用透明的后处理策略，在四个证据类别/子任务组合中三项排名第一，一项并列第一。关键发现包括：强指令微调模型已具备竞争力；任务配对结构是最大影响因素，基于可见字段的无泄漏配对先验显著提升准确率；多数残余错误源于视觉不可检测的标签映射错误或数据噪声，表明模型改进空间有限。此外，研究揭示了数据包装中的标签泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证科学声明与论文图表之间的关系，提升自动化科学论证评估系统的准确性与可靠性。

Method: 基准测试11个前沿多模态模型，采用统一、透明的每样本协议进行评估，并结合轻量级后处理；引入无泄漏配对先验机制以利用可见字段信息；通过案例审计识别错误来源；控制性微调与蒸馏验证结论。

Result: 在官方盲测排行榜上，SciTrue在三个子任务组合中排名第一，一个子任务并列第一；子任务1的配对准确率从72.2提升至93.5；多数错误为标签映射或数据噪声，真实性能高于测量值，模型改进空间有限。

Conclusion: 强指令模型已具备强大能力，任务设计中的配对结构和数据包装中的标签泄露是关键影响因素，当前系统性能接近上限，进一步提升需关注数据质量与标注一致性。

Abstract: We describe the SciTrue team's participation in both subtasks of the NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval task~\cite{sciclaimeval}, which asks systems to verify scientific claims against the tables and figures of a paper. Rather than tuning a single model, we benchmark eleven frontier and open multimodal models under one honest, per-sample protocol and combine them with light, transparent post-processing. On the official, blind test leaderboard (Section~\ref{sec:results}), SciTrue placed first by a clear margin in three of the four evidence-category/subtask combinations, and tied for first on the primary metric in the fourth. Three findings explain the result. First, strong instruction-tuned models are already competitive: Claude Opus~4.8 and Gemma-4-31B each exceed the strongest public baseline (o4-mini), and GPT-5.5 and Claude Fable~5 lead both subtasks (97.7 on Subtask~2). Second, the task's pairing structure is the largest lever: a \emph{leak-free pair prior} that recovers the Supported/Refuted pairing from the claim text alone (a visible field) and assigns Supported to the higher-confidence evidence raises Subtask-1 pair-accuracy from 72.2 to 93.5, far more than any model swap or ensemble weighting. Third, a case-by-case audit finds that most residual errors are visually-undetectable label-mapping swaps or dataset label noise, so measured accuracy understates the true ability and the fixable-by-modeling headroom is small. Controlled fine-tuning, distillation, and agentic consistency-checking support the same conclusions, and we document throughout a measurement leak---label information reaching a system through the packaging of the data rather than its content---in which the released file ordering encodes the label, including one instance that briefly misled our own pipeline.

</details>


### [352] [Drift-Aware LLM Routing with Sparse Contexts and Shared Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00662)
*Cheung Hao Lee,Patrick Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Drift-Aware Sparse Routing (DRS)的动态路由方法，用于多模型语言服务中的请求调度。该方法在存在计算、延迟、内存或成本等多重资源约束的情况下，通过滚动审计窗口估计奖励与资源消耗，采用悲观奖励和乐观成本进行路由决策，并在线更新资源影子价格，实施硬限制以确保承诺前的合规性。理论分析表明，在非平稳稀疏上下文环境下，其对动态流体基准的累积遗憾被有效控制，且在稀疏线性模型假设下，当漂移为零时达到标准 $O(\sqrt{sT/ρ})$ 的速率；当存在漂移时，引入 $O(T^{2/3}(s/ρ)^{1/3}V_T^{1/3})$ 的适应项，实现对系统变化的有效响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型语言服务需要在动态变化的请求负载和模型性能背景下，高效地将每个请求路由到最合适的模型，同时满足多种资源预算（如计算、延迟、内存、成本）的约束。然而，由于提示嵌入空间高维、仅少数方向具有预测价值，且模型性能随发布、微调、量化等操作持续漂移，传统的静态模型选择方法难以适用。因此，亟需一种能够应对非平稳性、稀疏性与多重资源约束的动态路由机制。

Method: 提出Drift-Aware Sparse Routing (DRS)，基于滚动审计窗口估计模型的奖励与资源使用，采用悲观奖励与乐观成本进行路由决策，结合在线更新的资源影子价格，并设置硬计量门限以防止超限。其设计将控制策略与统计估计分离，支持对漂移的自适应调整。

Result: 理论证明了在任意事件具有统一预测半径 $\{β_t\}$ 的条件下，相对于一个节奏化的动态流体基准，累积遗憾被限制在预测半径之和、容量缓冲项和 $O(\sqrt{T})$ 的节奏项之内。在稀疏线性模型与有界漂移 $V_T$ 假设下，后悔上界为 $\widetilde O\left( T\sqrt{\frac{s}{ρW}}+WV_T+\sqrt{T} \right)$，其中 $s$ 为稀疏度，$ρ$ 为审计率，$W$ 为窗口长度。优化窗口长度后，可恢复静态情况下的 $O(\sqrt{sT/ρ})$ 率，并在漂移存在时获得 $O(T^{2/3}(s/ρ)^{1/3}V_T^{1/3})$ 的适应项。

Conclusion: DRS是一种有效的动态稀疏路由框架，能够适应模型与请求分布的非平稳变化，同时严格遵守多资源约束。其理论保证在漂移存在时仍保持合理的性能退化，适用于实际部署中频繁更新的多模型语言服务场景。

Abstract: A multi-model language service must route each request while preserving workload-level budgets for compute, latency, memory, or monetary cost. Two features make this problem materially harder than static model selection. Prompt representations are high dimensional, so only a small subset of embedding directions may predict the incremental value of a model, and both the request mix and the model frontier drift after launches, fine-tunes, quantization changes, and system updates. We formulate nonstationary sparse contextual routing with multiple knapsack constraints and an optional shadow-audit stream that evaluates a small fraction of prompts on several models.
  We propose Drift-Aware Sparse Routing (DRS). The policy estimates reward and resource use from a rolling audit window, routes using pessimistic reward and optimistic cost estimates, updates resource shadow prices online, and applies a hard meter before commitment. The analysis separates control from statistics. On any event with uniform prediction radii $\{β_t\}$, regret against a paced dynamic fluid benchmark is bounded by the sum of the radii, a capacity-buffer term, and an $O(\sqrt{T})$ pacing term. Under a sparse linear model and bounded drift $V_T$, rolling estimation gives \[ \widetilde O\left( T\sqrt{\frac{s}{ρW}}+WV_T+\sqrt{T} \right), \] where $s$ is sparsity, $ρ$ is the audit rate, and $W$ is the window length. Optimizing $W$ yields the usual stationary $O(\sqrt{sT/ρ})$ rate when $V_T=0$ and a $O(T^{2/3}(s/ρ)^{1/3}V_T^{1/3})$ adaptation term under drift.

</details>


### [353] [Triple-Bottom-Line Sustainability of Language Models for Edge AI: A Comparison Between SLMs and Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00665)
*Jainil Dharmil Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在边缘部署中，原生训练的小语言模型（SLMs）与经过后训练量化压缩的大语言模型（LLMs）哪个更具可持续性。提出一种可复现的综合可持续性评分（HSS），涵盖经济、环境和社会三重底线：能力与系统效率、运行时GPU能耗、有害提示鲁棒性。对比了5个BF16 SLMs和5个不同量化方式（BF16、INT8、NF4 4-bit、GPTQ 4-bit、GGUF Q4）的LLMs共30种配置，评估指标包括零样本基准表现、延迟、吞吐量、峰值显存和能耗，以及对5个有害提示的攻击成功率。结果显示，Qwen3-30B-A3B/GGUF Q4在综合评分中排名第一（93.38），Mistral-Small-24B/GGUF Q4第二（92.40），而Phi-4-mini/BF16是表现最好的SLM（89.49）。因此，原生SLMs一定更可持续的假设并不普遍成立；优化后的量化LLMs可在整体上胜出，而SLMs仍因资源需求低保持竞争力。量化是一种系统级选择，而非简单的精度-效率权衡，且HSS结果依赖于比较池和代理定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前边缘AI模型选择通常仅依赖单一指标（如准确率、延迟、内存、能耗或安全性），但实际部署的语言模型需在五个维度间取得平衡。现有方法缺乏全面评估框架，难以判断原生小模型与量化大模型在边缘部署中的真实可持续性优劣。

Method: 提出一种基于三重底线的可复现综合可持续性评分（HSS），涵盖经济（能力与效率）、环境（运行时GPU能耗）和社会（有害提示鲁棒性）三个维度。评估5个BF16 SLMs和5个不同量化方式（BF16、INT8、NF4 4-bit、GPTQ 4-bit、GGUF Q4）的LLMs共30种配置，使用零样本任务衡量能力，测量延迟、吞吐量、峰值显存与能耗，通过攻击成功率评估安全性。

Result: Qwen3-30B-A3B/GGUF Q4在综合评分中排名第一（93.38），Mistral-Small-24B/GGUF Q4排名第二（92.40），Phi-4-mini/BF16为最高分的SLM（89.49）。表明优化后的量化LLMs可超越原生SLMs，具备更强的整体可持续性；同时SLMs因资源消耗低仍具竞争力。量化并非单调的精度-效率权衡，而是系统级决策，且HSS结果受比较池和代理定义影响。

Conclusion: 原生小语言模型并非在所有情况下都比量化大语言模型更适合作为边缘部署的可持续选择。经过优化的量化大模型在综合性能上可能更优，而小模型仍凭借低资源需求保持优势。量化策略应作为系统设计的一部分进行权衡，且可持续性评估需依赖具体上下文与比较基准。

Abstract: Edge-AI model selection is commonly driven by one isolated metric - accuracy, latency, memory, energy, or safety, even though a deployable language model must balance all five. Our work focuses on answering the question whether na- tively trained small language models (SLMs) or large language models (LLMs) compressed through post-training quantization offer the more sustainable edge- deployment trade-off. We introduce a reproducible Holistic Sustainability Score (HSS) organized around the triple bottom line: an economic pillar for capability and systems efficiency, an environmental pillar for operational GPU energy and a social pillar for harmful-prompt robustness. Five BF16 SLMs and five LLMs under different quantization approaches - BF16, INT8, NF4 4-bit, GPTQ 4-bit, and GGUF Q4 produce 30 measured configurations. Capability is assessed on five zero-shot benchmarks; efficiency uses latency, throughput, peak VRAM and energy; and safety is approximated by attack success rate on five harmful prompts. Qwen3-30B-A3B/GGUF Q4 ranks first in the combined pool (93.38), followed by Mistral-Small-24B/GGUF Q4 (92.40), while Phi-4-mini/BF16 is the highest- ranked SLM in that pool (89.49). Thus, the hypothesis that native SLMs must be the most sustainable edge choice is not supported universally; optimized quantized LLMs can win overall, while SLMs remain competitive through lower resource demand. Quantization is a systems-level choice rather than a monotonic precision- efficiency trade-off and HSS remains relative to its comparison pool and proxy definitions.

</details>


### [354] [Value Over Language Model: Detecting Original Contribution in Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00700)
*Vibhhu Sharma,Thorsten Joachims,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种名为VOLM（Value Over Language Model）的框架，用于衡量人类在使用大语言模型（LLM）辅助写作时所贡献的信息价值，而非仅依赖表面文本特征。该方法不依赖训练数据或标注，也不分析文本表层风格，而是通过多层次内容提取与重建来评估人类对信息内容的实际贡献。在新闻文章、ICLR同行评审和论说文三个领域中，VOLM能够有效区分人类撰写与纯由LLM生成的文本，并对内容保持不变的变换具有鲁棒性。研究还发现，更严格的语义内容提取能减少人类与LLM生成文本之间的残差差异，凸显了分离信息内容与表达风格的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM检测工具主要关注文本表面特征，无法准确衡量人类在信息内容层面的真实贡献。该研究旨在解决这一问题，开发一个能量化人类对内容创新性贡献的方法，以更真实地评估人机协作中的价值分配。

Method: 提出VOLM框架：从文档中逐步提取不同粒度的内容表示，利用LLM基于这些部分重建文档，并与仅基于任务描述生成的基准文档进行对比。通过比较重建质量差异，评估人类添加的信息价值。

Result: VOLM在多个领域成功区分了人类撰写与LLM生成的文本；对内容保持但风格变化的转换（如重写、翻译回译）保持稳定；更精细的内容提取器可显著降低人类与LLM文本间的差异，验证了信息与风格解耦的有效性。

Conclusion: VOLM提供了一种无需训练、不受风格干扰的评估人类在LLM辅助写作中贡献的新范式，强调了信息内容与表达形式分离的重要性，为未来人机协作评价提供了基础框架。

Abstract: LLMs have been rapidly adopted across writing tasks, prompting the development of tools for detecting LLM-generated text. Yet, these tools largely measure how much of a document's surface text was written by an LLM and aren't fundamentally designed to measure how much of the information content or ideas originated from the LLM itself rather than being supplied by the user in the prompt. In this work, we design a framework that measures how much value a person adds on top of what a language model could have easily produced by itself. The method requires no training or labeled data and never scores the document's surface text, insulating it from stylistic confounders. Instead, it extracts the document's content at increasing levels of granularity, uses an LLM to reconstruct the document from each partial representation, and compares these reconstructions with those produced from the task description alone. We call this framework Value Over Language Model (VOLM), which measures a document's contribution relative to a replacement-level document that an LLM could produce from the task description alone. We evaluate VOLM with a specific instantiation of this framework across three domains: news articles, ICLR peer reviews, and argumentative essays. VOLM separates human-authored documents from matched LLM-generated documents produced from generic task descriptions, while remaining substantially invariant to content-preserving transformations, including LLM-based reconstruction and round-trip translation. We further find that increasingly constrained content extractors reduce residual differences between LLM-generated and humanized text, demonstrating the importance of disentangling informational content from stylistic variation. We hope these results encourage further work on specialized instantiations of the framework and on assessing human contributions in LLM-assisted writing more generally.

</details>


### [355] [ChatDev 2.0: A No-Code Multi-Agent Platform for Developing Everything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00714)
*Yufan Dang,Shu Yao,Bowen Lai,Chenting Xu,Ruijie Shi,Wai-Shing Leung,Huatao Li,Chen Qian,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChatDev 2.0: DevAll 是一个无代码平台，用于构建、执行和检查异构多智能体系统（MAS），兼具高表达性和易用性。它采用声明式可执行图抽象与循环感知执行引擎，支持动态、循环的智能体交互；同时提供集成可视化界面，实现无需编码的智能体编写、运行、监控与调试，包括人机协同步骤。实验表明，DevAll 在三个代表性任务中达到与现有先进方法相当的性能，且无需针对特定任务编写编排代码，验证了其作为通用LLM驱动多智能体平台的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的多智能体系统开发面临表达性与工程复杂性之间的权衡：代码框架虽表达力强但开发成本高，而无代码工具虽易用但限制了智能体间的动态交互。需要一种既能保持高度灵活性又易于使用的平台来推动多智能体系统的高效构建与迭代。

Method: 提出DevAll平台，结合声明式可执行图抽象与循环感知执行引擎，实现对异构智能体及动态、循环交互的统一建模与执行；设计集成可视化界面，支持用户在不写代码的情况下完成智能体系统的全生命周期管理，包括人机协同环节。

Result: DevAll在三个典型任务上复现了当前最优多智能体系统的表现，性能相当，且无需为特定任务编写编排代码，证明其具备良好的通用性与实用性。

Conclusion: DevAll成功实现了无代码环境下多智能体系统高表达性与易用性的统一，是一个面向LLM驱动多智能体系统的通用、高效平台，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks, yet their development forces a tradeoff: code frameworks are expressive but engineering-intensive, while no-code builders simplify authoring but constrain agent interactions to author-defined workflows. We present ChatDev 2.0: DevAll (hereafter DevAll), a no-code platform for building, executing, and inspecting heterogeneous MAS that delivers both high expressiveness and ease of use. In terms of expressiveness, DevAll pairs a declarative executable graph abstraction with a cycle-aware execution engine, so that heterogeneous agents and dynamic and cyclic interactions can be represented and executed within a single framework. For ease of use, an integrated visual interface lets users author, run, monitor, and inspect MAS, including human-in-the-loop steps, entirely without writing code. Experiments demonstrate that DevAll reproduces state-of-the-art MAS across three representative tasks at competitive performance and without task-specific orchestration code, highlighting its effectiveness as a general-purpose platform for LLM-based MAS. DevAll is available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/ChatDev.

</details>


### [356] [A Closed-Loop Evaluation of Capability Loss and Recovery in Compressed Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00718)
*Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan,Nur Ahmad Khatim,Mansur Arief*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分阶段闭环评估方法，用于分析压缩驾驶策略在实际驾驶场景中的表现。通过将驾驶任务建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），并在Gym-Duckietown环境中使用近端策略优化（PPO）训练信念状态策略。随后对策略进行逐阶段压缩（剪枝、知识蒸馏、量化），并在五个驾驶课程上评估其性能。结果表明，结构化剪枝是导致驾驶能力首次丧失的关键阶段；知识蒸馏虽能提升剪枝后的策略，但受限于回放数据；整数量化会损失需停车再启动的课程任务，而未剪枝的原始策略则能保持全部任务性能。研究强调了传统指标的局限性，倡导以安全性和可靠性为核心评估压缩策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型压缩方法（如剪枝、知识蒸馏、量化）通常依赖聚合数值指标评估，但这些指标无法准确反映策略在与交通参与者交互时的安全驾驶能力。因此需要一种更贴近真实驾驶环境的评估方式，以确保压缩后的自动驾驶策略在部署时既安全又可靠。

Method: 将驾驶任务建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），使用近端策略优化（PPO）在Gym-Duckietown环境中训练信念状态策略；随后对策略进行分阶段压缩（剪枝→蒸馏→量化），每阶段后在五个不同的驾驶课程上进行闭环评估，以追踪性能退化过程。

Result: 结构化剪枝是导致驾驶能力首次丧失的关键阶段；知识蒸馏可改善剪枝后的策略，但提升受限于训练数据；整数量化会丢失需要精确控制停车与重启的任务；而未经剪枝的原始策略在所有任务中均保持完整性能。

Conclusion: 当前关于如何接受压缩驾驶策略的讨论应更加注重安全性与可靠性，而非仅依赖传统数值指标。本研究提供了实证依据，支持采用分阶段闭环评估来指导安全可靠的自动化驾驶功能部署。

Abstract: Many automobile and mobility companies deploy learned driving policies on embedded computers with limited memory and power. Pruning, knowledge distillation, and quantization are the standard methods to reduce the size and the inference cost of these policies. However, these methods are commonly assessed by aggregate numerical scores, and such scores may not reflect the ability of the policy to drive safely when interacting with other road users. In this study, we propose a stage-wise closed-loop evaluation approach to follow a driving policy through a compression pipeline. We formulate the driving task as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) and train a belief-state policy with proximal policy optimization (PPO) in Gym-Duckietown. We then extract the actor, compress it one stage at a time, and evaluate it on five driving curricula. We show that structured pruning is the stage at which the driving capability is first lost. Meanwhile, distillation improves the pruned actor, but the improvement is limited by its rehearsal data. Integer quantization of the improved actor loses some of the curricula that require the vehicle to stop and then resume. Interestingly, the same procedure on the unpruned actor preserves all five curricula. Our study thus provides an empirical analysis aiming to answer the currently active discussions on how to accept a compressed driving policy, so as to achieve a safe and statistically reliable deployment of automated driving functions.

</details>


### [357] [SOVER: Formal Certification of Optimization Reformulations via LLM-Assisted SMT Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00728)
*Swapnil Bhattacharyya,Mayank Baranwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SOVER框架，结合Z3和dReal实现对数学优化问题的语义映射与形式化验证，引入NLEquiv-150基准测试集，利用LLM提取映射后在150个配对中正确分类149个（99.33%），包括全部50个困难负例，仅1个错误源于映射提取不完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖求解器执行验证，但受局部最优、结构超时、数值误差及语义差异影响，可靠性不足；需一种能分离语义映射与形式认证的可靠验证机制。

Method: 采用LLM提取问题映射，利用Z3验证混合整数线性问题的域交叉可行性与目标顺序保持，使用dReal进行连续非线性问题的容差感知可行性、范围及ε-最小值检查，构建NLEquiv-150基准测试集评估性能。

Result: 在150个配对中正确分类149个（99.33%），所有50个困难非等价对均被正确识别，唯一错误源于LLM映射提取不完整。

Conclusion: SOVER通过分离语义映射与形式化验证，显著提升数学优化问题重表述的可靠性，为基于LLM的自动建模转换提供可信保障。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable promise in translating and reformulating complex mathematical optimization problems across modeling languages. However, validating such transformations through empirical solver executions alone is unreliable, as solver outcomes may be affected by local minima, structural timeouts, numerical artifacts, and subtle semantic divergence between formulations. We introduce SOVER, an LLM-assisted SMT framework that separates semantic mapping from formal certification: Z3 checks domain cross-feasibility and global objective-order preservation for mixed-integer linear formulations, while dReal provides tolerance-aware feasibility/range and $ε$-argmin checks for continuous nonlinear formulations. We also introduce NLEquiv-150, a public benchmark of 100 equivalent and 50 deliberately hard non-equivalent nonlinear reformulation pairs. With LLM-extracted mappings, SOVER classifies 149/150 pairs (99.33%) correctly, including all 50 hard negatives; the sole error is an incomplete mapping extraction.

</details>


### [358] [Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing: Methodological Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00731)
*Yingjian Pan,Xiaowei Ding,Kay Giesecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing（AEAP）的新范式，即由大模型代理自主完成资产定价的科学发现过程。作者定义了AEAP的核心组件，提出一个参考架构、严格的因子发现评估标准及跨样本回测方法。通过在两个美国股票面板上对SEADS系统与五个重新实现的基线进行对比，发现单一指标无法一致评价系统性能，强调需多维度综合评估。进一步的滚动重执行检验了发现过程本身的可靠性，同时揭示了未来AEAP系统评估中可能存在的陷阱和局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资产定价研究主要依赖人工设计因子并回测其输出结果，但缺乏对自主发现过程本身的评估机制。随着大模型代理的发展，亟需一种能够评估整个自动化科学发现流程的新方法，以确保其有效性、可重复性和稳健性。

Method: 提出AEAP框架，包括参考架构、多维度评估标准、基于跨样本和滚动重执行的回测方法；使用SEADS系统在两个美国股票数据集上进行实验，并与多个基线比较，评估因子发现性能及过程稳定性。

Result: 实验表明，没有单一指标能一致排名各系统表现，说明必须从多个角度评估；滚动重执行显示部分发现过程不稳定，暴露了评估中的潜在陷阱；报告了负向结果与系统局限性，提示未来研究需更谨慎地设计评估方案。

Conclusion: AEAP为资产定价提供了新的自动化研究范式，但其评估体系必须超越传统回测，涵盖发现过程的动态性、可重复性与稳健性，未来工作应建立更全面、严谨的评估标准。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents enable a new paradigm for asset pricing, which we call Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing (AEAP): systems that autonomously conduct the scientific discovery process itself. We define AEAP and identify its core building blocks. Existing evaluation practices backtest only the outputs (factors or trades), not the autonomous discovery system that produced them. We focus on factor discovery, contributing a reference architecture, a rigorous evaluation standard for discovered factors, and a method for out-of-sample backtesting the discovery system. As a concrete instance of that architecture, we evaluate SEADS against five re-implemented baselines on two US equity panels using this standard: no single metric ranks the systems consistently, motivating evaluation on multiple axes at once. A separate rolling re-execution then asks the complementary question of whether the discovery process itself, not one static output, is reliable. We also report negative findings and limitations that surface further evaluation pitfalls for future AEAP systems.

</details>


### [359] [Escaping Redundant Reasoning: Structure-Aware Search for Inference-Time LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00738)
*Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BASIN 是一种无需训练、结构感知的推理搜索方法，通过将推理状态分组为不同“基底”并惩罚重复访问相同策略，从而在固定计算预算下重新分配搜索资源，避免传统方法中因过度集中于相似路径导致的“推理基底坍缩”问题。该方法在 Game of 24 和 MuSR 任务上分别提升 22pp 和 6.7pp，其质量感知变体 QA-BASIN 进一步增强了鲁棒性。作者引入冗余差距 Δ 来衡量正确与错误预测下搜索分布的差异，表明 BASIN 能有效提升 Δ 值，实现更优的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于大语言模型的推理搜索（如 Tree of Thoughts）容易陷入少数结构或语义相似的推理路径，导致多样性不足，即‘推理基底坍缩’现象。这限制了对潜在有效路径的探索，影响最终推理质量。

Method: 提出 BASIN 方法，利用结构感知机制将推理过程中的状态聚类为不同基底，通过惩罚重复访问同一基底来引导搜索向更不同的路径转移；引入质量感知版本 QA-BASIN，平衡探索与高质量路径保留。

Result: 在相同推理预算下，BASIN 相比 ToT 提升高达 +22pp（Game of 24）和 +6.7pp（MuSR）；QA-BASIN 在复杂任务中表现更稳定。冗余差距 Δ 显示 BASIN 显著提升搜索分布差异，尤其在正确预测时更具区分性。

Conclusion: BASIN 表明结构感知的选择机制是提升推理阶段搜索效率与多样性的通用且有效的策略，无需额外训练即可显著改善大语言模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Inference-time search with large language models (LLMs) often concentrates on a small set of structurally or semantically similar trajectories, leaving alternatives underexplored---a failure mode we call \textit{reasoning basin collapse}. We introduce BASIN, a training-free, structure-aware selection method that groups reasoning states into basins and penalizes repeated visits to the same strategy, thereby reallocating search across genuinely distinct reasoning paths under a fixed compute budget. Under matched inference budgets, BASIN improves over Tree of Thoughts (ToT) by up to $+22$pp on Game of 24 and $+6.7$pp on MuSR. A quality-aware variant, QA-BASIN, further improves robustness by preserving high-quality basins when unconditional diversification over-explores. To explain when basin-aware selection helps, we introduce the redundancy gap $Δ$, which measures how differently search concentrates for correct versus incorrect predictions: standard ToT often operates near $Δ\approx 0$, while BASIN consistently shifts $Δ$ positive. More broadly, BASIN suggests structure-aware selection as a simple and general approach to improving inference-time reasoning. Code can be found at https://github.com/GitHubLuCheng/basin.

</details>


### [360] [ContextPipe: Database-Inspired Context Assembly for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00749)
*Peng Xu,Zuyu Zhang,Yuze Sun,Feng Tian,Long Wang,Chen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ContextPipe 提出一种五阶段管道（计划、绑定、优化、执行、反馈），将长时序大语言模型代理中的上下文组装问题类比为关系数据库的查询执行，通过结构化数据源目录、确定性缓存感知优化器和 EXPLAIN ANALYZE 跟踪，实现可审计、可重放、故障隔离的上下文管理。在 SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser 子集上的初步评估显示，相比追加式上下文构建策略，ContextPipe 降低 31% 的总令牌量、23% 的 LLM 调用次数、9% 的响应时间，代价是较低的 KV 缓存命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生产级智能体系统中，上下文组装逻辑分散于提示词构建器、临时压缩程序、缓存中断绕行方案及各供应商适配层，缺乏统一、可预测的机制；尤其在硬性上下文窗口限制和字节敏感缓存环境下，亟需一种结构化方法来高效管理历史信息的选取、排序与压缩。

Method: 提出 ContextPipe 管道，借鉴关系数据库的查询执行范式，采用五阶段流程（Plan Bind Optimize Execute Feedback），结合结构化数据源目录、确定性缓存感知优化器和可追踪的 EXPLAIN ANALYZE 输出，实现对上下文组装过程的显式控制与优化。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser 子集上，相较于传统的追加式上下文构建方式，ContextPipe 实现了 31% 的总令牌减少、23% 的 LLM 调用下降、9% 的响应时间缩短；同时具备可审计、可重放、故障隔离等特性，但以牺牲一定的 KV 缓存命中率为代价。

Conclusion: 上下文组装本质上与数据库查询执行具有结构同构性，通过引入数据库式的优化范式，ContextPipe 能有效提升长时序大语言模型代理的效率与可靠性，为复杂智能体系统的上下文管理提供了一种系统化、可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents require context assembly: the runtime must decide what to include in each prompt, in what order, and when to compact history under a hard context-window budget and a byte-sensitive prompt cache. In production agentic systems, this logic is scattered across prompt builders, ad hoc compaction routines, cache-break workarounds, and per-provider shims. We argue that context assembly is structurally isomorphic to query execution in a relational database: both execute under a hard budget, exploit a tiered cache, and leverage statistics. We adopt this discipline in ContextPipe: a five-phase pipeline (Plan Bind Optimize Execute Feedback) backed by a structured data-source catalog, a deterministic cache-aware optimizer, and an EXPLAIN ANALYZE trace. We show that context in ContextPipe is auditable, replayable, and failure-isolated. A preliminary evaluation using the SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser subset shows that, compared with the append-only context construction policy, ContextPipe reduces total token volume by 31%, LLM calls by 23%, and response time by 9%, at the cost of a lower KV cache-hit ratio.

</details>


### [361] [S^3martCirc: Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00755)
*Wendy Zheng,Yinhan He,Liang Wu,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出S^3martCirc框架，通过联合发现电路与解释功能，解决大语言模型可解释性中重要性与功能角色分离的问题。该框架将节点行为抽象为通用计算角色，并引入量化指标，实现重要性与功能的同步识别，实验表明其在电路发现上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的机械可解释性方法采用分阶段策略，先识别重要组件再分析其功能，但忽略了重要性与功能角色之间的内在关联，且功能角色依赖主观判断、缺乏量化标准。

Method: 提出S^3martCirc框架，将节点行为抽象为两种通用计算角色，设计可量化的指标以同时完成电路发现与功能解释，实现重要性与功能角色的联合建模。

Result: 在多个任务上的实验验证了S^3martCirc的有效性，其在电路发现性能上超越现有方法，展现出更强的泛化能力与可解释性。

Conclusion: S^3martCirc通过统一重要性与功能角色的发现过程，为大语言模型的机制可解释性提供了更高效、更客观的解决方案，推动了可解释人工智能的发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across diverse tasks, from text summarization to question answering. Despite these capabilities, their black-box nature obscures internal decision-making processes. Mechanistic interpretability (MI) aims to address this by reverse-engineering neural networks into human-understandable algorithms. Current MI approaches for LLMs typically follow a two-stage paradigm: first identifying important components (circuit discovery), where components are typically individual nodes such as an attention head or feedforward neuron, and second determining the role they play in a certain task (functional interpretation). However, this sequential approach overlooks a fundamental insight: a component's importance and its functional role are inherently codependent. Unifying these stages presents two key challenges: (1) functional roles are often tied to specific nodes or components, limiting generalization, and (2) their identification relies on subjective interpretation rather than quantifiable metrics. To address these challenges, we propose S^3martCirc (Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery), a unified framework that simultaneously discovers circuits and interprets functionality. S^3martCirc abstracts node behavior into two general computational roles that generalize across tasks and defines a quantitative metric for assigning them, enabling importance and functional role to be discovered jointly rather than in sequence. Extensive experiments show that our framework outperforms existing methods in circuit discovery.

</details>


### [362] [Automated Tree Knowledge Graph Construction using Ontology Expansion and Retrieval from Vietnamese History Textbooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00763)
*Ket Doan Nguyen,Minh N. H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端的框架，用于越南语低资源语言的知识图谱（KG）构建与检索策略评估。通过三阶段混合关系抽取管道实现自动KG构建，结合去重、近似跨批次搜索和大模型提取，并采用双大模型验证机制控制本体膨胀。设计了两层架构：不可合并的结构节点保留文档结构，可合并的内容节点用于知识聚合。在检索评估中，对比了自顶向下、横向和自底向上三种图遍历策略，在1,210个合成越南语查询上进行测试。实验表明，自顶向下策略结合结构信息在NDCG@10上优于向量基线4.7个百分点，证明树状结构信息对检索有效，但当查询不依赖结构上下文时性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG-based RAG方法在低资源语言如越南语中面临两大挑战：缺乏自动构建知识图谱的本体扩展方法，以及缺乏对利用层级结构的检索策略的系统性评估。因此需要一种可扩展、可控且适用于低资源语言的端到端解决方案。

Method: 提出三阶段混合关系抽取流程：1）基于并查集的批内去重；2）近似跨批搜索；3）大模型提取结合质心过滤以减少提示量，并引入五步双大模型验证机制防止本体过度膨胀。采用两层架构：不可合并的结构节点保持原始文档结构，可合并的内容节点用于知识融合。检索评估采用三种图遍历策略：自顶向下、横向、自底向上，基于109个子图生成的1,210个合成越南语查询进行测试。

Result: 从近400页越南高中历史教材中构建出包含750个节点和4,341条语义边的树状知识图谱，本体类型从40种稳定增长至41种，实现可控扩张。在检索任务中，自顶向下策略结合结构信息在NDCG@10上比向量基线提升4.7个百分点；结构信息在需要上下文依赖的任务中表现优越，但在无需结构信息的查询中反而降低性能。

Conclusion: 树状结构信息能够显著增强检索效果，尤其在需要理解层级关系的查询中，但其有效性依赖于查询的上下文需求。该框架为低资源语言的KG构建与高效检索提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Hierarchical Knowledge graph (KG)-based retrieval augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful approach for supporting large language models with structured knowledge. However, there are primary challenges: (i) the lack of methods for automatic KG construction using ontology expansion for low-resource languages such as Vietnamese, (ii) the absence of systematic evaluation for knowledge retrieval strategies leveraging the hierarchical structures. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end pipeline for KG construction and retrieval strategies evaluation. In the KG construction, we employ a three-phase hybrid relation extraction pipeline: intra-batch deduplication via Union-Find, approximate cross-batch search, and LLM extraction with a centroid filter that reduces prompts combined with a five-step dual-LLM validator to prevent bloated ontology. A two-tier architecture consists of unmergeable structural nodes to preserve the document structure and mergeable content nodes. The retrieval evaluation consists of three graph traversal strategies: Top-Down, Horizontal, and Bottom-Up, which are evaluated on a synthetically generated benchmark of 1,210 Vietnamese queries from 109 subgraphs, categorized by five query directions. In this paper, we construct the tree knowledge graph from Vietnamese high school History textbooks (nearly 400 pages) to produce 750 nodes and 4,341 semantic edges with controlled ontology growth from 40 to 41 types. Among experimental graph traversal strategies, the Top-Down strategy with structure surpasses the vector baseline by 4.7 percentage points in NDCG@10. As a result, tree-structural information provides valuable information beyond flat cosine similarity but degrades performance when the query does not require structural context.

</details>


### [363] [DiagEvo: Diagnosis-Guided Self-Evolution via Hierarchical Error Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00768)
*Xincheng Wei,Yifan Ding,Yoshua Li,Dongsheng Ma,Rongxiang Weng,Xunliang Cai,Wenjian Ding,Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiagEvo是一种基于自洽性诊断的自我进化框架，通过分析求解器的历史失败记录，构建层次化错误原因记忆库，并动态调整问题生成策略，实现无需外部任务资源的高效自进化。其核心创新在于利用求解器自身失败数据指导后续训练方向，显著提升多模型在九个基准测试上的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自对弈方法缺乏对求解器推理弱点的针对性引导，导致性能停滞或下降；而传统引导方法依赖外部任务资源，难以在封闭自演进循环中应用。本文旨在设计一种基于内部失败历史的自主引导机制，以实现更有效的语言模型自我进化。

Method: DiagEvo包含诊断器和挑战者两部分：诊断器从求解器失败历史中提取重复错误原因，构建层次化错误原因记忆库，按自洽性标记为‘活跃’或‘掌握’；挑战者根据记忆状态与出现频率，平衡针对特定错误原因的问题生成与自由探索；采用双置信度过滤机制保留中间难度问题，确保训练质量。

Result: DiagEvo在三个不同规模的求解器（Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、OctoThinker-8B）上均超越所有基线，在九个基准测试中平均准确率最高达57.4%，其中在五个数学推理任务上达到72.3%准确率，比R-Zero高出4.5个百分点，比DARC高出1.1个百分点。消融实验验证了层次化记忆与双置信度过滤的关键作用。

Conclusion: DiagEvo成功证明了仅依赖自对弈过程中产生的内部信息即可实现有效且可扩展的自我进化，为无外部监督的语言模型能力提升提供了新范式。

Abstract: Self-play is an effective paradigm for language-model self-evolution, but without guidance, solver performance can plateau or decline across rounds. Unguided methods steer question generation with signals such as difficulty, learnability, or diversity. These signals keep questions challenging and varied but do not specify which unresolved reasoning weaknesses later rounds should target. Guided methods obtain direction from external task resources, including human examples, document corpora, or specified difficulty targets, and therefore rely on task information supplied outside the self-play loop. We show that the needed direction can instead be derived from the solver's own failure history. We introduce DiagEvo, whose diagnostician extracts recurring error causes from this history and stores them in a hierarchical error-cause memory. The memory groups related causes under skill nodes and tracks each as Active or Mastered according to self-consistency on targeted questions. The challenger uses these states and recurrence counts to balance cause-targeted generation with free exploration. Double-confidence filtering retains intermediate-difficulty questions only when the most common solver answer has a clear vote lead. DiagEvo derives its curriculum from information produced during self-play, without external task resources. With the default 4B diagnostician, DiagEvo outperforms every baseline in mean accuracy across all nine benchmarks for each of the three solvers: Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and OctoThinker-8B. On Qwen3-8B, it reaches 72.3% mean accuracy across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, 4.5 percentage points above R-Zero. Its mean accuracy across all nine benchmarks is 57.4%, 1.1 percentage points above DARC. Ablations show that the hierarchical error-cause memory and double-confidence filtering both contribute to these gains.

</details>


### [364] [When Features Become Instances: Inverted Contrastive Learning for Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00782)
*Utsab Ghosh,Roshni Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICLFS proposes a feature-wise contrastive learning framework for unsupervised feature selection by leveraging representation consistency. It inverts the data matrix to represent features via sample-profile vectors, constructs masked positive views and shuffled negative views, and uses an InfoNCE objective to learn consistent projector-space representations. Feature saliency is determined by embedding magnitude, refined via Laplacian-Gated Ranking Correction to suppress redundancy. ICLFS outperforms existing methods on 10 of 12 benchmark datasets in clustering accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing unsupervised feature selection (UFS) methods rely on indirect structural criteria like similarity, locality, or reconstruction, which may not effectively capture feature utility. This work aims to reformulate UFS as a representation learning problem over features, using consistency across structured perturbations as a more principled criterion.

Method: ICLFS inverts the data matrix so each feature is represented by its sample-profile vector. It creates multiple masked positive views and a shuffled negative view. A projector learns representations that remain consistent across these views under an InfoNCE loss. Feature importance is inferred from projector-space embedding magnitudes, further refined using Laplacian-Gated Ranking Correction to remove local redundancies.

Result: ICLFS achieves the highest clustering accuracy on 10 out of 12 benchmark datasets compared to classical and neural baselines, demonstrating strong performance and robustness. It remains competitive on the remaining two.

Conclusion: Feature-wise contrastive representation consistency provides a powerful alternative to traditional UFS formulations based on neighborhood, cluster geometry, or reconstruction. The proposed ICLFS method effectively identifies informative features through consistent representation learning and ranking refinement.

Abstract: Unsupervised feature selection seeks a compact subset of informative features without access to class labels, making feature utility difficult to define. Existing UFS methods therefore rely on indirect structural criteria, such as similarity preservation, locality, sparsity, cluster geometry, or reconstruction quality. In this paper, we instead study UFS through representation consistency and propose Inverted Contrastive Learning for Unsupervised Feature Selection (ICLFS), a feature-wise contrastive framework that reformulates UFS as a representation learning problem over features rather than samples. ICLFS first inverts the data matrix so that each feature is represented by its sample-profile vector, then constructs multiple masked positive views together with a shuffled negative view, and learns projector-space representations that remain consistent across these structured perturbations under an InfoNCE-based objective. Motivated by recent findings that cosine-based and InfoNCE-based training affect embedding norms, we use projector-space embedding magnitude as the saliency signal for ranking features. The resulting norm-based ranking is subsequently refined through Laplacian-Gated Ranking Correction, which suppresses locally redundant candidates while preserving salient ones. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets show that ICLFS achieves the best clustering accuracy on 10 datasets against both classical and neural baselines under the standard clustering-based UFS evaluation protocol, while remaining competitive on the other two. These results show that feature-wise contrastive representation consistency provides a strong and effective alternative to neighborhood, cluster, and reconstruction-based UFS formulations.

</details>


### [365] [StudyBench: Can Self-Evolution Squeeze Textbooks for Olympiad Capability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00787)
*Yinghao Chen,Zixi Chen,Bingxiang He,Ziqing Qiao,Huan-ang Gao,Yinuo Xu,Yuxin Zuo,Zeyuan Liu,Yuhao Zhan,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StudyBench是一个受控的物理基准测试，用于直接衡量自我演化方法将训练材料转化为可迁移问题解决能力的效率。它包含应用集（评估吸收能力）和迁移集（评估迁移能力），实验发现现有方法在应用集上的改进很少能迁移到更难的迁移集上，存在显著的引导差距和计算饱和现象，表明当前瓶颈在于方法本身而非数据或算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对自我演化方法从原始训练材料中高效学习并获得可迁移问题解决能力的直接度量手段，亟需一个可控且可衡量的基准来推动该领域研究。

Method: 提出StudyBench基准，通过设计应用集（教材难题）和迁移集（奥赛级难题）来分别评估模型的吸收能力和迁移能力，并在三个基础模型上对代表性自我演化方法进行基准测试。

Result: 多数方法在应用集上的提升无法有效迁移到迁移集；最强方法仅能利用少量由上下文引导所释放的能力，且所有方法均在未耗尽算力前达到性能饱和，表明当前瓶颈是方法缺陷而非数据或算力限制。

Conclusion: StudyBench为自我演化研究提供了清晰、可控的测量目标，将原本开放的研究方向转变为可量化推进的科学任务，未来工作应聚焦于突破方法本身的局限。

Abstract: Humans need to study only a handful of well-written textbooks to master a discipline and attempt its hardest problems. We argue that an ideal self-evolution method should share the same property, that is autonomously learning from raw training material for transferable problem-solving capability. However, we still lack a direct measurement for it. We introduce StudyBench, a controlled physics benchmark that directly measures how efficiently a self-evolution method converts training material into capability. We organise the test set into an Application Set, consisting of difficult textbook problems and evaluating absorption ability, and a Transfer Set, consisting of olympiad-level problems and evaluating transfer ability. Benchmarking representative self-evolution methods across three base models, we find that improvements on the Application Set rarely translate to the harder Transfer Set. A guidance ablation exposes a Guidance Gap: even the strongest method closes only a small fraction of what the same material unlocks when supplied as in-context guidance. Besides, every method hits a Compute Plateau, saturating well before exhausting its compute budget. The remaining gap is therefore a method problem rather than a data or compute problem. By offering a clean and controlled benchmark, StudyBench turns self-evolution progress from an open-ended pursuit into a measurable target for future research. Our code is released at https://github.com/thunlp/StudyBench.

</details>


### [366] [Towards a Reliable and Practical Eval Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00805)
*Emma Thuong Nguyen,Abhishek Ghose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端的评估流水线，结合评估检查清单创建与学习聚合机制，以提升大模型评判者之间的一致性及与人类判断的准确性。该框架还提供自一致性、解释性和预测不确定性，实证验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多关注评估可靠性的单一方面，未能满足实际开发中对评估全面性的需求，因此需要一种整合多种要求的完整评估方案。

Method: 构建评估检查清单，并采用学习聚合方法处理清单响应；引入自一致性机制、解释生成和预测不确定性分析，形成完整的评估流程。

Result: 实验表明，该框架显著提升了不同大模型评判者间的一致性，同时提高了评估结果与人类判断的一致性，具备良好的实用性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的端到端评估管道有效解决了大模型软件系统在开发流程中对高质量评估的需求，为评估可靠性提供了全面且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based software systems increasingly require effective "evals" as quality gates in the development lifecycle. However, existing work typically addresses individual aspects of eval reliability rather than the full set of practical requirements. We present an end-to-end eval pipeline that combines eval checklist creation, with learned aggregation for checklist responses, to improve agreement across LLM judges and accuracy against human judgments. The framework additionally pro- vides self-consistency, explanations, and prediction uncertainty, and we empirically demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [367] [One Policy, Any Budget: Internalizing Budget-Aware Search via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00813)
*Xiaowei Sun,Jin Li,Yili Hong,Yikun Fu,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnySearch提出一种框架，使单一策略能在任意预算约束下进行预算感知搜索。通过训练架构和课程强化学习，在第一阶段注入显式预算状态并使用结构化推理提示引导高效分配；第二阶段移除架构，让代理在自适应采样的预算约束下自主运行。复合奖励函数结合答案准确率与预算效率，动态调整效率信号权重，实验表明该方法在七个多跳问答基准上优于基线，泛化到未见约束，且工具利用率高、词元开销小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在固定预算下训练，无法适应部署时变化的约束条件，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: 采用两阶段训练：第一阶段通过显式预算状态注入和结构化推理提示训练代理；第二阶段移除训练架构，利用课程强化学习在自适应采样预算下实现自主运行。复合奖励函数融合绝对与相对信号，动态调节效率与准确率的权重。

Result: 在七个通用和多跳问答基准上均超越基线，跨预算尺度表现优异，能泛化至训练范围外的未见约束，工具使用效率高且词元消耗低。

Conclusion: AnySearch实现了单策略在任意预算下的自适应搜索，具备强泛化能力与高效性，为大模型搜索代理提供了灵活可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning has enabled LLM-based search agents to invoke external tools, existing methods train under fixed budgets and cannot adapt when constraints vary at deployment. We propose AnySearch, a framework that enables a single policy to perform budget-aware search under any budget constraint through a training scaffold and curriculum reinforcement learning. In the first phase, we train the agent with explicit budget state injection and structured reasoning prompts that guide efficient allocation under linearly decaying budgets. In the second phase, the scaffold is removed and the agent learns to operate autonomously under adaptively sampled budget constraints, matching inference conditions. Both phases are optimized with a composite reward that couples answer accuracy with budget efficiency through absolute and relative signals, where an adaptive weight amplifies the efficiency signal for high-accuracy queries and attenuates it for low-accuracy ones. Extensive experiments on seven general and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that our method outperforms baselines across all budget scales, generalizes to unseen constraints beyond the training range, and achieves superior tool productivity without excessive token overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/xwsun01/AnySearch.

</details>


### [368] [AnalysisBank: An Expert Analysis Pattern Library for Financial Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00818)
*Yajing Yang,Yunshan Ma,Kelvin J. L. Koa,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出AnalysisBank，一种基于分析而非结构的财务报告生成方法，通过将专家报告提炼为可复用的分析库（包含数据信号、分析动作和来源段落），在推理时匹配输入信号并应用相应分析动作生成报告。实验表明，该方法在多个金融基准上显著提升了新颖且数据驱动的洞察比例（1.7-3.7倍），且该范式可推广至科学写作领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前财务报告生成多依赖结构化模板或主题划分，缺乏对数据深层洞察的挖掘；作者认为应从分析层面出发，基于数据信号与专家分析逻辑生成内容，以提升报告的深度与创新性。

Method: 提出AnalysisBank框架，将550份专家报告中的分析过程抽象为‘数据信号-分析动作-原文片段’三元组，构建可检索的分析库；推理时根据输入数据信号匹配对应分析动作，动态组合生成报告。

Result: 在两个金融文本生成基准上，使用四种LLM模型，AnalysisBank使报告中新颖、数据驱动的洞察比例相比传统结构化基线提升1.7-3.7倍；跨领域实验显示该方法在科学写作中也具泛化能力。

Conclusion: 财务报告生成应从结构性生成转向分析性生成，AnalysisBank通过可复用的分析知识库有效提升内容质量与创新性，且该范式具有跨领域适用潜力。

Abstract: We argue that financial report generation should operate at the analytical rather than structural level, composing content from data-derived insights rather than high-level topics or sections. To this end, we propose AnalysisBank, which distills expert reports into a reusable library of Analyses, each pairing a data signal, an analytical move, and the expert span it was derived from. At inference time, AnalysisBank matches input signals to library entries and applies the retrieved moves to compose the report. A study of Analyses distilled from 550 expert reports reveals a heavy-tailed distribution of 47-52 signal types spanning 13 move types. On two financial benchmarks across four LLM backbones, AnalysisBank increases the proportion of novel, data-grounded insights by 1.7-3.7x over structural-level baselines. Transfer to scientific writing suggests that the distinction generalizes beyond finance. Code and the distilled Analysis library are available at https://github.com/yajingyang/AnalysisBank.

</details>


### [369] [Polished but Unresolved: Identifying Late-Stage Pressure States in Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00823)
*Haoyang Chen,Yi Liu,Jianzhi Shao,Xiaozhou Xu,Zhe Sun,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了长时序工具使用代理在决策何时最终提交答案时面临的晚期压力状态，即代理倾向于在关键约束未解决的情况下提交看似完整且精致的答案。通过训练线性探测器识别隐藏状态中的压力信号，并利用激活干预验证其影响，发现调整隐藏状态可改变压力评分并影响继续使用工具或提前提交的决策。进一步实验表明，清晰的约束和明确的动作映射能缓解压力。基于此，提出轻量级的Probe-Sensed Pressure Relief (PSPR)插件，在中等压力下施加压力缓解，在高风险下转向结构化组织。多基准测试结果表明，该方法显著提升现有代理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序工具使用代理不仅需要搜索与规划，还需判断何时最终提交答案。然而，代理常因晚期压力而过早提交不完整的答案，导致任务失败。因此，亟需理解并缓解这种压力状态，以提升决策质量。

Method: 首先训练线性探测器识别隐藏状态中的压力信号；随后通过激活干预沿压力方向调整隐藏状态，观察对压力得分及工具使用行为的影响；再通过控制上下文操作，分析约束清晰度与动作映射对压力的缓解作用；最后设计PSPR插件，根据压力水平动态应用压力缓解或结构化组织策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，PSPR方法在多个长时序任务基准上均能一致提升现有代理的性能，有效缓解晚期压力带来的负面影响，提高任务完成率与输出质量。

Conclusion: 晚期压力是影响长时序工具使用代理决策的关键因素。通过识别并干预压力信号，结合约束清晰度与动作映射优化，可显著提升代理的鲁棒性与表现。PSPR作为一种轻量级、自适应的压力缓解机制，为构建更可靠智能代理提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-use agents need not only to search and plan, but also to decide when to finalize. We study late-stage pressure states, in which an agent is biased toward submitting a final answer that appears complete and polished while key constraints remain unresolved. We first train a linear probe to show that this pressure state is identifiable from the agent's hidden states. Then, we use activation interventions along this pressure direction and find that shifting the hidden states changes both the pressure score and whether the agent continues tool use or submits early. Through controlled context manipulations, we further see that the pressure is mitigated by constraint clarity and action mapping. Based on these findings, we propose Probe-Sensed Pressure Relief (PSPR), a plugin that applies lightweight pressure relief direction under moderate pressure and moves to structured organization under high pressure risk. Experiments on multiple long-horizon benchmarks show that our method consistently strengthens existing agent methods.

</details>


### [370] [FLaG: Frequency-Domain Latent-attention Gated Pooling for Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00831)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Xueli Wang,Xiwen Gong,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Ruochi Zhang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLaG是一种可插拔的聚合模块，通过在傅里叶域重新表达编码器输出来改进传统池化方法。它利用rFFT谱的实虚部拼接表示非冗余频谱，通过可学习的潜在查询总结频谱令牌，生成样本相关的通道门控，并重建调制后的令牌表示用于下游聚合。在多个任务中表现优异，包括抗菌肽活性预测、图像分类和语言任务，尤其在AMP相关任务中显著提升性能。机制分析显示低频敏感性主导多数层，高频敏感性随层数增加，且存在肽特异性位置响应。残差门控放大频谱通道但保持低频能量主导，而潜在交叉注意力实现样本与物种特定的频谱分配。总体上，FLaG为蛋白质、视觉和文本表征提供了可迁移的频域聚合偏差，其优势依赖于主干网络和下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有池化方法仅在原始令牌空间操作，难以捕捉跨序列或跨模态的频率结构信息，限制了表征聚合的效果。为提升聚合能力，需引入对频域特征的建模，以更有效提取关键模式并增强泛化性能。

Method: 提出频率域潜在注意力门控池化（FLaG），将编码器输出转换至傅里叶域，使用实虚部拼接表示rFFT谱，引入可学习的潜在查询对频谱令牌进行摘要，设计样本条件化的通道门控机制，并通过重构调制表示实现高效聚合。

Result: 在四种抗菌肽（AMP）预测设置中，FLaG取得最高宏平均Spearman相关系数、最低RMSE和最高Recall@50；在CIFAR-10上达到最高top-1准确率；在七项语言任务中的五项取得最佳平均指标，尽管均值池化在STSBenchmark上仍最优。

Conclusion: FLaG通过引入频率域建模，提供了一种通用且可迁移的聚合策略，适用于蛋白质、图像和文本等多种模态数据，在不同主干网络和任务下展现出显著优势，其性能提升源于对频域结构的有效利用与自适应门控机制。

Abstract: Token aggregation converts token-level representations into fixed-dimensional sample representations, but most pooling methods operate only in the original token space. We introduce Frequency-Domain Latent-attention Gated Pooling (FLaG), a plug-in aggregation module that re-expresses encoder outputs in the Fourier domain before final pooling.
